Chapter 401: Provisional Recognition

“These guys…” Hu Li rigidly glared at the dragonman youngster’s group, hatefully gnashing his teeth.

In this two versus one, he had been firmly winning and was about to turn that advantage into victory in but a moment.

In the future, it would have been a dazzling military success that could have been flaunted to all.

But a disruptor appeared.

Not one, but three silvers!

Hu Li had to use his full strength to take on two. Now there were five against him…

This was not things becoming difficult for him, rather he was courting death.

“What’s more, there is a quasi-gold level among them.” Hu Li’s primary attention was focused on Zong Ge; he was more afraid of him.

After all, before all this, he, Zong Ge, and the rest had been trapped in the auction hall.

He saw with his own eyes that Zong Ge’s silver battle qi contained a trace of gold light within it.

“Boss, we shouldn’t attack! They just broke out of the port battlefield, and that guy, Long Fu, is too ferocious! He fought Er Gua Zi in an all out clash, yet that only weakened him a little.” At this time, an underling delivered this first hand information.

Hu Li’s heart and head jolted.

“Right, Shi Qi is quite strong, if Long Fu isn’t stronger than him, how could he be the commander?”

“Retreat!”

Hu Li loudly ordered.

“The boss is wise!” The frogmen all let out a breath.

Pirates fought to get richer.

The frogmen were already very discontented.

When they came ashore to Two Eyes Island, they first planned to set up arrangements near the port, waiting for the chance to steal the Vinegar Jar.

Although squads were arranged, they had only stolen a few things before meeting Qing Xin and Men Shi.

Hu Li had led his people against these people for revenge’s sake.

Their vengeance hadn’t been achieved when the dragonman youngster and his troops barged in.

The enemy was too strong.

The frogmen had to bury the hatred in their hearts.

The Applaud Pirates retreated.

The dragonman youngster’s group didn’t pursue either.

It wasn’t worth fighting these pirates to the death.

“The Applaud battle squad…when will my Quick Knife battle squad reach that scale?” Zong Ge sighed.

“The Applaud Pirates, the Stench Pirates, and the Lightning Pirates are equally famous, each capable of moving unhindered through the seas, all have strength.” Chi Lai also sighed ruefully.

Qing Xin and Men Shi gave their thanks.

“Thank you for getting us out of that, Commander Long Fu.” Men Shi walked to the dragonman youngster, knowing who the leader was.

Before the auction, he had come to Two Eyes Island to gather a lot of information.

“I and my clan owe you a favor, no matter when it is, I and my clan will welcome you as guests.” Men Shi looked sincere.

The dwarfs rushed forth, exclaiming admiration for the dragonman youngster’s group.

When the latter arrived, they had pressured the frogmen pirates into retreating.

Although the dragonman youngster only attacked a few times, his imposing fierceness overturned the will of the people.

This world belonged to the strong.

Worshiping the strong was universal and prevalent.

Although these dwarfs had been cheated by humans, the dragonman wasn’t considered a human.

Therefore, the dwarfs’ favorable impression of the dragonman youngster wasn’t diminished.

The dragonman youngster looked at the two with a smile.

He had specifically come to help Qing Xin.

But outwardly, he was merely marching and happened to come this way.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had no close connections with Qing Xin, Men Shi, or anyone else here.

“I saw you guys fighting.”

“This time, I come bearing a heavy responsibility from Hun Tong.”

“We have to go to the underground depths and destroy the enemy’s casting.”

“You two are silver levels, come fight with us.”

The dragonman youngster deliberately gave an invitation mainly so his assistance had an excuse.

Qing Xin and Men Shi suddenly looked confused.

They were actually quite baffled.

They didn’t have good relations with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, why would they help them?

So, this was the reason why!

Upon facing the dragonman youngster’s direct invitation, the two silvers both looked distressed.

Qing Xin slowly shook her head, “I am in a bad state, it’ll be difficult to continue fighting. Many apologies!”

Men Shi pondered, then replied, “If you meet danger, I and my clan will brave it no matter what it is. But since this is for Hun Tong, we won’t do so, for we dwarfs pay particular attention to clear grudges!”

“Yeah.”

“That’s right!”

The other dwarfs loudly echoed.

Although they hadn’t been abducted here, they had been sold as slaves here.

It would be strange if these dwarfs had a good opinion of these bandits of Two Eyes Island.

These responses were unexpected.

The dragonman youngster nodded, “I will respect your decision, and won’t insist.”

“I regret that I cannot fight alongside you both.”

Seeing the dragonman youngster’s lenient attitude, Qing Xin, Men Shi, and the others relaxed.

After all, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ robust strength was before them.

At this time, Cang Xu said nearby, “Commander Long Fu, thanks for saving our people. Surely Commander Long Fu saw that we purchased these humans at the auction.”

He had changed clothes and was disguised as he looked during the auction.

When Qing Xin saw Cang Xu, her eyes suddenly contracted.

Men Shi coldly snorted, his expression wary and his heart fearful.

At the auction, Zi Di and Cang Xu worked together to hold the most limelight.

They had substantial wealth, and their strength was also considerable.

Because without that strength, how could they hold such astonishing wealth?

The dragonman youngster was displeased and immediately questioned Cang Xu, “They were fighting Hu Li for a long time, why did you not act? But now that it’s safe, you’ve come to take things over?”

The dragonman youngster displayed malice for Cang Xu.

Men Shi couldn’t help but feel closer to the dragonman youngster.

At the auction, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had stood off against Zi Di and Cang Xu.

Men Shi couldn’t help but feel anger towards a common enemy.

He looked at Qing Xin, deciding to say, “My people are all free, yet my comrades in arms are still slaves, this won’t do!”

When the dwarfs thought of that, they decided to get Qing Xin and the rest out of their predicament.

The dragonman youngster shook his head and said to Cang Xu, “I look down on those who tell half-truths and take advantage of others.”

“What happened to all of your prestige during the auction?”

“We’re both humans, yet when Two Eyes Island was attacked by fishmen, I didn’t see you fighting anywhere.”

“Do you not have a gram of righteousness within you?”

Chi La nodded heavily.

The dragonman youngster’s demand for an explanation was what he wanted to express.

The dragonman youngster moved to look at Qing Xin, “Magician Qing Xin, what is your decision?”

“If you want to cast aside slavery, I Long Fu wish to help you do so.”

“Hehehe.” Men Shi laughed heartily, if he wasn’t so short, he would have wanted to clap the dragonman youngster’s shoulder, “Commander Long Fu said what I wanted to say. Count me in!”

Qing Xin’s gaze tightened, “Thank you both.”

“Indeed, nothing is more important than freedom.”

“Wati a moment.” Cang Xu raised both hands above his shoulders, “I think there is a misunderstanding between us.”

“Miss Qing Xin, we purchased you because we noticed you and did so in order to save you.”

“This is the same motive Lord Men Shi had when he rescued his clansmen.”

“We are level seven elders of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, and Miss Qing Xin is a Wisteria Merchant Alliance elder too.”

“Most importantly of all.”

“Miss Qing Xin, perhaps you might have guessed who we truly are.”

“We are on your side; you are one of us.”

“I believe I can prove this.”

After saying this, Cang Xu took out an alchemy device.

“My companion is a bit tied up for the time being, but she wants to contact you urgently.”

“She also asks that you talk to her alone.”

“Qing Xin skeptically took the communication device and walked to a corner.

She turned on the communications device and heard Zi Di’s eager voice, “Sister Qing Xin, it’s me!”

Qing Xin’s eyes suddenly widened, “Zi…”

A moment later, she realized that wasn’t appropriate and instantly covered her mouth.

“The person in the room, was it really…you?” She looked pleasantly surprised but still had a trace of doubt in her.

“It was me, sister Qing Xin. Due to things beyond my control, I cannot expose my identity. But I had to save you. I never thought I’d see you at the auction. I thought you died.” Said Zi Di.

She had a bellyful of words to say.

Qing Xin sighed, “If I hadn’t played dead, how could I escape?”

“Becoming a slave was also unexpected.”

“I really didn’t expect that I’d run into you here.”

Zi Di interrupted Qing Xin’s lament, “Don’t elaborate things right now, first you need to board a ship and leave.”

“We have stolen a magic ship. It is called the Iron Lump, and it has already reached shore.”

“My companion will lead you there.”

Qing Xin was stunned for a moment, then replied, “Wait a moment, can we not take the others with us? To some degree, they met disaster because of me. They are kindhearted people, especially the mother and child, who normally looked after me.”

“Understood.” Said Zi Di, “Then please take them with you, older sister, board the ship together.”

Zi Di had seen this coming.

If Qing Xin wasn’t worried about these people’s safety, she would have used her superior mobility to escape already.

It was because she had seen it that Zi Di had intentionally stolen the Iron Lump.

This magic ship was more than enough to accommodate Qing Xin and her people.

“Wait a moment, before I board the ship, I still need to see you once again.” Replied Qing Xin.

Zi Di expressed her awkwardness, “Older sister Qing Xin, I can’t come right now, but I understand your misgivings.”

“As such, ask some questions about us, older sister.”

“I will immediately answer you, to make clear that I am me.”

Qing Xin nodded, then asked a few questions.

Zi Di’s answers were not only snappy, but they also pointed out the falsehoods Qing Xin deliberately manufactured.

Qing Xin finally believed that it was Zi Di, “Little Zi Di, I’ll bring my people to you now!”

Qing Xin returned to the dragonman youngster, Men Shi, and the others.

Cang Xu smiled, then faced the dragonman youngster and dwarfs, “See, were my words not correct?”

Qing Xin now had a vastly different expression.

Qing Xin nodded to Men Shi and Long Fu, “Thank you both for upholding justice, they really are close friends of mine!”

Chapter 402: Shipwrecked Barracks

“Since that is so, we will go our separate ways! I still have my mission.” The dragonman youngster immediately took his leave.

“Commander Long Fu, please allow me to express my thanks in the future.” Qing Xin stated her gratitude to the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster didn’t turn around, he simply waved his hand, “Don’t take it so seriously, I was merely passing by.”

Such forthright presence increased the Men Shi’s opinion of the young man.

A dwarf asked Men Shi, “What will we do next?”

Qing Xin gave an invitation to Men Shi, “Perhaps we can board the ship together.”

“It has already come ashore for us.”

The dwarfs were very interested.

Both had become slaves, then fought alongside each other, thus they believed Qing Xin.

Qing Xin looked at Cang Xu, “Excuse me, is there enough space for them on the ship?”

Cang Xu’s eyes narrowed.

He immediately sensed that this was Qing Xin probing into him.

If they really wanted to cause Qing Xin harm, they would likely refuse the dwarfs onto the ship.

After all, Men Shi and the other dwarfs had some strength.

“I didn’t expect that after saving Qing Xin, we also gained some dwarfs too?” Cang Xu was secretly happy.

This clan of dwarfs had lived alone deep in the mountains, they were innately simple and honest, their awareness was limited, and their grievances were obvious.

“Perhaps they will be the workers of Zi Di’s envisioned alchemy factory?”

The necromancer nodded on the spot, “The ship has plenty of space! Dwarven friends, we welcome you warmly!”

And so, the two groups parted ways.

The dragonman youngster continued to forge ahead.

Qing Xin, Men Shi, and the other people followed Cang Xu, seeing the Iron Lump when they arrived at the shore.

“Isn’t that the Iron Club Mercenary Corps’ flagship?” Men Shi was bewildered.

Cang Xu replied, “Commander Tie Bang lost his life and Commander Beng Dai was injured when they forced their way into the Vinegar Jar.

“Before that, the Iron Lump had fallen into fishman hands.”

“I commanded alchemy puppets to kill the fishmen and take it back.”

Men Shi’s expression eased a bit.

Not everyone was Zi Di or Hun Tong who both knew the ins and outs of the entire battle.

While Men Shi had been fighting Hu Li’s pirate group, he had been ignorant of the state of the other battlefields.

He led the dwarfs, Qing Xin, and the other humans onto the Iron Lump.

The Iron Lump was dripping with blood, corpses were lying about, and there were sword traces everywhere, it was clear there had been a desperate battle here.

“Sorry, there wasn’t enough time to clean up these fishmen corpses.” Said Cang Xu.

The swordsmen guard puppets weren’t good at cleaning up battlefields.

They were good at fighting, and if they were forced to clean up this magic ship, they would damage it greatly.

Specialization.

Therefore, alchemy puppets were organized by categories and many types of models.

The basic maintenance puppets the survivors had were barely suited to clean up battlefields.

As for the deep sea salvaging puppets, they were made to bring back dead ocean beasts or gather seafloor coral and plants in the ocean.

But the basic maintenance puppets had been exposed and had since been used to maintain the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ordinary ships.

Zi Di was cautious and didn’t reuse those puppets here.

To avoid people associating them with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Men Shi carefully observed the dead fishmen and quickly found that they were mainly slain by the swordsmen guard puppets.

The other surrounding battlefield traces also proved Cang Xu’s words – he had retaken the magic ship from the fishmen and not from the Iron Club Mercenary Corps.

The difference between these two things to Men Shi was like night and day.

Men Shi relaxed his mind and began to trust Cang Xu.

He said to a subordinate, “You guys go with Qing Xin first.”

“I am a little worried about Commander Long Fu.”

Destroying the fishman’s casting will be very dangerous.”

“I can’t help them, but I can guard places near them.”

“If Long Fu and the rest encounter danger, I can support them. If that happens, then I can repay their kindness while not helping Hun Tong at the same time.

When the dwarfs heard this, they all shouted that they wanted to go, so they could personally repay that kindness too.

But Men Shi rejected them, “You are too weak, don’t be a burden!”

The dwarfs were aggrieved, but they were forced to concede to the facts.

Cang Xu didn’t anticipate Men Shi’s choice.

Men Shi’s wholeheartedness to repay the debt of gratitude caused the necromancer to have a whole new level of respect for him.

“I don’t have any reason to dissuade you, Lord Men Shi.” Cang Xu said, “Please be at ease, your people will be safe on this ship.”

“We won’t be leaving Two Eyes Island completely either.”

“After all, the fishman main army and pirates are in the seas, it isn’t safe at all!”

“We will continue to observe and await the conclusion of this battle.”

“Truth be told, we think Hun Tong has a better chance, it seems he hasn’t used his full strength yet.”

Men Shi jumped off the Iron Lump and followed the road he had taken.

The Iron Lump slowly went offshore.

Meanwhile, another magic ship was also moving calmly.

It was the Vinegar Jar.

Xi Dai Zi had replaced Yao Guan Zi as the controller of this magic ship.

Er Gua Zi was fighting Song Shou nearby in a mighty battle.

Xi Dai Zi decided to move the Vinegar Jar from where it was and broke away from the port.

The Vinegar Jar staying here would only encumber Er Gua Zi and cause the latter to worry more as he fought.

Xi Dai Zi smoothly left the port.

Song Shou did try to stop it, but Er Gua Zi was stronger than him and entangled him.

Xi Dai Zi not only sailed off, but he also took the remaining fishmen at the port with him too.

Thus, the Vinegar Jar had three silver levels and about 250 troops in it.

The fishmen had lost far more troops at the port than the humans did.

The Vinegar Jar took these troops with it, then charged the city guard barracks.

The dragonman youngster clearly wanted to destroy the casting underground, but Xi Dai Zi didn’t choose to pursue them.

The dragonman youngster’s ferociously powerful “final form” had driven Xi Dai Zi into hiding and inflicted great wounds and terror onto Peng Ding Zi and the fishman knight.

If he decided to pursue, wouldn’t he be throwing his life away when he fought the dragonman youngster?

Xi Dai Zi naturally didn’t want to commit this folly.

Concerning the underground casting matter, he knew some inside information about it.

“This isn’t our business.”

“The most important thing to us is retaking our homeland!”

“By eliminating the city guard barracks, we can wipe out a barrier to the main force.”

“In fact, this is the best way we can contribute at this time.”

Xi Dai Zi sighed to himself.

He never felt this powerless.

This kind of powerless frustration not only came from his prebattle wounds that made him incapable of displaying his entire strength but more came from the fact that the enemy was too fierce.

The dragonman youngster’s ring mounds had left a deep imprint on his mind.

“Even if I was at peak condition, I wouldn’t be able to defeat him.”

The Vinegar Jar’s movement was followed by many eyes.

The Applaud Pirates.

“Boss, the ship is escaping!” An underling shouted loudly.

Hu Li grumpily slapped their head, “Idiot, are you blind? It’s a big ship; you think I can’t see it?”

His good thing had been wrecked by the dragonman youngster, and now he was still feeling embittered and wronged.

“Boss, what are we doing next?” The second mate Bei Xiang asked, “I fear we have stolen the least amount of stuff.”

Hu Li didn’t treat his second mate as impulsively as the rest.

He restrained his bad temper, looked at the Vinegar Jar’s “beautiful” figure, and after pondering for a moment, still decided, “Catch up with the ship!”

“If I can steal that ship, will there be no island we cannot ransack?”

“You guys need to look at things in the long term!”

Hu Li didn’t give up hope on seizing the Vinegar Jar.

Meanwhile, Zi Di was using an alchemy device to inform the dragonman youngster.

“The Vinegar Jar has broken away from the port and is now charging at the barracks?” The young man’s gaze tightened, “A little strange, according to common sense, they should be coming to stop us.”

Zong Ge analyzed, “There are two possibilities.”

“First is that the fishman caster is self-assured, they aren’t worried at all that we can stop them.”

“The second possibility is that it isn’t them.”

“If they were from the Blue Green Coral clan, then this branch clan would be extremely powerful.”

“With this kind of power, they wouldn’t have been slaughtered and expelled by Hun Tong.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, “So, they are actually different, causing their tactics to not agree.”

Upon saying that, the dragonman youngster looked at Chi Lai, “What do you think?”

Chi Lai pondered on it, then said, “I don’t suggest we help the barracks, despite our strength surpassing the fishmen in the Vinegar Jar.

“Regardless of whether our enemy’s goals are the same, preventing the magician from using their magic is absolutely more important right now.”

Chi Lai had just joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and still didn’t consider himself a part of them.

At the same time, he was a believer in the Justice God and felt strongly in walking the path of righteousness and was willing to sacrifice himself to do so.

However, in this first round of talks, the dragonman youngster had gained a deeper understanding of Chi Lai.

The dragonman youngster didn’t say much more, instead he waved his hand and shouted, “Speed up! Advance!”

Soon after, the barracks came into Xi Dai Zi’s view.

“There is only 27% left in the magic power reservoir.”

“More critically is that seawater needs to be replenished greatly, every water shell needs to consume a lot of it.”

“I have to reduce the bombardment frequency.”

“Let’s first try smashing in, use the Vinegar Jar’s defense to try breaking into the barracks.”

Xi Dai Zi looked boorish but was quite refined. After a spot of reflection, he drove the Vinegar Jar into the barracks’ gate.

Rumble.

The gate collapsed.

A light cannon beam came directly at it.

It was the barracks’ sole light cannon turret.

As for the barracks, it was nearly devoid of people.

Because Hun Tong had ordered that the entire garrison be dispatched with the pharmacist to disrupt the underground casting.

Only the light cannon tower was manned with a few people. With them controlling it, normal enemies couldn’t approach.

The light cannon hit the Vinegar Jar, but it only shook the hull and didn’t prevent it from moving forward.

Although the light cannon was penetrating and very accurate, it lacked kinetic energy.

Unlike artillery shells that had substance and impact force.

Xi Dai Zi was pleasantly surprised.

He didn’t expect the barracks to be so empty.

He continued to drive the Vinegar Jar in, and with a bump, knocked down the light cannon turret and all of the people inside to the ground, then proceeded to grind them underneath it.

“The barracks have been taken!” The fishmen’s morale went up.

Xi Dai Zi also took in a breath of joy, “Our flagship doesn’t have a traditional bow or aft, it looks like a water jar, thus, with the hole angled to be in the back, the light cannon can’t shoot into it.”

“If that wasn’t so, it would have been far more dangerous.”

Chapter 403: Sewer March

The dragonman youngster’s group reached their destination.

It was a tower.

The tower had a door plate, station 3 was written on it.

The tower’s door was completely wide open, with a city guard soldier standing on either side of it.

Seeing the dragonman youngster’s group approaching, they promptly ran to greet them.

The city guard was being led by the auxiliary commander and pharmacist, and because of the close distance, they had entered the underground before the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps got there.

After the dragonman youngster agreed to be hired by Hun Tong, the latter immediately notified the city guard.

“The Lord Officer has intentionally waited for everyone to catch up.” The city guard soldier lead the way ahead.

The tower interior was simple and had a round pillar standing in its center.

Around the pillar was a revolving staircase.

Everyone followed the stairs down to enter the new island’s sewers.

The sewers were three meters tall and five meters wide, thus, the tall and sturdy dragonman youngster felt cramped when he entered it.

Every so often, there were fluorescent kelp growing on the sewer walls.

The kelp emitted a light blue light that offered a faint brightness.

The dragonman youngster observed and noticed that the sewer was filled with traces of recent construction, it was evident it had just been completed.

The city guard soldier presented it at his own accord, “Two Eyes Island is a coral cage ball tree, and it is always growing slowly.”

“When it sinks into the sea, it will grow very quickly.”

“When it is floating on the surface and absorbing sunlight, it will grow far slower.”

“Therefore, when the new island’s sewer is excavated, they dig as much as possible, so as to not be forced to dig more because of the growing coral in the future.”

Everyone followed the sewer.

Hun Tong had labored to build up the new island, the sewers extended in all directions and didn’t only have a single level to the system.

There were three sewer levels in all.

Regardless of which sewer level it was, all were very expansive.

The dragonman youngster’s group noticed that the further they descended, the tighter the defenses.

At the third level, not only were there alchemy monitoring devices, but there were also long tracks of many traps.

The city guard explained, “After Hun Tong captured this place, fishmen often snuck onto the island to cause havoc.”

“They know a secret spell that can influence Two Eyes Island to make temporary underground passages in it.”

“To guard against these enemies, we built these sewers to defend against raiding fishmen.”

“This time, the fishmen that snuck in must be at a depth deeper than the sewer’s third level.”

“Alas, if only we were able to detect them in advance.”

The group of people turned a few times through the watercourse before finally arriving at an underground cavity.

This was the base station.

The base station had a small magic power reservoir and ten alchemy puppets.

These puppets were primarily maintenance types, used for daily sewer cleaning.

There were also two battle puppets.

The main model was the Sliding Track Honeycomb Crossbow type.

This crossbow arrow was as big as a horse cart and could quickly slide across a track. The honeycomb looking object could shoot out several hundred arrows within a brief window, instantly exploding with formidable firepower.

When the auxiliary city guard commander saw the dragonman youngster, he promptly walked over and smiled, “Glad you all came.”

“We are sure to engrave everyone’s help in our memories!”

“You’ve really helped us out of a bind.”

The auxiliary commander was a mere iron level, and he was very self-aware of that.

The silver level pharmacist and alchemist had suffered a crushing defeat against the purple scaled fishman.

The pharmacist alone attempting to find trouble with her now would bring about his own destruction.

Furthermore, the traitorous Shi Fa who had teleported away had likely converged with the purple scaled fishman already.

The auxiliary commander could be said to be anxiously awaiting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

At long last, their hope had arrived.

The dragonman youngster smiled back, “We are humans and people of the empire, dealing with the fishmen is the righteous way! Of course, the other important reason is that Feudal Lord Hun Tong’s high price truly touched us.”

The city guard burst into laughter.

The dragonman youngster’s frank manner immediately won over the auxiliary commander and the pharmacist.

In fact, the pharmacist already had a good opinion of the dragonman youngster.

Because during the auction, the dragonman youngster bravely stepped forward many times to publicly stand off against Zi Di and Cang Xu.

Now that the dragonman youngster had led his troops here to block the casting, the pharmacist’s good impression had deepened further from the former’s honesty, duty, and acceptance of responsibility.

A harmonious relationship and mood was naturally good for this recently established alliance.

At that moment, a rumble came from both sides of the passage simultaneously.

Something was quickly approaching!

Seeing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps on guard, the pharmacist explained at once, “Don’t be tense, that was from us.”

“While we were waiting, we were transferring all the troops we could to us.”

Just as he said that several alchemy puppets walked in, now visible to everyone there.

These alchemy puppets were shaped like spiders. They had long slender legs that were tipped with spiral drill bits and sawtooth wheels lining their joints.

“They are subterranean patrol puppets.”

“The Feudal Lord purchased them from Crown City for a large sum.”

“In fact, the sewer blueprints and alchemist came here by Crown City’s invitation.”

“These subterranean patrol puppets are responsible for defending the levels below the sewers.

“These are the Feudal Lord’s treasures, every one of them has a considerable construction cost, and aren’t deployed often ordinarily, and if they get damaged, they can’t be repaired.”

“In other words, the current situation is very urgent, the Feudal Lord is willing to use anything now.”

The pharmacist sighed.

He was a silver level and knew more secretive information than the auxiliary commander.

The dragonman youngster nodded.

He knew of Crown City.

It was known as Machine Crown City, a place where alchemist masters gathered in the present age. It had many alchemy workshops, alchemy schools, and was best at building machines.

Hun Tong had traveled far to Crown City so he could invite alchemy masters and others, along with spending large sums building the sewer system, it could be clearly seen that he invested his heart’s blood into Two Eyes Island yet was willing to part with that investment.

Zong Ge also seemed to ponder.

The port and city guard barracks battlefields were in a state of emergency, the fishmen were rushing in like the tide, and Two Eyes Island’s future appeared dark.

Yet these alchemy puppets hadn’t been dispatched.

“Hun Tong is still holding back!”

This was evidence of that conjecture.

This was the city guard.

“It is unreasonable for these people to be waiting here for us.”

“Even though they are two cowardly to advance, Hun Tong is paying close attention and can order them to suicide charge.”

“But Hun Tong didn’t do that.”

“However, according to common sense, the enemy’s large-scale magic must be prevented at all costs.”

“That said, Hun Tong has formidable methods too that haven’t been used yet.”

“Furthermore, he is very confident that when these trump cards appear, they will secure his victory!”

The subterranean patrol puppets were under the pharmacist’s control.

The pharmacist directed them to dig down.

When the subterranean patrol puppets dug, their limbs withdrew into their bodies, then they morphed into the shape of a textile shuttle.

All of their limb tips are set at their foremost point, allowing them to easily drill through the ground.

The sawtooth disks along their edges rotated rapidly, they could not only cut obstacles around them, but they could also eject out the earth and rock fragments created during the digging behind the puppets.

“Let them take the lead.” The pharmacist laughed.

“We also have some detection type alchemy puppets; they will go second.” Said the dragonman youngster.

“That’s wonderful.” The pharmacist smiled cheerfully.

The dragonman youngster then said, “Next, we will walk in, you behind us.”

The pharmacist and the auxiliary commander both grinned widely.

This was their greatest wish.

It just wasn’t good to bring up directly.

He didn’t expect the dragonman youngster to make arrangements this way.

“Commander Long Fu really dares to take it upon himself!”

“Running into someone like him is truly lucky.”

The two people sighed to themselves, and their opinion of the dragonman youngster increased further.

Because of the casting, the purple scaled fishman’s location was very visible, like a firefly at night.

The subterranean patrol puppets digging constantly in a straight line, charged in first.

Slam.

Not long after, they encountered a magic trap.

The magic trap triggered an explosion.

The subterranean patrol puppets were expensive, and the explosion merely blackened their surface.

The pharmacist appeared grave, “Encountering a magic trap here. I fear there are many more!”

Zong Ge suggested, “We should slow down.”

“Some magic traps can be triggered with direct collision, but there are many more types of trigger mechanisms.”

“Although the situation is urgent, we should go steady and strike hard.”

“I believe that of the magic traps the enemy has deployed, there are sure to be those that activate upon sensing life aura.”

Zong Ge’s words had to be pompous, while in reality he wasn’t thinking that.

Zong Ge’s true opinion was that since Hun Tong had been holding back and wasn’t worried, while the city guard was cowering and unwilling to take the lead, then the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as an outsider and hired help, shouldn’t be rushing.

Zong Ge used a look to communicate with the dragonman youngster.

The two had a mutual understanding, thus the dragonman youngster nodded at once and asked the pharmacist if he had any method of detecting magic traps?

The pharmacist looked distressed and claimed he was a magician that only made medicine.

The dragonman youngster frowned, “You are our only caster, only you can sense these magic traps.”

“Being a magician doesn’t mean you can detect magic traps. In fact, many battle skills are able to target these magic traps.” The pharmacist grimaced.

“Cough, cough, cough.”

He suddenly coughed, and clutched his chest, “I was gravely wounded in the last battle, it’d be difficult to offer assistance in this respect.”

The pharmacist’s heart was filled with cowardice and guilt, he didn’t dare to face up to the dragonman youngster.

He had outnumbered the purple scaled fishman in the last fight yet still lost. To save his tiny life now, he no longer wanted to fight.

The dragonman youngster sighed, “Since that is so, then we will have to use alchemy puppets to find them as best as possible, but if that happens, then our marching speed will be too…”

The pharmacist and auxiliary commander nodded repeatedly, the latter even said, “We cannot fall into the enemy’s evil schemes! If the magic traps kill or injure too many, even if we get to them, we will lack the power to fight.”

Their reactions were actually within the dragonman youngster’s expectations.

He deliberately asked because he wanted to obtain that answer.

Although Hun Tong had offered a high price, the dragonman youngster didn’t want to exchange his troops’ lives for these riches.

Chapter 404: Shi Fa’s Sacrifice

The subterranean depths.

An expansive underground space.

The cave’s four walls, ceiling, and floor had all kinds of magic runes drawn into them.

These magic runes made up a temporary magic formation.

Some runes were bright and some were extinguished, the magic formation’s radiance moved like the ocean tide, traveling up and down, appearing and disappearing.

The one presiding over this magic formation was the purple scaled fishman.

While the actual caster was Shi Fa.

He still looked human, and at this moment, his brows were tightly wrinkled, his eyes were tightly shut, and sweat was dripping from the tip of his nose.

He was using everything he had to maintain the magic; it was very difficult.

While the purple scaled fishman controlled the magic formation, her spirit connected with Shi Fa to assist him as best he could.

This was the first time Shi Fa had used this magic and was depending on the purple scaled fishman’s spirit connection to construct the magic model and maintain its continued operation.

“A few magic traps have been activated.” The purple scaled fishman quickly communicated to Shi Fa through their spirit connection.

Shi Fa was alarmed, “The enemy has emerged so fast?”

The purple scaled fishman consoled him, “Not as close as you envision. I have many magic traps augmented by the Drifting Bottle spell.”

“These magic traps are like a message in a bottle, they are unlikely to stay in place, and will instead move slowly about, spreading around in a disorderly fashion.”

“This will hide the magic traps better and will also guard against enemies sneaking in from any direction while covertly approaching them.”

When Shi Fa heard that, half his concerns were relinquished, then he asked, “Who is coming?”

He knew the magic traps and this magic formation were connected, and when one of them were triggered, they would bring back information.

These included the enemy’s appearance, and lots of their life aura and sounds.

Putting these pieces of information together, one could construe intelligence on the enemy.

“Take a look.” The purple scaled fishman shared the information through the spirit connection.

Shi Fai’s glabella knotted, “Shit.”

His heard gushed with strong fear, “It’s actually the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“Ignoring the gold levels, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is the worst foe.”

“Our arrangements cannot stop him.”

The purple scaled fishman nodded, expressing acknowledgement, “I also think that too.”

They were the most crucial fishmen right now.

Naturally, they were given precious information too.

The dragonman youngster’s performance at the port battlefield, the devastation of the Vinegar Jar, and his cowering of the three silver level fishmen were known to them.

The dragonman youngster was a most thorny existence to the two magicians.

Because his red dragon scales not only protected him from physical attack but also magic too. It was an exceedingly outstanding composite defense.

Ordinary magic merely tickled the dragonman youngster.

What also truly threatened the magicians was the dragonman youngster’s speed.

“His final form gives him too much strength and speed.”

“This is also the underground.”

“In terms of movement speed, we are no match for him.”

“If he discovers us, fleeing is nearly hopeless.”

The purple scaled fishman sighed her analysis.

Shi Fa nodded, “I have spoken to Long Fu, he is an outstanding leader and has a tenacious determination.”

“Hun Tong hiring them to stop us is indeed a wise decision.”

The dragonman youngster hadn’t even arrived, yet he had brought immense pressure on them.

The purple scaled fishman exposed her helplessness. At that moment, she had a premonition of defeat, “What to do? Our magic just activated.”

“Then speed it up!” Shi Fa glared.

The purple scaled fishman shook her head, “This is as fast as it can go.”

“No, it can still go faster!” As Shi Fa said that his nostrils began to leak blood.

The purple scaled fishman’s heart shook, “If you do that, you will destroy your foundation, after this casting, you will become a cripple!”

Shi Fa’s face blossomed with a smile, “So what?”

“Since the moment I became a spy, I have been prepared to make sacrifices.”

“I have also thought about how I would sacrifice myself.”

“My identity was discovered, I was surrounded, I failed to break out, and then I died.”

“Perhaps it would be during war, I would sacrifice myself to establish an advantage on the front lines, or to destroy the enemy’s provisions.”

“It was also possible that I would never be used my entire life, I would die of old age as a nobody, my true self buried in the earth and covered by dust.”

“This is amazing, my battle companion!”

“I was most afraid of dying of old age, because that would be the most worthless sacrifice.”

“At this important moment, I was used, it is my good fortune.”

“Under such urgency, I have to sacrifice myself! Doing so will increase the success rate of the operation. If sacrificing a minor silver level like myself can accomplish our goal, then it would be worth it!”

The purple scaled fishman looked at Shi Fa, somewhat flabbergasted.

To be honest, although she knew spies needed to do all kinds of tricks, Shi Fa’s act of massacring fellow fishmen to obtain humanity’s trust was something she was wary of and despised.

But at this moment, those feelings and attitudes was swept away.

It was superseded by admiration and emotion!

“My friend!” The purple scaled fishman choked out, “First look for the cemetery.”

“Hai Ping was once buried there. At the time, he concealed his identity, and no one knew who he really was.”

“It has been too long, and with the coral cage ball tree’s constant growth, his grave vanished without a trace.”

“But the cemetery is a starting point, there should still be a bit of a trail left behind.”

“Even if it is the most minute trace, with this magic, it can reveal clues.”

“As long as we find a trail, we can trace it through and determine his exact position.”

Shi Fa nodded heavily, “Understood!”

Magic power surged continuously like an incorporeal tide that rolled through the entire cemetery.

There was a silver level hiding in the graveyard.

It was the grave robbing and funerary object stealing Hong Zhu.

The magic power horrified Hong Zhu.

“Why is the fishmen’s large scale magic targeting the cemetery?”

“It seems it is some kind of position searching magic?”

“I can’t stay here any longer.”

Hong Zhu didn’t know what the fishmen were really planning, but since they were searching the cemetery, this place was likely to become a new battlefield.

After Hong Zhu said that she left.

Despite knowing there were still lots of funerary objects left buried in the ground.

But she knew when to stop and could restrain her avarice.

Hong Zhu was a senior adventurer.

She had experienced many traps.

Although many traps were very rudimentary, they could trick people because they exploited human greed.

Hong Zhu’s sneaking ability was very high, and she left the cemetery without incident.

She gazed into the distance and saw the Vinegar Jar wreaking havoc in the city guard barracks.

“The city guard barracks has been destroyed?”

“The situation outside has gotten this bad?!”

Hong Zhu’s heart suddenly sank.

She had been in the cemetery by her lonesome, wantonly plundering the cemetery, unaware of the battle’s progress.

She also thought that the city guard had been wiped out and wasn’t aware that the auxiliary commander and pharmacist had left with most of the soldiers beforehand.

“The port battlefield only has two gold levels fighting in it!”

“The fishmen hold a huge advantage.”

“It’s no wonder their large-scale magic went without a hitch; no one came to stop them.”

“Two Eyes Island is in danger!”

“But how should I leave?”

Hong Zhu judged that Two Eyes Island’s future was bleak.

She was a loner and immediately thought of running off.

But how to escape?

She didn’t have a ship.

Even if she did, the port had been destroyed already.

Even if she was fortunate enough to still have said ship, Song Shou and Er Gua Zi were fighting fiercely at the port, and Hong Zhu didn’t dare get near them.

Hong Zhu couldn’t think of a good way to get out for the time being, and was forced to sneak to the coastline first, then play it by ear.

Hu Li deeply sighed.

“These humans cannot be counted on!”

“The grand Two Eyes Island’s barracks has surprisingly been wiped out.”

“Where are the barrack’s defenders? Instead of fighting, they retreated, they really made the Sheng Ming Empire lose face.”

Hu Li cursed loudly.

Naturally he didn’t consider it from the human’s point of view, rather it was because the hollow barracks was unable to weaken the Vinegar Jar.

This caused the Vinegar Jar to maintain its prior condition, giving him no gaps he could get into.

He had been tailing the Vinegar Jar, and when he saw it charging at the barracks, he was inwardly happy.

As a result, mutual losses didn’t occur, and his plan to seize the Vinegar Jar was once again postponed.

“Boss, our plan to steal the Vinegar Jar a bit improbable.”

“Yea, boss. That ship has three silver level fishmen inside, and the amount of fishmen in there outnumber us greatly too!”

“We can still rob other places!”

His pirate underlings were grumbling.

Since coming ashore, although the Applaud Pirates had plundered some things, their earnings couldn’t compare to the other pirate groups.

Their commander was dedicated to stealing the Vinegar Jar yet never found a chance to.

This in the eyes of the other pirates was him wasting a big opportunity to get rich!

Hu Li looked about, “You guys are short-sighted!”

“Just do what I tell you to do.”

“Otherwise, I will kill you!”

Hu Li suddenly erupted with vicious power, shutting up the other pirates immediately, none daring to speak any longer.

“Hahaha, too fine, I have never done such a robbery.”

“Two Eyes Island is so rich; this has been a wonderful experience. It would be amazing if every island looting was like this.”

There were pirates roaming everywhere through Two Eyes Island, some were in groups, some were alone, and all were in ecstasy.

They had a truly ample harvest.

Of them, the Lightning Pirates and the Stench Pirates were among the strongest.

After the two pirate groups left the church, they met at the city government building gate.

“You again!” Yang Cong Tou glared.

“Hey old Yang Cong Tou, you aren’t late this time.” Dian Wan made fun of him.

Yang Cong Tou coldly snorted.

The pirates had nearly plundered everything on the new island.

Some places they decided to avoid.

For example, the Flint Store had the silver Ku Feng and Mei Ban working together to protect it, a truly hard nut to crack.

After calculations, the most enticing place left was the city government building.

Dian Wan sternly said, “Since I robbed the last two places first, as my senior Yang Cong Tou, I’ll allow you to enter this one first.”

The city government building was right in front of them, but Dian Wan suddenly declined to go in modestly.

Yang Cong Tou gave a dirty look, “That’s enough! Hun Tong is a sinister person; I don’t believe his city government palace doesn’t have defenses. You want us to attack first, allow you to pick up the pieces after us?”

“Time is limited, in short, we either cooperate or I’ll throw you in first!”

Dian Wan laughed, “I have long wanted to work and fight with you together!”

Chapter 405: Hun Tong’s Hidden Trump Card

The Lightning Pirates and Stench Pirates had fought at the Wealth Church, but now in the city government building, they began to cooperate.

Pirates were like this.

Profit seekers!

But as they planned their attack, they suddenly heard fishmen shouting.

“Agm”

“Rarlgrglnanlgm”

“Anlgarlnarlnglnmarg”

“lnaglnr”

The shouts were coming like the tide, they layered upon each other in unending prestige that was growing ever vaster.

Dian Wan, Yang Cong Tou, and the rest were experienced fighters and immediately changed color when they heard it.

“How can there be so many fishmen here?”

“Those sounds, the fishmen have come ashore.”

“What is Hun Tong doing? Is the frontline dying?”

“Hahaha, if Two Eyes Island is taken back by the fishmen, the Duo Mu Clan’s reputation will be in the sewers.”

Some pirates were bewildered, others rejoicing in Hun Tong’s misfortune.

Dian Wan and Yang Cong Tou glanced at each other, both saw the other’s grave expression.

The military situation had changed dramatically.

They no longer dared to attack the city government building.

The city government building had defenses, and if it depleted them too much, even if they stole the valuables, they would soon drown in a tide of murderous fishmen.

“Go, scout out what the situation is.” Dian Wan ordered.

Yang Cong Tou also looked at his first mate, the latter immediately went to investigate.

Zi Di keeping watch in the Deep Sea Monster Fish was the first to discover the dramatic change.

She was amazed when she saw the fishman army through the projection, “What happened? How did those fishmen get ashore?”

Nothing obviously peculiar had happened.

Zi Di flipped back to a past magic image, but didn’t find anything.

These fishmen suddenly appeared on the coastline, then began charging ashore.

Now, there were large amounts of fishmen constantly appearing on beaches, following the fishmen that were shaking the skies with their rallying cries as they charged with all of their strength.

Zi Di watched closely, her expression gradually changing.

“That is an exceptionally good camouflage magic, no, it is a divine spell. “She looked at the clues, “This divine spell’s power is similar to the Pearl Bubbles?!”

Zi Di promptly informed the dragonman youngster via alchemy device about the latest battle progress.

Divine spell – Pearl Illusion.

The one using this divine spell was the naga priestess.

To do so, she didn’t hesitate to use a Pearl Bubble that had been in her collection for many years.

The deity she worshiped was Charming Blue Goddess.

But the Charming Blue Goddess’ divine artifact, the Mermaid Fairy Tale, had been lost for many years, and the sect could no longer produce them, thus they were extremely precious.

If the naga priestess wasn’t trying to protect the purple scaled fishman and Shi Fa, she wouldn’t have gritted her teeth and used it.

The divine spell “Pearl Illusion” was an excellent camouflage and deception.

The naga priestess used the divine spell on the fishman main army on the frozen land whale.

The fishman main army then quietly entered the sea and stealthily swam to Two Eyes Island.

Even though Hun Tong, Yan Ding, and the others were constantly watching, even Zi Di on the Deep Sea Monster Fish had been duped, causing them to be astonished when the fishman main army began to attack.

“I know, you believe in the Charming Blue Goddess!” Yan Ding’s eyes flashed as he blurted that out.

“Impudent, you dared my Goddess’ name like that!” The naga priestess exploded in anger, and she used a divine spell to attack Yan Ding violently.

Yan Ding urged his black cloud back quickly as Hun Tong’s clone thrusted his blade towards the naga priestess like lightning.

“I don’t think so!” The old fishman elder shouted.

The frozen land whale sprayed a stream of water.

The fishman elder rode the jet of water to Hun Tong’s clone.

Both sides ruthlessly clashed, and the clan elder succeeded in blocking the alchemy clone’s thrust.

“Let’s have a good battle this time!” The old clan elder spat out, most of the pressure on him had alleviated.

Before, his hands had been tied defending the fishman main army and he was forced to take a beating.

Now that the fishman army had gotten onto the island and launched their offensive, he was no longer worried.

The naga priestess used a divine spell to turn from defense to offense, allowed the fishman main force to use their military might, and also freed up the clan elder, doing two things at once.

This was why casters needed to be eliminated first during battles.

Compared to fighters, casters had many more tricks and after they were used, they were harder to counteract, usually costing several folds the casting cost.

Like now, when the naga priestess used a divine spell to break the impasse and reestablish the fishmen’s dominance.

The fishman main army intentionally went around the port and landed on another seacoast.

When the war began, the human troops were mainly concentrated in three places.

The auction hall, the port, and the city guard barracks.

Now, the cultivators in the auction hall had dispersed and were falling apart. Some sacrificed themselves, and others were conversely looting Two Eyes Island.

At the port battlefield, in the wake of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps breaking out, and the Vinegar Jar relocating, it had been reduced to the place where Song Shou and Er Gua Zi were fighting.

After the city guard barracks relocated almost all personnel, Xi Dai Zi and his people reduced it to rubble.

Thus, the fishman main army didn’t have any obstructions and marched straight in unchallenged.

Fragmentary resistance was instantly drowned in the surging fishman assault.

Although many of the fishmen had been slain by Yan Ding, after merging with the reserves, there were still a considerable number of them.

In terms of quantity, they had the most troops on Two Eyes Island.

These fishmen also had exuberant morale.

They were retaking their homeland for their clan.

Every fishman fighter that fell would be remembered by kith and kin for a lifetime.

Every fishman that died in battle would cause much grief and hatred.

That hatred encouraged the fishman main army morale!

“The fishmen are ferocious!”

“We can’t defend.”

Mei Ban shot several times, and after he saw the dauntless fishmen didn’t fear death, he judged that he should quietly retreat.

He had protected the Flint Store for long enough.

Inside the store, when the wandering ronin Ku Feng received Mei Ban’s message, he immediately urged, “Quickly, organize the people, it is time to leave.”

Many salesclerks still hoped for the best, and one amongst them yelled, “We’ve won!”

“Neither Dian Wan nor Yang Cong Tou dared to offend us.”

“Perhaps we can stop defending!”

Ku Feng glanced at them and coldly replied, “Then you can stay behind, I will go first.”

The salesclerks’ hearts jumped, and they changed color one after the other. None dared to stay, and all of them followed Ku Feng as he evacuated.

The fishmen had safety in numbers, and they rapidly flooded the streets around the Flint Store.

“Hahaha, our main force has come!” In the Vinegar Jar, Peng Ding Zi and the others cheered.

“Abominable!” Hu Li gnashed his teeth.

The fishman main force’s arrival had reduced his chances of seizing the Vinegar Jar to near zero.

“We must go!” Hu Li waved his hand, swiftly departing with the resentful frogmen.

In front of the city government building, Dian Wan and Yang Cong Tou knew things were far from good, and they both retreated with their groups.

Pirates were slippery.

They only thought of getting rich, and stopping the fishman main force was in no way their business.

They moved as quickly as the wind.

In addition, the fishman main force’s goal was capturing Two Eyes Island, and the hastily retreating pirates were no concern to them.

Soon after, the pirates reached the water and climbed onto their boats.

These were landing skiffs and all of them sunk deeply into the water.

They were weighed down by the riches they had plundered from Two Eyes Island!

At the subterranean abyss magic formation.

“I found it!” Shi Fa suddenly opened his eyes; they were full of pleasant surprise.

The purple scaled fishman’s method was very sensible.

By searching the cemetery, they found traces.

The sum of these clues led many ways.

After eliminating most of those paths, and through trial and error, Shi Fa found their true objective.

“Well done!” The purple scaled fishman cheered.

“So, it was actually there. Without this special search magic, how could we ever find it?”

The purple scaled fishman sighed, then said, “The Blue Green Coral Clan’s fishman army has already attacked the island, let’s take advantage of this golden opportunity to retrieve Hai Ping’s corpse.”

The real reason the naga priestess decided to use a divine spell to protect the fishman main army was to protect their transportation of that corpse.

·

Battle skill – Large Tentacles, stage five!

Qi You flew through the air, gold battle qi gushing all over him.

Battle qi condensed into octopus tentacles, and with gold brilliance, swept towards Hun Tong.

Hun Tong moved nimbly, leaving an afterimage as he dodged it.

Qi You used the battle skill again, and his large tentacles returned to protect him from all sides.

“Qi You, got any more tricks? If you don’t use them now, your Two Eyes Island will really come to an end!” Qi You cackled, complacent on the outside, but vigilant within.

The more he fought Hun Tong, the more he felt the latter was putting on a show, and that he was calm and collected beneath the surface.

Hun Tong suddenly stopped high in the air.

He indifferently glanced at the new island, at the fishmen army fighting all over, and also saw all of the ships sailing away with their valuables.

“It is time to attack.” Hun Tong said to himself.

The fishman main army wasn’t the main target.

Hun Tong focused more on those pirates.

He wouldn’t look on passively as these pirates ran off with all of the island’s riches.

If he missed this opportunity, hunting down these pirate groups large and small in the boundless ocean would waste an immense amount of time, energy, and so on, along with possible losses inflicted during the recapturing.

Hun Tong put away his dagger and smiled at Qi You, “You feel it, don’t you.”

“I have to thank you, Qi You.”

“Thank you for putting on this show with me.”

“Now, as thanks, as a token of my thanks, how about I give you the gift of death?”

Qi You was completely focused, his entire body tense.

A moment later, all of his hair prickled, and an alarm bell rang in his heart!

Whoosh!

A pillar of light fell from the sky.

Before Qi You could react, the pillar of light shot him head on.

With a loud bang, he was launched into the ocean, creaking a gigantic wave.

Such a startling change attracted the gazes of every party.

Hun Tong floated in the air, raised his arms, and shouted, “It is time for you to come onto the stage.”

“My flagship, I summon you!”

“Defend our riches, slaughter these bandits.”

“Glorify the Duo Mu clan!”

The light beam that shot Qi You thinned, weakened, and quickly dissipated.

From the light beam that shot through the clouds, a magic ship slowly fell.

“That’s?”

“A flying airship!”

“A gold level magic ship!”

Chapter 406: The Golden Flash!

A magic ship was hanging high in the air!

It was shaped weirdly; it looked like an enormous disk.

All around the disk’s chassis were “scales” that glittered gold.

The disk had five light cannon turrets on it, evenly distributed across its surface.

In the middle of the disk, there was its main cabin that was a prism crystal that was far more towering and gigantic than any of the light cannon turrets.

The magic ship emitted a strong gold level aura.

This was what Hun Tong had kept waiting until the right moment, the Golden Flash!

Before this, it had been hiding high in the clouds, and its first attack had shot Qi You down, stunning the entire battlefield.

At this moment, Song Shou and Er Gua Zi stopped fighting as they marveled at it.

The former was pleasantly surprised while the latter was solemn.

In the Deep Sea Monster Fish, Zi Di gazed at the magic image, her eyes glinting.

While buying treasures from the auction, she had been planning to build a flying magic ship, she didn’t expect to see one now, especially one more advanced than the one she wanted to create!

Hun Tong’s clone intensified its attacks.

The old clan elder and the naga priestess had to hold it back while paying more attention to the Golden Flash.

The card Hun Tong had been hiding was grander than anyone expected.

“A gold level magic ship is usually owned by saint level figures. Yet Hun Tong has one?”

“Abominable, he’s earned so much from Two Eyes Island these last years!”

“Hun Tong’s bloodline has reached its limit, rather than increasing his equipment or his personal strength, perhaps he thought to pool everything he had into something big.”

Everyone rolled with thoughts and constant estimations.

A gold level magic ship coming down was like a king descending to snatch away the svelte all other magic ships here.

Not just that.

It could also fly!

A flying magic ship was quite rare.

Controlling air superiority was an immense advantage.

Normal ship cannons on the sides of ships found it difficult to aim high into the sky.

Although deck cannons could shoot into the sky, there were too few of them.

Only magic could strike the Golden Flash.

But which magicians could?

Surveying the battlefield, there were no more than 10 silver level casters, two of which were dead already.

Of the gold level fighters, not all of them could fly.

Neither Er Gua Zi nor the old clan elder had flying battle skills.

Therefore, they could only look on helplessly and impotently.

It could be said that the mere fact the Golden Flash was floating at a high altitude gave it a seemingly invincible position.

Song Shou burst into laughter, “Er Gua Zi, I concede you are stronger than me, but so what? We will still be the true winners!”

Human morale soared.

Yan Ding grumbled, “Hey, if you had this kind of ship, why didn’t you deploy it earlier? Why did you trouble me so greatly until now?

Hun Tong’s alchemy clone glanced at him as it continued to attack violently.

Xi Dai Zi unconsciously held his breath.

Compared to the Golden Flash, the Vinegar Jar he controlled paled by comparison.

Xi Dai Zi had a stronger premonition – the Vinegar Jar couldn’t withstand the gold magic ship’s bombardment!

“What should I do?”

“How can I compete with that ship?”

All kinds of thoughts flashed in Xi Dai Zi’s mind.

“Damn it!”

“There’s nothing.”

“If we found out about it beforehand, we wouldn’t be so helpless now.”

Xi Dai Zi felt how sinister Hun Tong was.

He actually had his biggest card hidden in the clouds.

When the fishmen were scouting out Two Eyes Island, how could they check the sky?

They couldn’t probe that part of the new island.

This arrangement demonstrated how wise Hun Tong was.

He had chosen the time and place the auction was held very carefully.

Every time one island floated up, everything on it melted away, it was white paper he could paint with his massive construction projects.

Spies like Shi Fa couldn’t get into the new island ahead of time to investigate it.

“Cough, cough, cough.”

Qi You coughed up blood into the sea.

“A gold level turret!”

The blast he took had seriously injured him.

“The main turret has the might of at least 5 light cannon turrets!”

“Crucially, the light beam is very accurate and fast.”

“I had been entirely on guard, yet I wasn’t able to evade it.”

“This was also while I was on the defensive.”

“With a turret like that, recklessly flying into the air will turn one into an easy target.”

Qi You’s fighting spirit plummeted to the depths.

He didn’t fly back up, rather he swam through the sea towards his flagship.

Qi You was a gold level pirate boss, if he chose this, the other pirates did too.

After the pirates broke from their stupefaction, they reacted one after the other.

“Row! Row as hard as you can.”

“Go faster, faster.”

“My god, Qi You was struck down in a single shot, that ship is too formidable.”

Hun Tong smiled as his half closed eyes revealed a cold glint.

“Where do you think you are going?”

“I kindly persuaded all of you to be treated as guests.”

“But you obstinately clung to your habits and looted my domain!”

“Since you’re so undiscriminating, then you’ll leave your lives here.”

A moment later, the Golden Flash’s hull’s innumerable “scales” automatically separated themselves from the ship and flew down in a whirl.

Hundreds of these “scales” quickly hovered over every place, each of them rotating ceaselessly.

The main light cannon turret’s tip began to pour out five thick beams of light.

These beams of light imbued the 5 light cannon turrets around it.

A moment later, all of the light cannon turrets fired a light beam simultaneously.

The perfectly straight light beams first hit the nearby circular “scales”, scattering the beams into three parts.

The three light beams were far thinner and were cast at more “scales” in the distance.

By means of the “scales”, the light beams were constantly refracted and scattered to form an immense light beam network across the skies above the battlefield.

The tips of this light beam network precisely hit enemies.

Pirate landing skiffs, fishmen troops, the Vinegar Jar, and other foes all received “consideration”.

In just a split second, a thousand pirates died violently!

Many landing skiffs sank.

Cultivators ran away in a sorry state, few of them survived this danger.

The Vinegar Jar’s hold rumbled from the ruthless light beams.

The refracting light beams precisely seared holes into the Vinegar Jar.

A second later, the treasure laden pirates that were proudly departing and the tumultuous fishman army were swept away in a mighty fashion.

After another second, pirate blood sprayed into the ocean, ships sank, and the fishman army’s offensive was stopped due to massive casualties, their howls of grief ever present now.

After shooting Qi You, the Golden Flash’s second attack had instantly overturned the entire war situation!

“No wonder Hun Tong was so calm!” Zi Di looked at the magic image, turning pale, “With that magic ship, he was always in control of the entire battlefield.”

She compared the Deep Sea Monster Fish to the magic ship before her.

Both were gold level.

But utterly different.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish was a stealthy deep water submarine, while the other was an airship.

One was in the sea; one was in the sky.

In terms of applications, the Deep Sea Monster Fish had more.

Because this was the ocean.

Most natural resources were in the ocean.

Airships had to land to resupply.

“In terms of speed, the other ship hasn’t displayed anything yet, so no comparison can be made.”

“In terms of military power, the Deep Sea Monster Fish has two gold cannons and two types of special gold level artillery shells.”

“The other ship has 5 silver level light cannon turrets. The main turret is definitely gold level and serves as its main firepower.”

Zi Di calculated the firepower.

The light beam that had shot Qi You should have come from the main turret, its power was somewhat less, but its accuracy and speed were extremely high.

If the Deep Sea Monster Fish fired a gold artillery shell from one of its cannons, its power might be greater, but hitting a gold level person was quite difficult.

In terms of offense, the Golden Flash was very impressive.

With one attack, it had toppled the war situation, caused mass casualties, scored many military victories, and had done more in that single attack than Yan Ding had done the entire battle.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish had ways of wounding groups too.

It was called the Hedgehog Firepower Counterattack.

After being activated, the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s entire hull would create jet holes that could shoot out three alchemy arrows every second from each one.

With 5000 jet holes and this rapid firing rate, it could shoot 15,000 alchemy arrows per second!

“The group culling abilities of both are about equal.”

“But they have different inclinations.”

“The Deep Sea Monster Fish emphasizes defense, and through comprehensive firepower cover, it can counteract an enemy’s offensive.”

“While the other ship is all about precise attacks that focus on every light beam hitting its target. This is impossible for the Deep Sea Monster Fish to do.”

“What exactly allows the light beams to lock onto their targets?”

“Don’t tell me that ship has been outfitted with a powerful ship spirit?”

Zi Di guessed to herself.

“Its concealment ability allows it to hide in the clouds, and even the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s detection methods didn’t notice anything.”

“This ability is already very strong.”

“But it cannot compare to the Deep Sea Monster Fish.”

On this point, Zi Di was very confident.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish had formidable camouflage capabilities. It was outfitted with the Deceptive Disguise and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy formations, both the best of their type in the world.

In this respect, even if the flying magic ship was overestimated, it still couldn’t compare with the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

“The Deep Sea Monster Fish’s greatest advantage still rests with the fact that it can do alchemy.”

“Its alchemy cabin has exceptional alchemy installations; it is a mobile first-rate alchemy workshop.”

“This cannot be achieved by the other ship.”

“The two ships have very different uses.”

“The Deep Sea Monster Fish is a submarine used to conceal and camouflage as War Merchant collected resources and moved around.”

“While this magic airship must have cost Hun Tong most of his wealth, he spent that humongous sum to build a warship to be used to patrol the seas and deal with enemies.”

“According to this categorization, that magic ship’s flight speed should be decent, it should also have long distance and scale detection capabilities.”

Zi Di immediately transmitted this crucial information as well as her conjectures to the dragonman youngster.

The underground depths.

“The aura over there, two hundred meters straight ahead.” The dragonman youngster was solemn.

Zong Ge, Chi Lai, and the rest were the same.

As they were planning to storm in boldly, they got Zi Di’s information.

The dragonman youngster and the others looked at each other for a moment.

Soon after, they changed their tactics.

Since they now had the advantage, they no longer needed to risk charging recklessly.

Chapter 407: Controlling the Battlefield

Hun Tong flew to the ship and entered the main turret.

Without Qi You bothering him, he was a free man, and this process went abnormally smoothly.

On the fourth floor of the main turret, there was a control panel.

Hun Tong personally took control.

All around the control panel were crystal walls that depicted all sorts of changing magic runes.

Combined with the Golden Flash’s powerful detection methods, the walls could promptly display everything in a radius of a thousand miles.

In the Deep Sea Monster Fish, Zi Di had hypothesized close to the truth – the Golden Flash really had wide scale and long distance detection methods.

Hun Tong stood perfectly straight, his hands on the control helm, and scenes all across the battlefield depicted on the walls around him.

Countless landing skiffs were pierced by light beams, destroying a number of their hulls, causing them to quickly sink into the ocean.

The bleeding corpses of pirates followed them down.

Along with great amounts of money and valuables.

These invaluable things quickly sank to the seafloor.

However, Hun Tong wasn’t worried about that.

Those valuables in the sea could be salvaged after the battle.

“You want to plunder me, to steal my money, hehe, how could you?” Hun Tong sneered proudly.

“My detection component is the Gold Eye!”

“A gold level alchemy creation with the Wealth Church’s divine spell – Appraisal!”

It can detect every object within its range and appraise it.”

“These greedy pirates, as long as they are laden with treasures, they will be high value targets and will be firmly locked onto!”

“Thinking about it, it really is a little ironic.”

“None would expect these pirates to do everything possible, through their devastating looting, to leave a trail that attracted the Grim Reaper’s sickle towards them.”

The surviving pirates swam back towards their fleets, their lives depending on it.

The fishmen who saw this were utterly stunned.

They heard the human fighters cheering as they counterattacked in waves.

Hun Tong took a deep breath; he couldn’t help but grip the red wheel tighter.

A tight squeeze.

The ship wheel’s wooden felt even better.

By being in control of this wheel, he was in control of the entire battlefield.

“Everything is under my control.”

“The fishmen who decided to come court death and the lethally greedy pirates. Hehe, along with those incompetent mercenary corps and those selfish merchant alliances.

“This time, I won’t just finish off the troublesome Blue Green Coral fishman clan, I will also eradicate all of the pirates around the island.”

“Thanks to you all coming here to attack, I can catch you all in the same net.”

“You will all become my merits!”

Hun Tong’s eyes flashed brightly.

The most the Golden Flash could do was land on the ocean for a short resupply, but it couldn’t dive into it and fight.

The Blue Green Coral fishman clan deciding to attack the island was building towards the circumstances Hun Tong wanted.

While the looting pirates would let him free his reputation from all that tainted it and preserve his trustworthy image with the merchants and nobles.

Persuading the pirates to restrain themselves was actually him putting on a performance.

By showing weakness, he aroused the pirates’ greed.

The very first to fall into the trap was the strongest gold level pirate boss, Qi You!

“The pirates looted the entire island.”

“According to imperial law, when I kill them and fish out those valuables, they will all belong to me as spoils of war.”

“Many thanks to you for letting me seize the Wealth Church’s and every large store’s wealth, hahaha.”

“After a large fire, the prairie next year will be verdant and shine with new vitality. My Two Eyes Island will be like that too.”

“After this violent upheaval, the prior agreements with the merchant alliances will be resigned, and even the five great merchant alliances will pay me a sum in exchange.”

“After all, the contract at the time said we were to resist external enemies together.”

“But in the end, when that time came, they sailed their ships away, waded through my magic traps, and damaged our defensive line at their own accord!”

Hun Tong was not just scheming against the fishmen and the pirates, but also the Wealth Church and every merchant alliance.

His gaze deepened, as if recalling something, “The empire’s order must be carried out.”

“In the future, Two Eyes Island will become a supply line hub, therefore it has to be transformed!”

“From a commerce island into a transportation base.”

The empire’s order was not within Hun Tong’s plan.

For these last years, Hun Tong had been constantly thinking of how to transition himself.

Previous business contracts shackled him, and he also couldn’t unilaterally alter those contracts. As an island feudal lord, his background was far lesser than the six great merchant alliances.

In the future, he also had to ensure the transportation base’s security!

However, the merchant alliance ships were sure to be traveling to and fro, and according to the trade agreements, he couldn’t constrain them, this was a conflict with his administration.

If there was a breach of contract, he had to compensate with large amounts of money and even lose parts of his territory.

But by doing this, the merchant alliance will pay him money!

“In the following negotiations, I will seize the opportunity to shred those business contracts and free myself.”

“I didn’t expect the Wealth priest to die in battle.”

That had been his partner in the establishment of Two Eyes Island.

His sacrifice made Hun Tong sigh incessantly.

“Thinking about it carefully, that’s fine.”

“When a new priest is dispatched, there will be a vacuum period.”

“That will be the optimal moment to strengthen my control.”

“I will seize the opportunity to introduce other churches and divide the religious aspects here into two, allowing them to check and balance each other.”

Hun Tong was not a cleric, he was a noble, a representative of monarchical power.

Monarchical power and divine power were unified but were also opposed to each other!

“Wonderful, the future really is too wonderful!”

When Hun Tong thought of these merits, he couldn’t help but chuckle.

In his calculations, the future would be bright, the previous haze all cleaned away.

The round “scales” flying in the air evaded attacks as they changed their angles.

These were parts of the gold module – Flying Whirling Compact Discs.

The main turret poured light into the five light cannon turrets again.

The light network was formed once more.

The Golden Flash’s third bombardment!

For a moment, fishmen and pirates howled and screamed.

The casualties were innumerable.

The gold level magic ship could slaughter as many enemies as it liked.

The two bombardments had inflicted casualties on most of it already.

It was difficult to imagine that this was once a tremendous military that could sweep across the entire island.

“Retreat, retreat!!” the old clan elder yelled, his eyes flowing with tears of blood.

He wanted to retake their homeland, for this he had used his full strength.

But now, the clan was nearly extinct, and taking back their homeland had become a dream.

The Golden Flash fired a fourth time!

This time, Hun Tong mainly targeted the ocean battlefield.

Many Flying Whirling Discs flew above the ships there.

A hundred thousand light beams fired out perfectly straight, instantly forming a forest of lights.

If the Deep Sea Monster Fish fired its cannons, it would be constrained by worries of friendly fire.

But these light cannon turrets were extremely accurate.

Especially with the Flying Whirling Discs’ help, it simply was like sticking a muzzle to an enemy’s forehead, how could it miss?

“No!!” It was Er Gua Zi’s turn to have his heart pierced with sorrow.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates were already having a hard time staying strong, as the enemy merchant ships outnumbered them and there were more silver level merchant alliance representatives than pirates of the same level.

The reason why they could last this long was mainly because the merchant alliance representatives cherished their property and were more focused on worrying about and saving their fleets.

At the same time, they also cherished themselves and weren’t willing to take risks, afraid of a gold level retaliation.

With this bombardment, the Vinegar Jar Pirates suffered a mortal blow.

Although the fishmen pirates didn’t suffer many losses.

Their ships began to slowly sink.

In a blink, this large gold level pirate group only had one seaworthy ship left.

It was their flagship, the Vinegar Jar.

In the Vinegar Jar, Xi Dai Zi’s head was filled with cold sweat.

“If the next bombardment targets the flagship…we won’t be able to hold on!”

Ku Feng and Mei Ban were dumbfounded.

Hong Zhu slowed down.

She had originally judged that the fishmen held an immense advantage, therefore she decided to go to the water and think of a means of taking a boat from the island.

But in the end, after Hun Tong unleashed his trump card, the treasure hunter discovered how wrong she was!

The ocean battlefield went silent.

The merchant alliance representatives watched the fishmen doing their best to mend their vessels and saw the fishmen corpses strewn across their decks.

Even friendly forces were astonished by the bombardment’s might.

“Hun Tong, this guy. If he had called this magic ship out earlier, we wouldn’t have suffered such immense damage!”

“He is too treacherous; this is a scheme against us.”

“Repulsive, hateful!”

At this moment, Hun Tong’s voice rang throughout the battlefield, “Everyone, if you don’t press the attack now, when will you?”

The merchant alliance representatives were startled.

Hun Tong’s tone contained a menacing implication.

With that gold level magic ship, he was the strongest force on the field.

Not a single merchant alliance representative could compete openly with the high spirited Hun Tong.

“Kill!”

“Kill those fishmen.”

“Steal their ships to make up for our losses.”

As if waking from a dream, the merchant alliance representatives rushed out of their flagships.

The Golden Flash’s bombardment had inflicted heavy losses on the Vinegar Jar Pirates, and the latter could no longer do anything to the merchant alliance ships, liberating the merchant alliance representatives.

“The Vinegar Jar Pirates are finished!” Cang Xu saw this from a distance, even though he wasn’t an accomplished military man, he immediately judged the situation correctly.

He then glanced at the orbiting “scales” in the air, causing Cang Xu to cough.

During the prior bombardments, he was very nervous and afraid that he’d become a target of them.

But Hun Tong didn’t target the Iron Lump.

Probably because Cang Xu was on the bow, looking like how he did during the auction, along with the fishman corpses left strewn across the deck, Qing Xin, Men Shi, and the other humans making it clear where they stood.

At this moment, Zi Di transmitted, “With that gold level magic ship, Hun Tong has established victory, our prior conjecture was correct, he was holding back and still had this trump card.”

“But you should still leave first, keep a distance from Two Eyes Island.”

“The Iron Lump is the Iron Club Mercenary Corps’ flagship, I am worried that after the battle, that mercenary corps will leverage its connections to pressure us into giving it back.”

“We must sail away first, it would be best if they never got the chance to do this.”
