“After so many days, there hasn’t been any response to our ransom letter. This makes me very worried, and I have a bad premonition about it.”

Chapter 408: Cang Xu’s Troubles

Zi Di’s cautious proposal was deeply approved by Cang Xu.

Mystifying Monster Island had cultivated the survivors into treating everything prudently, and keeping space to retreat was a good habit.

However, when he tried to pull the Iron Lump away from the new island, two silver levels appeared unexpectedly to bar Cang Xu’s way.

“Finally, I’ve been waiting for this! Where do you think you’re going?” One of them was a female silver level with a gloomy face and emitted a strong hostility.

Seeing this suddenly emerging enemy, Cang Xu’s complexion changed slightly, “Ya Ma?”

Ya Ma once had friction with Cang Xu and Zi Di, and after the two had defeated her, they forced her to give her auction ticket to them in exchange for her freedom.

“Because of you, I missed the auction.”

“Now, you’ve stolen the Iron Lump, you think you can just walk away from it all?”

“Hehe, in your dreams!”

“I only lost to you guys because I didn’t have enough time to cast, giving you a cheap shot.”

“This time, we’ll settle our old debt.” Ya Ma spat out.

She was a summoner.

When Cang Xu and Zi Di faced her last time, they had used fire path rings to launch an undefendable magic barrage to win.

This time, he saw that she was riding a giant flying ant she had summoned and knew she had come prepared.

The Iron Lump was already some distance from shore, and it was through riding on this giant ant that Ya Ma was able to bar Cang Xu’s way.

Cang Xu’s head wrinkled as he looked at Ya Ma, then gazed at her companion.

It was someone familiar to Cang Xu.

She was broad and fat, had an owl-like face, and was the beastman dancer Yue Ban.

Ya Ma had come for vengeance, Cang Xu could connect the dots.

But why was Yue Ban here?

Just as Cang Xu was about to speak, ready to negotiate, Yue Ban spoke first, “Magician, I have also been looking for you.”

“While doing so, I received Magician Ya Ma’s invitation to meet with you.”

“During the auction, you purchased a pair of dancing shoes, you should remember them.”

“Those Mushroom Dancing Shoes are very important to me.”

“Can you sell them to me please?”

“I need them!”

Cang Xu faintly nodded, “Mushroom Dancing Shoes…gold level equipment. Lord Yue Ban, how much do you plan to offer for them?”

“You lost to us during the auction, and those Mushroom Dancing Shoes now belong to us.”

“Now you want to buy it, don’t tell me you’ll force us to sell it at a loss?”

“What can you offer for it?”

Yue Ban was silent for a moment, “That’s right, I don’t have enough money.”

“That doesn’t matter.” Cang Xu shook his head, “This world does not value just money. In fact, there are many things money is unable to weigh in on.”

“If you can help me deal with the trouble before me, we are willing to discuss things based on your contribution and give you a discount on the Mushroom Dancing Shoes.” Cang Xu said after mulling on it.

He wanted to negotiate, turn an enemy into a friend, and bring Yue Ban onto his side.

But this move of his drew a sneer from Ya Ma.

Since Ya Ma had invited Yue Ban over, she naturally understood what was happening. If she let Cang Xu so easily convert her ally over, then she’d be quite the dullard.

As expected, Yue Ban shook her head, “Sorry, I can’t do that.”

“If I didn’t get Magician Ya Ma’s help, I wouldn’t have been able to find you.”

Yue Ban was also flying on a giant ant.

She continued, “Order cannot be overturned, I won’t bite the hand that feeds me.”

“Furthermore, my clan has a close connection with Ya Ma.”

Ya Ma smiled, “My summoned Yellow Wasp lives in Yue Ban’s hometown and has signed a summoning contract.”

“Therefore, sire don’t waste your saliva, obediently surrender without a fight and you can avoid bodily suffering.”

Yue Ban agreed, “Hand over the Mushroom Dancing Shoes and I will help you plead for leniency.”

Cang Xu laughed back, “Since that is so, then let us settle this, I hope you can treasure your time. If you wait for my companion to come and help, then you won’t have another opportunity.”

Qing Xin had already flown into the air.

Since a powerful enemy had suddenly arrived, she had no choice but to keep fighting.

Cang Xu was at a severe disadvantage.

He looked like a silver level, but that was merely a disguise, his real life level was only at the iron level.

Furthermore, he was relying on fire path rings.

His true skills couldn’t be used publicly.

“A pity that Men Shi left the ship to look for a chance to repay the commander’s kindness. Otherwise, the situation wouldn’t be this bad.” Cang Xu regretfully thought.

He didn’t use a communications device to call for help in front of his two enemies.

Because he knew Zi Di would notice the emergency here.

An unexpected fight unfolded on the Iron Lump.

The summoner Ya Ma and the beastman dancer Yue Ban versus Cang Xu and Qing Xin!

In the subterranean depths.

Bang.

An explosion caused smoke and dust to scatter about.

The dragonman youngster stormed into the center of the formation.

“This is the place the fishman caster is!”

“But where are they?”

Soon after, the pharmacist hurried in, then after using magic to examine the area, he said, “They used camouflage magic, they actually relocated before we got here.”

Zong Ge frowned, “The fishman magician using the large scale magic cannot just be bait. They must have left because they attained their objective. This facade they induced just delayed our battle with them.”

“There is nothing we can do about it.” The dragonman youngster shook his head slightly.

If the enemy wasn’t stopped, the rewards afterwards wouldn’t be much.

But for the sake of his and his subordinates’ lives, the dragonman youngster didn’t regret adopting a steady and slow strategy.

At this moment, Zi Di transmitted the latest information.

The dragonman youngster’s heart quaked, “What? Cang Xu ran into trouble?”

The young man was serene outwardly but inwardly be frowned and felt prickled.

Cang Xu was using the face of a Wisteria elder, thus, it was very inconvenient for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to help him.

“But no matter what, it is better to leave this place first and get closer to Cang Xu.”

Thinking of that, the dragonman youngster immediately ordered, “The enemy magician has left already, our operation has failed. We should immediately return topside to deal with the enemy’s defenses.”

The auxiliary commander and the pharmacist both laughed, completely at ease.

The latter believed that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps didn’t know the latest intelligence and proudly stated, “Don’t be so tense, Commander Long Fu. The Feudal Lord has truly acted, and now the situation is utterly titled towards us, we have already secured victory!”

“Oh? Really?” The dragonman youngster was startled, “Tell me now, what’s going on,”

The pharmacist joyfully recounted the tale as the group quickly made their way back.

The purple scaled fishman and Shi Fa struggled to drag a fishman corpse through a dark path.

To conceal their auras, they didn’t choose to use magic.

The fishman corpse was t he long dead Hai Ping.

Despite the years, his corpse hadn’t decayed, it was still full of flesh, seemed true to life, and he looked like he was in a deep sleep, that if he opened his eyes, he would awaken.

At first glance, the purple scaled fishman lamented, it really was the Surging Wave bloodline.

The purple scaled fishman suddenly stopped, “I smell the ocean. We are nearly off of Two Eyes Island. How are you doing?”

The purple scaled fishman threw a worried glance at Shi Fa.

Shi Fa was in a bad state.

He was deathly pale, his skin was colorless, his body was verged on collapse, he was weak all over, and his spirit was nearly spent.

Shi Fa smiled, “I’ll do my best to keep up.”

“It doesn’t matter.” He instead consoled the purple scaled fishman, “I have already performed my function.”

“This matter is finished.”

“Originally, I thought about staying behind, fighting to give you time.”

“But you were right, with my state right now, it would only give the enemy a victory and wouldn’t stall for any time at all.”

“When we enter the sea, I can use the last of my magic power to disguise myself with your aura and go another direction.”

“Good luck!”

The purple scaled fishman opened her mouth, but didn’t say anything, instead merely nodding.

They had found Hai Ping’s corpse far quicker than they had expected.

Thus, Shi Fa hadn’t sacrificed his life.

The purple scaled fishman respected Shi Fa, and when she heard Shi Fa wanted to stay behind, her advising him to come with her was due to her wanting to save his life.

Now that they were about to dive into the ocean, Shi Fa still chose to sacrifice himself in another way.

And this time, the purple scaled fishman didn’t stop him.

Because the two had received the naga priestess’ message and knew the battlefield on the island had dramatically shifted.

The fishmen were routed.

There wasn’t much time for the two to retreat!

When the two reached the end of the secret path, the purple scaled fishman quickly cast a spell that caused the vines and branches before them to slowly separate, allowing seawater to bubbling through small cracks.

As the passage expanded, so too did the flow of seawater.

The purple scaled fishman and Shi Fa were fish back in water and dove down into it.

The two looked at each other, silently nodded, and separated.

The purple scaled fishman tugged Hai Ping’s corpse deeper and spared no effort to haul it away from the island.

After a moment, Shi Fa cast a spell to disguise himself with the purple scaled fishman’s aura and swan in another direction.

“They’re out, I need to support them!” The naga priestess’ eyes flashed briefly.

But she was still entangled with Hun Tong’s clone and couldn’t free herself.

Hun Tong’s alchemy clone was unafraid of death and was very troublesome.

The naga priestess knew she wasn’t very mobile, and if she acted abnormally, it was bound to provoke the gold level magic ship’s indiscriminate bombing.

The situation was utterly in tatters, but there was one thing the naga priestess was happy about: to protect his benefits, Hun Tong hadn’t shot these gold levels yet. The bombardments were motivated by him wanting to “stop valuables from leaving and to protect his territory”.

Thus, the naga priestess looked at the old fishman elder.

The fishman elder suddenly felt his heart move, realizing it was time for him to sacrifice himself.

He deeply sighed and entrusted his walking stick to a fishman next to him.

The walking stick wasn’t just gold level equipment, it also had significant meaning.

It was the symbol of the clan elder’s authority!

Chapter 409: Yellow Nest Wasps

When the old fishman elder gave his walking stick to another, the meaning was self-evident.

“Lord Clan Elder!” The fishman confidant shouted.

The old clan elder bitterly laughed back, apologizing in a tired and aged tone, “So sorry everyone, I really was an incompetent elder.”

“Our homeland was lost by my hands.”

“I knew my own daughter had been kidnapped and was being tortured by the enemy, but I couldn’t act.”

“To save her, I didn’t hesitate to deceive our allies and violate my convictions and principles.”

“To have this chance to redeem myself in self-sacrifice really makes me fortunate.”

“Before this battle, I lay down my will.”

“Everything in this will shall be done.”

“Lord Clan Elder…boohoo.” The people choked with sobs and wept.

The old clan elder gazed hopefully at the naga priestess, “Lord Priestess, I leave my clansmen and daughter in your care!”

The naga priestess was solemn, although they had cheated her slightly, his willingness to sacrifice himself was a righteous achievement and more than made up for it.

The naga priestess nodded faintly, “With that ship present, I can only do my best.”

“Be at ease. That ship will find it difficult to attend to you soon.” After the old clan elder said this, he firmly stamped his feet and shouted the Frozen Land Whale’s childhood name.

The Frozen Land Whale was a magic beast the clan had raised.

Since the year they had been banished, the clan began to raise it, and didn’t hesitate to ration their own food, all to retake their homeland.

But Hun Tong was an excellent businessman, and he had enough money to build a gold level magic ship, which far outstripped the bred Frozen Land Whale.

The Frozen Land Whale had been trained to have a tacit understanding, and without the old clan elder saying anything, merely just stamping his foot, it knew what to do.

Racial magic – Ascending Spring!

Seawater flowed from its breathing hole and sprayed out, shooting the old clan elder into the sky.

The fishman elder flew at lightning speed as he gained altitude.

The old clan elder had kept this trick hidden, originally he had intended to find an opportunity to rise suddenly into the sky to ruthlessly attack Yan Ding or Hun Tong’s clone.

But he never found that chance.

Due to the crucial military situation, he had to use it now.

The old clan elder’s skill really went against Hun Tong’s expectations.

He hastily manipulated three of the five light cannons.

The light beams hit the old clan elder, however, he stiffly went against the bombardment and rose to the altitude of the Golden Flash, then wounded and bloody, he landed on the Golden Flash’s deck.

“There is still 75% of the magic power reservoir, that’s enough.”

“But I won’t be able to personally control it anymore.”

Hun Tong’s eyes shrank.

He couldn’t ignore the old fishman elder and let him devastate his flagship.

The Golden Flash didn’t have good defenses.

Especially when the old clan elder was staking his life on this, Hun Tong wouldn’t dare let a gold level do what he wanted!

Hun Tong prodded the magic runes, giving the Golden Flash hurried orders to continue bombarding, then immediately flew below to fight the fishman elder.

The fishman elder was unarmed, but his long green seaweed like hair and his bubbling golden battle qi danced as he caused chaos everywhere.

“Come, let us fight to the death!” The old clan elder was filled with determination, ready to die.

Hun Tong’s eyes shrank again.

The enemy before him was most troublesome.

He was a gold level too, and did not care about living.

More crucially, he was on the Golden Flash.

If anything was broken, he wouldn’t be able to repair it for the time being!

He couldn’t repair the underground patrollers, let alone the Golden Flash.

Previously, the old clan elder had to protect the fishman army from Yan Ding.

Now, it was Hun Tong’s turn to be sullen.

Bang, thump, boom.

A fierce fight on the Golden Flash unfolded.

Two old enemies pitted themselves against each other once more.

This time, it was both victory and defeat, life and death!

“Shoot them down!”

The pirates and the fishmen yelled.

For a moment, all kinds of arrows and bullets soared through the air, along with even cannons in a constant rumble.

Magic spell – Water Arrow!

Xi Dai Zi was barely able to cast.

He was in a terrible state, but the Vinegar Jar still had magic power reserves.

Battle skill – Musical Scale.

Hu Li jumped onto his musical scale, leaped into the air, and used his palm to slap down a Flying Whirling Disc.

These discs were crucial, it was through them that the light cannon towers could split their attacks in countless directions.

The discs suffered all kinds of attacks, but few of them broke.

They were gold level components.

Since they had to take the light cannon towers’ gigantic light beams, then scatter and refract them, they had to have considerable defenses.

These attacks were fruitless, but the pirates and the fishmen didn’t give up.

On the contrary, they began to attack more frantically.

Because they knew full well that if they didn’t smash these Flying Whirling Discs, they would die!

Although the Golden Flash was a better target, they couldn’t reach it.

The targets they could attack were the Flying Whirling Discs.

Only if they smashed these discs to pieces did they have a hope of surviving.

But although the discs seemed frail, few were shot down.

The main light cannon diffused light once again, and another light net bombardment fired.

But this time, the fishmen and pirates suffered fewer casualties than before.

The survivors were both startled and delighted as they reacted one after the other.

“Even if we can’t smash those disks, we can still interfere with them!”

“That’s right, continue attacking, don’t stop.”

So long as the Flying Whirling Discs were off tilt, when they refracted the light beams, its attacks would veer massively.

The discs resisted these interferences by rotating in the air.

The level of interference wasn’t that big actually.

In fact, many light beams brushed right past their targets.

However, because the light beams were very precise, and their might was concentrated, too excessively so, if it brushed past an enemy, it was enough for them to survive.

“We can live; we can survive!” A pirate roared.

The fishmen were also clamoring.

There were many unafraid of death as they gathered the scattered valuables around them.

“Mine, all mine!”

“Even if I die, I will die on a pile of gold coins.”

A pirate’s greed could be seen from this.

“Hold on, what is that?”

Many diving pirates seeking valuables were shocked when they discovered the shadows of beings in the depths of the seafloor.

These shadows looked like people with snake tails.

They were also gathering valuables!

“Naga!”

“Not only are there fishmen, but there are also nagas on the seabed.”

“They are taking our sunken treasures. Damn them, we went through great trouble to steal them!”

The pirates felt aggrieved, but they couldn’t do anything.

It was difficult for them to dive to the bottom of the seabed.

Even if they could, their strength would tumble, could they fight the naga down there?

Of course, those weren’t really naga.

They were deep sea salvagers.

When Zi Di saw valuables tumbling down into the sea, she had dispatched these salvaging alchemy puppets.

These valuables on the seafloor came from the big stores, houses, and churches the pirates had robbed.

Two Eyes Island was a bustling commerce island, and its development had led to both its officials and citizens all having great wealth.

The generous funerary objects they were buried with was an indication of that.

“I didn’t expect that there’d be easy money!” Zi Di’s mouth twitched.

This pleasant surprise was brief, as her heart was filled more with concern.

Qing Xin and Cang Xu were in a bad situation.

Ya Ma and Yue Ban were in peak condition, and genuine silver levels.

Cang Xu was an iron level pretending to be a silver level.

Although Qing Xin was a silver level magician, the long fight with Hu Li had depleted her strength, in addition, her lack of equipment left her in a sorry state.

With summons surrounding her, Ya Ma grew more confident, “It was a true disgrace I actually lost to you.”

“Are you really a silver level?”

“Are those the only fire spells you can use?”

Before, when there was friction between Ya Ma, Zi Di, and Cang Xu, she didn’t have enough time to summon anything, and had been suppressed by fire magic, unable to catch a breath.

Under that pressure, she was forced to admit defeat.

Now that she had space, she was full of mettle.

Qing Xin could only fly into the air, and repeat the tactics she used against Hu Li, constantly moving and relying mostly on evasion.

Cang Xu also used a flying scroll, and adopted evasion tactics, doing as much as he could to stall for time.

He consciously drew closer to the dragonman youngster.

When the battle began, he decided to stay far from the Iron Lump.

As for the dragonman youngster, it was troublesome for him to help out Cang Xu and Qing Xin.

After all, during the auction, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps constantly antagonized Cang Xu and Zi Di, winning the good graces of the masses and building a hostile relationship with them.

Now, if the dragonman youngster interfered, and helped Cang Xu, then that behavior would be inconsistent with his previous actions.

It would surely provoke suspicion!

“Oh dear, this really won’t do, I have no choice but to act.” The dragonman youngster sighed to himself as he hastened his pace.

Compared to external suspicion, Cang Xu’s life was of course more important!

“Where do you think you are going?” Ya Ma laughed.

She had grown impatient with this chase and decided to cast.

A magic formation formed in the sky.

The disk shaped formation rotated once and turned into a bright circular gate.

From the round light gate flew out an elephant sized wasp.

The wasp was covered in tiger-like skin, black and yellow alternated across its body, its compound eyes were pitch black and covered in dark luster, it looked very strong.

Most unusual was its rear section.

Its rear was enormous and looked somewhat deformed.

Its rear was five long pillars, and at their ends were many small holes.

The wasp emitted a silver level aura.

The small holes also emitted many strange auras too.

When Ya Ma summoned the silver level magic beast, she immediately pointed her finger at Cang Xu and ordered, “Blow him up.”

Buzz, buzz, buzz…

A moment later, many alchemy wasps flew from those holes.

The wasps looked like a black cloud covering Cang Xu.

Cang Xu immediately felt his head go numb, and as he cursed to himself, he tore open a scroll.

The scroll’s magic took effect, creating ten phantoms of himself that flew in all directions.

The wasp cloud was forced to divide itself into eleven, each part pursuing a phantom of Cang Xu.

Bang, slam, thump.

After chasing down their target, the small wasps exploded one after the other.

The phantoms instantly blew up, turning the surrounding houses and streets into ruins in a surge of smoke and dust.

Cang Xu’s real body was quickly found.

The remaining wasps buzzed over, surrounding him.

“Not good!” Zi Di turned pale when she saw this.

At this moment, a fatal crisis was looming over Cang Xu!

Chapter 410: Saint Corpse Exposed

The yellow nest wasps were magic beasts from the Wilderness Continent.

Wasps built nests in the ground.

These yellow mud nests were usually two to three meters tall.

When the hive is in danger of being destroyed, the wasps would fuse with the hive to form one gigantic wasp.

From this giant wasp’s tail, one could still make out the form of that hive.

According to the wasp colony’s scope, the hive’s quality, the state of the wasps at time of fusion, and so on, the life level of the giant wasp could range from bronze, iron, and silver, it was inconsistent.

The highest it could attain was the gold level.

The Yellow Nest Wasp that Ya Ma was ordering around was not gold level, just a silver level one.

To capture such a magic beast was quite troublesome.

Ya Ma had relied on the local beastman clan’s help and was fortunate enough to capture one alive.

Through meticulous training, this silver level Yellow Nest Wasp was the strongest of its level.

Because Ya Ma had spent a hefty sum on giving it surgery, remolding the Yellow Nest Wasp through alchemy.

It wasn’t uncommon for alchemy to be used to augment a magic beast to make it stronger.

Ya Man not only transformed the Yellow Nest Wasp’s body, but also made it produce large amounts of special small wasps.

These wasps were alchemy things and ranged from the size of a pinkie to an adult human’s fist. Their quality included those of ordinary, bronze, iron, and silver levels.

Now, many of these alchemy wasps were surrounding Cang Xu, blocking any way out for him.

Most of them were ordinary level, but there were over a hundred bronze and iron level ones, along with a dozen silver level ones.

Ring of Fire.

Cang Xu used his fire path rings to conjure a ring of fire that expanded around him.

Some of the alchemy wasps were burnt away, while many of them were pushed away.

But the Ring of Fire’s power quickly diminished, and most of the wasps grew near Cang Xu.

At that moment, Zi Di nervously wished she was by Cang Xu’s side.

Despite knowing that even with her strength, the two couldn’t deal with the massive yellow wasp swarm, her desire to share her comrade’s burdens and urge to defy a powerful enemy was growing from her heart.

The dragonman youngster was quickly rushing over.

But there was still some distance from them.

He couldn’t blink over in an instant.

“Is there no other way?” In this crisis, Cang Xu immediately prepared to cast necromancy.

Although normally he kept himself hidden as much as possible, in this life and death crisis, he wasn’t able to do anything else.

Without life, any possible future was beyond reach.

“If I use necromancy I still have hope!”

Cang Xu planned to brave death and risk it all.

His body hid many ghosts in it, and when they were unleashed to deal with these alchemy wasps, it might give him a chance to escape.

However, it was only a chance.

These alchemy wasps from a certain point of view greatly restrained ghosts.

Because these wasps weren’t alive and didn’t have souls.

Ghosts targeted normal living beings and held an advantage over them. But against these alchemy wasps, that advantage didn’t exist.

But when Cang Xu was about to stake it all and expose himself, an unexpected figure joined the battle.

A person took out a small ball and smashed it into the ground.

Ding.

The small ball exploded with lightning.

The lightning looked like an inflating balloon as it quickly expanded and swept across the alchemy wasps all around them.

The wasps struck by the lightning were gravely perturbed.

The silver level ones recklessly soared about, their bodies swaying. The iron level wasps vibrated and were barely able to stay airborne. The bronze and ordinary level ones fell to the ground with a light clang.

“Move!” The person shouted, “My Electric Interference Sphere won’t last.”

Cang Xu hadn’t foreseen this happening, and he blankly stared back.

“Hong Zhu?” After he recognized them, he responded by quickly flying to them.

His savior really was Hong Zhu.

The fishman’s casting had alarmed her.

She didn’t dare dig for more treasure in the cemetery and snuck away.

After judging the situation, she immediately went to the coastline, looking for a chance to escape the island.

She first noticed Ya Ma.

Ya Ma was flying a giant ant, a chance for her to escape the island.

However, just as she wanted to strike up a conversation with Ya Ma, Ya Ma decided to find Yue Ban and invited her to deal with Cang Xu together.

Hong Zhu was excellent at stealth, and she quietly made inquiries, hearing that Cang Xu now had the Iron Lump.

Hong Zhu had a new idea.

“Rather than dealing with Ya Ma and paying a hefty price to make her summon a flying ant, it would be better to leave the island on board the Iron Lump!”

The flying ant couldn’t contend with maritime wind and waves, it could only fly for a limited time, and her safety would be in Ya Ma’s hands.

The Iron Lump could vie with rough waters and was naturally far stronger than a flying ant.

Furthermore, if Hong Zhu helped Cang Xu, taking the initiative to sell her services, then she might ride on the Iron Lump for free and even gain Cang Xu’s gratitude.

During the auction, not everyone felt malice for Cang Xu and Zi Di.

At least, not Hong Zhu.

Because Cang Xu and Zi Di had paid a hefty sum for her goods, gaining her many gold coins.

During the auction, Hong Zhu wanted to contact Cang Xu and Zi Di.

When Ya Ma saw her good time had been wrecked, she instantly turned gloomy and shouted at Hong Zhu, “Hong Zhu, what is the meaning of this?”

Yue Ban was also chasing Cang Xu.

Hong Zhu laughed as she jumped up and used a battle skill to stop Yue Ban.

“Hong Zhu, are you helping this person? Speak! What is your relationship with them?” Ya Ma controlled the alchemy wasps and attacked again.

Most of the wasps that had been disabled had recovered and were coming back over.

In fact, Zi Di and Cang Xu also found it strange that this famous treasure hunter was helping them.

You know, there had been dealings between them in the past, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had worked with Hong Zhu to hunt fish.

Zi Di and Cang Xu were currently appearing as Wisteria Merchant Alliance elders and had no such connection with Hong Zhu.

Hong Zhu laughed, “Two Eyes Island is in a desperate situation, yet you only care about your personal grudge. Say, could you be part of the fishmen or pirates?”

Hong Zhu was very forceful and threw a basin of sewage at them.

Ya Ma turned pale.

Yue Ban shrugged her shoulders and indifferently replied, “I am a beastman, what business do I have with the humans?”

She attacked again.

Hong Zhu blocked her.

The two were evenly matched, and for a moment, it was hard to tell who was better.

While on the other side, Cang Xu and Qing Xin teamed up to deal with Ya Ma.

Cang Xu let out a breath, feeling the pressure on him diminish.

Before this, Yue Ban charged ahead as a fighter and made it difficult for the two of them to cast spells.

But now, with Hong Zhu pinning down Yue Ban, and Ya Ma’s summoned beast not being that wise, Cang Xu and Qing Xin were in a much better state.

Zi Di also guessed at what motivated Hong Zhu, “As a treasure hunter, Hong Zhu is perhaps thinking of using this chance to build a relationship with us.”

“We were quite prominent during the auction; everyone considered us rich and imposing.”

“When Hong Zhu goes looking for treasure in the future, she will definitely make a move.”

“But Two Eyes Island is already dying, and after this battle, no pirate will dare to sell things here.”

“This will be detrimental to Hong Zhu.”

“Therefore, she decided to find an inroad in us, something that will benefit her greatly.

Zi Di communicated her guess to Cang Xu.

Cang Xu agreed with it.

But he also kept his vigilance against Hong Zhu, to prevent Hong Zhu from pulling a fast one over him.

After all, both sides didn’t understand each other.

Regardless of why Hong Zhu had decided to interfere and disrupt things, Cang Xu finally felt the crisis of exposing himself as a necromancer pass.

The Golden Flash fired another massive light beam bombardment.

The pirates loaded with valuables were specifically targeted.

The two pirate commanders, Dian Wan and Yang Cong Tou, were struck with many light beams, causing them to cough up blood.

“Quick, return to the flagship!”

“Throw most of them away, damn that Hun Tong, I must save the rest.”

When it came to their own earnings, the two silver level pirates showed complete toughness.

The equally famous Hu Li was in a much better situation than them.

Because the Applaud Pirates had stolen less, for they had wasted time.

Zi Di also looked somewhat unsightly.

This new volley of light beams had directly penetrated the seawater, destroying many deep sea salvaging alchemy puppets.

“Wait a moment, I’ve found something.”

Zi Di surveyed the overall situation, and through the many magic images, she made out a pattern.

Her eyes flashed and a divine light seemed to appear from her, “Hun Tong is fighting the old clan elder, no one is controlling his flagship.”

“It seems the flagship is bombarding whoever it finds has the most wealth?”

“Therefore, my deep sea salvagers attracted its gaze.”

Zi Di’s discovery needed to be validated.

Meanwhile, the purple scaled fishman dragging Hai Ping’s corpse was swimming above the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

To protect Hai Ping’s corpse as much as possible, the purple scaled fishman had crushed a Pearl Bubble!

The Golden Flash didn’t detect her.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish was the same.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish’s camouflage ability was top notch and was pretending to be a piece of seafloor reef, therefore the purple scaled fishman didn’t detect anything peculiar.

“The mission will be completed.”

“When I return with Hai Ping’s corpse, the reward for this merit might cause my clerical status to rise a level!”

“This group of idiotic humans…”

The purple scaled fishman was already envisioning her beautiful future.

Then at that moment!

The Deep Sea Monster Fish’s divine artifact, the Mermaid Fairy Tale, slightly vibrated.

The camouflage around the purple scaled fishman and Hai Ping’s corpse vanished in a flash, absorbed completely by the divine artifact.

The purple scaled fishman: “?!”

Zi Di: “?!”

The Golden Flash’s Gold Eye component seemed to break for a bit, then soon exploded in a magnificent flash.

It detected Hai Ping’s corpse.

The latter’s worth exceeded all other targets!

Hai Ping had been a saint level when alive, naturally his corpse was regarded as a saint level material.

More crucially, he also contained a badly damaged god level bloodline, the Swelling Wave Fishman bloodline!

The Golden Flash’s bombardment followed its doctrine of targeting the most valuable thing, but it also discerned between friend and foe.

Merchant Alliances, mercenary corps, along with people like Cang Xu and Ya Ma weren’t targeted.

The Golden Flash mainly looked for fishmen and pirates.

Now, the most valuable target suddenly appeared.

Furthermore, the target was also being dragged by a silver level magician.

Was this tolerable?

It absolutely was not!

Thus, a moment later, the main light cannon immediately gushed with white light and a large amount of Flying Whirling Discs dove into the sea to target the purple scaled fishman.

The purple scaled fishman turned pale with fright.

Zi Di was also tense.

Because the Deep Sea Monster Fish was underneath the purple scaled fishman.

Chapter 411: The Agreement Fulfilled!

To retrieve Hai Ping’s corpse, the fishmen had set up a detailed plan.

Among them included how they would safely transport the corpse back after it had been found.

After much debate between their higher-ups, they vetoed using storage equipment.

The benefits of doing that was that it was quick and kept things hidden.

But there were many problems with it.

There were two main problems.

First was that there was no storage equipment that could carry Hai Ping’s body.

Hai Ping was saint level, and was essentially saint level material, thus one needed at least a gold level storage device to put him in.

Second, it wasn’t safe.

If the storage device was damaged, it would cause the space inside to fluctuate, creating a spatial shattering phenomenon that would consequently damage the goods inside it.

Hai Ping’s corpse was just a pile of materials and wasn’t able to resist the spatial shattering.

If a spatial hole formed, Hai Ping’s body might be sucked through it and be banished to another place, at best to somewhere in the main world, for if it was banished to another plane, it would be difficult to relocate it.

After the fishmen higher-ups investigated for a good while, they decided to use the Pearl Bubble.

To them, it seemed this was the safest measure!

The God they believed in was very powerful in the domain of deception.

Many skills, even if they were legend level, found it difficult to see through the Pearl Bubble’s disguise.

The flaw to this plan was its high price.

The divine artifact, the Mermaid Fairy Tale, had been lost, and one Pearl Bubble used meant one fewer left.

But considering how important this operation was, the sect’s higher-ups could only bear the pain and use one.

Regardless of whether it was them or the purple scaled fishman, neither of them ever considered that the Pearl Bubble’s power would suddenly vanish!

Even though it happened before.

But in the records, this was when it faced a god’s power.

In this situation, where not even a saint level was present, the Pearl Bubble’s power vanishing instantly caught the purple scaled fishman unprepared and gob smacked.

She was exposed, and the Golden Flash immediately reacted to an extent.

Bang!

A light beam suddenly fired from the sky, locked onto the purple scaled fishman.

The prior bombardments were light net ones; this was massive and focused.

This light beam was fired by the main light cannon alone.

This had gold level might.

This was what had struck Qi You and seriously injured him.

Hai Ping’s corpse was exposed, causing the Golden Flash to burst forth with all of its power.

The instant it fired, the purple scaled fishman felt her heart go crazy with warnings. But the mortal aura of death bound her tight!

She wanted to flee, but it was too late!

The light beam was too fast.

Although the purple scaled fishman was strong, she was merely silver level and was buried into obscurity by the light beam.

The light beam’s power did not decrease as it exploded into the seafloor.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish was disguised as a reef, and was also underneath the purple scaled fishman, thus it was what the light beam had exploded.

The magic alchemy ship shook violently, ear piercing alarms rang out, and flashing wild red lights filled the hold of the ship.

Zi Di clenched her teeth and persevered inside the cockpit.

She didn’t move the Deep Sea Monster Fish out of hiding, a wise decision.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish was fast, but not faster than the light beams.

Even if it could hide, it couldn’t escape.

If it decided to reveal itself, then the consequences would be massive. No matter which power it was, all of them would burst if they suddenly discovered another gold level magic ship.

At that time, the Deep Sea Monster Fish would probably be targeted by all.

“Endure it for now!”

Zi Di was resilient.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish stiffly resisted the blow and succeeded in maintaining its reef camouflage.

But to an outsider’s eyes, the reef was broken in half, as to not expose the illusion.

The greater half that remained was scorched black and appeared hot enough to churn the nearby seawater and produce massive amounts of bubbles.

This was the ingenious effect of the Deceptive Disguise spell.

It wasn’t stuck in stone and was malleable to change.

If it was immutable, a common reef withstanding a gold level light beam cannon would provoke suspicion from anyone observing.

With the Deceptive Disguise spell, the Deep Sea Monster Fish could match changes in the outside world and produce a corresponding shift with it.

Of course, this had a weakness.

The part of the reef exploded by the light beam had sunken completely into oblivion.

But in reality, this part of the hull was still there.

Therefore, if a lifeform touched this piece of “blank space”. They would be stopped, unable to move through it.

Of course, touch was one of life’s senses.

This could also be affected by the Deceptive Disguise spell.

Lifeforms wouldn’t likely see through this flaw.

But if an alchemy puppet carried out an investigation, then it would fail.

“Was that a silver level fishman magician?”

“Her camouflage magic suddenly failed, drawing the light cannon turret’s fire.”

“Who is that dead fishman? It’s actually a saint level!”

Zi Di was filled with doubt.

The light beam soon dissipated, leaving a deep hole in the seafloor.

Hai Ping’s corpse slowly sank down, like the valuables before it, gradually reaching the bottom of the hole.

At the edge of the hole sat the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Without the Pearl Bubble’s deceptive power, the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s detection abilities immediately functioned.

Zi Di also noticed how impressive the corpse was.

“Why didn’t that fishman magician ever show themselves in battle, but instead tried to abscond with a saint level corpse?”

Zi Di couldn’t be certain who the dead purple scaled fishman was.

The latter had leaked no life aura.

After the Pearl Bubble was absorbed by the divine artifact, she didn’t have enough time to cast anything before the light beam blew her up.

“Don’t tell me the fishmen’s true goal was this saint level fishman corpse?”

“Who is he really?”

“Even death, what could compel these fishmen to sacrifice themselves?”

Zi Di was skeptical.

At the same time, she felt a rush of excitement.

No matter who this fishman was in life, he was extremely valuable.

No matter what, he could be regarded as a pile of saint level alchemy materials!

“Perhaps it was precisely because of its high worth, it provoked the main light cannon’s fire.”

“What just happened validates my guess.”

“That flying magic ship can differentiate between friend and foe, then it will target its light beams based on who is worth more.”

“This style…is it worthy of being Hun Tong’s flagship?”

Zi Di looked at Hai Ping’s corpse in a magic image, her heart itched, but she didn’t act.

She had already guessed at the Golden Flash’s bombardment pattern and knew if she grabbed the saint level fishman corpse, it would provoke the light cannon’s bombardment.

The purple scaled fishman was the best warning to heed.

“Wait for an opportunity.”

“There should be an opportunity.”

Zi Di watched the old clan elder, the naga priestess, and the others with more expectations.

The naga priestess was ashen.

She presided over this operation and was the highest authority for it.

The Blue Green Coral fishman clan’s attempt to retake their homeland was not a high priority to her.

Both sides only had a mutually beneficial cooperation with each other.

The naga priestess only had one objective.

To retrieve Hai Ping’s corpse as intact as possible!

Now, the purple scaled fishman was slain and Hai Ping’s corpse was exposed.

The entire operation was only one step from success!

But with Hun Tong’s clone and Yan Ding working together to obstruct her, there was nothing she could do.

“No, even if I break out, I will still face a gold level magic ship’s fire!”

“It’s time.”

The naga priestess deeply sighed, revealing her frustration and determination.

Hun Tong had his trump cards, and so too did she.

The trump card was the Devil Gold Coins!

The naga priestess produced a mirage, temporarily attracting the enemy’s firepower as her true form began to pray.

“Great Devil Lord, one of the masters of the abyss, God of Sincerity, God of Trustworthiness.”

“Ally of my Goddess!”

“A priestess of the Charming Blue Goddess prays to you.”

“I beseech thee to display your power, to fulfill your agreement, to display your trustworthiness.”

“Bend the enemy’s insincerity, make them more noble beings!”

A priestess praying to a God was almost identical to a templar knight, there wasn’t a 100% success rate.

Because the Gods were not focused on every believer at all times.

But this time, the naga priestess was very confident.

Because the Charming Blue Goddess and the Sincerity God had a contract of alliance, and also because they had gathered many Devil Gold Coins.

These gold coins came from the Sincerity God.

It was the Sincerity God’s sacred tools!

The naga priestess finished her prayer and immediately looked at Yan Ding.

But the outcome disappointed her completely.

Yan Ding’s offensive was uninterrupted and didn’t suffer any disturbances.

According to the priestess’ deduction, Yan Ding should be the leader of the imperial secret spies dispatched to Two Eyes Island, Shadow.

At the time, the imperial secret spies had placed agents inside the Vinegar Jar Pirates.

The spies were discovered, and the naga priestess beat them at their own game, allowing the Vinegar Jar Pirates to dispatch Xi Dai Zi to Two Eyes Island to make an insincere deal, for their true goal was to give them the Devil Gold Coins.

The Devil Gold Coins were the Sincerity God’s sacred tools and were a most formidable contract.

The contract was as stated: “help the Vinegar Jar Pirates attack Two Eyes Island”.

When the contract took effect, the coins’ master would use divine power to make them wholeheartedly fulfill the contents of that contract.

This was one of the naga priestess’ hidden trump cards.

According to her understanding of the imperial secret spy organization, these gold coins were liable to end up in the leader’s bag.

When the agreement took effect, it could convert an enemy into a friend and turn a strong human against their fellows on the eve of battle!

When that happened, it would likely catch the enemy off guard and cost them dearly, with one side losing while the other gained, tipping the scales of victory severely in the other direction.

However, Yan Ding was completely unchanged.

“What’s going on?”

“Did the humans see through my stratagem and deal with those gold coins?”

“Perhaps Yan Ding isn’t an imperial secret spy?”

“Or perhaps the Great Devil Lord didn’t respond to my prayer?”

The naga priestess’ lips were dry with constant misgivings.

According to her assumption, when the agreement took effect, Yan Ding would not only let them pass, but he would also help protect them.

When that happened, she would have a great opportunity to retrieve Hai Ping’s corpse.

But it wasn’t so.

Yan Ding was unaffected.

The naga priestess was pained, “What can be done?”

The naga priestess thought she was wrong about who Yan Ding was, but he really was an imperial secret spy.

However, the Devil Gold Coins had never fallen into the imperial secret spy’s hands.

When Xi Dai Zi contacted Shadow to verify it, he had insincerely said that he had taken possession of the gold coins due to face.

While in reality, these gold coins had fallen into the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ hands.

Chapter 412: Zi and Cang in Opposition

The entire 50,000 Devil Gold Coins had astonishing power.

If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps still held these coins, it would have been terrible.

But afterwards was the auction.

At the auction, Zi Di and Cang Xu had showered others with these gold coins to increase their strength and didn’t stint on spending.

Therefore, most of these Devil Gold Coins have been spent by them.

Because of the auction hall’s dramatic change, Cang Xu and Zi Di hadn’t spent the last of the gold coins and returned them to the mercenary corps’ storage.

The two of them had some gold coins on them.

But where did the vast majority of the gold coins end up?

On…Hun Tong’s body!

Two Eyes Island’s auction was carried out differently than other auctions.

Because the buyers were special, therefore, transactions were carried out on the spot.

The buyers paid gold coins to the auction hall to obtain their goods.

The sellers received gold coins from the auction hall.

But the gold coins they received were not the same ones.

The specific situation pertaining to the Devil Gold Coins was this: Cang Xu and Zi Di received goods and gave a portion of the Devil Gold Coins to the auction.

The auction took out prepared gold coins and gave them to the seller.

This wasn’t exclusive behavior to Two Eyes Island’s auction; other auctions did the same.

The main reason was to prevent buyers from tampering with said gold coins and using fake money to buy real goods.

The seller who received the fake money would naturally be unresigned, leading to a noisy dispute that would harm the auction hall’s reputation.

As far as the auction hall was concerned, since it was the host of this trade event, they would naturally play their part and bear the risks of doing so.

Therefore, the auction hall was both responsible for the buyers and had to look after the sellers.

To an auction hall, reputation was far more important.

Without reputation, without buyers and sellers participating, an unattended auction would be the worst thing ever.

As a result, an auction hall had liquid funds stored, verified as genuine gold coins that were handed to the sellers.

As for the buyer’s gold coins, the auction hall would certainly appraise them.

Under normal circumstances, the appraisal wouldn’t make any mistakes.

Even if there was a mistake, and the auction hall received fake money, they would only lose a portion of the auction’s turnover, and most importantly, their reputation would be preserved, and so that kind of loss could be endured.

The money Two Eyes Island’s auction received were strictly guarded. After the auction finished, it would be escorted by a massive force of city guards to the treasury.

But this auction was very unusual.

The entire auction hall became a cage to imprison cultivators.

How could Hun Tong fear at ease at leaving these gold coins somewhere outside?

Therefore, while he was leading everyone in their breakout, he also put all of the gold coins from the auction into his storage device.

When the naga priestess failed to see her prayer do anything, she couldn’t help but suspect whether it had worked.

While as a matter of fact, she succeeded.

But the agreement’s power was wordless and uncommunicative.

For it didn’t give off an imposing light nor trigger any shaking sounds, only the devilish whispering coming from the affected targets’ heart.

Consequently, Zi Di’s eyes suddenly went red.

Her mind was still very clear, but her motivation completely changed.

“Attack the island!”

“Help the fishmen attack Two Eyes Island!!”

A moment before, she had wanted to be patient and wait for a good chance to steal Hai Ping’s corpse away.

But now, her mind was filled with thoughts on how to effectively fulfill the agreement.

She immediately took control of the Deep Sea Monster Fish and turned it into its fishman form.

Two holes appeared on both sides of its hull, where from with8in extended a framework.

Vehicle armor quickly fitted across the framework, forming the shape of a fishman.

The entire hull began to bend, and the tip of the keel automatically separated from the hull and morphed into a gigantic harpoon.

A dozen seconds later, the Deep Sea Monster Fish completely changed into a giant fishman holding a harpoon.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish had three forms, the fishman form was the strongest combat form!

To fulfill the agreement, Zi Di displayed the most sincerity she could.

Her reason was intact, and knew she couldn’t rely on her own power, for she was but a mere bronze level necromancer.

Only with the help of the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s might could she be the greatest help towards the fishmen in their capture of Two Eyes Island.

Changing into the giant fishman was only the beginning.

A moment later, the fishman stood on the seafloor and looked up into the sky.

It opened its large mouth, exposing the gold cannon, the Electric Arc Cannon.

A gold level Python Stray Bullet shell was conveyed through the fishman’s throat, slid into the cannon’s rear, and entered the cannon’s firing chamber.

Magic power from the reservoir ferociously poured in.

The Electric Arc Cannon’s mouth crackled with lightning.

A vast gold level aura seeped everywhere!

Alarms resonated throughout the Golden Flash’s main cabin.

It detected that there was a giant fishman on the seafloor, and that the fishman was in the process of gathering strength for a powerful gold level attack!

Zi Di did not know Hun Tong had also suffered the same influence.

Her reason told her this: to help the fishmen to the best of her ability, she had to prioritize eradicating the strongest enemy.

An overall survey of the entire battlefield determined who else but the gold level magic ship in the sky was the strongest foe?

Battle skill – Raging Flame Dancing Skirt.

Silver battle qi cured around Yue Ban’s body, acting in concert with her body hair as it ignited.

The meticulous flames formed the shape of a dancing skirt.

The raging hot flames twisted the air around her.

“This dancer is so strong!” Hong Zhu’s hands that withstood the fire were quickly burnt black as she found Yue Ban.

She retreated back, and with the time gained, she promptly drank a potion.

A fire resistance potion!

Hong Zhu and Yue Ban continued to fight, but Hong Zhu was gradually losing.

However, it wasn’t easy for Yue Ban to win either.

Hong Zhu’s artifacts appeared like flies.

She constantly adventured outside, sometimes by herself, therefore, she had a habit of equipping herself fully.

Therefore, she usually had abundant supplies.

Several hundred meters above them, Qing Xin and Cang Xu were fighting Ya Ma.

Qing Xin and Cang Xu were working together, but they too were losing.

Thanks to her flying ant, Ya Ma had outstanding flying capabilities.

She not only had the flying ant, but also the Yellow Nest Wasp at her side.

The latter was a mobile fort that was a huge threat to the enemy!

In this fight, the advantages of a summoner had been adequately displayed by Ya Ma.

It would be for the best if those opposing Ya Ma had a plan that could deal with the summoner instead of being bogged down by the summons.

Ya Ma was very strong and could be said to be quite a thorny problem.

Even if the dragonman youngster was present, it wasn’t enough to deal with her.

The dragonman youngster had both attack and defense, completely able to break through to get at the summoner, but he had one massive problem.

He couldn’t fly.

At best, the dragonman youngster could use Surge to jump long distances.

As a matter of fact, this kind of jumping capability in terms of attacking flying opponents couldn’t compare to Hu Li’s Musical Scale battle skill in terms of practicality.

“How can you be this weak, are you really this weak?”

“That I lost to you is simply a great embarrassment!”

Ya Ma constantly hooted, enraged over a perceived humiliation.

Cang Xu on the surface looked like a silver level, his disguise couldn’t be seen through, however, Deceptive Disguise could not improve his battle strength.

Therefore, his displayed military power was actually at the iron level.

Even with the help of the fire path rings, they were only at the iron level, that’s all.

“Where is your fellow?”

“We have been fighting for a while, yet she hasn’t appeared yet.”

“Did they run away?”

Ya Ma used words to attack Cang Xu’s morale and manipulate him mentally.

At the same time, she commanded her golden swarm to chase Cang Xu and Qing Xin around, forcing them into a difficult situation.

Qing Xin was barely able to cast a wind blade spell.

The wind blade flew out.

Ya Ma’s flying ant straightened its back and stiffly resisted the blade.

The summoner’s defensive line was quite stable, and so far, none of her defensive magical artifacts had been used.

“Not even a magic staff.”

“You really humiliate magicians.”

Ya Ma sneered at Qing Xin, then took out a snake shaped staff.

She first imbued it with magic power, then threw the snake shaped magic staff.

With magic power bubbling out, wings grew from the magic staff.

The snake shaped magic staff instantly activated, and conforming to the magical wings, it became a silver level feather snake then started to chase after Qing Xin.

Qing Xin was in grave danger.

For a moment, she could only retreat, unable to look after Cang Xu.

“Even if someone helps you, you will still fall by my hands.”

“Ultimately, you are beyond hope.”

“Even if I give you a chance, you are still beyond hope!”

Ya Ma burst out laughing like an arrogant bully.

However, a moment later, Cang Xu suddenly raised his head, his eyes red.

He also had a few Devil Gold Coins on his person.

He was influenced by the agreement.

Cang Xu raised his head and roared.

Countless ghosts flew out from his body, and for a split second, an evil wind blew into the battlefield, the chillness wilding nipping the air.

“Huh?!” Ya Ma’s smile froze completely, her eyes widened, and her pupils violently contracted at this startling development she hadn’t foreseen.

“Necro, necromancer?!” Hong Zhu and Yue Ban both sucked in a cold gasp and slowed their attacks.

Qing Xin also looked bewildered.

To fulfill the agreement, Cang Xu decided to act in utter sincerity, and would naturally use his full strength to make it so.

Thus, his necromancer identity was subsequently exposed.

As the dragonman youngster was rushing over, he spotted this happening, his heart utterly agonized.

He had nearly gotten to Cang Xu.

But he failed at the last hurdle!

How could he help him?

Before Cang Xu revealed his necromancer background, the dragonman youngster could explain helping him.

But now, if he helped, he couldn’t explain it away.

He had run over with great difficulty but could no longer help.

This outcome left the dragonman youngster helpless.

Zong Ge tightly scowled, his heart filling with puzzlement, “Why? Cang Xu definitely could have continued to stall, although he was in a pinch, it didn’t warrant using necromancy!”

The dragonman youngster also saw Cang Xu’s deep red eyes, and his thoughts stirred, “Something is wrong with Cang Xu!”

Cang Xu’s eyes were scarlet red, he was howling constantly, and his bursting killing intent terrified everyone.

His reason hadn’t lessened one bit.

The agreement’s power only twisted his will.

Chapter 413: The Greatest Undiscovered Traitor Appears

Cang Xu pondered with all his mind how he could help the fishmen take back Two Eyes Island as best as possible.

“The greatest obstruction to the fishmen is that flying magic ship!”

“With my strength alone, it is impossible to take that ship.”

“What about destroying it?”

“Unless I am in control of the Deep Sea Monster Fish can I bombard it.”

“The Deep Sea Monster Fish is also a gold level magic ship, capable of threatening Hun Tong’s flagship.

“However, the flagship’s full functions haven’t been displayed yet.”

“The Deep Sea Monster Fish might not necessarily be capable of shooting it down.”

“Furthermore, it is under Zi Di’s control, while the commander, Zong Ge, and the others won’t stand by and watch.”

Cang Xu decided to forgo returning to take control of the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

He continued to deeply ponder, “I am only an iron level, I can’t do anything about Hun Tong’s flagship, but I can menace a gold level like Hun Tong.”

“However, my ghosts and necromancy cannot inflict serious damage to gold levels.”

“Gold levels are not suitable targets, but silver levels can be dealt with!”

Cang Xu knew that as an iron level, he wasn’t strong enough to go against Hun Tong and the other gold levels.

But with the necromancy path’s properties, it was feasible for Cang Xu to kill the silver levels that were the backbone of Two Eyes Island.

At this point in the battle, Hun Tong didn’t have many silver levels left.

The city guard commander had died first.

Not long after, the purple scaled fishman killed the alchemist.

This left the pharmacist and One Eye still in play.

“Kill those two first!”

“Ku Feng, Mei Ban, and the other silver levels can be considered loners.”

“As long as the human silver levels crumble, that will undoubtedly affect the gold level clashes, allowing the fishmen to gain the advantage.”

Cang Xu’s line of thought was very clear, and in nearly a second, had drawn up an opposition plan.

He was once a noble steward and knew the hearts of men.

The humans indeed had the momentum while the fishmen were completely on the downturn, but the fishmen weren’t completely hopeless yet.

If the situation went sour, loners like Ku Feng and Mei Ban would waver.

When they fled, that would cause others to flee too, ultimately creating a rout.

Morale was always a crucial element.

In the history books, there were many battles were the few defeated the many, and the weak were victorious over the strong.

If Hun Tong had half the training skill Zong Ge had, it wouldn’t be like this.

But he didn’t.

He was a talented businessman, and had sufficiently visible military power, and with his greatest trump card that was his gold level magic ship, he really was powerful.

But he also had flaws and failings.

He had run Two Eyes Island for many years, yet didn’t have a single battle squad.

His troops were small.

His silver level subordinates were not reliable, none of them could take charge and share the burden with him.

“Hun Tong emphasized commerce development too much.”

“The scale of his troops is like this because long term, the military is an immense expenditure.”

“Establishing a battle squad is also very costly.”

“He didn’t spend money hiring formidable silver levels, the few under his command were enough during peacetime.”

“Because he wanted to attract pirates to rinse their money at Two Eyes Island, he didn’t increase his military and troops, at least not enough to make the pirates feel threatened.”

Cang Xu was constantly analyzing.

Hun Tong’s weak points lie with his troops and his backbone.

By attacking these weak points, perhaps the situation could be redeemed.

A large quantity of ghosts circled around Cang Xu, increasing his ice-cold terror.

Ya Ma retreated!

To her, it seemed that Cang Xu was still a silver level.

“No wonder during the last fight, he only used fire path magic and magic scrolls.”

“It seems he never used his true skills!”

“Abominable.”

“He is actually a silver level necromancer!”

“Does he have a connection with that necromancer who died here on Two Eyes Island?”

Ya Ma’s intent to flee was very obvious.

Cang Xu could now freely move.

He gazed towards the ocean, in the direction of the patrol fleet.

One Eye was the fleet’s leader.

If he died, then the patrol fleet would be tremendously crippled.

“Too far.”

“If I fly towards them, I will be shot down by the flying magic ship.”

“It would be better to…”

Cang Xu turned to look elsewhere.

The dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, Chi Lai, as well as the pharmacist and the auxiliary city guard commander were rushing over towards him in a group.

The idea obviously moved Cang Xu.

“That silver level pharmacist has low military power, weakened by his prior fight with the fishman magician.”

“With necromancy, it is likely I can kill him.”

“There is also the auxiliary city guard commander, he is only an iron level fighter, and by killing him, the city guard will have their head removed.”

“The commander and Zong Ge are allies. When I attack the pharmacist and auxiliary commander, they will feign their attacks and won’t be genuine threats to me.”

“The real trouble is Chi Lai.”

“That person has just been recruited, and doesn’t know who I am. He is also a Justice Church believer and will treat me ruthlessly.”

“However, he doesn’t have equipment, and has fired the gold cannon, he isn’t at peak condition.”

The more Cang Xu thought, the greater his killing intent rose.

“He’s actually a necromancer!” The pharmacist was shocked.

“Was he that other necromancer’s partner?” The auxiliary commander was utterly bewildered.

The two didn’t know they had become Cang Xu’s target yet.

They were following behind the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge, and although they were surprised by Cang Xu’s identity being revealed, they still felt safe.

A moment later, they saw Cang Xu’s ghost army in the distance headed right towards them.

“He’s charging over!”

“Don’t worry, with Commander Long Fu here, that necromancer is coming to throw his life away!”

The auxiliary commander and the pharmacist shouted loudly, doing their best to stabilize the city guard’s morale.

The dragonman youngster glanced at them, then looked at Zong Ge.

Zong Ge furled his brows, feeling quite annoyed.

Chi Lai fumed with rage and rolled up his sleeves for battle, “Necromancer, wait until righteous punishment smites you disciple of evil!”

On the Golden Flash’s deck.

The old fishman elder and Hun Tong were fighting fiercely.

The old clan elder was bursting with signs of death, vigorously attacking while defending less, it was epic in scope.

Hun Tong’s figure was frequently dodging, with his extraordinary nimbleness, he moved around the old fishman elder and got several sneaky jabs in.

The old clan elder was gasping for breath.

He braved death to attack the enemy flagship, he wanted to damage it, but Hun Tong’s concentrated offensive forced him into defensive and counterattack tactics.

Hun Tong was also tired.

He was good at ambushes, and had strong offense, but likewise, he had weak defense.

He had to attack with everything he had to restrict the old fishman elder’s assault to protect his flagship as best he could.

Despite that, the fight between them had resulted in much of the deck being wrecked.

Hun Tong was incessantly distressed and attacked all the more violently.

He surged with an intensive offensive while the fishman defended himself as much as he could to stall for time and give his clan as much time as possible to retreat.

The old fishman elder was in grave danger, because if he was a little careless, any blow from Hun Tong would critically injure him.

Both used their full strength.

Therefore, not long into the fight, both of their battle qi reserves had severely declined, along with their stamina and energy.

Suddenly, Hun Tong jumped, then pounced from the old fishman elder’s left.

The old fishman elder was just about to turn around to face Hun Tong when he heard a gale whistling from the back of his head.

The clan elder growled, gold battle qi exploded, and during that perilous moment, it congealed into a specially made helmet to cover the back of his head.

Ding.

Hun Tong’s alchemy clone struck the battle qi helmet, blowing it to bits.

But its attack was also offset.

The fishman elder used this opportunity to counterattack, striking Hun Tong’s clone back.

But a moment later, the real Hun Tong instantly stooped down to a meter in height, then drilled towards the clan elder’s lower back.

The gold level dagger stabbed the clan elder’s kidney.

The clan elder felt his body go cold, cursing to himself, he wanted to turn around, but it was too late.

Just as his heart sank into the abyss, clenching his teeth for the severe wound, Hun Tong’s main body unexpectedly turned to the side, pulled back himself, and rushed backwards, putting distance between him and the clan elder.

“Huh?” The clan elder stared, completely bewildered.

What just happened?

He found it hard to believe.

His life was in his old enemy’s hand, yet his enemy let a golden opportunity to cripple him slip by and instead decided to pull back?

“Are my eyes deceiving me?” The clan elder was somewhat skeptical of his eyes.

But a more magical scene happened a moment later.

After Hun Tong pulled back, he stood in place, and when he opened his eyes, they were red, and he said to the clan elder, “Truce! I can help you retake your home!”

The clan elder was completely dumbstruck, seriously suspecting whether this was an auditory hallucination.

But it wasn’t just Hun Tong’s main body, his alchemy clone stopped attacking too.

“You can kill me, if you attack now, I won’t resist.” Hun Tong sincerely declared.

After he said that, he tossed his gold dagger to the deck.

The clan elder blinked repeatedly, he was about to attack, but he restrained himself, doubtful of whether this was a human machination.

Hun Tong continued, “But if you kill me, I won’t be able to help you in the best way possible.”

“The biggest threat is my flagship, the Golden Flash!”

“The second biggest threat is me, as well as the gold level Song Shou and Yan Ding.”

“Below that are all the silver levels.”

“If you kill me, you’ll be unable to seize this ship, and will be counterattacked by Song Shou and Yan Ding.”

“Even if you can beat back those gold levels, expel the numerous silvers, and annihilate the city guard, patrol fleet, and other forces, how many clansmen will you have left in the end?”

The clan elder stroked his forehead, “Hold on, you are…”

“Could this be the Devil Gold Coins?”

Hun Tong stared blankly, “I see, so it is the Devil Gold Coins.”

“Such a wonderful trick!”

He stuck out his thumb in sincere admiration.

Seeing his opponent exclaim in admiration, the clan elder made a weird face, both pleasant surprise and a trace of terror.

The Devil Gold Coins’ power was astonishing; it could actually change his old enemy so thoroughly!

This was the power of the gods.

The clan elder also knew about the secret Devil Gold Coins.

Beforehand, the naga priestess and he communicated.

Moreover, the Vinegar Jar Pirates that handled the Devil Gold Coins from the Blue Green fishman clan; both groups had an inseparably close relationship.

“How many Devil Gold Coins do you have on you? No, forget about it, that isn’t important.” The clan elder shook his head, “How can you help us?”

Hun Tong replied, “I can feel that although the power altering my will is formidable, it is quickly waning.”

“Therefore, we must make every second count!”

“First I will use the Golden Flash to deal with the gold level Song Shou and Yan Ding, then I will massacre my subordinates as best as possible, along with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and every merchant alliance person large and small to clear all obstacles for you.”

“Then I will give you command of the Golden Flash.”

“Finally, I will kill myself. You will use my corpse to crush the human’s morale.”

Chapter 414: Bombardment of the Golden Flash (Part 1)

As he looked at Hun Tong, the fishman elder was momentarily speechless.

The agreement’s power had been unleashed, causing Hun Tong to put his enemy’s needs before all else.

The agreement’s effects on more neutral parties like Zi Di and Cang Xu was not as bad. But when it was applied to an enemy, it was catastrophic for the person affected!

“If after the war, Hun Tong is still alive, and the agreement’s power dissipates, he will consider himself the greatest undiscovered traitor in his recollections, he would be the main culprit of losing the war, would he die of shame then?” The old fishman elder couldn’t help but think.

Ignoring that they were old enemies, the fishman elder actually admired Hun Tong.

The fishman elder could also feel Hun Tong’s business acumen.

When Two Eyes Island fell into Hun Tong’s hands, it had actually burst with enormous business potential, thereby building a bustling and magnificent landscape.

The fishman elder could never have accomplished that.

“You stole away our home, treated this as your family territory, depleted your heart’s blood to develop it, and in the end, it was you who destroyed it.”

The fishman elder at that moment felt how fickle and callous fate was.

He actually felt a bit of sympathy and pity for Hun Tong.

“Alas, I didn’t expect that this was how things would turn out in the end.”

When the fishman elder charged onto the Golden Flash, he assumed he would die in battle, and although he would be slain by Hun Tong’s hands, he would render a heavy toll in response and win precious time for his people to retreat.

Before the battle started, the old clan elder envisioned that he would kill Hun Tong, retake his homeland, wipe away his humiliation, and be pleased for the rest of his life.

He didn’t expect that in the end, it would be Hun Tong himself who took his life.

The cause of death was a pile of gold coins.

The Devil Gold Coins.

“The Sincerity God, one of the great lords of the Abyss, how formidable!”

At that moment, the old clan elder revered the master of those gold coins far more.

To be honest, before this, he didn’t have high hopes for these gold coins.

Because they were unreliable.

The gold coins had to be in the target’s hands, only then could they work.

They also couldn’t be covertly planted on the target; they had to be passed through transactions.

Through the transaction, the contract had to be made explicit.

Regardless of whether it was the old fishman elder or the naga priestess, none of them thought the gold coins would affect Hun Tong.

They didn’t know that the imperial secret spy had been working with the local Feudal Lord Hun Tong for a long time.

Imperial secret spies often worked alone.

One of their duties was to supervise nobles of all types for the imperial household.

The fishmen only found traitors within the Vinegar Jar Pirates. After cruel punishment and torture, they found out they were working for an imperial secret spy.

Hit by inspiration, the naga priestess decided to use the Devil Gold Coins she had gathered.

Because this would let them beat the spy at their own game, they would pretend to not have noticed, and during the deal, they could specify the agreement details.

The naga priestess believed that the imperial secret spy organization was no exception to human corruption. Mot of those gold coins would be embezzled and fall into the leader of the secret spies.

The code name for the imperial secret spy leader responsible for the waters around Two Eyes Island was “Shadow”.

She had been conspiring against “Shadow”.

Through a series of unexpected occurrences, the gold coins were misplaced.

“So, Shadow actually deceived Xi Dai Zi and never got the gold coins, rather they ended up with Feudal Lord Hun Tong?”

“It is also possible that they did a secret deal, leading to some of the gold coins to fall into Hun Tong’s hands.”

“But why does he have so many of the gold coins on him?”

The fishman elder was guessing.

He surmised a portion of the facts but couldn’t be certain.

The complete and complex truth exceeded his imagination.

Hun Tong led the fishman elder to the main light cannon turret.

“Enemy detected entering the main light cannon.”

“Warning, warning!”

“An enemy has infiltrated the main light cannon turret, life level surveyed to be gold level!”

The alarm rang to no effect.

The fishman elder overtly walked in, for before him Captain Hun Tong was leaving the door open for the thief and showing the wolf into his home.

If there was a ship spirit, perhaps it would be crying already.

Hun Tong quickly took control of the Golden Flash.

Outside, numerous Flying Whirling Discs moved on a broad scale.

In the Vinegar Jar, Xi Dai Zi, Peng Ding Zi, and the rest were tense.

But a moment later, they were baffled to see the discs flying away from them.

Hun Tong moved the Flying Whirling Discs to the most important targets, the gold levels.

The naga priestess and Yan Ding were doing battle.

Er Gua Zi and Song Shou were still fighting, the port was a scattered mess around them.

“The first bombardment will be the most unexpected, and also the easiest to get a battle result.”

“Can you contact the naga priestess and Er Gua Zi? If they can coordinate with the bombardment, the battle result might be astonishing!”

“The main light cannon turret has been fired…twice, you could see its might from when it struck Qi You.”

Speaking of which, Hun Tong was looking at the firing records.

The first time it had locked onto Qi You.

But the second time, he was busy fighting the fishman elder.

“The second cannon alignment was aimed at the deep sea.”

“Strange…it just annihilated a silver level fishman magician?”

“Huh, so as it turns out, there was a saint level fishman corpse, its extreme value along with the light network shelling being underway was why the main light cannon turret was fired.”

“That fishman magician was covertly transporting a saint level fishman corpse, but her camouflage magic suddenly failed midway.”

“Shouldn’t this be one of your true objectives? Was that large scale magic being used to look for that saint level corpse? What makes him so important?” Hun Tong asked.

The fishman elder was callous, “That doesn’t concern you, hurry up and open fire.”

“I can’t contact them.”

“You broke the communication device on me.”

The clan elder warily looked at Hun Tong.

He maintained his vigilance.

When he noticed the agreement was about to fail, he would step forward and finish off Hun Tong.

The Devil Gold Coins was divine power, Hun Tong couldn’t counter it, but that didn’t mean he couldn’t guard against it.

Because the Sincerity God was not the only god in the world.

He was just one of many gods.

The fishmen could use divine power, so too could Hun Tong.

Many godly blessings and constant divine spells could interfere and break the agreement’s power.

Hun Tong sighed, “Please believe me, I am earnestly and sincerely helping you deal with me.”

“I just want to understand all of the reasons, so I can utilize all factors to remove any potential impediments.”

Hun Tong said this while inputting the commands.

He was multitasking, and from the fishman’s point of view, really was doing everything he could.

However, just as he prepared to open fire, another alarm rang.

The content of this warning had changed, different from the one before.

“High energy reaction detected on the seafloor.”

“The vessel has been targeted.”

“Warning, the Golden Flash has been targeted.”

“Energy level detected is gold level, probability of 98% that it is a gold level cannon.”

“Current hull defense is weak, estimated probability of severe damage from this cannon is 47%, minor damage is 50%, and crashing is 3%.

“Recommend full speed evasion, recommend full speed evasion!”

Hun Tong suddenly turned pale, his heart jumping.

Chapter 415: Bombardment of the Golden Flash (Part 2)

The Golden Flash was categorized as a bombardment warship.

In fact, it was also an experimental magic ship.

It used light cannon turrets and a main light concentration turret; this has never been seen on any magic ship before.

The Sheng Ming Empire had been developing the light cannons. Their attack style was very unique, and was poles apart from conventional alchemy artillery.

Compared to substantive alchemy shells, light cannons had a distinct advantage.

They were faster and more concentrated.

Therefore, the light beams were an immense threat to high level lifeforms.

Take Qi You.

The light beam was too fast for him to react to.

With its force restrained and concentrated within it, Qi You couldn’t defend himself against it. One must know that he had been using a defensive battle skill before it hit.

But a single light beam wounded him grievously!

The goal of testing the experimental Golden Flash was to see if its unique design was effective at restraining high level lifeforms. If it could target them, threaten them, harm them, and even kill them.

If it worked in practice, then the Sheng Ming Empire would mass manufacture this type of magic ship.

From this position, one could see why the Golden Flash emphasized offense first.

Second, to be able to probe the enemy and accurately lock onto them, its detection performance had to be outstanding too.

Offense was the top priority, then detection, and third was speed.

The Golden Flash was brazenly designed to be a flying magic ship!

There were few flying magic ships!

Magic ships were normally outfitted with large amounts of cannons, after all, long range bombardments were a main part of maritime combat.

A ship without hundreds of cannons would feel embarrassed to leave the shipyard.

But these cannons were usually very heavy, and some alchemy cannons with massive power were also extremely heavy.

If the cannon material was made of bad materials, it was liable to explode and will instead menace the ship it was on.

In addition, solid shells also had noticeable weight.

Ammunition dumps of solid artillery shells had to number in the thousands, and if it was less than a thousand, then one would be low on ammunition.

The combined weight of cannons and shells was an immense figure.

This weight made it hard to design flying magic ships around.

But the light cannon turrets didn’t have this problem.

Their light beams had no substance, and the light cannon turrets were far lighter than a bunch of cannons.

Since it had this giant advantage, wouldn’t it be silly to not design a flying magic ship like the Golden Flash?

Flight was always a great attribute to have.

Many troops couldn’t fly.

After the Golden Flash entered the scene, the fishmen and pirates got thrashed, and the cannons on both sides found it difficult to aim up enough to target the Golden Flash.

One had to be at minimum a gold level fighter to fly.

In fact, only a minority of gold levels had flying battle skills.

Casters could fly.

Fighters with special items could also fly.

However, as they flew towards the Golden Flash, they would be bombarded with light beam cannons.
