Flight still retained its superiority.

It would also be helpful to increase one’s speed.

The reason was very simple – air resistance was far smaller than water resistance.

It is because it could fly that the Golden Flash’s speed exceeded magic ships of the same level.

Its speed would become its defense.

The Golden Flash had comparatively weak defenses.

Since it emphasized attack, detection, and speed, its defense and other attributes unavoidably because of its weak links.

On the other hand, being designed around flight also restricted how much its defenses could be improved.

Because solid vehicle armor was too heavy, and installing it would greatly reduce speed and flight altitude.

There were excellent materials that could make both light weight and formidably solid hulls.

But doing so was not cost effective.

The Golden Flash had another big advantage.

Cost!

The Sheng Ming Empire had developed this magic ship to serve the Wilderness Continent expedition and to protect the long maritime supply lines.

The Sheng Ming Empire was powerful and wasn’t worried about extensive military battles.

In open combat, they were very confident.

What worried them was that guerrilla raids would destroy their ocean supply lines.

If an enemy adopted this kind of doctrine, the Sheng Ming Empire would find it difficult to protect themselves and the cost of it would be extremely high.

They had to dispatch many cultivators to man every node in order to ensure a degree of safety.

Therefore, if they could develop ships like the Golden Flash, and use them to protect their fleets, investigate the seas, and respond to guerrilla ambushes, then it would always be worthwhile to try.

If this project succeeds, then the empire would mass produce the Golden Flash.

And if one wanted to mass produce, then the biggest consideration was cost!

The light cannon turrets were cheap and were already mass produced.

By using these turrets, the Golden Flash had managed to control a key component of the cost.

Therefore, materials both lightweight and solid were not considered, they were too expensive.

It is also worth mentioning that the Golden Flash was designed well, and its gold treasures made up for its defensive weaknesses.

It was the Flying Whirling Discs!

The designer intentionally included these components.

In peacetime, these discs would be attached to the hull, and like a layer of dragon scales, they offered considerable defense.

Not only in physical attacks, but it could also reflect and scatter energy type attacks too.

The Flying Whirling Discs could be used for attack and defense.

When attacking, these discs would fly out, spin in the air, and were capable of shooting down and scattering the light cannon shots, forming a precise large scale strike.

Overall, the Golden Flash was a high offense and low defense flying magic ship.

This was the reason why Hun Tong had kept it out of sight until the later stages of the battle.

To accurately hit the pirate boats and stop Two Eyes Island’s wealth from leaving, Hun Tong had unleashed the flying discs.

The light net bombardment shocked both enemy and ally, a gigantic battle result.

But now a problem had arisen!

Suddenly faced with a gold level cannon bombardment, Hun Tong didn’t have time to recall the Flying Whirling Discs.

Upon hearing the alarm, the old fishman elder was more worried, “Evade, evade now!”

Hun Tong had said that he wanted to give this magic ship over to the old fishman elder.

How could the old fishman elder not be worried?

But despite hurrying, it was too late.

A moment later, the giant fishman’s cannon fired from the ocean depths.

Gold level Electric Arc Cannon!

Gold level Python Bullet!

A thick and solid electric current charged into the sky.

In an instant, the world lost color.

The terrifying electric pillar left scorching flashes in everyone’s retinas.

Boom!

Even though the Golden Flash did its best to evade, it was still struck.

A piece of hull as large as a building broke from the main body.

A light cannon turret completely crumbled.

Flames were ignited.

As everyone watched on flabbergasted, the Golden Flash tilted in the air, and with a trail of dark smoke rising, it began to fall towards the ocean.

Chapter 416: The Cloud Ball Rises Again

“Warning, naval vessel 1,200 meters from the ocean surface.”

“Warning, naval vessel 1,000 meters from the ocean surface.”

“Warning, naval vessel 800 meters from the ocean surface.”

…

Warnings sounded out urgently throughout the Golden Flash.

The old fishman elder grabbed at the operating controls to stabilize himself in the shaking main cabin, and said to Hun Tong, “Do something!”

“Never mind that, the ship has emergency measures!” Hun Tong yelled back.

He had a far better balance than the fishman elder.

No matter how violently the hull swayed and slanted, Hun Tong’s feet at the helm seemed to be nailed to the floor.

He had already used his spirit to mobilize one of the Golden Flash’s abilities.

A moment later, two parts of the Golden Flash bent to form cavities.

Two balloons jetted out from those cavities.

The two balloons were made out of a special material and there seemed to be a layer of white paint across their surfaces.

When they were rolled up, balloons were small in volume.

But after being ejected and inflated ferociously, the two balloons swiftly looked like two white clouds that completely covered the skies above the Golden Flash.

The two “white clouds” were connected to the hull with many chains.

The “white clouds” produced immense buoyancy.

The Golden Flash’s falling speed plummeted.

Soon after, it floated in the air once more.

“500 meters from the ocean surface,” The warnings ceased.

“These are Cloud Balls, silver level components, but very practical.” Hun Tong explained to the old fishman elder.

After the hull stabilized, he enabled the Golden Flash’s thrust module.

Everyone watched as the Golden Flash slowly rose back into the sky after dropping some distance.

In the main light cannon turret, Hun Tong apologized to the old fishman elder, “My ship driving skill is quite low, I can only operate it at a base level. I can’t push the hull back into flight while the hull is tilted and shaking, it would be more liable to go out of control.”

“Hull damage evaluated as minor.”

“But mending is very troublesome. It can’t be done on Two Eyes Island.”

“In fact, it can’t be d one at most shipyards and alchemy workshops.”

“This is an experimental imperial magic ship.”

Hun Tong wiped his sweat away as he said this.

The old fishman elder urged him, “Don’t explain such nonsense, who and where is the enemy? If another attack like that comes again, can you maneuver the flagship awa from it?”

“I can only do my best!” Hun Tong frowned.

Before the old fishman elder had urged it, he had begun do investigate already.

Through his spirit control, the wall projected a magic image.

The image transmitted was one of the Deep Sea Monster Fish hidden on the seafloor raising its head to shell the Golden Flash.

Upon seeing the culprit, Hun Tong and the clan elder pursed their brows.

“A gold level magic beast?”

“No, it is a giant fishman. A fishman and giant hybrid is normally retarded.”

“His mouth just sprayed out an electric current, what kind of skill was that?”

“Perhaps it is the Thunder Titan bloodline?”

The two gold levels constantly guessed.

They didn’t recognize that the Deep Sea Monster Fish was really an alchemy magic ship.

The Deceptive Disguise formation fooled them.

The Golden Flash had outstanding detection capabilities, but it too identified the Deep Sea Monster Fish as a giant fishman-like gold level life form.

This appearance was exactly what War Merchant wanted to show to the outside world.

After checking who the culprit was, Hun Tong hesitated, “It’s also on the seafloor.”

“Now the question is, is it one of your trump cards?”

Hun Tong looked at the fishman elder and asked a crucial question.

The clan elder hesitated for a bit, then shook his head as he told the truth, “I’m not sure.”

The naga priestess and the Blue Green Coral fishman clan were just working together, not knowing the other’s foundation was very normal.

The naga priestess’ group was higher in status to the fishman clan.

The clan elder had hidden one of his true intentions from the naga priestess, fundamentally deceiving her to smoothly rescue his daughter.

He could assume that the naga priestess hiding a trump card from the fishman clan was completely understandable.

“If my communication device wasn’t broken, I could directly contact the Lord Priestess.” The fishman elder sighed.

Without that, he couldn’t verify it instantly.

Lacking a communication device really was inconvenient.

Hun Tong looked vexed and ashamed, “Blame me, when I saw the communication device in your bosom, I intentionally broke it first.”

“Actually, we don’t need to contact her, we can guess that this giant fishman is very likely to be part of the naga priestess’ force.”

The old fishman elder nodded, approving this conjecture.

Just from the giant fishman’s shape, one would notice that it was bound to have an inseparably close connection with the fishmen race.

It obviously attacked the Golden Flash because it was supporting the fishman and Hun Tong was the enemy.

Actions declared one’s position stronger than words!

Hun Tong thought even more, “Since I kept the Golden Flash out of sight until the right moment, why can’t the naga priestess do the same too?”

“In this respect, she is even more patient than I.”

The Golden Flash sustaining this attack is also my blunder.”

After the two people judged it as so, they decided not to retaliate against the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The old fishman elder certainly wouldn’t fire upon his own people.

Because of the agreement’s influence, Hun Tong was more sincere than him.

Since the giant fishman was determined to be “one of them”. Hun Tong wouldn’t incite internal friction.

He was indeed doing his best to help the fishmen take back Two Eyes Island!

“I have some bad news to tell you, my body has a constant divine spell on it.”

“As the flagship was falling, the divine spell was triggered, and the agreement’s influence is currently weakening rapidly.”

“We have no time to waste!” Hun Tong heavily confessed to the old fishman elder.

All kinds of magic exist in the world.

There were no shortages of skills that affected will.

People of high position often wore defensive equipment or maybe constant magic and divine spells to protect themselves.

Hun Tong was no exception.

However, the Devil Gold Coins’s power was somewhat unique.

Before now, the divine spell’s power hadn’t reacted.

But as Hun Tong continued to be under the coins’ influence, the divine spell was consequently stimulated.

The old fishman elder’s face changed and he instantly asked, “How much time is left, can you accomplish your proposed plan?”

Hun Tong shook his head bitterly, “I don’t know, I am not a divine official, I am not even a caster! I have no experience with the confrontation and depletion of divine spells.”

“It is possible that this speed will persist until the end.”

“It is also possible that when the agreement’s power is depleted to a certain degree, it will collapse and dissipate faster.”

“It is also possible that my divine spell’s power will be exhausted, and the depletion of the agreement’s power will decrease.”

The old fishman elder was immediately troubled by what he heard.

Both he and Hun Tong were fighters.

Hun Tong didn’t know, and neither di the old fishman elder.

The old fishman elder though on it, then shouted, “Attack the gold levels first!”

He also thought of other plans.

Such as leaving the Golden Flash and converging with the naga priestess to inform of the situation.

This would also get her to order the giant fishman to stop attacking the Golden Flash and instead fight together with it to inflict heavy casualties on the human powers.

The old fishman elder wasn’t worried of winning the naga priestess’ trust.

The pile of Devil Gold Coins had come from the naga priestess.

But if he did this, then it would take too much time. So long that the giant fishman on the seafloor would attack a second time.

More crucially, the old fishman elder was nervous of Hun Tong’s condition.

If he was entangled not long after departing, and wasn’t able to make a swift return, would the agreement’s influence over Hun Tong end during that time?

The old fishman elder had many misgivings.

He thought of something else, “When we begin bombarding the gold levels, whether or not we kill the targets, it is sure to trigger severe repercussions.”

He had done this before.

With the help of the Frozen Land Whale’s racial magic, he stormed onto the Golden Flash’s deck.

“In the event that a gold level attacks this ship, if I am still on it, I can aid Hun Tong’s alchemy clone in ambushing them.”

“Maybe I can severely wound and even kill them!”

“At minimum it will guarantee that Hun Tong isn’t disturbed, allowing him to fully display this flying magic ship’s destructive power!”

The old fishman elder was of high status, old and wise, and after carefully pondering, he chose a dependable plan.

His gaze always stayed on Hun Tong.

“If Hun Tong reacts fishy, I will kill him thoroughly!”

“I cannot allow him to recover and wield the Golden Flash in a wanton slaughter of my people.”

“However, I also need to act cautiously, and not too hastily.”

“The agreement specifically states this: aid the fishmen in retaking Two Eyes Island.”

“He is under the Devil Gold Coins’ influence, and the content of this agreement is his true doctrine of conduct.”

“If I act against him too early and he judges that his survival will be more beneficial for the fishman’s recapture of the island, then he will still retaliate and fight me.”

“Because he can control the Golden Flash, this magic ship affects the entire war situation, far more than a gold level fighter like me!”

The clan elder didn’t have a young fishman’s impulses, he was very old, had led his clan for many years, and his rough life experience had given him much.

Despite the developments and changes being so sudden and strange, he had adapted to the situation in a short amount of time and after sufficient pondering, he carried out the tactics most beneficial for his side.

“After the bombardment, I hope the Lord Priestess realizes Hun Tong’s change.

“Thereby preventing the giant fishman from attacking the Golden Flash!”

The clan elder was unable to attend to other things right now and had no choice but to hope that his ally’s mutual understanding was compatible with his actions.

“Rise up, rise up!”

“Our ship hasn’t crashed!”

“Victory belongs to us.”

When the human fighters saw the Golden Flash soaring back up, they cheered one after the other.

The prior scene had scared them so much that many of their hearts jumped a beat.

Now they were overjoyed, and morale stabilized, maintaining their high condition.

By contrast, most of the fishmen had become ashen with defeat.

The Golden Flash’s silhouette high in the sky put enormous psychological pressure on them.

None of them expected the prior bombardment.

Almost none of them had any way of attacking the Golden Flash, and they could only passively take a beating from the light beam bombardments.

Of the enemy and ally, those that should be happy aren’t, and those that shouldn’t be celebrating were very excited.

As this ironic and ridiculous situation played out, the main light cannon opened fire once again!

Chapter 417: The Optimal Bombardment

The main light cannon turret fired!

Boom!

A thick light beam shot straight at the pier.

In the port battlefield, the gold level Song Shou and Er Gua Zi were fighting each other.

The light beam was extremely fast, and even if they were on guard, they wouldn’t be able to react.

Er Gua Zi had been maintaining a defensive battle skill, and when he realized the light beam was shot towards them, it was already too late.

He could only clench his teeth, prepared to get through the pain.

But the white hot light beam brushed past him and struck Song Shou.

Song Shou also couldn’t react in time, but he too was maintaining a defensive battle skill and also had a special movement battle skill.

Battle skill – Grass Movement!

When the wind blew, the grass moved.

The fighter who created this battle skill was ingenious.

The principle behind the Grass Movement battle skill was this: when an immense attack was drawing near, the user could take advantage of the new situation and in passing, immediately relocate and avoid the attack. If it couldn’t be avoided, then when the attack hit the fighter, part of its power would be drained away.

This kind of evasion movement was not subject to the fighter’s control, like the wind blowing small grass, it was triggered by and responded to external pressure.

The main light cannon’s explosive attack had immense might.

To Song Shou, the moment before the bombardment hit, it set off a squall.

The light beam had taken aim at him, and before his mind could react, the Grass Movement battle skill had flung his body backwards.

A moment later, the place where he had been standing had a deep hole blown into it by the light beam bombardment.

“The incandescent light beam hit nothing!” The hearts of Hun Tong and the old fishman elder shook.

The first two times, the main light cannon had hit its target, but on the third time, it had achieved nothing.

“What happened?” The cannon shot caused Er Gua Zi’s heart to shiver in fear and bewilderment, “Did the shot deviate?”

“He thought he was going to be hit and suffer serious damage.

In the end, he noticed the light beam cannon had shot at Song Shou.

But Song Shou didn’t get hit.

Er Gua Zi looked at Song Shou, clearly afraid of him.

“The light beam shot towards him.”

“But even at so short a distance, he actually wasn’t hit.”

“Song Shou reacted too quickly!”

“He was actually able to dodge it?!”

Er Gua Zi couldn’t help but narrow his eyes; at that moment, he suddenly realized the truth.

That was despite him being on the up, suppressing Song Shou, Song Shou wasn’t using his full strength.

“This guy still has hidden skills!”

Song Shou had been conserving his strength.

He had used eight to nine parts of his strength, always holding back one or two parts.

After all, this wasn’t his territory.

However, Song Shou’s face was gloomy.

“Just now, it seems the cannon was aimed at me?”

“What’s going on?!”

“What is Hun Tong up to? What has happened to him up there?”

In the main light cannon turret.

Hun Tong sighed, “I didn’t hit Song Shou!”

“Before, I was able to hit Qi You because the Golden Flash surprised him and also because I diverted most of Qi You’s attention.”

“If we had a gold level coordinating with our fire, then the hit rate would be substantially improved.”

The clan elder’s heart was also filled with regret, “His battle skill isn’t ordinary, it doesn’t seem to be consciously controlled.”

“At such close range, I wouldn’t be able to evade it.”

On the other side, Yan Ding and the naga priestess also took note of this strange scene.

“Why did the magic ship bombard its own people?” The naga priestess felt baffled.

“Was it because the hull was unstable, causing the attack to deviate? Or was it due to the prior attack damaging the light cannons?” Yan Ding also looked skeptical.

“Which to shoot next? Continue to target Song Shou or switch to Yan Ding?” The fishman elder restrained his disappointment but was growing more nervous.

Because he knew this opportunity was too rare!

He rapidly pondered.

“Qi You was heavily injured, is hiding in his flagship, and hasn’t shown his head again, he is a pirate and isn’t a prime target.”

“Song Shou got a fright, that battle skill of his seemed to allow him to dodge the light beam cannon and shooting him now will be at a lower accuracy than before.”

“Yan Ding…he hasn’t revealed any explosive movement skills so far, but he also uses defensive battle skills, and most crucially, he can fly!”

“He is a bigger threat to the Golden Flash.”

“He should be ‘Shadow’, a leader of the secret spy organization, if he is shot dead, the vile effects won’t just be limited to just a gold level dying.”

“Then let him taste this power.”

“Even if he isn’t killed, as long as he is bombarded, it will allow the Lord Priestess to understand that something unexpected has occurred on the Golden Flash. She should be able to make the connection that the Devil Gold Coins are affecting Hun Tong!”

Thinking of that, the old fishman elder said to Hun Tong, “Shoot Yan Ding!”

Hun Tong nodded, but then shook his head, “I still need more time until the light cannon turret is able to fire again.”

“The alchemy components need to cool and aren’t able to take consecutive discharges.”

The old fishman elder stared blankly.

He recalled something.

Indeed, there was a gap between every time the main light cannon turret shot.

It never did consecutive shots.

Hun Tong then said, “We can now use a light net bombardment.”

“The light net bombardment, as a matter of fact, is shot by the side light cannon turrets, their alchemy components have already cooled off.” Hun Tong asked the old fishman elder for instructions.

Regardless of which bombardment was sent, all of them were risky, and Hun Tong couldn’t judge which option was more advantageous.

The old fishman elder’s heart slammed and jumped.

Faced with this problem, he was more nervous than he had been while fighting Hun Tong.

From somewhere mysterious and inexorable, he had a strong premonition.

This bombardment would likely decide the outcome of this war!

“Shooting a gold level is liable to hit nothing.”

“The target might not necessarily even die from it!”

“Not only that, but it cannot be fired right now.”

The light net bombardment can cover a large area but is even less of a threat to the gold levels.”

“No matter which is picked, after this bombardment, everyone will realize the Golden Flash has had an unforeseen event on it.”

“When the humans counterattack, the gold levels will be the greatest threat to us.”

…..

The old fishman elder’s head was burning with thoughts.

The more he thought, the more his mind was in chaos.

All kinds of feelings were entangled with each other in an increasingly jumbled mess.

Overall, shooting a gold level needed time and had a low accuracy. If an enemy gold was heavily damaged, the advantage established would be massive.

Firing a light net bombardment didn’t require waiting, and the hit rate was high, however even if it hit a gold level, it wouldn’t hurt them much.

“Decide, decide now!”

“The longer time drags on, the fewer chances there will be.”

The old fishman elder understood that the situation was urgent and needed to make every second count.

When met with a hard choice, one’s temperament played an immense role.

The old fishman elder finally growled, “Use the net attack, Hun Tong.”

According to his nature, he decided not to take a gamble, and instead chose the most dependable bombardment pattern.

The discs spiraled in the air.

Bang.

The main light cannon turret shuddered and erupted with vast flowing light.

The flowing light dispersed to the remaining four light cannons; there had been five, but the Deep Sea Monster Fish had destroyed one of them.

Four light beams shot at the closest Flying Whirling Discs and immediately scattered, once, twice, thrice.

A light net quickly formed in the air.

There were thousands of light beams at every tip of the light net.

The light beams shot into human fighters, causing innumerable slaughter!

Hun Tong didn’t forget to “show consideration” for Song Shou and Yan Ding.

The two gold levels couldn’t keep evading and seemed to be in a difficult situation.

But Hun Tong’s bombardment was focused on the silver levels.

Commander Beng Dai couldn’t protect himself and was struck by two at the same time, killing him on the spot!

“The commander, the commander has been killed!” The Bandage mercenaries howled in grief, and their morale was at the edge of collapse.

Commander Beng Dai never anticipated that the light beams would target him.

He was careless.

Now he was dead.

There was also anguished wailing from the merchant alliance flagships.

The merchant alliance representatives were killed and wounded.

By initial estimates, at least three silver level merchant alliance representatives were dead!

On the island battlefield, the pharmacist was struck by a light beam in the thigh, causing it to vanish and leave his lower leg still standing on the ground.

The pharmacist fell to the ground, clutching his thigh and howling in anguish.

The human soldiers had suffered a calamity, while the patrol fleet was even more of a mess.

The Golden Flash’s bombardment really had a deceitful nature this time.

The humans would have never expected that their trump card had suddenly become the enemy’s firepower!

When many of them saw the Flying Whirling Discs above their heads, none paid them any heed.

As a result, they became targets one after the other.

Hun Tong had an astonishing battle result with this bombardment!

An unprecedented military success.

From a bird’s eye view of the battlefield, one could notice that there were hundreds of empty holes where the human lines had been bombed.

What an irony, Hun Tong’s optimal bombardment success was not against his enemy, but against his own soldiers.

This backstab really was too ruthless!

“Why?” Many people were deathly pale.

“No, this can’t be!” Another person held their head, unable to accept the facts.

“What just happened, is someone controlling the Feudal Lord?” After getting past their shock, the cultivators frowned as they looked up.

They sensed something fishing going on up there.

The fighting between the clan elder and Hun Tong had already ceased.

Everyone had forgotten about that due to the Golden Flash being stuck and its descent to the ocean surface.

The naga priestess’ eyes flashed radiantly, and she immediately guessed the truth, “Perhaps it is the Devil Gold Coins. I didn’t expect this large sum to actually end up in Hun Tong’s hands.”

“This…is truly wonderful!”

This was the best surprise the fishmen had got since the battle commenced.

The situation changed dramatically.

The scales of victory violently swung towards the fishmen!

Song Shou decided to retreat and quickly reached the edge of the port battlefield.

Yan Ding also stopped attacking the naga priestess.

A dense black cloud eagerly lifted him up towards the Golden Flash.

The old fishman elder’s judgement was sound.

The naga priestess recovered her freedom.

She didn’t fly up to the Golden Flash to help defend the old fishman elder.

She instead looked towards the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The expedition to Two Eyes Island was her heavy responsibility.

From start to finish, she didn’t forget what her greatest objective was.

The Golden Flash’s bombardment also caused Zi Di to blankly stare.

She also thought she would suffer the Golden Flash’s counterattack.

She had already made preparations.

But they weren’t needed.

“If that is so, do I keep shooting it?”

“But why did it help the fishmen?”

Chapter 418: Lord Divine Envoy!

Zi Di was different from the old fishman elder.

The old fishman elder knew about the Devil Gold Coins, therefore he easily guessed the truth.

But Zi Di didn’t know.

“Why did Hun Tong’s flagship suddenly turn its guns around and shoot those on our side?” For a moment, Zi Di was unable to understand.

“Wait a minute!”

“My will was tampered with. Don’t tell me Hun Tong is also the same?”

Although Zi Di was putting the fishmen first with all sincerity and wholeheartedly wanted to help the fishmen retake Two Eyes Island, she hadn’t lost any reason.

She also understood her condition and hadn’t been deceived.

“Hun Tong might be in the same situation as me. Not only him, but also Cang Xu!” Zi Di looked at a magic image.

This magic image showed Cang Xu fighting the dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, and the others.

When the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge saw Cang Xu was acting amiss, they naturally wouldn’t try to kill Cang Xu and were merely superficially flexing their prestige.

The real threat to Cang Xu was Chi Lai.

However, Chi Lai wasn’t in good condition. He didn’t have any equipment and much of his battle qi reserves had been depleted by his cannon fire and previous fighting.

More crucially, the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were “fumbling their coordination” to slow down and hinder Chi Lai’s offensive.

Chi Lai was being dragged down, but he didn’t suspect anything.

After all, this was the first time he, Long Fu, and Shi Qi had fought together, not having a mutual understanding with them was normal.

Their display was no surprise to Cang Xu.

It was because of this “consideration” that Cang Xu was able to have the strength to deal with the pharmacist and the auxiliary city guard commander.

The pharmacist had been distracted by the ghosts filling the sky, leading to a light beam taking his leg.

Zi Di withdrew her sight from Cang Xu and quickly scanned the other magic images.

“If Hun Tong has changed to the fishman’s side, then the fishmen hold the obvious advantage in terms of gold levels.”

“That bombardment annihilated many human silver levels. More than half of the core force has perished!”

“Cang Xu is similarly affected, so the remaining obstacles are the commander, Zong Ge, and the rest.”

“I just bombed the flying magic ship, and Cang Xu’s state will surely cause them to have suspicions.”

“Net, they will probably capture Cang Xu, try to return to the Deep Sea Monster Fish, and check on me.”

“That is out of the question.”

Zi Di was bent on helping the fishmen, and if the dragonman youngster and the others did this, then it was bound to interfere with that greatly.

“The old fishman elder and Hun Tong had been fighting fiercely, but now the flying magic ship is calm.”

“The old fishman elder is a gold level, and it is slim that he was beheaded in such a short time.”

“Therefore, my condition is probably due to the fishmen.”

“The old fishman elder and Hun Tong are working together!”

When Zi Di’s analysis reached that point, she realized what she had to do next.

“Although I don’t know how I got tricked, if I join with the fishmen’s higherups, it is very possible that I will gain their trust.”

Since the flying magic ship was a turncoat, Zi Di wouldn’t shoot it again.

“First I need to converge with the naga priestess.”

“The fishmen’s influence on me is rapidly weakening.”

“After finding the naga priestess, I must give her authority as soon as possible!”

“By giving the fishmen the Deep Sea Monster Fish, I can help them take back Two Eyes Island even more.”

“At the same time, with a gold level at my side, I can deal accordingly with the commander, Zong Ge, and the other silver levels, along with myself when I recover!”

“I can tell the secrets of Mystifying Monster Island to the naga priestess.”

“If that happens, she can use those secrets to blackmail me and the other survivors.”

“Under those circumstances, the survivors can aid the fishmen!”

Thinking of that, Zi Di knew what she had to do.

She was a magician and had gathered much information with the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Through intelligent analysis, she had many new judgments.

With this new basis, she changed her prior plan and decided to try converging with the naga priestess.

Of course, before departing, she had the Deep Sea Monster Fish recover Hai Ping’s corpse.

The “giant fishman” opened its mouth and temporarily placed Hai Ping’s corpse in it, then began to swim quickly towards the naga priestess.

On the other side, after the naga priestess saw Hun Tong bombard the humans, she immediately guessed why.

Yan Ding had disengaged with the naga priestess and was rushing towards the Golden Flash.

The naga priestess’ heart was tied to Hai Ping’s saint level corpse, and didn’t stop Yan Ding. Instead like an arrow loosened into the ocean, she swiftly swam towards where the purple scaled fishman had died.

She used both a detection and an acceleration divine spell.

Then she saw the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s fishman shape.

At that moment, the naga priestess’ heart shook, and her voice faltered, “Lord Divine Envoy?!”

The naga priestess’ bearing changed distinctly in the magic image.

“What’s going on? Her expression is a little strange.” Zi Di was puzzled.

She didn’t stop moving and drove the Deep Sea Monster Fish to face the naga priestess.

“Priestess.” Said Zi Di.

“I pay my respects to the Lord Divine Envoy!” The naga priestess looked up at the giant fishman with a pious greeting.

“???” Zi Di was incessantly confused, it seemed the naga priestess had mistaken her for another.

She wanted to keep talking when suddenly, her expression changed dramatically.

Because at that moment, the power of the agreement in her body suddenly collapsed and rapidly dissipated!

Zi Di had returned back to normal.

Chapter 419: Deceiving the Priest

Although the survivors had stolen all of the Devil Gold Coins, most of them had been spent at the auction.

The scant few were in Zi Di’s and Cang Xu’s hands.

Of those, Zi Di had less than Cang Xu.

Therefore, Zi Di recovered first.

She also understood how the agreement’s power worked: when the agreement was active, its power was constantly decreasing, but as it weakened, it maintained a constant level of influence. Until it reached its time limit, when it would suddenly collapse and quickly dwindle to nothing.

This also caused Zi Di’s misjudgment.

If she had known about it earlier, she would have adopted a different strategy.

Zi Di had returned to normal.

For a split second, she blankly stared in place.

When her will reinstated itself from before, an intensely complex mood swept over her heart like a huge wave.

Dread.

“I actually did that, unimaginable, how did I fall into this trap?”

Joy.

“Heavens, fortunately the agreement has dissipated before I transferred the Deep Sea Monster Fish.”

Self-blame.

“My conducts and deeds put everyone into danger. I was just a step from revealing all our secrets.”

Vigilance and nervousness.

“The naga priestess is right before my eyes, what should I do?”

She was a gold level caster.

While the Deep Sea Monster Fish was just a gold level magic ship.

If there were some distance between them, then there’d be some leeway to contend with. But now, they were nearly face to face, the distance was too close.

The naga priestess was completely able to break through and kill her way into the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The ship didn’t have a gold level defending it, or even a silver level.

No, to be more precise, there is a silver level.

The ghost Lan Lu.

In life, he was a silver level two headed ogre magician.

In death, he was a silver level magician ghost.

But Lan Lu had been wounded when it ambushed Xi Dai Zi, it was in poor condition and without soul crystals, it would be difficult for it to recover completely.

A peak condition ghost Lan Lu couldn’t be the naga priestess’ match either.

Zi Di had no confidence he could deal with a gold level.

“I can’t let her see any flaws! Otherwise, the Deep Sea Monster Fish might change hands.”

“It is very lucky indeed that she mistook who I was.”

“No, that shouldn’t be so!”

“The Deceptive Disguise spell, she hasn’t seen through it and falsely thinks the Deep Sea Monster Fishh is a lifeform. A giant fishman.”

“At the same time, its characteristics are obvious, almost impossible for the naga priestess to be wrong.”

“In other words, does such a divine envoy fishman exist?”

Zi Di racked her grains, guessing a portion of the truth.

Her eyes glistened, “It’s likely that when War Merchant bult the Deep Sea Monster Fish, he deliberately designed its form this way.”

“War Merchant is a goblin, not a human, and an enemy to the empire. He is an arms manufacturer and a massive smuggler, so why did he design this form like this?”

“Don’t tell me he has contacts with the fishmen and sells weapons to them?”

“Or does he have some crafty plot, desiring to masquerade as a fishman divine envoy to attain some secret goal?”

Zi Di’s train of thought diverged the more she pondered.

Seeing the fishman divine envoy had sunk into silence, the naga priestess questioned, “Lord Divine Envoy, do you have Hai Ping’s saint corpse?”

Zi Di was startled out of her thoughts.

However, that question did give Zi Di some hints.

She had some guesses when the purple scaled fishman suddenly appeared with Hai Ping’s corpse.

Now that the naga priestess asked about it, Zi Di increasingly felt that it was one of the fishmen’s main goals.

Zi Di was just about to admit that Hai Ping was in her hands.

But after thinking about it, she felt that wasn’t appropriate.

If by any chance they demanded it, she didn’t want to hand the saint level corpse over to them.

It was an extremely valuable thing.

Not something that was easy to buy on the market.

For example, Two Eyes Island’s auction was of unprecedented scale, but not a single piece of saint level material was there.

The saint level was above the gold level, and even if was decayed, it wasn’t something the survivors could obtain.

“Furthermore, spitting it out might become a flaw.”

Zi Di took a deep breath and deliberately used a serene tone, “Don’t be nervous, priestess, everything has gone to my God’s plan.”

She didn’t say she had Hai Ping’s corpse, nor did she deny it, it was all ambiguous.

But the priestess quickly imagined what she wanted, and she smiled cheerfully, “Praise our God!”

She knew she had been assigned this heavy burden, but after doing everything she could, the purple scaled fishman had died, a blow that chilled the naga priestess’ heart.

She wasn’t able to understand why the purple scaled fishman was detected, causing her to be shot by the light cannon, and was exceedingly worried that this operation would fail at the last step.

Filled with wishful thinking, she dove towards the scene and discovered a giant fishman.

From her point of view, she immediately imagined that her God predicted this outcome. Therefore, not only was I dispatched, but also a fishman divine envoy. The divine envoy was hiding on the seafloor, and when the light beam cannon was about to damage the saint corpse, they acted promptly and saved the plan!

“If it weren’t for the Divine Envoy, and just me, this operation would have been a complete failure already!” The naga priestess rejoiced to herself.

She guessed that Hun Tong was being affected by the Devil Gold Coins, but didn’t suspect it had been caused by the fishman divine envoy before her.

Zi Di continued to use a serene tone, “Next, priestess, you will command the fishmen to retreat. If they don’t listen to your orders, then you will leave immediately; no need to look after their fates.”

“This?” The naga priestess had misgivings.

She guessed that Hun Tong “defected”, the current situation was very good.

But the divine envoy was making her retreat?

After a moment’s hesitation, the naga priestess immediately bowed in salute, “I understand, Lord Divine Envoy!”

Despite having misgivings, she chose to comply.

She was a priestess.

Although she was unable to make sense of the divine envoy’s order, the matter of Hai Ping’s corpse had already proved that the divine envoy was correct.

“My God predicted this.”

“The good situation before us is nothing but a facade.”

“That’s right.”

“The Devil Gold Coins can work for a time, but there are many things that can counteract its power.”

“Which pantheon of gods is the strongest? It is without question the Sheng Ming Empire’s!”

“I can’t rely too much on the Devil Gold Coins, after all they are tools of an external god.”

The naga priestess asked, “I will move at once, what about you Lord Divine Envoy?”

Zi Di answered, “You don’t need to get involved. Your mission has already been accomplished, priestess. Go now, this is all part of our God’s plan.”

Zi Di placed everything on the gods.

The naga priestess was not a fighter, nor a magician, she was a clergy.

As expected it worked great!

By touching upon the god she believed in, the naga priestess unconsciously stopped pondering and only wanted to obey.

Even if there were some doubts, the depths of her heart would persuade her.

The naga priestess left and began to contact the other fishmen.

Zi Di drove the Deep Sea Monster Fish somewhere else.

Both sides gradually grew far apart, and Zi Di had successfully deceived a gold level enemy.

She finally let out a breath, and exhaustion filled her thoughts.

But a moment later, she immediately used an alchemy communication device to inform the dragonman youngster and the others of everything that had happened to her.

Zi then proposed, “I hope you can converge with me to protect the Deep Sea Monster Fish.”

“I don’t know how I fell into that state.”

“But just in case it happens again, those affected can be stopped by our comrades.”

“The Deep Sea Monster Fish cannot be lost!”

“The secrets of Mystifying Monster Island cannot be exposed!!”

Zi Di didn’t know about the Devil Gold Coins.

As a matter of fact, the agreement only took effect once.

But she was really scared, and her proposal was very sensible.

“That actually happened!” The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were mentally and physically shaken.

Their exchange with Zi Di had confirmed their prior conjecture – Cang Xu really was acting wrong!

“But how were they affected?”

“No matter what, we have to join Zi Di as soon as possible.”

“How do we deal with Cang Xu?”

“He knows too much, and if he is forced, what will we do if he starts shouting out our secrets?”

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were equally perturbed.

At this time, Cang Xu’s eyes suddenly cleared.

He had also gone back to normal.

“I, this…damn it!”

Strong feelings battered his mind, causing him to freeze in place.

Chi Lai’s eyes suddenly flashed, and seizing upon the enemy’s weakness, he hurled the stone pillar he had been using as a weapon at Cang Xu.

But a moment later, the dragonman youngster jumped at Cang Xu, and as chance would have it, was hit by the stone pillar.

Chi Lai was alarmed, and he soon apologized, “Sorry, commander, that wasn’t my intention.”

The dragonman youngster dropped to the floor, his head and face covered in grim. He nodded to Chi Lai, “Never mind that, after this, we really should train our coordination together.”

When Chi Lai saw the dragonman youngster wasn’t blaming him, he couldn’t help but be moved.

He noticed that Long Fu was a tolerant leader.

This kind of situation had appeared many times already, but he was never blamed.

On the other side, Cang Xu had received “cover” and reacted by immediately withdrawing.

Countless ghosts screamed, lifting a bone c hilling wind as they shielding him in all directions with terrible and astonishing grandeur.

“Exposing myself as a necromancer really is troublesome.” Cang Xu sighed to himself.

He knew he couldn’t stay here long and decided to leave the island and battlefield on the spot.

“But what to do about Qing Xin?”

Cang Xu slowed his flying speed and looked at Qing Xin not too far in the distance, not knowing what to say to win the trust of them.

However, Qing Xin unexpectedly approached, flew to Cang Xu’s side and yelled, “We are leaving, why are you still dawdling?!”

Cang Xu was stunned.

Qing Xin’s reaction was quite bizarre!

She knew he was a necromancer.

But immediately afterwards, Qing Xin added, “You will explain things later, but we will be departing first and use the chaos while it lasts to get out of Two Eyes Island.”

“Ok!” Cang Xu and Qing Xin flew towards the Iron Lump together.

Ya Ma tenaciously gazed at the two’s backs but didn’t pursue.

After Cang Xu used necromancy, her summons suffered great losses.

Ya Ma wasn’t confident she could face a necromancer on the same level as her.

Chapter 420: Slave For Long Fu

Hong Zhu wanted to cry but had no tears.

She had helped Cang Xu fight Ya Ma and Yue Ban because she thought him a big customer.

She never expected that he was actually a necromancer!

The moment Cang Xu revealed who he really was, Hong Zhu went completely numb.

“I’m in trouble.”

“So much trouble!”

“No matter if they live or die, I will be investigated.”

“Collaborating with a necromancer?”

“Gods above, I am wrongly accused!!”

“That won’t do, I have to hide!”

Hong Zhu was very remorseful.

If she had known this was how things ended, she wouldn’t have helped Cang Xu even if she was beaten to death.

This auction had given her more than enough funds to prepare for her next adventure.

But now, that adventure had to be shelved, for she had to first flee the limelight.

“Don’t leave!”

“Stop!!”

Men Shi shouted.

He ran with his short legs rapidly across the ground, attempting to chase after Cang Xu and Qing Xin.

The silver level dwarf had been on the ship but left to look for a chance to repay the dragonman youngster’s kindness.

As a result, not only did he not find a chance to repay his debt of gratitude, he also saw who Cang Xu really was.

He had been stupefied.

Now seeing Cang Xu flying towards the Iron Lump, he seemed to be struck by electricity.

Because his clansmen were still on that ship!

Men Shi was very anxious and wanted to save his clansmen from the necromancer.

But he wasn’t that fast, and when his pursuit reached the coast, he could only look onwards helplessly as Cang Xu and Qing Xin landed on the Iron Lump.

The alchemy puppets on the Iron Lump were under Cang Xu’s control.

The people all knew what Cang Xu really was.

But Qing Xin immediately placated the blond mother and child and told that that although Cang Xu was a necromancer, he was still deserving of trust.

The dwarfs were restless.

Cang Xu promptly stated that he wouldn’t harm them.

This statement didn’t do much.

The dwarfs began to become threatening.

Cang Xu immediately turned hostile and coldly snorted, “Dwarfs, look at your relatives next to you, your old parents, your young children.”

“I hope you aren’t impulsive.”

“It would be an easy thing for me to kill you all!”

Cang Xu’s “true” silver level aura and the many ghosts emerging throughout the ship added to his might.

The dwarfs felt threatened, and a number of them indignantly and furiously glared at Cang Xu, their necks flushed red with anger.

But in the end, they didn’t do anything drastic.

Cang Xu let out a breath, and had an alchemy puppet escort these dwarfs into the hold of the ship.

After finishing this, he immediately drove the magic ship away from Two Eyes Island.

“What? We’re retreating?” Xi Dai Zi and the others had received the naga priestess’ order.

Xi Dai Zi had been involved with the Devil Gold Coin affair.

The upending of the Golden Flash had caused Xi Dai Zi to guess that Hun Tong was affected by the coins.

The situation was great, why were they retreating?

Xi Dai Zi was very puzzled.

“Boss, you have to decide!” Xi Dai Zi communicated with Er Gua Zi.

Er Gua Zi was fighting Song Shou.

He had been “shown consideration” by the Golden Flash’s last bombardment.

Song Shou had been fired at a few times, but it didn’t harm him in the slightest.

Compared to the main light cannon, the light net bombardment didn’t threaten gold levels.

But with their side’s trump card being reduced to an enemy thug, Song Shou’s fighting spirit had slumped, thus, he stopped blocking and was mainly evading.

Er Gua Zi glanced up at the Golden Flash.

High in the air, Yan Ding was riding a black cloud and besieging the Golden Flash.

On the Golden Flash, the old clan elder and Hun Tong’s alchemy clone were vigorously defending.

Both sides were deadlocked.

Suddenly, Yan Ding was struck by the old fishman elder’s battle skill, and he seemed to crumble like smoke.

“A fake!”

“Where is the real one?”

Yan Ding feinted, and a moment later, smoke condensed into a human form on the port battlefield

It was Yan Ding’s real body.

“Song Shou!” Yan Ding shouted, “We fight together.”

“The Golden Flash cannot fall into enemy hands.”

“Otherwise, not even I cannot escape into the vast ocean.”

“If Hun Tong dies in battle, we’ll split the Golden Flash equally!”

Song Shou sighed, and immediately responded, “Carry me up!”

Black smoke curled up and lifted Song Shou into the sky.

Er Gua Zi attacked, but Yan Ding stopped him.

He didn’t have a flying battle skill.

After a few rounds against each other, Song Shou had reached an altitude where Er Gua Zi could only look onwards helplessly.

“Xi Dai Zi, send me up!” Er Gua Zi yelled, “Take the others away!”

At this crucial juncture, Er Gua Zi decided to help the old fishman elder alone, and had the others evacuate the island.

Because the naga priestess’ order caused him to misjudge the situation.

They were winning, yet the naga priestess had commanded a withdrawal.

She must have realized the enemy had another trump card!

Er Gua Zi didn’t want his clansmen and subordinates to sacrifice themselves in vain.

Too many fishmen had sacrificed themselves in the battle already.

The fishmen corpses left on the port battlefield could be piled into a small mountain.

But Er Gua Zi was unreconciled and wouldn’t give up this amazing opportunity.

He would rather take a risk and wanted to see what the enemy’s trump card was!

Xi Dai Zi expressed his difficulties, “Boss, I don’t have magic that can send you up there.”

“Then use the water cannon!” Er Gua Zi was very resolute.

Since Song Shou had left already, Er Gua Zi quickly converged with Xi Dai Zi and the others.

Bang.

The Vinegar Jar opened fire once again.

A pillar of water rammed into Er Gua Zi, forcing him high into the air.

After Cang Xu withdrew, the dragonman youngster and the others immediately rushed to the port.

Upon hearing the cannon fire, they all raised their heads at the sky and saw Er Gua Zi flying out of the water cannon, coughing up blood throughout the trip as he dashed up bravely with no thoughts to his safety.

He was a fishman, not a dragonman, and his fish scales were far less defensive than dragon scales.

However, setting aside that they were enemies, the fishmen didn’t lack firm determination.

“The flying magic ship has become the gold levels’ final battlefield!” The dragonman youngster sighed.

“We cannot interfere there.” Zong Ge coldly replied.

Chi Lai then said, “As long as we find the gold cannon, perhaps I can still shoot it, and injure the flagship.”

The gold level cannon needed a massive amount of energy.

Last time, a magic formation had stored up a lot of energy.

But if it was just Chi Lai shooting by his lonesome, he needed to imbue it with all of his battle qi, a terrible consumption.

After all, Chi Lai was just a silver level fighter.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ship also had been sunk by the Vinegar Jar, and the gold cannon had subsequently fallen to the seafloor.

Chi Lai had been involved and knew what had happened.

The gold cannon really had been damaged somewhat.

But how specifically was unknown, if they found it and got an artillery shell, perhaps they could still fire it!

It wasn’t an ordinary cannon, and gunpowder wasn’t used to give it motive power. Therefore, dampness wouldn’t impede it at all.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge had returned to the port to salvage the cannon.

However, this was merely a pretense.

Their true goal was to dive into the water, get into the Deep Sea Monster Fish, and converge with Zi Di to protect their rear!

The dragonman youngster ordered, “Chi Lai, do your best to rest. Don’t enter the water, recover as much battle qi as possible, we’ll dredge up the c annon for you.”

“Di Bi, inspect the ships at port now, we need sea worthy ships.”

“We need to prepare for two things. One is a continued battle with the enemy and the other is looking for a way to retreat.”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps loudly complied.

“Wait for me.” Just as the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge wanted to dive in, Men Shi ran over.

“Commander Long Fu!” Men Shi panted as he pleaded, “Help me!”

“My clansmen are being held captive by that wretched necromancer.”

“Help me, I….I don’t have any other options!”

Men Shi was self-aware.

He knew that he couldn’t overtake the Iron Lump by swimming. It was also likely that while in the ocean, he would be sniped by magic.

There also wasn’t just a silver level necromancer, there was also Qing Xin.

He had paced back and forth on the coast, fretfully indignant, exasperated, and powerless.

He could do nothing but watch on helplessly as the Iron Lump grew farther away.

Then he quickly noticed the dragonman youngster was leading the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps back to the port.

Song Shou and Er Gua Zi were charging towards the Golden Flash, leaving the port deserted.

It was just as Men Shi had said – he was out of options.

Therefore, he could only ask the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps for help.

With the latter’s help, he could have a little faith.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge glanced at each other.

The dragonman youngster raised his head and sincerely sighed, “Everything is a mess.”

Immediately after, he clapped Men Shi’s shoulder, “Men Shi, don’t worry too much. Since that necromancer hasn’t slaughtered your clansmen, perhaps he is treating them as his hostages. For now, at least, they are still safe.”

“We will help you, let us fight alongside each other once again!”

Men Shi was very moved, he wanted to say something in thanks, but the words couldn’t leave his mouth.

He was an honest man, and emotionally reserved, usually not expressing them.

He could only choke out sobs as his mind surged.

True sentiments are seen in hard times!

The dragonman youngster agreeing to help was him providing help in Men Shi’s hour of need.

“Your kindness has become even greater. How can I repay such kindness?” Men Shi’s heart was filled with vexation.

“Now is not the time to address that, the most important thing is saving your clansmen.”

“At worst, from now on, I will join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and give my all to Long Fu to repay his grace.” Men Shi said to himself.

The dragonman youngster adjusted his plan.

He had men Shi guard Chi Lai.

While he and Zong Ge prioritized retrieving the gold cannon.

After returning, they would embark together on a ship to pursue the Iron Lump and wait for an opportunity to attack the flying magic ship.

A dispute broke out on the Vinegar Jar.

Peng Ding Zi roared, “The situation is in our favor, why are we withdrawing?!”

“It was the Lord Priestess’ direct order, don’t be deceived by the facade before you.” Xi Dai Zi was cold.

“The commander has also ordered us to withdraw immediately.”

As a matter of fact, Xi Dai Zi was also doubtful of the naga priestess’ order for a time.

If he still had battle strength, Xi Dai Zi might have disobeyed and firmly remained on the battlefield, waiting for an opportunity to aid his clan elder and commander.

The fishmen had paid too high a price.

No fishman was willing to retreat now!

But Xi Dai Zi was in a terrible state.

His ears were buzzing, he was dizzy, and he was barely able to control the Vinegar Jar.

Under these conditions, Xi Dai Zi knew the heavy responsibility on him, and also knew he had no choice but to obey and lead the fishmen’s retreat.

Chapter 421: Wealth Security Spell

Xi Dai Zi and the others didn’t encounter any meaningful resistance as they retreated.

The reason was simple.

The gold levels were focused on the Golden Flash.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was at the port, preparing a path of retreat.

The pirates were all fluttering on the sea, carrying their reduced valuables back to their respective pirate ships.

With a big hole in it, the Vinegar Jar entered the sea.

It killed its way through the ocean battlefield.

In the ocean battlefield, there were vessels large and small intertwined with each other.

The humans were on the down low.

Because the bombardment had killed and injured too much of their high level strength.

The Golden Flash’s revolt had made human morale crumble further.

The Vinegar Jar’s retreat was like a tiger entering a flock of sheep, easily picking up their people.

Not only that, but they also stole several human ships.

Soon after, they reformed a small fleet and broke out of the siege.

Human ships were also freed as a result, and they routed and dispersed in all directions.

At this time, the Iron Lump was on the ocean.

Bloody corpses and burning sinking ships floated around it.

The Iron Lump was sailing in a circle and didn’t sail straight out of Two Eyes Island.

The reason was that Cang Xu had received the dragonman youngster’s message.

The dragonman youngster had him release the dwarfs and return them to Men Shi.

Cang Xu thought that since he had already been exposed a necromancer, he might as well go whole hog and collect souls. It just so happened that it gave his commander and the others a chance to save these troublesome dwarfs.

Necromancy spell – Soul Summoning!

That was how the Iron Lump was roaming the ocean.

Cang Xu was standing on the ship’s bow, constantly using Soul Summoning.

This environment really suits a necromancer!

He summoned many ghosts forth and gathered them at his side.

“A silver level ghost!” Cang Xu’s mind shook as he dredged up a giant fish!

“There should be more.”

“Not just one silver level.”

Cang Xu’s expectations grew, such a good opportunity was rare.

Bang, bang, bang…

Gold level battle skills created shockwaves and fierce explosions detonated everywhere.

The Golden Flash violently shook, it had become the final battlefield.

The gold levels understood that this was the final juncture of the battle, thus they went all out and the contest was extremely fierce.

Yan Ding and Song Shou battled the old fishman elder, Hun Tong, and his alchemy clone.

As for Er Gua Zi, he wasn’t able to get aboard, for he had been slapped down by Yan Ding on the way up.

The water cannon’s firing arc was too predictable.

Er Gua Zi didn’t have a flying battle skill and got the worst of it.

Yan Ding and Song Shou were on the offense, holding the initiative, but they hadn’t been able to break through for a long time.

The old fishman elder, Hun Tong, as well as his alchemy clone were defending the Golden Flash with all they had.

The Golden Flash was frail, and it couldn’t bear the gold level ruination upon it.

To save this ship, the three gold levels were in a passive state.

The old fishman elder never thought he would protect his enemy’s flagship, but thinking that this would become his clan’s, he exerted himself to the utmost.

However, Hun Tong’s alchemy clone in the end brought Er Gua Zi onto the ship.

Yan Ding tried to stop them but failed.

Hun Tong’s clone put Er Gua Zi onto the deck to stand guard, then immediately shouted to the fishman elder, “Follow me upstairs!”

The fishman elder’s heart shivered as he followed close behind.

The two advanced into the main light cannon.

Hun Tong came to the control room, looking anxious, “I can sense the agreement’s power weakening greatly.”

“To prevent accidents, I will now pass authority over the Golden Flash to you now!”

Hun Tong was in a different situation compared to Zi Di and Cang Xu.

He had a constant divine spell on his body.

While Zi Di and Cang Xu completely lacked defenses against the coins, Hun Tong’s defensive divine spell was gravely eating away at the Devil Gold Coin’s power.

Therefore, despite him having the most coins, and the agreement’s power being the strongest on him, it was being depleted violently.

The old fishman elder was also worried about Hun Tong’s state.

After a process, the authority transfer was at its last step.

“Do you confirm that you will transfer primary authority to another?” The control room’s monotonous voice asked.

“Confirmed!” Hun Tong sincerely said without the slightest hesitation.

“Please confirm the transfer target.”

A bright pillar of light appeared in the control room

The old fishman elder got Hun Tong’s indication and immediately stood in the light pillar.

The light pillar scanned the old fishman elder, gathering his information and characteristics.

Soon after, a needle appeared.

The old fishman elder suspiciously looked at Hun Tong.

Hun Tong explained at once, “Iti is a necessary procedure.”

“It will collect your bloodline, so the Golden Flash can belong to you and your entire clan.”

“In the future, even if you sacrifice your life, your bloodline descendants will still possess the foremost authority!”

Hun Tong had purchased the Golden Flash not only for himself, but also for his clan and his own bloodline.

But now, he surrendered this ship to the enemy without resistance.

The old fishman elder nodded and allowed the needle to stab into him and draw a lot of his blood.

Swiftly after, the monotonous voice stated, “Transfer target confirmed.”

“Please input the unlock password.”

Hun Tong promptly used his spirit to silently deliver a series of numbers.

“Transfer target, please input the unlock password again.”

The old fishman elder looked at Hun Tong.

Hun Tong explained, “Connect your spirit to the ship, and in your thoughts, say the following numbers: 3.14159265358979323846”

The old fishman elder was gradually dumbfounded, and couldn’t help but interrupt Hun Tong, “How many?”

Hun Tong sighed, “Although the cipher has a hundred digits, it is actually easy to remember, because it is π.”

“π…what is that?” The old fishman elder was stupefied, suffering from his great lack of mathematical knowledge.

Hun Tong was impatient, “Now is not the time to explain, I will say ten digits, and you will repeat them, quickly!”

Although the password was a hundred digits long, with Hun Tong’s method, it was quickly inputted.

“Unlock password confirmed.”

Upon hearing that, the old fishman elder let out a breath. When he was inputting the password, he was very worried that he would misstate a digit.

However immediately afterwards, he heard the Golden Flash say, “Please input the authority transfer password.”

“What is the meaning of this?” The old fishman elder scowled.

Hun Tong instantly replied, “That was the unlock password, this is the authority transfer password.”

“Don’t worry, this password is much shorter.”

“638256924”

The old fishman elder inputted the password correctly.

“Correct password.”

“Authority transfer in process.”

“5, 4, 3, 2, 1…..authority transfer successful.”

“Please input the final confirmation password.”

“Another password?!” The old fishman elder’s emerging smile stiffened and couldn’t help but glare.

Hun Tong said, “There are three passwords altogether.”

“This is the Wealth Church’s Wealth Security spell!”

“The Golden Flash is very valuable, and to be safe, this divine spell is always active.”

“The final password is 729073781, input it quickly!”

The old fishman elder repeated it once through, then immersed his spirit to input it.

Pop.

A moment later, a gold dagger pierced his heart.

Bubbling battle qi shattered his heart completely soon after.

A mortal blow!

The old fishman elder stared blankly for a moment before reacting.

He cracked his head around to see a malevolent looking Hun Tong.

“Stealing my ship, you go so far as to dare steal my ship!”

“Die, die for me!!”

Psh.

Sic.

Plop.

Three dagger stabs eviscerated the old fishman elder’s internal organs utterly.

The old fishman elder raised his head and roared.

His roar was filled with sorrow, anger, and remorse.

Hun Tong had changed too quickly.

The old fishman elder had actually been watching him the entire time but didn’t notice anything.

If he had known earlier, the old fishman elder would have definitely killed Hun Tong first.

Of course, there was a possibility that Hun Tong would resist.

After all, the agreement made Hun Tong help the fishmen retake the island as best he could, and if he believed his survival would be more beneficial to complete the agreement, he would do so.

Now, the old fishman elder was dead, and Hun Tong had turned back to normal; now on the Golden Flash, the only enemy gold level left was Er Gua Zi.

Hun Tong really was treacherous and cunning.

Er Gua Zi’s end goes without saying.

“No! Even if I die in battle, Er Gua Zi cannot, the clan cannot be without a gold level.”

As he faced death’s door, the old fishman elder used all of his strength to self-detonate!

Since they had been driven away from their home, the old fishman elder had cultivated the Self-detonation battle skill, prepared that if he lost the war in the future, he still had hope he could take down Hun Tong with him!

The main light cannon turret was blown into two by his Self-detonation battle skill.

The top floor fell, leaving only a portion of it still standing.

From the explosion, Hun Tong flew out and held half the old fishman elder’s head.

He flew high into the air and shouted, “The fishman chief is dead!”

For a moment, countless people’s faces changed color.

Chapter 422: The Chaotic Warfare Concludes

“In the end, Hun Tong still triumphed.” Qi You sighed as he looked into the distance.

He had been heavily injured by the main light cannon turret, but after recuperating, the injury was stabilized.

“Withdraw.” Qi You gazed at the Golden Flash, that magic ship was too big a threat.

The Ten Touch Pirates withdrew first.

They were the only gold level pirates, and their every move affected the actions of others.

“Withdraw!” Dian Wan howled, even though he was dispirited. The light cannon beam had left him miserable.

Yang Cong Tou was wounded, sighing deeply as he gazed at the ocean surface, “Most of the valuables fell into the sea, what a true pity.”

The Applaud Pirates were all bitter.

From beginning to end, they had looted the least.

“Blame this on the captain!” Many people thought this of Hu Li.

They didn’t know that because Hu Li hadn’t stolen as much, they weren’t bombarded as much either, leading to their pirate group to suffer the least casualties.

“Where do you think you’re going?”

“Leave my money behind!”

When Hun Tong saw what the pirates were doing, he flew into a rage and immediately dispatched his alchemy clone to pursue them.

On the Golden Flash, there were only three light cannon turrets remaining out of the five, and the main light cannon turret was destroyed.

Hun Tong flew back and began to control the cannons to conduct a light beam bombardment.

High in the sky, the Golden Flash’s three light cannon turrets fired in succession, smashing some pirate ships.

Cang Xu was scared witless by this.

He was very worried that the Iron Lump would become a cannon target and immediately evacuated.

Originally, he still wanted to stay for a while, so the dragonman youngster and the others could carry out their hostage rescue show.

But he couldn’t wait any longer.

The Iron Lump quickly sailed away from Two Eyes Island.

“You can’t escape!” Men Shi stood on the port, gazing at the fading Iron Lump, his hands and fingers outstretched as pain and remorse racked his face.

Chi Lai was in the middle of adjusting the gold cannon’s components.

Zong Ge had fished it back up, but noticed that the cannon barrel was bent, and needed to be repaired before it could shoot.

As for the dragonman youngster, he was inside the Deep Sea Monster Fish, guarding against anything influencing Zi Di.

Zi Di was in the process of controlling the deep sea salvagers.

These alchemy puppets were great at cleaning the battlefield, especially on the sea floor.

The pirates’ painstakingly stolen treasure had all fallen to the seabed and were harvested by the alchemy puppets.

“A little strange.” Zi Di was tense.

“What’s going on?” The dragonman youngster was puzzled.

Zi Di spoke her mind, “According to the prior battlefield performance, I surmised the flying magic ship shouldn’t have a ship spirit. It was locking onto targets based on their worth, thereby accomplishing precise large scale bombardment.”

“Should I chase them down?” Yan Ding returned to ask Hun Tong.

Hun Tong was gloomy, “No. These damned fishmen will be thornier in the sea. Don’t forget, there is also that giant fishman.”

To him it seemed that although the old fishman elder was dead, there was still Er Gua Zi, the naga priestess, and then that giant fishman emerged.

The fishmen had three gold levels, the same amount as the humans.

The deep sea.

Fishmen were dead and wounded.

She believed in the Charming Blue Goddess and wasn’t good at healing. However, gods were omnipotent and possessed healing divine spells.

Clerics possessed the strongest healing skills and could easily obtain prestige.

His Vinegar Jar Pirates only had their flagship left and had been devastated more completely.

Chapter 423: Wisteria Cache

Qing Xin’s words held deep meaning, causing Cang Xu’s eyes to contract slightly.

Zi Di: ???

Should she resolve this misunderstanding?

This world didn’t just have humans, it also had elves, dragons, dwarfs, barbarians, goblins, and more.

Qing Xin explained it all in detail.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Once upon a time, her father wanted her to marry.

But after she refused, her father never asked that of her ever again.

Chapter 424: Because of You, I Have No Regrets

The Wisteria Cache!

Zi Di couldn’t help but sink into silence.

This important information surprised her greatly.

Before now, she never knew about it.

“I took over as president, and sorted out my father’s things, but didn’t find anything about it.”

“Perhaps because father’s death was too sudden, he was unable to leave a trail to the cache behind.”

“His things and home were definitely searched many times, and perhaps during the course of this, the trail was destroyed.”

“Father…”

When Zi Di thought that, she recalled her father’s voice and face, his tight brows, and felt his haggard heart and body.

Too many things have happened in these last few days.

First was the battle of wits at the auction.

Soon after, a chaotic battle involving Hun Tong, the fishmen, pirates, mercenary corps, merchant alliances, and other parties unfolded. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps couldn’t stay out of it and were unable to stop themselves from being completely drawn into the melee.

After Zi Di had left the auction hall, she kept watch from the Deep Sea Monster Fish. While doing so, in the final phase of the fighting, she suffered the agreement’s influence and narrowly avoided a serious mistake. She still didn’t know about the Devil Gold Coins’ existence and was still on edge.

Just now, she had heard about the Wisteria Cache from Qing Xin’s mouth.

In her jumbled train of thought, an idea suddenly appeared in Zi Di’s mind.

“Does Fei She know about the Wisteria Cache?”

Fei She was Zi Di’s father’s confidant and an elder who had helped found the Wisteria Merchant Alliance. He helped Zi Di’s father through their tough journey together, watching as the Wisteria Merchant Alliance sprung up, expanded, was divided up, and also saw Zi Di grow up.

Thinking of this, Zi Di was unable to hold herself back and contacted Fei She with the alchemy communication device.

Each of the survivors had one of these communication devices.

As an ordinary person, Fei She was looked after greatly. After escaping the auction hall, he had been hiding in a corner and never joined the melee.

With the Deceptive Disguise spell and a few self defense tricks, he was able to get through the chaotic warfare unscathed.

The communication device quickly connected.

Zi Di’s voice transmitted, directly asking, “Elder Fei She, did you know about the Wisteria Cache?”

Fei She was at first silent, then he replied, “Did Qing, Qing Xin tell you?”

Fei She was a spectator and knew many things that happened on the battlefield.

Zi Di responded, “The nobles have always been searching for the Wisteria Cache, consequently forcing older sister Qing Xin to live incognito and disguise herself as a slave. Elder, please tell me all that you know.”

Fei She deeply sighed, “President, my, my answer might dis, disappoint you.”

“I know about the Wis, Wisteria Cache, but I don’t know the true inside story.”

“The old president was constantly scurrying away and transferring resources to accumulate a, an extremely value, valuable resource. It was to be used for a resurge, resurgence.”

“Once upon a time, The Wisteria Ca, Cache was also our final ho, hope against our challenges. Because it was too import, important.”

“As a matter of fact, this was done deliberate, deliberately. It was because I wasn’t involved in it that I was able to survive the pur, purge.”

Zi Di’s lips tightened.

Elder Fei She’s response was actually within her expectations.

Suppose Elder Fei She knew about the cache, wouldn’t he have informed her of it already?

Even if he wanted to keep everything for himself, he wouldn’t have gone to the Wilderness Continent with her, experiencing life and death along the way.

Zi Di was silent for a moment before finally vocalizing her most deeply thought question.

“Elder Fei She…please, you must answer it. If I had agreed to the marriage connection…”

But Fei She immediately interrupted her, “President Zi Di!”

On Two Eyes Island, Fei She clutched the alchemy communication device and gazed at the scarlet sunset glow, his face solemn, “I have some, something I must tell you.”

“Your fa, father has always been proud of you!”

“What?” Zi Di was puzzled.

“When you were living alone, and would, wouldn’t use another’s money, your father was worry, worried from beginning to end.”

“On more than one occasion, he commended you in front of me, said that you real, really grew up, and that you were worthy of being hi, his daughter.”

When Zi Di heard this, her pupils momentarily dilated, and she blankly sat there.

Fei She added, “At the same time, he also felt ashamed, remorseful, and blamed himself.

“He said his tall, talent was too low, and that if he were a pow, powerful cultivator, he could pro, protect you, and wouldn’t let y, you receive such grievances.”

“In fa, fact, your father appreciated you President Zi Di.”

“It was because of your re, resistance that his courage was stimulated. Who would wan, want to see their hard earned we, wealth be partitioned up by others? We and your father were extreme, extremely unreconciled!”

“In fact, we were all prep, prepared for that d, day. But from beginning to end, we never regretted anything.”

Your father had the le, least regrets!”

“In short, I, I want to say this to you President Zi Di. Don’t look down on your fat, father.”

Zi Di was silent.

Her eyes gradually glistened with teardrops.

Endless memories engulfed her.

At that moment, she felt paternal love again.

Paternal love was deep and worthy of the greatest admiration.

The old Wisteria president wasn’t powerful, but he still used his own shoulders to tenaciously hold up the heavens.

During those difficult years, he shouldered the rising pressure alone, racking his brains and exhausting every method to content with those lofty jackals and wolves.

With regards to his daughter, he silenced his heart and deliberately maintained distance.

His love, his true feelings couldn’t be conveyed directly, because he didn’t want outsiders to know that his daughter was his weakness, he didn’t want to drag his daughter into a gigantic whirlpool.

Perhaps because of this reason, those nobles finally let Zi Di go.

After all, she was a small iron level cultivating at college, her potential and gifts were known to all.

“No, there is a bigger possibility. Those nobles purposely let me live, perhaps thinking they could find the Wisteria Cache through me?” Zi Di coldly flashed.

She suddenly comprehended that she was in a nigh identical situation to Qing Xin, she must have been monitored.

At this moment, she thought of her assassination attempt.

Not long after taking office, an assassin came for her.

Was someone really resentful that she’d become president?

Or was it really a spy?

“Perhaps they wanted to probe into what power I kept concealed, or force me to use the Wisteria Cache? If I had the cache at that time.”

However, fate was mysterious and unable to be predicted.

The assassin was defeated, Jian Zuan was drawn out, and War Merchant got involved.

The string of incidents that happened on Mystifying Monster Island really were hard to explain succinctly.

Finally, now, outside of Two Eyes Island’s waters, Zi Di had at last met with Qing Xin and understood what had happened behind the scenes.

A portion of the inside story.

Zi Di had a greater understanding of her father and his paternal love for her.

It was because of this love that another strong emotion flourished in her heart.

Hatred!

The more she understood her father and felt his paternal love, the more she hated!

“Who are they, those people who annexed the merchant alliance?” She asked both Fei She and Qing Xin.

Elder Fei She stated, “Too many to count.”

None of the six great merchant alliances were innocent, and the great nobles backing them had been the executioners.

Qing Xin replied, “There is a leader. It is said to be a member of the imperial family, but who they are has never been divulged.”

“What? A bloodline descendant of Heavenly Emperor Sheng Ming?” Zi Di’s heart shook.

Like a bucket of freezing water, her body and mind were soaked ice cold.

Not all of Heavenly Emperor Sheng Ming’s descendants were elite.

But since this bloodline descendant was capable of serving as leader and could coordinate each large noble well to successfully partition the Wisteria Merchant Alliance…that kind of strength and influence was sure to belong to an important member of the imperial household.

If Zi Di wanted vengeance, wouldn’t she need to set herself against the imperial household, against the entire Sheng Ming Empire?

The enemy was too powerful.

Vengeance…was hopeless.

However, as Zi Di’s heart gradually sank, the dragonman youngster extended his dragon claw and turned the communication device off.

He consoled her warmly, “Don’t worry, Zi Di, I am still here.”

Zi Di stared blankly, then raised her head slightly to look back at the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster had returned to the Deep Sea Monster Fish to guard against the agreement from taking hold once again.

While Zi Di, Qing Xin, and Fei She had been conversing, he had been listening.

The dragonman youngster hid the pity in his heart and smiled to Zi Di, “Zi Di, I will help you.”

He extended his dragon claw to Zi Di’s face.

Zi Di unconsciously stretched out her hand and put it into the dragon claw’s palm, feeling the dragonman youngster’s generosity, power, and stability.

The dragonman youngster’s tone was resolute, “No matter how lofty a noble is, when they rob another of their wealth to satisfy their own greed, they abandoned their honor and nobility, each and every one of them is a disciple of evil and sin, none of them are a righteous person!”

“They must receive a just punishment!”

“I firmly believe that taking revenge against them is the righteous path.”

The dragonman youngster leading the survivors in their redemption with the empire was not them bowing their heads to power, just a survival strategy.

As a believer in the Justice God, the dragonman youngster never feared power.

In this aspect, he and Chi Lai were alike. The latter had offended a bigwig to carry out justice and had been framed and reduced to slave for doing so.

“No, this is my personal grudge…” Zi Di shook her head.

The enemy was too powerful, she didn’t want to implicate her beloved.

But the young man’s answer had ruthlessly hit the softest depths of the young lady’s heart.

“Silly.”
