“Are you forgetting? I am you’re your knight.”

The dragonman youngster slowly leaned over and carefully embraced Zi Di into his chest.

Since Zi Di had been forced to become a ghost, the two touched each other carefully.

The Deceptive Disguise spell was just a disguise; it couldn’t alter one’s essence.

The ghost girl didn’t have substance, and those that weren’t familiar with her situation would be affected by the Deceptive Disguise spell, and even if they poked through the ghost girl, they would be deceived and be unable to sense it.

But the dragonman youngster knew the truth, and Deceptive Disguise affected him much less.

Therefore, when the two touched, they were very careful. If they moved too much, they would pass through each other’s body.

During this prudent embrace, Zi Di’s heart came under a wonderful serenity, as if she was immersed in an ocean of happiness at this moment.

She had a completely new answer to that wretched and tortuous question pertaining to the marriage connection.

No regrets!

If she agreed then, how could she have met the dragonman youngster now?

Even if fate gives me more torment and suffering, because of you, I have no regrets.

The dragonman youngster then said, “Don’t worry.”

“Even if it is a member of the imperial household, we aren’t hopeless.”

“Right?”

Zi Di’s heart shook, she instantly knew what the dragonman youngster was implying.

“Yes.”

“We have the Bloodcore!”

“We have infinite growth potential!”

“The enemy is indeed formidable, but only for now.”

“In the future…”

“So long as we are given enough time to grow, the hope for vengeance will grow too!”

Chapter 425: Hun Tong’s Recruitment

Night.

Sprinkling moonlight illuminated Two Eyes Island.

The tide lapped the shore.

Last night’s magnificent landscape had been reduced to ruins.

The weeping mourning of the deceased, desperate whimpers, and resentful curses rang out from time to time through the night, mixing with and shrouding the residents of Two Eyes Island.

Tonight, Two Eyes Island was full of gloom and sorrow.

The chaotic warfare had concluded, and in the stillness, bloody wounds seemed to ache deeper.

Not only did Hun Tong’s troops suffer disastrous losses, the poor people, freemen, and other citizens had everything stolen from them.

Don’t ever underestimate a pirate’s plundering, for they were professionals at it.

Many families had lost members.

In the fighting, not only were pirates swinging blades at you, but also the fishmen and even other humans.

To the inhabitants here, the loss of their wealth brought about pain, even more than the ones they had lost.

Because this was a Wealth Church parish.

People generally believed in the Wealth Goddess.

In the Wealth Church’s doctrine, wealth was above all. One could look at their burial customs to know this. Even if they died, as long as their wealth accompanied them, they would be happy.

Death wasn’t scary, being poor was.

Unfortunately, most of the people were still alive, but their money was gone!

Even though it was late into the night, the city government building’s gate was still congested with people.

At the front were representatives of the five great merchant alliances, behind them were the people of the smaller merchant alliances.

They were looking for City Lord Hun Tong.

But the gate remained locked.

“I have said many times already, Lord Hun Tong is wounded, he is in the middle of recuperating and won’t see any guests tonight.” One Eye guarded the city government building’s gate.

Hun Tong used to have four silver levels under his command, but two had died in the battle.

The pharmacist had lost his thigh and was sleeping inside Hun Tong’s city government building.

One Eye was the only one left that could keep up appearances.

The merchants were furious!

“What? No guests? Are we guests? We are victims!”

“My representative was shot by a light beam, Lord Hun Tong, will you not explain that?”

“Do you think you can avoid us by not meeting us?”

“Our stores were pillaged by pirates and fishmen, nothing of them remains. Hun Tong is the feudal lord, he is responsible for our protection, and so, we want compensation!”

Some merchants were questioning, some were cursing, and some were beating their chests and stamping their feet.

One Eye had a massive headache.

He wasn’t experienced in dealing with this kind of situation.

As far as the five great merchant alliances were concerned, he couldn’t dare to manhandle them, their background was too deep!

One Eye also didn’t want to offend the other smaller merchants either.

Two Eyes Island was a commerce island, and when the will of the people collapsed, most of Hun Tong’s foundation was done for.

Today’s battle had just concluded, and after the pirates had ransacked Two Eyes Island, its reputation had fallen greatly, its customers felt danger, and fewer of them would ever come back.

Pirates had come here to dispose of booty, producing much economic vigor, but following this, they would forever desert this place.

Under these circumstances, if merchants left in droves too, how would Two Eyes Island recover?

One Eye had no way out of the situation before him, the merchants were growing more agitated and would soon attack the city government building’s gate.

At that moment, the city government building’s gate suddenly opened.

From the gate walked out a silver level.

It was the pharmacist.

The pharmacist looked gloomy, and was leaning on a crutch, “Are you done making noise?”

Just as his words left him, he pulled a trigger.

An alchemy firearm.

Ding.

He shot a merchant at the edge of the crowd dead!

For a split second, everything was quiet as a cold cicada.

The merchants were stunned.

The pharmacist’s killing intent boiled over as his cold gleaming eyes swept across the crowd.

He was in a terrible mood.

He had been seriously wounded, and the light beam had severed his thigh, leaving his lower leg on the ground.

After the fighting finished, he was recuperating in the safest place, the city government building.

As a result, he had been woken up by the noisy merchants.

The pharmacist’s vented anger had intimidated the troublesome crowd.

In fact, even One Eye was subdued by him.

The latter used an incredulous look to take the measure of the pharmacist.

Sometimes, pain and tribulation really tempered a person.

Before the battle, the pharmacist had immersed himself in research, complied with Hun Tong’s orders, was a yes-man, and was a coward scared of dying.

After the battle, he had survived a serious wound, toughening his guts enough that he could kill, he was far more courageous than before.

The merchants were subdued, but none of them retreated.

The shock and fear subsided somewhat, and many of the merchants looked at the pharmacist coldly.

Some of the merchant alliance representatives had died in battle, but there were still three silver levels present here.

Sensing that the situation might get out of control any moment, One Eye’s heart was in his throat.

But at that moment, he heard the pharmacist say, “Do you see my injury? I am not in a good mood.”

“Because I was shot by a light beam!”

“Of course, I know how badly you feel.”

“Therefore, I’ll give you all a chance.”

“Come, rush in.”

The pharmacist actually stepped aside, opening the gates to the city government building wide open to the crowd.

“Don’t blame me for not warning you. Breaking into the city government building breaks the law! If you are killed by the garrison, it will be in vain.” The pharmacist sneered incessantly.

The merchants looked at each other.

All of them remained where they were, none of them went in.

The pharmacist sneered, not bothering to cover up his scornful look.

“Don’t say I didn’t give you a chance.”

“I will give you half an hour, you lot can discuss whether you should attack the city government building!”

“We’ll be going.”

The pharmacist indicated to One Eye.

The two walked into the city government building, leaving behind the wide open gate.

The merchants weren’t obstructed, but all of them were at a loss on what to do.

“What should be said?” One of the five great merchant alliance silver levels deeply frowned, they felt that the situation before them was impossible to stop halfway.

“We can’t break into the city government building, isn’t it handing someone the sword hilt?” Another silver level asked.

“Then should we stay here?”

“Alas, then let’s disperse, I still need to return to my shop over there to deal with the terrible mess. I believe Hun Tong will give us an explanation on the matter of compensation.”

“Then we’ll come tomorrow!”

If no one gave them a way out, the troublesome crowd could only give themselves a way out.

After everyone had a lively discussion, they sighed one by one and walked away with raised brows.

Not long after, the pharmacist and One Eye came out to the gate.

“You really dared to take a gamble?” One Eye asked.

“Merchants, humph, a group of cowardly profit seekers, how could they have the guts to attack the city government building?” The battle had given the pharmacist a thorough look into the natural disposition of merchants.

One Eye shook his head, still feeling it was an immense risk, “What if you lose your gamble?”

The pharmacist sneered, “They didn’t attack the city government building immediately, what makes you suppose that they will have the courage to do so later?”

“Besides, what is there to be afraid of?!” The city government building is tightly guarded and filled with magic and mechanical traps. If that group charged in, most of them would lose their lives, and the few silver levels would be a dirty mess!”

Upon mentioning that, the pharmacist regretfully exclaimed, “Those gangs of pirates were indeed crafty, they surprisingly never attacked the city government building!”

“By the way, where is Lord Hun Tong?”

One Eye whispered, “His Lordship has already left clandestinely for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ interim encampment.”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships were still berthed at the port.

The captain’s room at the aft house.

An open window let moonlight in.

The seawater’s moisture brought in the lingering scent of blood into the captain’s room.

Inside the captain’s room, there was just Zong Ge and Hun Tong.

Zong Ge led Hun Tong to his seat, then asked his purpose in coming, “Feudal Lord, paying a visit so late into the night, do you need us for an urgent mission?”

Hun Tong smiled and shook his head, “No I don’t. I have come to express my thanks to your corps for their outstanding contribution to the battle.”

“Commander Long Fu?” Hun Tong asked.

The dragonman youngster was still on the Deep Sea Monster Fish and wasn’t present.

Zong He was prepared for this, “So sorry, Feudal Lord. During the battle, the commander used his final form and is currently recovering.”

Hun Tong immediately understood, “Commander Long Fu was a flourishing dashing figure, and with his final form’s incredible power, he was capable of fighting Er Gua Zi head on and was only a bit weaker than him.”

“To tell the truth, I obtained information about your corps, learning of how exceptional you performed during the naval battle where Rou Zang was captured.”

“But during today’s battle, I finally realized that I greatly underestimated you all.”

“Commander Long Fu isn’t a peak silver level, yet he was able to accomplish such things. Adding on you, Shi Qi, a peak silver…the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is a quasi-gold level mercenary corps.”

Hun Tong was misled by Zong Ge into thinking that the dragonman youngster’s final form had a strong repercussion and was thinking that he was in the middle of urgent recuperation.

Zong Ge slightly frowned, he wasn’t good at dealing with this situation and didn’t like it either.

After thinking about it, he dryly replied, “Your Lordship is too kind.”

Hun Tong laughed, proceeding to sign praises of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

He recounted the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ feats. He exclaimed in admiration of how on the port battlefield, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was a tower of strength. Without the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ distinguished display, the fishmen would have taken the port.

He also spoke of how the dragonman youngster accepted his mission, and in spite of the huge risks, he entered the subterranean and disrupted the enemy’s spell casting. Regardless of how it ended, their choices and actions were in stark contrast to those of those merchants, and the Iron Club and Bandage Mercenary Corps. They were warriors of humanity!

After that, Hun Tong took out a large amount of gold coins and supplies from a storge device.

Zong Ge’s brows wrinkled deeply, “Feudal Lord, your reward is too much. We weren’t able to complete the mission of destroying the subterranean casting.”

“Even if we did, we shouldn’t be receiving this many rewards!”

“It isn’t much, I don’t think it’s enough.” Hun Tong then explained his true purpose in coming, “I want to use this remuneration to hire your corps, to officially join Two Eyes Island!”

Zong He couldn’t help but raise his eyebrows and head.

Hun Tong continued, “If you are not satisfied with this price, then we can continue discussing it.”

“I want to say that this time, I have come in immensely good faith!”

Zong Ge nodded and thought, “Looking at this sum of money, I can sense Hun Tong is recruiting in good faith. He really is rich and imposing! I didn’t think that even after this battle, he was still so wealthy!”

Zong Ge looked at Hun Tong.

As a matter of fact, this was Hun Tong’s savings.

“But it doesn’t matter. The seafloor has massive riches! The underground patrol puppets have already dredged them out of the sea. At dawn tomorrow, I will have all of the island’s riches! Compensating those merchants will be a trivial matter.” Hun Tong felt that everything was under control.

Chapter 426: Hun Tong Doesn’t Panic

During the battle for Two Eyes Island, Hun Tong felt he had no subordinates!

He had four silver level subordinates, but on the battlefield, they out debased each other.

Under the silvers, there were no battle squads.

This led to all of the war pressure being resisted by Hun Tong alone.

His subordinates were more used as bait to lure out the enemy’s military and objectives.

After the melee finished, Hun Tong felt this deeply.

Because he really didn’t have anyone.

Of the four silver level subordinates, two were dead, one was seriously injured, and one was whole.

Most of the city guards were lost and the barracks was in ruins.

The patrol fleet was also decimated.

All of the light cannon turrets Hun Tong had secretly built all over the new island were destroyed.

Not only were the alchemy cannon turrets gone, so too were the gunners inside them.

Hun Tong had defended his territory, but too much of the island’s strength had been depleted, and it urgently needed replenishment.

Since distant water couldn’t quench thirst now, Hun Tong planned on taking the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Truth be told, during the battle, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ performance left a profound impression on Hun Tong!

Therefore, the night the battle finished; he was unable to keep himself from sneaking here to extend an olive branch to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Zong Ge shook his head, “Lord Hun Tong, you seem to forget that we aren’t just a mercenary corp.”

The implication pointed to their underlying identity – arms smugglers.

Hun Tong nodded, “I understand what you mean, Shi Qi.”

To be able to smuggle weapons, one needed to have a deep background.

The survivors upheld this sham identity to bluff, imperceptibly resolve many troubles, and make things more convenient.

Hun Tong coughed, “To tell the truth, it is that layer of identity that I consider you even more suitable to join my Two Eyes Island.”

“If you want to sell something, is this not the best place to do so?”

“Of course, I know that you have your own channels and buyers that you need to transport goods for.”

“Two Eyes Island can still help you. In the future, I will turn it into a regional transportation hub.”

“Furthermore, I believe that Commander Long Fu is strong enough to guarantee your actions.”

“Shi Qi, the person I really want to recruit is you!”

Hun Tong was a clever person.

If he wasn’t, he wouldn’t have been able to build Two Eyes Island up from nothing into a great commerce island.

Like the pharmacist, this battle had given him intangible wealth.

For example, he recognized where the weakest parts of his territory was.

He didn’t have a subordinate capable of taking charge!

He couldn’t do everything by himself, he needed more people to defend this foundation.

This subordinate needed to be at least silver level.

But just having a life level was not enough.

They also needed to be very elite!

Hun Tong thought previously that his four silver levels were a well-trained and powerful force.

Now, Hun Tong has a completely new realization, having a high quality silver level subordinate was far more important than having a lot of them!

When a crisis approached, many silver levels were useless. Sometimes they would ignorantly cause negative effects.

Such as his former city guard commander.

That guy took advantage of Hun Tong standing guard at the auction hall to leave the barracks and run around outside to have fun.

This resulted in the silver level powerhouse Dao Hen cutting him down by himself.

With him dead, the city guard didn’t have a commander. Their military power declined severely, and during the entire battle, the city guards got thrashed far more than it went on the offensive.

Apart from this, the alchemist and the pharmacist were beaten by the fishman caster in a two versus one. This really lost too much face for humanity.

Hearing Hun Tong’s main purpose in coming, Zong Ge merely raised his eyebrows and waited silently.

Hun Tong commended, “Shi Qi, you have a peak silver life level, your battle qi already has a golden luster, and you’ll become a gold level sooner or later.”

“You are a human, your bloodline is exceptional, at least capable of bringing you to the gold level, are you really willing to serve a silver level dragonman?”

“I have seen your battle talent with my own eyes both against Rou Zang and this last battle.”

“You can also drill troops.”

When he mentioned this, Hun Tong paused, then laughed, “That Quick Knife battle squad you established, you were also the one training it too.”

“It really was capable!”

“At the port, they were able to fight a silver level fishman for a time.”

“When I heard how long that battle squad had been established for, I didn’t dare believe it.”

“In so short a time, you were able to train them to this degree, your talent really has impressed me.”

“Shi Qi, ah, Shi Qi.”

“You have such skill. But you’ve become an arms smuggler, don’t you think that’s too regretful?”

“Even an outsider like me feels a deep pity for you.”

Hun Tong’s expression and voice were very sincere.

But Zong Ge remained just as calm as he had been.

Hun Tong smiled and tossed out his true bargaining chip, “Are you really happy in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps? You had been persistently looking for a healing potion and purchased a gold level one at the auction.”

“You see, that is all the support the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps can give to you.”

“But if you join my territory, and need a gold level healing potion again, I will give it to you immediately!”

“Your will be paid 50,000 gold coins in your first year! It will increase every year, and when you become a gold level, it will be at least 200,000 gold.”

Whether it was 50,000 or 200,000 gold, both exceeded common standards.

“As long as you hold the position of Two Eyes Island’s city guard commander for three years, I will work with you to have the empire award you the title of baron.” Hun Tong continued to say.

Zong Ge couldn’t help but be moved.

Nobility!

Hun Tong had unexpectedly tossed out a major bargaining chip; his sincerity really was great.

Nobility in the Sheng Ming Empire was not easily conferred, many knights that strived for their entire lives would never be made nobles.

The Sheng Ming Empire’s nobility was incredibly precious.

But Zong Ge shook his head, his tone steady as he replied, “Frankly, I don’t think Two Eyes Island has a future.”

“First, the fishmen haven’t been thoroughly purged. Given enough time, this fishman clan will surely multiply and strengthen. I saw their determination; they won’t abandon their former homeland. In other words today’s conflict will repeat in the future.”

“Second, Lord Hun Tong, in this battle, you suddenly betrayed us and had the magic ship open fire on friendly forces. That caused immense casualties and damage, and although the other powers won’t be too bad to deal with, what will you do about the five great merchant alliances?”

“Third, another necromancer appeared during the battle. That combined with the prior necromancer incident might trigger an imperial follow-up investigation. You better kill that necromancer before then.”

If it were Cang Xu or Zi Di, perhaps these things would have been said more tactfully.

But Zong Ge was straightforward and if he ran into a feudal lord of bad character, he might get angry on the spot.

But Hun Tong burst into laughter, “None of your concerns are enough to trouble me.”

“First point, the fishmen are nothing to worry about. Because Two Eyes Island will be appropriated by the empire as a future transportation hub. It will be an important part of the maritime transportation lines. At that time, the imperial navy will be garrisoned here, and even if the fishman clan grows in strength, the war they raise won’t endanger my territory.”

“Second point, my performance on the battlefield…cough, I can’t help but say that the fishmen are really crafty. They actually used Devil Gold Coins. I had the misfortune of falling to them.”

Hun Tong had learned about the Devil Gold Coins from the old fishman elder.

At the time, he had expressed his approval and offered genuine praise.

Upon mentioning it, Hun Tong’s teeth hatefully itched.

Zong Ge’s eyes flashed, “What gold coins?”

Hun Tong gave a detailed explanation on everything knew about the gold coins.

Zong Ge had a realization.

At that moment, he finally understood why Hun Tong, Zi Di, and Cang Xu changed sides.

“So the problem was those 50,000 gold coins.”

“Through us, most of those gold coins ended up in Hun Tong’s hands, causing him to be entrapped by them.”

“If he knew the truth, who knows what he would feel about it?”

Hun Tong continued, “Although it was indeed me who caused casualties to friendly forces, it was not my real intention, I was influenced by an external power. Everyone knows this.”

“Of course, the five great merchant alliances won’t be easy to deal with.”

“They are troublesome.”

“Therefore, I have made sufficient preparations. Regarding these merchants, I will give them satisfactory compensation.”

“Don’t worry about money.” Hun Tong smiled, looking calm and complacent, “On the seafloor around the new island are the treasures the pirates left behind.”

“My subterranean patrol puppets have already submerged to the seafloor and are searching all over for them.”

“Of course, others are thinking of fishing up this money, but my Golden Flash has been monitoring all of the coastlines.”

“This money is all spoils of war, and according to imperial law, is mine!”

“A small part of this sum is enough to satisfy the five great merchant alliances.”

Zong Ge: …..

He knew the treasures on the seafloor had already been absconded by Zi Di.

In terms of plundering seafloor resources, the deep sea salvagers were far better than the subterranean patrollers!

Hun Tong’s beautiful dream was destined to be shattered.

As Zong Ge watched the feudal lord of Two Eyes Island frankly giving assurances, he couldn’t help but feel a smidge of pity for him.

Hung Ton wasn’t aware of this as he kept going, “As for the third point, that necromancer…it is very clear that he was one of the fishmen’s hidden forces. That group of wretched dregs actually ganged up with a necromancer. Therefore, the necromancer that appeared on the island must surely be one of theirs too, wanting to destroy Two Eyes Island ahead of time for them.”

“I am completely innocent; I am not worried about any investigation.”

“It won’t be anything major.”

Hun Tong had an important reason for this, one he didn’t tell Zong Ge.

Cang Xu, that fish, was the imperial secret spy’s target, they wanted to put him on a long line to hook a bigger fish.

Therefore, during the battle, Hun Tong didn’t clamp down on Cang Xu ruthlessly.

After all, he and the imperial secret spy worked a lot together.

In fact, when confronted with a possible imperial investigation, the imperial secret spy could help him explain things.

Therefore, he didn’t panic.

Chapter 427: Destination – Ice Sculpture Island

With profound regret, Hun Tong clandestinely left the port.

He wanted to convince Zong Ge but wasn’t able to.

Zong Ge didn’t think Two Eyes Island had a good future and listed three problems.

Hun Tong felt that his answers were watertight and could be rated as perfect.

But Zong Ge still turned him down.

“A pity, truly a pity!” Hun Tong sighed incessantly.

He also knew that poaching was risky, and would likely offend the arms merchant behind the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

But Hun Tong really did lack capable people.

Especially after the battle concluded, the feudal lord suffered disastrous losses to his power.

Crucially, Zong Ge’s display of talent and abilities really made people drool.

Hun Tong’s main recruitment target tonight was always Zong Ge, the dragonman youngster was secondary.

To Hun Tong, it seemed that Shi Qi was more outstanding than Long Fu.

First, Shi Qi was a human, Long Fu was a dragonman. Different races had natural barriers, and recruiting a human elite would further consolidate the stability of Two Eyes Island.

Second, Shi Qi had a more guaranteed future than Long Fu. Shi Qi’s battle qi already had a gold luster, and given enough time, he was sure to become a gold level.

Third, the risks of offending an arms merchant could be mitigated. Two Eyes Island could become an arms provider location, and with a beneficial alliance, Shi Qi could be recruited and perhaps could be the connecting bridge between both sides.

Fourth, Long Fu was already the commander of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, from this position, one knew that he had a higher status in the arms merchant’s heart. Of course, it was far harder for Hun Tong to poach the big boss than the second in command. The price would also be greater.

“A pity he didn’t accept.”

“I shouldn’t give up on this.”

“Like Long Fu, Shi Qi is a silver level rarely seen.”

“I have to win him over with all I got.”

“As long as they haven’t left Two Eyes Island, I still have a chance.”

“Wait until tomorrow, when I receive the plundered seafloor treasure. I will come and negotiate again, even if I have to double the price, recruiting Shi Qi will be to my advantage!”

Hun Tong secretly resolved himself.

This battle had made him fed up with his incompetent subordinates.

Zong Ge returned to the captain’s room, and after inspecting the area around him, activated a communication device.

“Hun Tong just came by. He wants to recruit our corps and really wants to hire me at a high price. From him, I learned about the Devil Gold Coins. It was those that caused your temporary betrayal!”

Hun Tong deciding to drop by had given them important information.

Zi Di and Cang Xu learned of this crucial factor.

Zi Di and the dragonman youngster were in the Deep Sea Monster Fish, while Cang Xu was alone in the captain’s room of the Iron Lump.

Through three alchemy communication devices, and through their discussion, learned of the causes and effects of the Devil Gold Coins.

“It’s no wonder the fishmen were so magnanimous and took out 50,000 gold coins.”

“The Sincerity God…their sacred tools are indeed impossible to block.”

“No.” The dragonman youngster shook his head, his heart deeply worried and sick, “We must meet our failings head on. Against these types of skills, we have no defensive measures.”

“Cang Xu immediately approved, “This is indeed a large weakness of ours. In fact, the top brass of powers large and small have such preventative measures.”

“Hun Tong had a massive amount of Devil Gold Coins, but he was able to swiftly recover in that crucial moment, that was the efficacy of his defensive measure.”

Zi Di spoke, “We indeed need to cover for this weakness; however, it is very difficult.”

“Generally speaking, preventative measures are all constant divine spells on the body.”

“In our current situation, going to any temple, church, or other religious institution is fairly risky.”

The gods were aloof and remote.

As long as one built a temple or a church, they would have a likeness of them, have abundant divine power, and have the god’s eyes and ears there.

The survivors entering these places was them placing themselves rich in front of the gods’ eyes.

Despite having the Deceptive Disguise and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spells, it was still rather risky.

Basically, before they redeemed themselves and washed themselves clean, these churches and temples were dangerous places for the survivors. Avoid it if possible.

Cang Xu voiced, “It is not just divine spells that can protect us, there are other ways.”

“In fact, I don’t believe that is the most concerning problem.”

“Don’t forget that our primary goal was to pass on a message to leverage the Bai Zhen Clan’s connections with imperial higher-ups.”

“Therefore, we came to Two Eyes Island.”

“But now, we haven’t received a response yet.”

“After so many days, this is not normal. What actually happened? Did the Bai Zhen Clan receive the message, did they reply back? Did anyone discover and obstruct the letter?”

Cang Xu’s words immediately got the others’ approval.

Zong Ge said, “Currently, the Wisteria Store on the new island is closed.”

“Cang Xu has been exposed as a necromancer, and this entails that the Wisteria Merchant Alliance channel has also suffered a calamity.”

Zi Di added, “To be honest, losing one store isn’t that big of a deal. What’s key is that the Sheng Ming Empire will likely carry out a follow up investigation. If this trail is investigated deeply enough, will it implicate us?”

“Will the blackmail letter be investigated?”

“Although the letter’s content is blackmail in name, it doesn’t indicate who we are, but it is still an important clue.”

“If nothing else, using these clues to divine us will deplete much of our power. After all, every time the Anti-investigation and Prophecy formation is activated, it comes at a steep price.”

In the spell of silence, everyone’s thoughts were springing with urgency.

Their main purpose in coming to Two Eyes Island was to send that message.

But it failed.

Not only did they not receive a reply, but it also involved more problems.

The dragonman youngster then said, “We have to hurry up and send a second message, we will find the issue.”

Cang Xu replied, “It won’t be easy to find. In fact, the message might have been misplaced. These alchemy devices are not 100% reliable. Therefore, important information is sent in at least three different ways and transmitted repeatedly.”

“We might have made a mistake with the encryption, and the receiving alchemy device got nothing but gibberish.”

“We are very far from the Sheng Ming Continent and need many communication devices to relay the transmission. There could be a problem at any point, we would be hard pressed to find where the issue is.”

Zi Di added on, “Regardless of what it is, I believe that we can still try the Wisteria Merchant Alliance channels again.”

“Of course, it will be impossible to use the Two Eyes Island’s communication device again.”

“The next closest is located at Ice Sculpture Island.”

“We can go there.”

“Ice Sculpture Island? I know of it.” Even Zong Ge, one unfamiliar with the seas, knew of Ice Sculpture Island’s reputation.

Ice Sculpture Island was an ultra-large island; its development far surpassed Two Eyes Island!

Even before the battle, Two Eyes Island couldn’t compare to Ice Sculpture Island.

After all, Two Eyes Island was a coral ball cage tree, it was constantly moving, which hindered much of its development.

Because the coral ball cage tree itself was growing, and no matter how good the infrastructure, it would collapse after a time.

Ice Sculpture Island was a normal island and had a vast population, given enough reproduction and business, it had surpassed Two Eyes Island by many times.

Two Eyes Island had only been under Hun Tong’s control for ten years.

Ice Sculpture Island had been settled for over a thousand years.

In the beginning, the settling races were divided by categories and races mixed together. Ice Sculpture Island had abundant resources and was treasured greatly in the boundless ocean.

In the following decades, snow elf communities continued to migrate there.

At the time, the barbarian clan started a war on the Ice Continent. The snow elf clan was no match for this army, and one after the other, they were routed and dispersed, those that could flee chose to settle on Ice Sculpture Island.

After a few centuries, a snow elf nation had been established on Ice Sculpture Island

In the beginning, the snow elf nation only held a small part of the island.

In current times, the entirety of Ice Sculpture Island belonged to the snow elf nation, they had become quite the powerful maritime kingdom!

Zi Di knew the dragonman youngster had lost his memory and decided to give him an introduction, “Snow elves are a type of elf, they are most suited to an environment of ice and snow.”

“Elves are different from us humans, they have a long life span, and are experts at magic, music, and more.”

“Ice Sculpture Island has snow elves that are good at magic, and even better at alchemy.”

“The alchemy puppets they make employ ice sculpture technology, cheap and satisfactory, a famous specialty.”

“Not just alchemy puppets, they also make ice breakers, cold ice forts, and more, they are especially suitable for fighting in frigid environments.”

The dragonman youngster’s eyes shone, “Perhaps we can purchase some magic ships there.”

“We lack magic ships we can overtly use.”

“If we have a silver level magic ship, we can safeguard the gold cannon and supply it sufficiently for it to display its true firepower.”

“Unlike the last two times, the enemy destroyed it after its first shot.”

Zi Di nodded, “We are very wealthy now!”

“Buying some magic ships is completely feasible.”

“In fact, we really need a true alchemist!”

When Zi Di said this, the dragonman youngster blinked.

Zong Ge didn’t know about the Bloodcore and their plans; he was completely in the dark and felt somewhat baffled.

Cang Xu said, “That’s right.”

“Although we have basic maintenance puppets, they can only maintain ordinary ships.”

“It is difficult to maintain alchemy formations with just alchemy puppets.”

“The Deep Sea Monster Fish was hit by the main light cannon, and needs to be repaired, and after going through the battle, the Iron Lump needs to be repaired too.”

“We have lost many alchemy puppets already.”

“The scouting puppets are not a problem and aren’t worth maintaining. But the deep sea salvagers, the swordsmen guard puppets, and the broken yellow crab alchemy puppet all need to be repaired by an alchemy master.”

“Some important alchemy formations need to be constructed by an alchemy master.”

This sentence contained the main point.

Cang Xu and Zi Di would struggle to construct the Bloodline Purification formation.

Zi Di and Cang Xu weren’t gifted in this aspect.

Zi Di was good at medicine and Cang Xu was an experienced administrator from his many years as a noble steward.

If one wanted to cultivate an alchemy master, it would be very costly.

In Crown City’s alchemy college, one needed to read at least a hundred introductory alchemy books to have an inkling of an alchemy foundation.

Chapter 428: Willpower Protection Spell

When the survivors spoke about needing an alchemy master, Zong Ge felt curious, “Is Magician Qing Xin not good at alchemy?”

Zi Di shook her head, “She is best at the wind path magic called Wind Booster, it can allow a caravan to travel quicker.”

Zong Ge immediately understood.

Zi Di sighed, “Even though she is a talented magician our merchant alliance cultivated, her talent isn’t that high. How many at the lowest rungs of society have powerful bloodlines?”

Cang Xu consoled, “If Magician Qing Xin was really gifted, she might not have remained alive till today.”

Then the necromancer changed the topic, “Speaking of Qing Xin, I learned something important from her.”

“There is a secret gathering of necromancers being held on Weeping Pine Island.”

“It is a very rare gathering. They not only exchange information, but also necromancy spells and necromancy cultivation resources.”

“I strongly propose we participate in it.”

“We need many necromancy path cultivation resources; it will be difficult to replenish in other places. Especially soul crystals, they play an irreplaceable role for my and Zi Di’s cultivation, as well as healing our ghosts.”

The dragonman youngster was silent for a bit, then asked, “Where is Weeping Pine Island?”

Zi Di opened an ocean chart and cast it onto the wall, “Weeping Pine Island is located…in that direction, in the complete opposite direction from Ice Sculpture Island.”

Cang Xu nodded, “But it isn’t far, right?”

He had readied his wording for a long time.

The necromancer continued, “My proposal is this, let me leave for Weeping Pine Island alone, so I can join the necromancer meeting and exchange some resources.”

“When the gathering finishes and the necromancers dispel, we can still stage a battle there.”

“After the fight finishes, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will have successfully beheaded necromancers and seized the Iron Lump, rescuing the many human and dwarf hostages.”

“Won’t this be to everyone’s delight and satisfaction?”

The dragonman youngster’s heart was immediately moved, “If that happens, we can actually use the Iron Lump, and perhaps on the basis of this, we can recruit Men Shi?”

Zong Ge’s voice transmitted, “I approve of leaving for Weeping Pine Island.”

“It’s because its location is completely opposite to Ice Sculpture Island.”

“We can fool people with this and make a feint.”

“Additionally, being able to use the Iron Lump normally will also be helpful. We really lack magic ships.”

The Deep Sea Monster Fish was a trump card and couldn’t be easily exposed.

“But if Qing Xin is on our ship, then the implication would expose our identities!”

“She knows about Zi Di and the necromancers. Even if she doesn’t know about Zi Di and Zhen Jin being shipwrecked, it will be easy for her to make discreet inquiries into it in the future.”

“If she joins the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and learns of the truth of it, then she won’t be far from accessing our core secrets.”

Zong Ge continued to speak bluntly, “Therefore, I oppose doing this!”

“Regardless of how much you trust this woman, mine doesn’t come so easily.”

“This is too risky for us!”

Zi Di’s brows wrinkled, and she retorted, “I can vouch for her. Older sister Qing Xin has been trained by my father, and when the Wisteria Merchant Alliance was divided up, she was living incognito and used all of her strength to resist. This proves her loyalty.”

“To take care of the blond mother and child, and the others, she would rather fight and do her best to save these weak people, this sufficiently illustrates her kindheartedness.”

Zong Ge coldly snorted, “Zi Di, your trust cannot supersede my opinion.”

“How can you know her innermost and truest sentiments?”

“Even if she is loyal, can she be controlled?”

“Think about your betrayal during the battle. You cannot even guarantee yourself; how can you guarantee others?”

“You!” Zi Di angrily glared.

She unconsciously looked at the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster smiled back and softly patted her shoulder, silently comforting her.

But the dragonman youngster didn’t instantly declare his position, rather he asked Cang Xu, “What is your opinion?”

The old necromancer had prudently kept silent, but now that his commander called him out, he was forced to say, “I agree with Zong Ge.”

This answer made Zi Di’s brows crease deeper.

However, the dragonman youngster was actually serene from beginning to end, as if he had anticipated it.

Although he had a closer relationship with Cang Xu and Zi Di compared to Zong Ge, at every strategic decision, Cang Xu Maintained his reason and consideration, he would independently reflect on it, unlikely to be hindered by external factors like emotions and status.

Cang Xun continued, “Qing Xin is indeed someone we cannot entrust ourselves to.”

“As the situation stands now, that’s how it is.”

“We trust each other, because Mystifying Monster Island is our solid foundation. With regards to Qing Xin, only Miss Zi Di stands by her.”

“This matter cannot be rushed.”

“We can freely probe and confirm her true intentions and detect if she has suffered any control methods.”

“We can also have her sign a contract.”

“There are many magics and divine spells that can help us, right?”

“In fact, I really hope that we can sign a contract and utilize the contract’s power to protect ourselves from something like the Devil Gold Coins from happening again!”

Cang Xu had been a noble steward for many years and his words were very fluent.

First, he maintained his opinion.

Second, he endorsed Zong Ge, but didn’t completely endorse him. He opposed Zi Di, but didn’t fully do so. He didn’t offend either person.

Once again, his words were very sensible and gave a resolution.

Finally, he also had a very reasonable proposal, he proposed that everyone sign a contract to guard against things like the Devil Gold Coins. This proposal really went deep into the dragonman youngster’s heart!

The dragonman youngster couldn’t help but lightly nod.

He looked at Zi Di, silently asking his beloved’s opinion.

Zi Di’s brows relaxed somewhat, and she muttered to herself, “We really need to guard against things like the Devil Gold Coins, we need to protect our will!”

“In this aspect, divine spells are the popular first choice.”

“In practicality, defensive divine spells are divided into two groups. The first is temporary protection, it only protects for a time and needs to be replenished afterwards. The second is constant divine spells, similar to constant magic spells, a defensive divine spell will be added perpetually to one’s body.”

“The first type needs us to frequently be in contact with a priest or higher level clergy, increasing our exposure risk.”

“The second is less suitable, constant divine spells need high level clergymen and casting facilities, they are often done inside temples. Because the bestowed divine spell’s power is everlasting, the church will strictly audit the receivers to prevent aiding the enemy. During the casting of the divine spell, it is extremely likely that the gods will be looking down at it.”

“In fact, when any high level or large scale divine spell is used, the higher the chance it will provoke a god’s attention.”

A fighter’s power comes from themselves, a magician draws from external elements, and the source of a clergy’s power comes from the god they believe in.

Therefore, if a powerful divine spell is used, the gods will naturally sense it somewhat.

The dragonman youngster nodded, “If divine spells aren’t suitable and too risky, is there another way?”

Zi Di nodded, “There is. Besides divine spells, there are spells, magic formations, medicine, alchemy formations, alchemy items, and so on that can be used.”

“For example, there is the Anti-magic Barrier can increase magic resistance. When willpower is distorted, it will reduce the control time.”

“There is also the classic arcane magic, Counterspell. It can counter the vast majority of spells.”

“However, it isn’t good as a preventative measure.”

“There are both divine spells and magic spells that can influence willpower, but there are many more of the former than the latter. Counterspell and the Anti-magic Barrier are magic focused.”

“Don’t tell me there isn’t one that can respond to divine spells, like a Counter Divine Spell?” The dragonman youngster curiously asked.

Zi Di forced a smile, “The gods are special, each god’s power belongs to them alone. In other words, the essence of each god’s divine spells are different, even if the results are the same.”

“Therefore, if we wanted to develop a Counter Divine Spell, it would be both extremely hard and costly. Even if developed, it would only be useful against a god and won’t be broadly applicable. Thus, the earnings from it are too low.”

“Even worse, modifying a divine spell is far cheaper than developing a Counter Divine Spell. If it is discovered that a divine spell has a counter, as long as the gods modify it a bit, the corresponding Counter Divine Spell will lose its effectiveness. The main god doesn’t even need to act themselves; holy spirits, angels, red clothed archbishops, and others can also modify them too.”

“I see.” Zong Ge sighed.

The dragonman youngster had lost his memory and Zong Ge had been living in the army and cultivating his battle qi for years on end, thus neither had a deep knowledge of these things.

The two had their horizons broadened greatly by Zi Di’s words.

As Zi Di spoke, Cang Xu remained quiet.

He understood propriety and didn’t pointlessly steal the show.

Zi Di continued, “Magic is in this situation, and there are also some magic formations that can protect the user’s original will.”

“In essence, it is actually an enchantment technique.”

“Through a formation, a body can be enchanted, and the magic runes attached will protect them.

“To go a step further, it is human alchemy.”

“Everyone knows about the beastman boxing champion Yi Chao. He decided to use alchemy on his right arm, making it into the Black Gold Rocket Fist, thereby augmenting his strength.”

“When the right arm was being created, five exceedingly precious gems were used, one of which could protect willpower greatly.”

“Therefore, the Black Gold Rocket Fist could safeguard his independent will.”

“To us, the most suitable method is medicine.”

“We have great medicinal manufacturing, thanks to the Magic Medicine Bathtub.”

“Of course, we aren’t able to make any high level medicine, but we don’t lack in production capacity for low level medicine, we just lack some of the corresponding materials, that’s all.”

“In future critical moments, we can drink this medicine to temporarily protect our willpower.”

Cang Xu followed with, “I approve of this plan.”

Zong Ge questioned, “This plan cannot guard against sneak attacks, magic, formations, potions…what about an alchemy item?”

Zi Di replied, “The Black Gold Rocket Fist can actually be regarded as an alchemy item.”

“Items that can protect will are rare and the vast majority of them are high level artifacts.”

“The reason is similar to magic.”

“It is difficult to guard against divine spells that can tamper with and distort will.”

“One can say there is a price but no market, one can encounter it, but can’t look for it.”

“Anyways…” Zi Di hesitated for a bit, “I heard that one of Ice Sculpture’s specialties, the Ice Heart, can be used.”

“Ice Hearts have many uses.”

“It can be ingested by someone. After taking it, a person will become extremely rational, and all of their emotions will be frozen, and their spirit power and willpower will be greatly improved.”

“Of course it can be made into medicine. Ice Sculpture City has at least nine types of medicine that uses it as material, the most well known of them is the Ice Head Flower medicine.”

“It is a medicine for snow elves. After a snow elf takes it, it will increase their ice magic’s effects by at most 30%!”

“The side effect of taking this medicine is that the snow elf will have a lot of dandruff, like a lot of it.”

“Ice Hearts can also be used to make alchemy items.”

“The most successful product is the Ice Heart Headband. When worn, it can keep a magician calm, amplify their spirit power, and increase their casting speed. Of course, it also protects willpower.”

The dragonman youngster laughed, “It seems that we have another objective on Ice Sculpture Island.”

But Zi Di shook her head, “It will be hard to acquire an Ice Heart, its production quantity is very small, don’t expect to get any.”

The four continued to discuss other matters.

Such as providing compensation payments, rewarding and promoting those that performed well, and so on, along with how to negotiate with Hun Tong.

Finally, the four decided to first proceed towards Weeping Pine Island, then travel to Ice Sculpture Island.

As for what to do with Qing Xin, that hinged on whether they could confirm her true condition and whether the survivors could trust her.

To ensure operational security, Zi Di suggested that they equip Cang Xu as much as they could with the things they got from the auction.

This suggestion was approved by the other three.

As dawn approached, the dragonman youngster snuck back onto the port.

When the sun ascended from the flat ocean horizon, Hun Tong opened his eyes.

Much of his pain had been alleviated, thanks to the healing potions he took.

The Wealth Church priest had died in battle, and the clergy on the new island weren’t capable of healing Hun Tong.

Hun Tong felt very cozy.

Although it had only been a few hours of sleep, his energy and stamina had been greatly restored.

He slowly sat up then shook the bell at his bedside.

Soon after, a servant knocked on the door, and after being permitted in, a few of them entered to wait upon Hun Tong, dressing him and washing him up.

The lead servant was Hun Tong’s personal manservant.

The manservant was somewhat stiff, his voice hoarse, “Master, last night you reminded me that I would immediately report everything concerning the salvaged treasures. Is now a good time?”

But after a few hours of sleep, Hun Tong was no longer as fretful.

He regained his noble bearing.

Right now, he was looking at a mirror and adjusting his collar, “It isn’t urgent, those spoils of war won’t be running away.”

“Unfortunately…” His personal servant was just about to speak when Hun Tong interrupted.

Hun Tong pointed at the window and ordered, “Open it.”

The personal servant promptly obeyed and opened the window.

The scenery outside the window was very beautiful.

The morning sun sprinkled across the ocean surface, glittering as resplendently as fish scales.

The sea breeze slowly blew in, causing Hun Tong’s mood to become splendid.

The only ugliness was the buildings ruined by war.

It was somewhat dazzling to Hun Tong’s eyes.

He encouraged himself, “It doesn’t matter, I have money!”

“I have a lot of money.”

“Give me time, and I will make this island better and more beautiful.”

Chapter 429: My Money?!

Hun Tong came to the dining table.

The dining table had gold plates laid out on them, containing sliced bread, milk, salmon, eggs, bacon, fusilli, and more.

To Hun Tong’s left, there was also a small cup that contained gold level sea salt. Each grain of sea salt was worth twice its weight in gold.

Hun Tong sighed, “The war just finished, I can be simple.”

He sat down and began to enjoy breakfast.

Not long after, noise could be heard from the city government building’s gate.

It was those merchants.

They were led by the five great merchant alliances, once again assembling in front of the city government building’s gate, demanding an explanation from Hun Tong.

Hun Tong turned a deaf ear and calmly ate.

His personal manservant was restless and impatient, “My Lord, the seafloor treasure salvaging matter, can I report it to you?”

Hun Tong shook his head, drank some milk, and smiled, “No need, my loyal servant. Forget that order, don’t be worried, you need to learn patience right now. Whether it is bitterness or happiness, all of life is beautiful.”

“Therefore, don’t tell me how much we got out, why don’t you give me something to look forward to?” When he said that, Hun Tong couldn’t help but laugh.

As a matter of fact, he had made a prediction.

It would absolutely be a tremendous sum that would far exceed everything he had earned in his life!

The main contributor of them was the Wealth Church.

During daily operations of Two Eyes Island, the church was also a gigantic gold eating beast. When Hun Tong made money, he often donated a portion of the profits to the church.

Because the Wealth Church monopolized Two Eyes Island.

Asking a clergyman to use a divine spell required payment.

Every time the Wealth Church presided over a large prayer, a priest did a burial, or other such things, the church would charge a big expense.

The masses always gave aid and support to the church.

The divine spells the Wealth Church were best at were contract spells. These divine spells can restrict cooperating parties and establish a base of trust.

Therefore, every merchant alliance big and small were also a source of Wealth Church funds.

“The Lightning Pirates stole almost everything from the Wealth Church.”

“A good robbery.”

“Without them, how could this sum of riches fall into my hands?”

“Apart from this, every store large and small has been pillaged, the two mercenary corps’ encampments also have many ill-gotten gains.”

“Many of the island’s inhabitants were rich, and although not worth attention individually, the pirates robbed the entire island. After adding up these little things, the total must be considerable.”

Hun Tong couldn’t help but sink into a wonderful daydream, overlooking his manservant’s increasingly difficult to mask fretfulness.

After finishing breakfast, Hun Tong spryly walked to the storehouse.

When he pushed the door open, he came to an empty room.

“Eh?” Hun Tong stared blankly at it.

In his imagination, this storehouse should have piles of gold, silver, and jewels. But the sight before him was completely different from his expectations.

Hun Tong couldn’t help but blink.

“Did I go the wrong way?”

He took a few steps back out of the room and looked at the room sign.

“Room 3, that’s right.”

Hun Tong blinked again, then glanced at his manservant behind him, “I should have given the order.”

“Yes, master. Last night, you ordered that all of the treasures fished out of the sea were to be delivered to storehouse 3.”

“Furthermore, you also specially reminded your subordinates that if room 3 was not enough, we could use storehouse 5 next door.”

Hun Tong frowned as he questioned, “Then, where’s the money?”

“Where are the seafloor treasures?”

“Where are my valuables?!”

The manservant turned pale, and overflowing with cold sweat, he swallowed his saliva and croaked out, “Feudal Lord, my master, this is precisely what I wanted to report to you! I don’t know why, but after the alchemy puppets searched everywhere several times, uh, they only found a few copper coins on the seafloor.”

“Those few copper coins were put in that corner of the storehouse.”

Hun Tong: …..

The news startled him.

His eyes went wide.

He felt incredulous!

“How is this possible? He turned around, looking at a corner in the room.

Sure enough, there were three copper coins there.

Copper coins were circulated in ordinary marketplaces.

The deep sea salvagers weren’t perfect and had left behind three coins.

Hun Tong rigidly gazed at these three copper coins; he stared at them!

Storeroom 3 was deathly still.

His manservant didn’t dare breathe, for fear a violent rage would descend upon him.

Hun Tong’s eyes coldly flashed.

These three copper coins were so unsightly.

They seemed to be ridiculing him, telling him that his beautiful dream was shattered!

Nothing would have been preferable!

“Impossible!” Hun Tong suddenly shrieked, causing the manservant to almost faint from fear.

Hun Tong raised his arms and shouted like an enraged lion as he rapidly ran two loops around the empty storehouse 3.

This is impossible!!” He shouted again; his yells already filled with killing intent.

He strode over to his manservant.

The manservant groveled at the spot, imploring, “Master…”

But a moment later, Hun Tong passed by him and quickly left the storeroom.

He flew into the air without a moment’s break, rushing out of the city government building, out of the new island, and into the skies.

Hun Tong had a flying battle skill.

Like a beam of light, he shot through the clouds and entered the Golden Flash.

“It’s Hun Tong.”

“He flew away!”

“Don’t tell me he wants to run away? How long will he keep hiding?”

The merchants around the city government building exploded like a pot, as one after the other, they verbally attacked Hun Tong.

Hun Tong had flown into the Golden Flash for the sole purpose of inquiring into the war detection images.

His eyes were very bloodshot and wide open as he attentively examined these images.

He had definitely used the light beam cannons to attack the pirates and fishmen, causing them to cry for their parents, and causing those treasures to fall into the seawater.

Why weren’t they there?

How could there only be three copper coins left?

“Who?!”

“Who was it?!”

“I must see who dares take my money!”

But the images contained no valuable clues.

The Golden Flash had strong detection capabilities, but not underwater, it was more partial towards range.

It could at most observe three meters under the water.

However, it was an experimental magic ship from the Sheng Ming Empire, and since it was planned to be mass produced, it definitely had all kinds of alchemy components paired up with a standard hull that could adapt to different battlefields.

However, at present time, a suitable underwater detection module hasn’t been developed yet.

Naturally, Hun Tong knew of this shortcoming, that was why he spent a lot of money to get a Gold Eye.

With that gold level component, even if the Golden Flash couldn’t detect what exactly was underwater, it could lock onto high value targets and guide the light beam cannons in accurate bombardments.

This was also the reason why Hun Tong didn’t detect the deep sea salvagers being active on the sea floor.

War Merchant had outfitted the Deep Sea Monster with deep sea salvagers, and no other puppets of a similar type, illustrated the capabilities of these deep sea salvagers.

These alchemy puppets were amazing at fishing out resources from the ocean.

During the battle, many of them had been destroyed by the light beam bombardments, but they had been retrieved by Zi Di.

And this bit gave Hun Tong a clue.

After examining the images fruitlessly, he then accessed the bombardment records from the battle!

All at once, his eyes went red.

He found the culprit!

“It seems you are the thieves!!”

“Naga! A group of naga shaped alchemy puppets!!”

“It was you who stole my treasures, hateful, abominable!”

Hun Tong roared.

It would take a good while for him to slowly calm down.

His face twisted with malevolence and his eyes emitted a threatening cold light, “Damn it, Were those alchemy puppets from the naga priestess?”

He first suspected the naga priestess.

“No, that mysterious necromancer is also a suspect.” Hun Tong recalled.

The Iron Lump had been seized by mysterious alchemy puppets, and those alchemy puppets obviously belonged to that necromancer.

Since he had one group of alchemy puppets, having another group of them was very normal.

“But I didn’t notice any of these treasures being sent to the Iron Lump?”

Hun Tong guessed for a long time, unable to determine an exact culprit.

“Damn it, damn it!”

He clenched his fists and ferociously smashed the helm.

With a definitive culprit, how could he get back this immense sum?

If he had a target, perhaps he could pursue them with the Golden Flash.

Too sullen.

His painstaking plot was now another’s gain.

Hun Tong gloomily wanted to cough up blood.

His scheme was very good. During the battle, he had once controlled the overall situation, and according to that development, he would become the biggest winner and absorb everything.

But the Devil Gold Coins, the sudden emergence of that giant fishman, and so on had caused his grand plan to go up into smoke.

When the battle ended, Hun Tong wasn’t frantic.

Because in his plan, the immense wealth on the seafloor would help him rapidly rise once again, and his future accomplishments would be far greater than the ones he had before the battle!

This sum of money to him, to Two Eyes Island, was very important!

Essential!

But it was gone.

It was all in vain.

Another had fished it up.

Unfortunately, Hun Tong still didn’t know who!

Hun Tong couldn’t help but clutch his stomach and close his eyes.

Sorrow.

Sorrow pervaded his soul.

As a pious Wealth Church believer, the pain of losing a fortune was a hundred times more painful than bodily injuries.

The loss was disastrous, too disastrous.

Hun Tong couldn’t breathe for a moment.

“What can I do?”

He slowly opened his eyes, blankly looking at the magic images.

The images were a panoramic view of the new island.

The pirates’ pillaging and fishman army’s attack had left a ruin so horrible one couldn’t bear to look at it.

Hun Tong’s mind cooled.

In his original plan, he would use a small part of the seafloor riches to wantonly rebuild the new island.

But now he couldn’t.

No money.

“What can I use to rebuild?”

“Do I have to use my old capital?”

He had a bit of savings, but it was a cup of water on a burning cart.

“Damn it, under these circumstances, how can I face those troublesome merchant alliances?”

Before he didn’t think they would be a problem, now his brows were wrinkled deeply.

In the magic images, groups of merchants assembled at the city government building’s gate, point at the Golden Flash hidden in the clouds, shouting and screaming at him.

“If I had known this earlier, I wouldn’t have given the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps such a big reward!”

Hun Tong felt great regret.

Without money, everything was different for him.

“Eh?”

At that moment, Hun Tong saw a group of people disembarking off a ship and headed towards the city government building.

The leader was Long Fu, joined by Shi Qi.

Apart from this, there were also some people carrying some chests.

Hun Tong saw familiar chests.

Last night, he had filled up these chests with silver coins, using them to reward Shi Qi in an attempt to snatch him away.

“They are looking to give me money, for what reason?”

“Do they think the reward is too much and want to return some of it to me?”

Chapter 430: A Heart Aching Discount Sale

Hun Tong pondered on it, first activating the Gold Eye to investigate who the most valuable bombardment target on and around the new island was.

The result was that the most important target was the dragonman youngster’s delegation!

The second target was his city government building.

“My government treasury has a gold level treasure to cover and protect my property.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps bringing so much money must mean they want to trade with me!”

Hun Tong guessed the dragonman youngster’s group’s objective.

He immediately flew down.

If the Golden Flash was in better condition, perhaps he could bring the magic ship down, inviting the dragonman youngster and the others to watch, a silent deterrent and flaunting.

But after the battle, the Golden Flash’s deck was covered in holes, only half of the main light cannon was left, and there was only one of the five light cannon turrets remaining.

With it in this state, wanting to ostentatiously fly down with it would undoubtably embarrass him.

Hun Tong also didn’t want to entertain the dragonman youngster and the others at the city government building.

Because the city government building’s gate was blocked by a group of troublesome merchants.

Letting them see this would be too awkward and really harm his majestic presence.

“Since this group wants to trade with me, I cannot let those merchants see the sum I receive.” Hun Tong still had a layer of apprehension.

Due to the aforementioned things, he had to go to them and meet the group halfway.

Thus, just as the dragonman youngster and his companions left the port, they saw Hun Tong flying down to them.

“Feudal Lord.” The dragonman youngster and the rest bowed.

Hun Tong held both hands behind his back, he slightly raised his head and adopted a prestigious noble bearing.

But in the eyes of the dragonman youngster and the others, his eyes were very red and dejected, his complexion was pale, and his forehead was soaked in a fine layer of sweat.

“He knows the seafloor treasures have disappeared without a trace.” The dragonman youngster thought.

Hun Tong’s movement had been detected by the Deep Sea Monster Fish, and Zi Di had informed them in real time.

Seeing Hun Tong, the dragonman youngster couldn’t help but think of the first time they saw each other.

Then, Hun Tong had been easy going and looked down with noble grace. Now that he was strong in appearance but weak in reality, it formed a stark contrast.

Zong Ge’s eyes concealed vigilance.

If Hun Tong knew the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been the ones that took away the seafloor treasures, then he would tear himself apart.

Hun Tong ran his eyes across the group of people, did his best to maintain his noble air, and asked the dragonman youngster, “I saw that you are going to the city government building, are you looking for me?”

“Correct.” The dragonman youngster seized the opportunity to candidly state that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanted to leave Two Eyes Island, they had come to pay their respects to the feudal lord to both say goodbye and to make a transaction.

“You will be departing Two Eyes Island?” Hun Tong sighed quite reluctantly.

He still wanted to recruit these people, especially Shi Qi.

His gaze stopped at Shi Qi, but the latter didn’t show a trace of being reluctant to part.

Hun Tong’s heart chilled.

He felt short of breath.

If he had gained the seafloor treasures, he would have thrown a large sum out and done his best to hire Shi Qi, and even the entire Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

But now…he was poor.

No confidence.

The group of talents before him were something he couldn’t reach for.

He was even somewhat vexed, why had he been so extravagant yesterday with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps rewards?

“Honestly, the battle just ended. Your corps has taken massive losses; it would be better to rest and reorganize on the new island for a few days. Before setting out to sea, it would be better to be a bit more stable.” Stated Hun Tong.

But the dragonman youngster had a perfect excuse ready.

He slightly stood aside, suddenly revealing the dwarf Men Shi behind him.

He explained to Hun Tong, “Feudal Lord, during the battle, we got to know a group of true comrades.”

“Now, Men Shi has joined my corps. But his clansmen were kidnapped by that mysterious necromancer.”

“Therefore, we must leave the island as soon as possible and use all of our strength to save his clansmen, our comrades-in-arms.

Hun Tong was amazed for a bit, then slightly perked up his brows.

To him it seemed that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were a mixed group, now they were bringing in dwarfs. For one silver level military power, this transaction had unknown risks, and the merits and drawbacks were still yet to be seen.

Men Shi looked at the dragonman youngster from behind, his heart brimming with appreciation.

He was very worried about his clansmen, incessantly anxious, but he also knew of the severity of the situation and never spoke up to urge the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to save his people.

Because he knew that it was very rare that onlookers would risk fighting a necromancer to save his clansmen.

Was he, Men Shi, unable to recognize another’s good intentions and not satisfied with small gains?

Of course not!

Men Shi knew he needed propriety.

But he didn’t expect that the dragonman youngster would inform him that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ simple discussion last night was about saving his clansmen, they would leave the island today and start the pursuit!

The tide of thankfulness drowned Men Shi in an instant.

He decided on the spot to join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, despite the dragonman youngster’s protests.

Now, when he saw Hun Tong looking at him, Men Shi coldly snorted to himself, “Surely he believes that to save my clansmen, I sold myself into service to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“Humph, even if this human is a gold level, he cannot compare to Commander Long Fu!”

Men Shi didn’t have a good opinion of Hun Tong.

His people had been auctioned off at Two Eyes Island, treated as slaves to sell.

As Hun Tong scanned Men Shi over, he saw Chi Lai in the crowd.

At the auction, he wasn’t that interested in Chi Lai.

Things are different now.

He only had two silver level subordinates left, unreliable ones at that.

He now thought that although Chi Lai wasn’t a perfect silver level subordinate that didn’t get along with Two Eyes Island and him, if he had become his subordinate, it would greatly help tide Two Eyes Island over this time of emptiness!

Unfortunately, he had lost his chance.

Chi Lai now belonged to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Hun Tong felt increasing regret when a thought suddenly jumped into his mind, “The battle had caused immense damage to all sides. Yet the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ lineup has instead gotten more powerful. These people…they really are lucky.”

Hun Tong didn’t know that Magician Qing Xin was also on the Iron Lump.

When that came to mind, he felt the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had grown more captivating.

If these people could stay on Two Eyes Island, it would be amazing!

Even if they left one or two silvers, that would be very advantageous.

Despite not having much hope, Hun Tong forced out a laugh and tried one final time, “Last night, I gave my proposal to Shi Qi, have you considered it Commander Long Fu?”

The dragonman youngster deeply sighed, “So sorry, Feudal Lord.”

“Two Eyes Island is certainly a blessed place, and under your governance, it is bound to flourish beyond its past glories.”

“Your Lordship’s sincerity really moved us; it is our regret that circumstances are beyond our control.”

The dragonman youngster tactfully declined.

Hun Tong nodded; this response was expected.

His face was heavy, especially when he saw the treasure chests behind the dragonman youngster, thinking of his “sincerity” last night made his heart ache even more.

The dragonman youngster took out a list of items and handed it to Hun Tong, “These are the goods my corps wishes to purchase from the Feudal Lord.”

Hun Tong scanned it, he saw on the list that there were many coral materials, the Fragrant Gill Tea, ships, and more.

One of Two Eyes Island’s specialties was its various types of coral, because Two Eyes Island was a gigantic coral ball cage tree.

The Fragrant Gill Tea was something Hun Tong had intentionally invested in, and after countless failures, he had made it.

“Your corps has excellent taste.” Hun Tong praised the dragonman youngster, which was actually praising himself.

“Eh, water pigmen?” When Hun Tong saw this towards the end of the list, he paused slightly.

He soon recalled that he had purchased a group of these beastmen slaves at the auction.

Those slaves were water pigmen.

They had come from the upper reaches of Three Color River, a human slave team had captured them and reduced them to slaves.

In the survivors’ original purchase plan, Zong Ge wanted to buy these water pigmen a lot.

At the time, he had listed the many merits of the water pigmen.

“We need these water pigmen, their bloodline is very compatible with the Backwater Battle Qi technique. Not only do I know the cultivation method of the Backwater Battle Qi technique, but I also understand how to establish a Backwater Battle Squad.”

“With these water pigmen, we will have top quality sailors.”

“Water pigmen are easy to provide for and aren’t picky eaters. They can fish for themselves and normally, every adult water pigman is an expert fisher. During famine, they can even use river sand to allay their hunger, their powerful stomachs can let them digest it barely.”

“During naval battles, we will have an all new route of attack – we can attack enemy ships with the water pigmen.”

“Backwater Battle Qi can let someone operate in water for a long time, and after it is given to the water pigmen, they will redouble its power.”

But because of Qing Xin’s sudden appearance, the survivors had spent a large sum purchasing the blond mother and child, along with the other human slaves, causing them to lose the water pigmen to Hun Tong.

Now, the survivors were swimming in cash, making impressive money from the battle.

Therefore, they wanted to purchase them from Hun Tong.

Hun Tong thought deeply.

He originally bought these water pigman slaves because the price was decent, for at the end of the auction, everyone had spent most of their money.

In addition, the water pigmen were well suited for the constantly sinking and rising island environment.

But circumstances are different now.

Two Eyes Island had suffered disastrous damage.

Half the silver levels were dead, the city guard had been decimated, and everything needed to be rebuilt.

Under these circumstances, having many of these water pigman slaves here would instead be a hidden danger.

To Hun Tong, these beastmen needed to be strictly disciplined.

As Two Eyes Island was now, there wasn’t the energy to spare to discipline these beastmen.

Thinking of that, Hun Tong said, “This makes me embarrassed, at the time, when I purchased those water pigmen, I naturally didn’t plan on selling them.”

“Truth be told, if another person asked for them, I would have immediately refused them.”

But…if it’s your corps, I will sell them as a favor, so long as the price is appropriate.”

This was the patter of a businessman.

Since he didn’t have the seafloor valuables, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps deciding to drop by was a good opportunity that Hun Tong wouldn’t let slip by.

He wanted to negotiate this transaction as best he could, so he could obtain more valuable capital.

Following their plan, the dragonman youngster didn’t reply, instead Zong Ge stepped forth, “Feudal Lord, this transaction is with me.”

“I am somewhat interested in the water pigmen, just a personal interest.”

“The corps put up with it and added it to the transaction list.”

“I can only afford this amount. I can’t buy it at any price above this.” Zong Ge raised four fingers.

In a split second, Hun Tong became furious.

“I bought it at a price many times the one you’re offering!”

Zong Ge retracted his fingers, shook his head, and went back into the crowd, “Since I can’t afford it, I won’t buy them.”

“Huh?” Hun Tong stared blankly.

Zong Ge’s unconventional move made him feel at a loss.

Normally, you should push a bit to raise the price.

Why don’t you want to buy it?

The dragonman youngster stepped forward and brokered a compromise, “Feudal Lord, please don’t blame Shi Qi. This is his nature, straightforward. That price really is all of his personal savings; it is the limit of his financial resources”

“I understand.” Hun Tong nodded, his gaze back at Zong Ge nevertheless filling with more admiration.

He seized the opportunity to say, “Shi Qi, if you join Two Eyes Island, I will gift these water pigmen to you as a present!”

Zong Ge forced a smile and shook his head.

When the dragonman youngster saw Hun Tong staring at Zong Ge, he took a step forward to obstruct his gaze with his body.

“How about this,” the dragonman youngster said, “I will pay too, and increase Shi Qi’s price.”

Hun Tong’s face sank, “Even if you add to it, it won’t match my purchase price. Commander Long Fu, if you want to keep the hearts of men, a bit of money is not enough to keep them.”

He used Zong Ge to pressure the dragonman youngster into increasing the price.

The dragonman youngster clenched his teeth and increased the price.

As Hun Tong said that Zong Ge interjected, “Commander, forget about it, I have a passing interest in these water pigmen, that’s all.”

“Is that so…” The dragonman youngster sighed.

Seeing that the two people seemed to be giving up, Hun Tong’s eyelids suddenly jumped open.

How is this possible?

This group of water pigmen can be sold for capital.

Hun Tong also deeply sighed, “Enough.”

“We are all friends.”

“Let’s go with the final price! To tell the truth, I have never sold something at such a loss. But you are different, Shi Qi, selling it to you will make me happy too.”

As he said that, Hun Tong’s heart ached faintly again.

This was a bargain price.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge glanced at each other, thinking the same thing: Zi D’s plan really was effective!

Chapter 431: Buying a Magic Ship

The water pigman slaves were the most important item in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ transaction list.

Reaching an agreement on this caused both the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge to let out a breath.

Next, they returned to look at their ships at port.

The port had been one of the main battlefields and was left an atrocious ruin.

The port had many ships berthed there.

The source of these ships was complicated.

Some were from mercenary corps, some from merchant alliances, and plenty were pirate ships.

During the final phase of the melee, Hun Tong had used the light beam cannons to deal immense casualties to the enemy, many pirates perished, leaving many ships behind.

Some ships sank, but most were saved and towed to shore.

The ships had varying degrees of damage, some were still seaworthy, and others needed overhauling. Some keels were broken apart, requiring testing and calculation and if the repair costs were too high, they would be abandoned to be rebuilt or purchased by another.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps required many ships.

During the fighting, all of their ships remained at port, unlike the other mercenary corps and merchant alliance ships that had sailed out.

Of course, the latter’s fate wasn’t good.

They were first intercepted by magic traps, then engaged in fierce fighting with the Vinger Jar Pirates. They were also ruthlessly backstabbed by Hun Tong during this time, leading to a mournful end.

In any case, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships also suffered calamities.

When they came to Two Eyes Island, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had 12 ships. Of them, 2 were medium sized, and the rest were small sized ships.

During the beast tide, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had lost 5 ships.

After the battle for Two Eyes Island, only two small sized ships still remained in working order.

Luckily there were many ownerless ships at the port.

Before making the trade with Hun Tong, the survivors spotted their targets.

On average, ordinary ships cost 50 gold. Ship volume influenced price, and small sized ships usually cost less than medium sized ones. But different sea environments can have different ship prices. At the same time, ship composition is also an important factor. For example, how many masts and sails there are, how big the cargo hold is, how many cannons it has, and more.

Hun Tong didn’t need these ships, not only were they not useful to him, but he also needed to spend a considerable amount on maintenance and repair for them.

The patrol fleet hadn’t suffered much damage during the battle.

One Eye used bombardment tactics.
