This had its benefits, that is it conserved the strength of one’s fleet.

The downside was that One Eye barely stood out on the battlefield.

Hun Tong didn’t lack ships, but money, thus he needed to sell these ships for as much as possible.

He wore a smile, spoke frankly with assurance, and with constant business tricks that never repeated, he displayed his true “bargaining strength”.

But the dragonman youngster and his entourage held him off.

Hun Tong had fallen into Zi Di’s scheme and sold off the water pigman slaves on the cheap.

If Zi Di was currently present, she could vie with Hun Tong, so too could Fei She.

But the people here right now were not Hun Tong’s match.

Zong Ge didn’t speak, he understood this was a battlefield he wasn’t an expert in.

Men Shi was glaring, amazed that a human Lord as cunning and sinister as Hun Tong could speak so benevolently.

Chi Lai was quiet.

The dragonman youngster was the only one that could tackle this difficult job himself, and as expected, started to concede.

“Can I say a few words?” During this challenging situation, Mu Ban suddenly interjected.

Seeing an ordinary person butt in, Hun Tong was puzzled and his brows slightly wrinkled.

Who was he?

He was a gold level, an imperial noble.

To be honest, him discussing business with a silver level mercenary like Long Fu was demeaning him, now an ordinary person dared to cut into the conversation.

This was an offence.

But Hun Tong restrained himself.

If it was one of his people, he would punish them severely on the spot.

He looked at Long Fu.

The dragonman youngster’s eyes flashed.

He trusted Mu Ban.

On Mystifying Monster Island, they had experienced tribulations and fought power enemies together, he was a comrade he could entrust his back to!

“Go on, you have something to say?” The dragonman youngster supported Mu Ban.

Mu Ban took a deep breath, and with a touch of nervousness, began to recount a ship’s defects.

He was a veteran carpenter, good at making crossbow arrows. Though not familiar with ships, since escaping Mystifying Monster Island, he had been attentively studying many books on ships.

This information had come from War Merchant’s database, very detailed, reliable, and valuable.

Mu Ban not only acquired this knowledge, but he also put it to practical use.

As the ships set sail, he served as a repairman that maintained the ships.

Therefore, Mu Ban was very familiar with the structure of ships.

“That is a paddle sailboat, a Tardia model ship. It requires more sailors to paddle it, putting more pressure on the trip and supplies, its range also cannot compare to a true sailboat.”

“Don’t be fooled by the fact that that ship has a stern, it is actually an improved model Nef ship. Originally used as small coastal fishing operations and trade vessels. Although this ship is an improvement, with a bigger volume and has 3 more masts, because of its hull design flaws, it is very heavy.”

“That Cog ship seems to be pretty good. Upon careful examination, it is built from oak wood and uses shiplap joints, but it is too old. You can see moss and shellfish attached to the bottom of the ship. Despite not suffering too much damage during the battle, it can only sail for a few more years, and any large gale will decrease its already limited life span.”

…

Hun Tong was dumbstruck.

The more Mu Ban spoke, the more confident and accomplished he felt, “A day really came when I could refute a gold level imperial noble, a feudal Lord at that?”

With Mu Ban’s help, the dragonman youngster finally stabilized his feet in the negotiation.

“Your corps really is a galaxy of talent.” Hun Tong wore a smile as he spoke with deep meaning, “At the same time, he’s got big guts too!”

He had no choice but to concede that this ordinary person was talented.

But there were many talented ordinary people, and they all lived at the lowest rungs of society!

In this world of the strong, what truly decided one’s status was how powerful they were individually.

As an ordinary person, Mu Ban being able to interfere in this conversation could be said to be the greatest highlight of his life.

Most of all, it was the dragonman youngster who gave him the chance and space to display his talents.

Finally, the dragonman youngster purchased 7 ships.

Two medium sized ships, one to the dragonman youngster, the other to Zong Ge. The other 5 were small sized ships.

Blindly purchasing one type of ship model was not proper.

One needed to have ship models matched up with each other.

Like how troops were partitioned up into groups such as long range and close range, each had their duty and worked with each other, fleets were the same.

Small sized ships were often fast and agile, large ships had much more firepower and defenses, and medium sized ships were the backbone, more balanced.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet wasn’t that big, and buying big ships would encumber their speed, so medium sized ships would do.

There was another reason too, there weren’t many large sized ships for sale on the port, and those available were of poor quality.

After selling the seven ships, Hun Tong received several hundred gold coins.

He was still doing his best to sell more, “There are still many ships I don’t need. How about this, Commander Long Fu, the more ships you buy, the bigger discount I’ll offer. The 8th ship will only cost 45 gold, the 9th will only be 40 gold, and so on.

The dragonman youngster forced a smile and shook his head, “The remaining ships are too damaged, and them, we will need to repair them.”

“What’s the difference between the repair cost and the normal market value?”

Hun Tong laughed, “Doesn’t your corps have alchemy puppets? They can repair these ships.”

The survivors had used basic maintenance puppets to repair their ships.

This information had been leaked and collected by Hun Tong.

The dragonman youngster shook his head, “Those alchemy puppets expend magic power and repair materials.”

Hun Tong muttered to himself, then replied, “How about this, I will drop my price by another 5 gold coins.”

The dragonman youngster stared blankly for a moment and couldn’t help but be moved.

Finally, he bought 3 more ships, all of which were small sized ships that were relatively undamaged.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had lost many members and needed to recruit more in the future.

But the dragonman youngster didn’t intend on replenishing on Two Eyes Island.

Since they were going to Ice Sculpture Island, the personnel on that large island would undoubtedly be more outstanding and have a wider selection of choices.

After considering it for a bit, he decided to buy a few ships ahead of time to fit more future mercenaries in them.

Hun Tong had given him a rare chance – the more he bought, the lower the price, and in accordance with Hun Tong’s discount plan, the price would eventually hit 0!

But that was actually a pitfall.

On the one hand, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had finite members and couldn’t operate so many ships.

On the other hand, after several rounds of actual combat, the dragonman youngster understood that during naval warfare, ordinary ships were at best expendable, the most reliable ships were magic ships!

“Don’t you want to buy supply ships?”

“Look at this, this large ship is much more suitable!”

“When you leave the island, many accidents can happen on the seas, and one more supply ship can store more food and water, guaranteeing your voyage more.”

Hun Tong was still not satisfied; he did his very best to sell the best looking large sized ship.

The dragonman youngster shook his head, “We aren’t planning to have a long voyage, too many supplies will go unused.”

Hun Tong suddenly smiled, “There is one ship I believe you’ll find interesting. But perhaps it will be somewhat troublesome, who knows if you’ll dare buy it.”

Hun Tong’s indirect statement grabbed the dragonman youngster’s and the others’ interest.

They didn’t know. Which ship would cause Hun Tong to say this?

The deal with Hun Tong had already been completed.

Actually, after obtaining the rights to those ships, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had used a number of basic maintenance puppets to rapidly repair them.

Hun Tong personally witnessed these alchemy puppets in action and was somewhat moved by them.

He noticed that these basic maintenance puppets had 30% greater work efficiency than normal models.

He asked if he could purchase one or two from the dragonman youngster.

But the dragonman youngster tactfully declined.

The basic maintenance puppets had come from War Merchant, and the survivors didn’t want these puppets wandering outside to avoid becoming a flaw that would provoke suspicion.

By noon, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet set sail and slowly began to leave Two Eyes Island.

They had 11 ships altogether.

A few maintenance puppets were still banging about, conducting their repair tasks.

The dragonman youngster stood on the port, saying his final farewells to Hun Tong.

“Feudal Lord, thank you for dispatching your subordinates to transport the supplies to our ship holds, they saved us a lot of time.” The dragonman youngster thanked.

Hun Tong reached out his hand and wanted to clap the young man’s shoulder, but because of their height differences, he could barely touch his upper arm, “We fought side by side, you are a comrade in arms. Remember that Two Eyes Island will forever welcome you all. I also look forward to having a deeper collaboration together.”

At that moment, a group of people rushed into the port, indignant at injustice and streaming out with torrents of insults.

“Our flagship is sailing off!”

“That’s our ship!”

“Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps! Do you want to make war against our Bandage Mercenary Corps?”

“Lord Hun Tong, how could you secretly sell off another’s ship?”

The dragonman youngster was stunned, and his brows immediately wringed up, “Lord Hun Tong, didn’t you say the White Bandage was yours? This is an entirely different kind of trouble that I thought.”

Hun Tong laughed, unconcerned, “I defeated the enemies, drove back the pirates and fishmen, otherwise the Bandage Mercenary Corps would have been wiped out.”

“Therefore, the White Bandage is naturally my spoils of war!”

“Naturally, I admit, these mercenaries are somewhat obstinate.”

“That doesn’t matter, this transaction has no problems. I will handle these matters well enough.”

“Go on then, that ship has sailed.”

The dragonman youngster looked at the furious and wronged Bandage Mercenary Corps, and couldn’t help but feel remorseful, but like Hun Tong said, the ship had sailed already.

He had no choice but to bite his teeth, jump up with great strength, and land onto the last ship.

Standing on the aft, he gazed at Hun Tong’s figure on the port, his impression of the latter becoming worse.

“Commander Beng Dai died by Hun Tong’s hands.”

“Without its sole silver level, the Bandage Mercenary Corps probably won’t survive.”

“For his own benefits, Hun Tong began to act against this power.”

“This absolutely isn’t a righteous action; such table manners are too unsightly.”

Chapter 432: Pirate Trial

Hun Tong watched the dragonman youngster and the others leave, then his smile gradually dwindled into nothing as his gaze became darker.

Next to him, One Eye’s voice contained an implicit hidden bitterness, “My Lord, why not keep the White Bandage? That magic ship could serve as the patrol fleet’s flagship. Although we have a bronze level magic ship, it can’t compare to the White Bandage.”

“That said, we collected the treasures from the sea, we don’t lack money.”

“But it isn’t easy to place an order for a magic ship from Gear Island’s dockyard.”

“Too many people place orders; it will take at least half a year to reach ours.”

When the seafloor treasures were mentioned, Hun Tong’s heart seemed to be stabbed.

He sucked in some cold air and unhurriedly replied, “Although the seafloor had money, too many places need funds.”

“The entire new island needs to be rebuilt.”

“Compensation needs to be provided.”

“Those merchants are troublesome.”

“Another necromancer appeared; the empire will likely dispatch an envoy to begin an investigation. At that time, I will need bribes.”

Most crucially are the light cannon towers and the Golden Flash, we need to spend a large sum to hire an alchemy master to come repair them!”

“Money needs to be spent on those things.”

“The White Bandage is only bronze level, adding a ship like that won’t improve our strength by much.”

“Repairing the Golden Flash is more important.”

“Having a gold level magic ship is incomparable to having more ships like the White Bandage.”

“The White Bandage Mercenary Corps’ performance really disappointed me.”

“That mercenary corps and the Iron Club Mercenary Corps are too close to those merchant alliances. Don’t tell me you still want then to keep it and allow those annoying merchants to add to our troubles?”

“Of course, I also didn’t casually sell it. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps are not normal, didn’t you see their performance during the battle?

One Eye nodded, stating his acknowledgement.

To him, of the humans besides the gold levels, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had performed the brightest.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had stabilized the port battlefield, then agreed to Hun Tong’s mission, taking the risk to leave for the subterranean depths to try to destroy the fishmen’s large scale casting. They had acted far more reliably than the Iron Club and Bandage Mercenary Corps!

Both of them had only acted for their own benefits.

By this contrast, Hun Tong, as feudal Lord, had a very good opinion of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Hun Tong’s view of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet gradually shrank as he assessed, “Although they recruited Chi Lai and Men Shi, Chi Lai’s bloodline concentration isn’t high, silver level is his limit. Men Shi has stagnated for ten years, his potential is reaching zero.”

“But Long Fu and Shi Qi are different.”

“Shi Qi’s battle qi already has a gold luster, and given enough time, he will become a gold level.”

“Their backer has good prospects for Long Fu and appointed him leader, pushing down Shi Qi. His bloodline must be more outstanding. During the battle, he even fought Er Gua Zi directly, only a bit weaker than him.

“In a few years, both of them might become gold levels.”

“Selling them some good things will benefit us.”

One Eye nodded repeatedly, “My Lord, your consideration is correct!”

Hun Tong’s eyes coldly flashed, he looked tranquil, but his heart was fretful.

When One Eye brought up the seafloor treasure matter, although it stung him, it also warned him.

Only a select few knew about the seafloor salvaging matter.

Even One Eye, who knew about it, didn’t know how much Hun Tong had fished up.

“I cannot let anyone else know that the seafloor only had three copper coins!”

Hun Tong made a firm resolution to conceal the truth.

The business world was like a battlefield.

On the battlefield, if morale slumped, no matter how big the force, they would crumble, scatter, and flee.

The business world was the same.

If one’s deficit was too high, others would become bearish, popular sentiment would collapse, inevitably triggering a bank run and other nasty events.

“I must guard this secret and let others mistakenly think that I dredged out much wealth, this will be advantageous for me.”

“If that happens, at least to others, I will still be the winner.”

Hun Tong was filled with vigilance.

After the battle, his situation became very vile.

He didn’t have much capital, even when combining his own capital and gains from the auction.

This included the Devil Gold Coins; he had to keep those to deal with imperial investigation.

After all, he had ruthlessly backstabbed friends during the battle with his optimal bombardment.

Just as he explained to One Eye, he had to spend money on too many places.

“If my weakness is revealed, I fear I will meet a similar end to the Bandage Mercenary Corps.”

Thinking of this, Hun Tong looked at the Bandage Mercenary Corps at the edge of the port, the cold light in his eyes growing stronger.

He then faintly ordered One Eye, “They don’t understand the situation, how stupid. One Eye, arrest those people.”

One Eye hesitated somewhat, “My Lord, that isn’t a good idea.”

One Eye was feeling a little guilty and diffident.

They not only sold off their flagship, but they also wanted to arrest them. What difference was there between them and pirates?

But a moment later, One Eye saw Hun Tong’s ice cold gaze, and his heart shivered, causing him to rush to comply.

One Eye, with the help of the city guard, dealt with the Bandage mercenaries with little difficulty.

Because Commander Beng Dai was dead.

Hun Tong soared into the sky and flew to the Golden Flash.

He used the Gold Eye to investigate the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet that was still in its range for one final investigation.

The result didn’t go against his expectations.

“The seafloor treasures…are not on them.”

Hun Tong, to probe into what was really going on with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, had intentionally helped them and deployed many people to go into their holds with supplies.

Now with the Gold Eye’s probing result, Hun Tong’s few suspicions were completely dissipated.

“Therefore, the most likely culprits are the naga priestess and that necromancer disguised as a Wisteria Merchant Alliance elder.”

The naga priestess and the remnants of the Blue Green Coral Clan were far from Two Eyes Island, deep in the ocean.

Even if it was them, Hun Tong had no way of finding them.

As for the necromancer, Hun Tong still had hope.

Thinking of that, Hun Tong immediately used an alchemy device to contact the imperial secret spy Shadow.

“When will you head out?”

“How far has the necromance run?”

“Two Eyes Island has had two necromancers appear on it, and the proof is definite, likely prompting the empire to send a person to investigate.”

“When will you attack him? I can personally lend a hand.”

“If the empire investigates Two Eyes Island, me having that necromancer’s head in hand will benefit us both, right?”

Shadow’s voice came through shortly after, “Hehe, Feudal Lord, don’t be so worried.”

“An insignificant silver level necromancer is nothing.”

“Believe me, his backer is more valuable!”

“Truth be told, I have already made arrangements. Since he has been exposed, he can forget about stability.”

“Right, those seabed treasures…strictly speaking, doesn’t that also belong to us secret spies?”

Hun Tong coldly snorted, interrupting Shadow, “Don’t think so! Those treasures are Two Eyes Island’s.”

He couldn’t speak the bitterness in his heart.

Despite only fishing up three copper coins, everyone around him thought he made a fortune.

Now he has to pretend he really was rich!

The port was in chaos.

One Eye had charged into the scattering Bandage Mercenary Corps, behind him the city guard was arresting people all over the place.

The mercenaries didn’t have good morale to start with, and when confronted with the city guard arresting them, they didn’t have the courage to resist.

Many people were taken prisoner.

Some of the higher ups escaped.

“Damn Hun Tong, he really is acting against our corps!”

“Don’t be impulsive, hurry and leave.”

“We will seek the Mercenary Guild to redress this injustice!”

“Ask the five great merchant representatives for help!”

“There is also the Iron Club Mercenary Corps, their commander also dead. If they don’t act, their end will definitely be the same as ours!”

The Applaud Pirates.

Hu Li weakly opened his eyes.

“What happened to me?” He was perplexed and knew something was wrong with his body.

As his field of view gradually cleared, he was shocked to discover that he was tied tightly to a wooden chair.

A group of frogmen surrounded him.

The lead frogman spoke, “You finally woke up.”

Hu Li looked at the frogman speaking and immediately exploded in anger, “Bei Xiang, what do you think you’re doing?”

Seeing Hu Li struggle, the second mate Bei Xiang sneered, “Give up, I gave you the famous drug Sighing Water. I gave you quite a lot, I believe even a gold level will feel powerless for a time and will be unable to use any battle qi during that time. To say nothing of you, my captain.”

Hu Li narrowed his eyes, “You want to kill me for my position? Bei Xiang, you are only an iron level!”

“A peak iron level.” Bei Xiang laughed grimly, thumbing his finger at his back, “Look at my back, that is the trait of the Grand Elephant bloodline! I will become a silver level sooner or later.”

Hu Li retorted, “Humph, that is a future matter. If you kill me now, how will you convince the crew?”

The second mate Bei Xiang smiled, “I can’t do it by myself. What awaits you is a pirate trial! The outcome of the trial will be determined by all of the pirates!”

Hu Li stared blankly, soon after, the hope for survival welled up.

His chair was lifted up and carried out of the ship cabin and onto the deck.

It was late into the night, the moon was perfectly round, and its light was bright.

All of the frogmen were concentrated on the flagship’s deck, gazing at Hu Li with complex expressions.

Bei Xiang presided, “Begin the trial.”

Hi Li burst into laughter, “What is my crime?”

“I built this pirate group, I stole the flagship, I led you all in plunders again and again.”

“Xue Dao, if I hadn’t saved you, you would have died three years ago.”

“Liang Pi, back then it was I who used my body to block that ice breath, otherwise you would have become a frozen frog.”

“You too, Che Li, it was I who freed you from your iron cage. Otherwise, you would have become some magician’s experiment subject and might have died without even a bone remaining.”

…

Hu Li produced a torrent of words, speaking faster and faster.

Many of the frogmen he named lowered their heads in shame.

As he said his final words, Hu Li passionately gazed up at the moon, “Because of me, the Applaud Pirates exist, because of me, you have come together.”

“What qualifications do you have to put me on trial?”

“Who can judge my crimes?!”

The frogmen lowered their heads silently.

Only Bei Xiang narrowed his eyes and measured, “The rules of a pirate trial have been set by successive generations of Pirate Gods, you are being judged in God’s name, why do you refuse to accept it?”

“I admit, you have toiled and accomplished much, it was your hands that founded this pirate group, and you have shown many people kindness.”

“But have you done nothing wrong? Have you not treated us unfairly? During each looting, you were always the biggest beneficiary, and we only got the leftovers!”

“You are guilty, your biggest crime is that you haven’t made us rich!”

“Two Eyes Island was a good opportunity, but because of your stupidity, always wanting to steal the Vinegar Jar, we ended up empty handed!!”

“Utter rubbish!” Hu Li coldly snorted, “Short-sighted people, with the Vinegar Jar, we could have robbed more, your vision is too narrow.”

Bei Xiang shook his head, “I haven’t come to dispute you.”

He turned away, ran his eyes across the deck, and raised his arms, “Let everyone be a jury.”

“Starting with you.” He pointed at a frogman next to him.

The frogman shamefully hummed and hawed, “Guil, guilty.”

“Eh?” Hu Li glared.

“Guil, guilty.”

“Guilty.”

“I believe he is guilty.”

…

As the frogmen spoke, Hu Li’s mentality collapsed.

At first, the frogmen hesitated, but gradually, their judgments grew more powerful and more candid!

Finally, they even became one.

Bei Xiang was the final vote, he looked at Hu Li and exposed a winning smile as he shrugged, “You see, you are quite unpopular! You ignored everyone’s heartfelt wish; you are not fit to be our leader!!”

Ding.

A moment later, Hu Li suddenly struggled out of his ropes.

He summoned his battle qi, catching Bei Xiang off guard and blasting him into the air.

Bei Xiang coughed out a lot of blood as he smashed into a mast and fell to the floor, not rising afterwards.

The frogmen were in an uproar.

Hu Li stood proudly.

Bei Xiang, his stomach on the deck, looked up at Hu Li, “Impossible, I put so much Sighing Water in your soup, you are just a silver level, it is impossible for you to recover so quickly!”

Hu Li sneered, “I should probably tell you I also have the Poison Heart Bloodline.”

“I know you are strong enough to become first mate. You always resented nepotism, that I placed my relative in the position of first mate, a head above you.”

“I originally wanted to help you to the spot of first mate, unfortunately…”

“Traitors will die!”

Hu Li walked to Bei Xiang and was just about to kill him.

But a moment later, his movements stiffened like a stone statue.

A human female garbed in leather and holding a long whip appeared.

“You, you are…Lady Sea Snake?!” Hu Li croaked out with shock, “Why are you…”

Crack.

A moment later, his neck was broken with incorporeal force.

Lady Sea Snake stepped onto the deck with a thud, stood in front of Bei Xiang, and looked down at him, “Didn’t you say you could handle it?”

“There was…an accident.” Bei Xiang looked up to see Lady Sea Snake’s brown leather clothing illuminated by the moonlight, along with her perfectly round thighs and mountain range like chest curves.

“No need to remind you what happens next?” Lady Sea Snake smiled.

“Of course. First contact the Lightning Pirates and the Stench Pirates. Then go kill that necromancer!” Frogman Bei Xiang spat out.

Chapter 433: Pirate Alliance

Lightning Pirates.

In the captain’s room.

Pant, pant.

Dian Wan’s chest moved up and down, gasping for air. He wasn’t in a good state, as his face was pale and he was profusely sweating all over.

The first mate was next to him, holding a kitchen knife and rubbing it against Dian Wan’s left arm bone.

Dian Wan’s left arm was missing a lot of flesh, even a small pierce of arm bone was missing.

It was a grave wound created by a light beam cannon.

As an old pirate group with stores, they naturally had healing methods.

Flesh Cutting Knife!

The kitchen knife in the first mate’s hand was an impressive gold level item.

It came from the great saint level chef – Blood Sacrifice Butcher.

This butcher was a demon that was once active in the main world.

This gold level flesh knife was merely one of his cooking implements, its function was as it cut flesh, it stimulated the target’s vitality and made them quickly self-regenerate.

It was the cooking tool Blood Sacrifice Butcher acquired ingredients with.

The Lightning Pirates accidently obtained this cooking tool and used it for healing.

Like now, the first mate was cutting the edges of Dian Wan’s burnt flesh.

Dian Wan was injured while simultaneously having his vitality stimulated to rapidly heal himself.

Seeing the fringes of the black necrotic wound turning back to scarlet flesh, the first mate pressed the knife into Dian Wan’s arm bone again.

“Captain, endure this!”

After the first mate said that he began to saw Dian Wan’s arm bone with the knife.

Dian Wan gritted his teeth tightly and resisted the pain of this healing.

A spell of labor later, he pushed the first mate away, “Slow down, let me slow down!”

The first mate had a complex expression as he took out an anesthetic, “How about you smear some on, captain?”

Dian Wan stared blankly for a moment, then let out a torrent of abuse, “You had anesthetic, why didn’t you say so earlier?”

The first mate disdainfully looked back, “I wanted to say so, but captain you just shouted to stop talking and start healing!”

“Oh.” Dian Wan glared and went silent.

The awkward mood spread out.

Dian Wan coughed, and decided to shift the topic, “Oh dear, this looting really was sullen!”

“Many people lost their lives, but little of the treasure was recovered.”

“A big loss!”

“Hun Tong is too sinister; he concealed a gold level magic ship. If there is a chance in the future, I must take vengeance for this enmity.”

Dian Wan had a strong hatred for Hun Tong.

The first mate nodded repeatedly.

The most painful thing about looting Two Eyes Island was that things had gone smoothly and they had plundered much.

But in the end, things went cold, Hun Tong had cut them off, most of the treasures fell to the seafloor, and the pirates didn’t have a chance to retrieve them.

The Lightning Pirates had suffered lots of damage, including Dian Wan who was heavily injured by a light beam cannon.

“Captain, there has been a communication. It is the Applaud Pirates!” At this time, a pirate from the doorway exclaimed.

“Hu Li?” The first mate was puzzled, “What does that old frog want to talk to us about? Oh dear, captain, you can’t leave, you aren’t done healing yet. Wait a minute, let me at least wrap up your wound for the time being!”

Dian Wan had already left the captain’s room.

The first mate rushed out after him.

On the aft house stairs, Dian Wan took an alchemy communication device from one of his underlings, “Hu Li, why are you calling me?”

Dian Wan wasn’t surprised he could be contacted.

Although the pirates had left Two Eyes Island, they were still within its vicinity.

As long as they weren’t out of range, alchemy communication devices could get in touch with each other.

Bei Xiang’s voice came through, “Big shot Dian Wan, I have business I want to discuss with you.”

Dian Wan immediately pursed his brows, “Who are you? Have Hu Li come speak with me.”

Bei Xiang coldly snorted, “Hu Li is already dead, I am the new captain of the Applaud Pirates.”

Dian Wan was flabbergasted, “You killed him?”

“Of course not!” Bei Xiang resolutely denied it and stressed, “He received a fair pirate trial. He was unpopular; therefore, I assumed his place to make everyone rich together.”

“Just you? Bei Xiang…I remember, you are the second mate, hehe, you aren’t even the first mate, what qualifications do you have to discuss business with me?” Dian Wan ridiculed.

Bei Xiang coldly snorted, “Don’t mention that rubbish first mate. I am already a peak iron level, and with my bloodline, sooner or later, I will become a silver level. Speaking of that business, I found where that necromancer is, are you interested in getting rich together?”

Don’t be so quick to decline.”

“He was the person in room 21.”

“How about that, are you interested now?”

Dian Wan suddenly took in a deep breath, “Oh? Those two annoying chaps?”

“They purchased many good things!”

Dian Wan recalled the time he bought those useless toxic dentures and immediately grew angry.

Of course, he felt more avarice.

The first mate saw things were not looking good, and hurriedly advised, “Captain, they are a silver level necromancer, and there were two people inside that private room, in other words, one of his accomplices is hiding. There is also that wind path magician…”

Before the first mate could finish, Dian Wan laughed, “Haha, deal!”

First mate: …..

Bei Xiang: …..

The frogman had a pile of stuff to say, but didn’t say any of it.

In the end, Dian Wan agreed.

Bei Xiang, “Cough, since that is so, then I will go contact the Stench Pirates.”

Those words had an element of probing to them.

After all, the Lightning Pirates and the Stench Pirates had a tense relationship.

Dian Wan paused, then nodded, “Go contact them, let’s rob together!”

Bei Xiang was just about to turn off the communication device when he heard the Lightning Pirates’ first mate unbearably grumble out, “Captain, you agreed to this too quickly!”

“Don’t forget, you are still wounded.

“This injury doesn’t mean anything!” Dian Wan casually replied, “The main thing is that the necromancer is too loathsome!”

“His combat strength isn’t that great either.”

“During the battle, Ya Ma could suppress him.”

“Most crucially, he doesn’t have suitable sailors, or even presentable sailors. Naval battles are different from ground warfare.”

“He has a few alchemy puppets barely able to operate the Iron Lump, and when the naval battle begins, the Iron Lump will become a living target! Hehehe.”

Dian Wan had a loose tongue, but he wasn’t just blindly impulsive, he still had brains.

But he was often impulsive, and when he had the urge, he wouldn’t use his brain.

The first mate nodded, and he approved of Dian Wan’s analysis.

The first mate replied, “During the battle, we can immediately sink the Iron Lump. They only have one ship, we have many.”

We don’t need to fight with swords, let him sink directly into the sea!”

“He has used flying scrolls, but any magic will consume magic power.”

“If we can deplete their magic power like that, then even if there are more magicians, it will be nothing to worry about.”

“Right, right, we’ll attack like that!” Dian Wan belly laughed, “Don’t forget about Yang Cong Tou and the rest.”

“They might also join in.”

“After all, Two Eyes Island has made many depressed!”

Elsewhere, the frogman Bei Xiang also smoothly contacted Yang Cong Tou.

But this talk was far more challenging than the one with Dian Wan.

Even though Bei Xiang had informed Yang Cong Tou that the necromancer was in room 21, it was hard to persuade them.

“The other side has at least two magicians, and another accomplice who hasn’t appeared yet, we don’t know anything about her.”

“There is also that wind path magician, she seems to be a slave.”

“It is very likely that those two had joined the auction to rescue her.”

Yang Cong Tou had layers of apprehensions.

Qing Xin had boarded the same ship as Cang Xu; this strange action had caused others to think that the two of them were part of the same group.

As Bei Xiang’s persuasions were going nowhere, Lady Sea Snake was forced to intervene again, “What if I was included? Yang Cong Tou, you haven’t forgotten my voice have you?”

Yang Cong Tou’s eyes suddenly widened, Lady Sea Snake’s voice was very enticing, but Yang Cong Tou looked like he had seen a snake or a scorpion.

He coldly replied, “It’s you, Lady Sea Snake!”

Why are you interfering?

Did this necromancer offend you?

Did the battle of Two Eyes Island also attract the gaze of the Fire Beard Pirates?

Lady Sea Snake had a complicated history; she was a member of the Fire Beard Pirates.

Compared with her silver level magician strength, people valued her layered identity more.

Lady Sea Snake tenderly laughed, “You think too much, Yang Cong Tou, this is a personal operation, nothing more.”

“Of course, if the war becomes unfavorable, I can naturally invite some comrades to come cheer for us.”

Yang Cong Tou sank into silence, then quickly pondered.

During that battle, he had also been shot by a light beam cannon, injuring him.

At first, he didn’t want to risk going to deal with Cang Xu.

He was a caster and had a different attitude from fighters like Dian Wan.

But now that Lady Sea Snake had appeared, he now realized that this matter had a high chance of success!

Yang Cong Tou muttered to himself, then replied, “Since you are joining us, then we can try too.”

“Who else are you inviting?”

“Qi You’s people?”

Lady Sea Snake sneered, “Ask the Ten Touch Pirates, even if we steal the treasures, how much will our share be?”

Those words caused Yang Cong Tou to laugh, “Right, as long as we can do it. Those people are chicks of the sea! There is only the Iron Lump, something easy to deal with.”

And so, a small pirate alliance was formed.

Still in the dark about this, the survivors were currently sailing.

Cang Xu in control of the Iron Lump sailed the furthest ahead, while below in the ocean depths, the Deep Sea Monster Fish quietly followed, and further in the distance, was the main force of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The Iron Lump, the captain’s room.

Cang Xu looked at three ghosts before him with a pained expression.

These three human ghosts were all at an impressive silver level!

This was the biggest spoil of war Cang Xu had harvested from the battlefield.

“These are all top notch ghosts.” Cang Xu sighed.

The ghosts were not just silver level, but there were also all humans. Human ghosts had ample intelligence and could understand some relatively complex orders.

Gathering ghosts really wasn’t easy.

Because this was a world of many gods and widespread belief.

Many times, when intelligent lifeforms died, their souls would be received by the god they believed in.

Nonbelievers were rare.

Therefore, when necromancers wanted to gather souls, they had to fight the gods for them and needed to start in advance.

“I am only an iron level, I can’t control three silver level ghosts, I have to abandon one of them.” Cang Xu’s soul strength was finite, and controlling too many ghosts would be an immense burden that he would find hard to bear.

But Cang Xu hesitated.

Dispelling a ghost was rather wasteful.

“I should join the undead gathering as soon as possible.”

“There, I can sell the surplus silver level ghost.”

Such goods must be in great demand.”

“Alas, a pity Zi Di isn’t iron level yet, controlling the ghost Lan Lu is already her limit, and she is unable to accommodate a second one.”

If it were possible, Cang Xu wanted to keep all three silver level ghosts.

Chapter 434: Cang Xu’s Equipment

Although the necromancy path was an emerging school of magic, it had developed three aspects.

They were bone aspect, corpse aspect, and soul aspect.

Cang Xu was a soul aspect necromancer, this aspect’s trait was making, housing, and controlling ghosts.

Ghosts were controlled similarly to how an alchemy component controls a magic ship.

The more components there were, the more functions a magic ship had and the stronger it was.

But this amount had a limit, magic ship designs had so many prefabricated locations and the magic power reservoir could supply so much magic, these both determined what type and how many alchemy components could be loaded.

Cang Xu had his soul quality and spirit strength.

A small flower pot couldn’t grow a big tree.

Cang Xu’s gifts were worse than Zi Di’s, and he was once a mere bronze level. After becoming a necromancer, his talent’s limit broke through into the iron level.

“Soul crystals can increase soul capacity, thereby automatically improving spirit strength.”

“My soul needs to be constantly strengthened before I can control three silver level ghosts.”

By strengthening one’s soul continuously, a soul aspect necromancer can eventually become a one man army that can control countless ghosts in battle.

“Furthermore, according to the necromancer inheritance I have, there are still higher level soul aspect spells. Among them are ghost transformation spells that can modify the foundation of a ghost to increase their strength and mobility or perhaps reduce the soul burden on the necromancer and so on.”

“Most importantly, there is a method that can decrease the erosion that negative energy has on the body.”

When Cang Xu thought of that, he couldn’t help but look down at his palm.

A magician’s power originated from external energies, for example fire path elements and wind path elements.

By using these energies, a magician’s body will inevitably be eroded by these elements.

Over a long time, the vast majority of magicians will have weak bodies.

This systemic problem was worse with necromancers.

They suffered negative energy corrosion, not only would their bodies deteriorate, so too would their temperament.

As time passed, necromancers would develop a loathing for living beings and even have a strong longing to destroy them.

The bad name of the necromancy school came in part from this.

Of course, the main thing was that they touched the taboo of souls and offended the core interest of the gods.

In order to vie with negative energy corrosion, Cang Xu had transformed his body into a living dead body.

He used all of his efforts to barely maintain his humanity.

But this condition also had imminent dangers.

Especially during the last battle, when he used many necromancy spells, causing his body to abruptly suffer heavier corrosion.

“The method I used to transform my body merely came from my own research.”

“The undead gathering will have magicians exchanging with each other, I should be able to gain something that can vie with negative energy corrosion better.”

Cang Xu looked forward to the undead gathering.

Because he had so many things he needed to acquire.

These things barely appeared in normal exchanges.

This was the first undead gathering Cang Xu would participate in; he lacked any experience in it.

It wasn’t that he hadn’t had the chance, he even obtained a few contact methods from the soul aspect necromancer’s inheritance that could let him join local undead gatherings.

But Cang Xu didn’t go.

He was very cautious, and to him, if he exposed himself, he would be ruined. He wouldn’t be able to continue serving as steward and his family would be destroyed as a result.

Cang Xu’s choice had caused him to miss virtually all opportunities. But thanks to that, he had survived until now.

Many powers disseminated fake information and arranged traps to lure necromancers into gathering so they could hunt them down!

Now, Cang Xu has decided to join an undead gathering.

His conduct style had undoubtedly radicalized by a lot.

On the one hand, he had true comrades and external help.

On the other hand, the boundless ocean was far from the Sheng Ming Continent, and there was more space for him to use his fists and feet.

Finally, the current situation was forcing him to do so.

During the battle, Cang Xu had been affected by the Devil Gold Coins and exposed himself.

Thinking of that now, his heart felt a chill.

Too dangerous.

Luckily, he had the layer of disguise as a Wisteria Merchant Alliance elder, otherwise it would be massive trouble!

There was also the blackmail message, but no reply came back, making Cang Xu very vigilant.

“According to common sense, the Bai Zhen Clan should have written back.”

“It seems a problem has appeared during it!”

“I must become stronger, to deal with possible unexpected events and crises in the future.”

The undead gathering also contained danger.

He needed to arm himself as best as possible.

Speaking of which, the survivors had purchased many good things from the auction.

The melee was not a good time to use them, to guard against making flaws that would expose themselves.

But now, the Deceptive Disguise and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spells had been cast on some important equipment, allowing them to be openly used.

Cang Xu inspected the equipment he was wearing.

One brass infusion bottle – the fresh water infusion bottle.

A delicate compass apparatus – the palm compass.

A badly damaged water path magic book – inside it had three gold level water path spells, the water arrow spell, the water snake book and thick fog spell.

There was also the voodoo substitute death puppet.

Beside the magic book, the other three items were silver level items.

The fresh water infusion bottle could meet the daily water needs of 200 people, and after the survivors talked it over, they temporarily put it on the Iron Lump.

The Iron Lump didn’t have many supplies, and after it sailed from the battle, the ship had even less supplies.

The compass was a maritime navigation instrument, and it was given to Cang Xu to prevent him from getting lost and to be able to determine his own course.

The survivors only had two magicians that could use the water path magic book, and now that Cang Xu was adventuring to the undead gathering, it was only natural he got equipment first.

Finally, was the death substitute puppet…

Looking at this item, Cang Xu’s heart couldn’t help but warm.

Originally, Cang Xu and Zi Di had purchased this from the auction for the dragonman youngster.

But the dragonman youngster decided to give this item to Cang Xu to prevent any accidents from happening.

Besides these 4 items, Cang Xu had some other things.

For example, in his sewn human leather storage pouch, he had a shroud, 5 fire path rings, a magic recovery mask, and a magic conduction bracelet.

The human leather storage pouch also had many casting materials, such as dead sea shell fragments, grimace mushrooms, ghost spider legs, and so on.

Some magic scrolls that mainly came from Mystifying Monster Island.

Many magic potions. The survivors had a comparatively strong medicine production capability, and after a few days, they were able to replenish their medicine somewhat and filled Cang Xu completely on medical stores.

There were other things that had been purchased from the auction.

But they didn’t suit Cang Xu.

For example, the arrowhead headband was not magician equipment, its service target was fighters.

For the same reason, the Mushroom Dancing Shoes and the mushroom tights didn’t suit Cang Xu either.

The Sunlight Disc was a gold level component used to build an artificial garden and other such projects.

The glass external armor and sacred plate armor didn’t suit magicians either.

“Finally, there is also the Feather Snake Sail.”

The Feather Snake Sail was in a wooden chest, folded neat and tidy, nearly filling said chest.

Cang Xu took the wooden chest, then left the captain’s room to come onto the deck.

Besides him, there was not a single other person on deck, magician Qing Xin was on the main mast’s lookout tower, responsible for keeping watch.

Upon seeing Cang Xu, she slowly flew down.

As a wind path magician, she was better at flying than magicians of other schools.

When she left the island, she had no equipment.

But now, her left wrist was wearing a magic conduction bracelet.

There was also the silver level treasure, the life protection cushion that had been temporarily allocated to Qing Xin.

The magic conduction bracelets had originally been a pair. One for Zi Di and Cang Xu. The one Qing Xin was wearing was the one Zi Di had loaned to her.

She also wore the life protection cushion; it was a good life saving treasure that could heal through absorbing blood.

Cang Xu and Zi Di couldn’t use it.

Because they were necromancers, and the life protection cushion’s healing energy violently conflicted with negative energy.

A normal person would be healed, while Zi Di would be harmed. If it was used on Cang Xu, it would break his body’s life and death equilibrium, causing him to suffer a backlash.

Seeing Qing Xin, a loathing immediately appeared in Cang Xu’s heart.

This was the aftermath of his usage of so many necromancy spells.

“Come, help me hand this sail up.” Cang Xu endured the hate as he opened the wooden chest’s lid, exposing the sail inside.

Qing Xin was amazed, “A Gold level sail?”

When Cang Xu and Zi Di had purchased this sail, she was still a slave imprisoned and didn’t know anything about it.

Magic spell – mage hand.

This was a very basic spell that could let a magician’s spirit meddle with matter and remotely control objects, it looked like a formless hand.

Because the magic model was very simple, it could be cast extremely quickly and consume a tidbit of magic power.

This also determined its upper limits, it was only suitable to use in daily life, such as writing remotely and for grabbing some materials when a magician was busy experimenting.

With Cang Xu’s mage hand, the Feather Snake Sail inside the wooden chest was hoisted, then unfurled it in the air.

“I can help you.” Qing X I also used mage hand.

She tore the old sail off the main mast.

Cang Xu seized the opportunity to change sails.

The Feather Snake Sail was very gorgeous, and after the change, it made Qing Xin disoriented.

But they weren’t finished at this step.

Because the Iron Lump was a magic ship, next Cang Xu still needed to connect the Feather Snake Sail to the magic power reservoir.

This was somewhat troublesome.

Because the Iron Lump was a mere bronze level magic ship, and when it was designed, it didn’t have any alchemy modules for sails.

Therefore, Cang Xu and Qing Xi had to construct their own channel.

With magic power pouring in, it was difficult to activate the Feather Snake Sail.

It was gold level equipment, and the magic power it consumed was hard for Cang Xu and Qing Xin to supply, thus, they had to rely on the magic power reservoir.

In fact, even with the Iron Lump’s magic power reservoir, it was a little hard to meet the Feather Snake Sail’s demand.

The Feather Snake Sail and the Iron Lump were clearly incompatible.

But this was temporary.

“When the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps beats us, this ship and sail will become legitimate spoils of war.” Cang Xu thought.

Chapter 435: Treasure List

The Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Fei She was walking through a metal passage.

The passage was lined with bright lights.

“Were all of the treasures piled up here?” Fei She was inwardly amazed.

He could barely squeeze through the once spacious passage.

Because there were all kinds of valuables piled up on either side!

It was devoid of people.

Besides Fei She’s footsteps, there was only the sound of his breathing in the passage.

When the survivors escaped Mystifying Monster Island, they lived in this ship, giving it character and even making it noisy.

Now, most of the survivors were on the ocean surface, this vessel only had Zi Di and Fei She who just clandestinely entered.

“Pres, president. I’m he, here.” Fei She saluted to Zi Di as he opened the cabin door.

An alchemy formation was glowing, and in the middle of it was the Magic Medicine Bathtub boiling a cauldron of palm black medicine.

The black medicine plopped as it boiled, emitting a strange odor.

Seeing Zi Di was in the process of refining magic medicine, Fei She hurriedly lowered his voice and didn’t dare disturb her.

Zi Di drew her attention from the Medicine Bathtub and turned to Fei She, “Elder, don’t be nervous.”

This alchemy formation is mainly managed by the tower spirit.”

“I am just observing.”

“Alas, we only have one yellow crab alchemy puppet, if the other one wasn’t damaged, our medicine refinement speed would have been at least 45% faster!”

Zi Di was refining medicine for Cang Xu.

The latter was joining the undead gathering.

Cang Xu needed all kinds of medicine to use and trade with at the gathering.

Quite the regret that Cang Xu was a living dead person.

Many medicines had a lower effect on him and even harmed him.

Zi Di then said to Fei She, “I’m too short on time. If Cang Xu was here, it would be fine, but he is doing more important things right now.”

“Therefore, I summoned you here to make an inventory of our spoils of war.”

“We took many treasures from the seafloor.”

“While transporting them into the cabins, I roughly partitioned them up into categories.”

“But that is far from enough.”

“We now need you to attentively divide them up and calculate their value.”

“Among them are some antiques, calligraphies, and other art pieces, we’ll need you to give them a market estimation,”

“This is a rough list of items, take a look.”

Zi Di handed Fei She a list.

The list was very thin, only three pages thick.

Fei She looked at it, and when he saw the initial valuation, he couldn’t help but stare, “63,000 gold?”

Fei She knew that an imperial viscount’s yearly income was roughly 15,000 gold. An imperial silver level cultivator (without lands) had an average yearly salary of 100 gold, and gold levels got 300 gold.

Therefore, the 50,000 gold coins the survivors at the auction was imposing and suppressed everyone there.

It seemed that Hun Tong earned a big profit from one of Two Eyes Island’s auctions, but that wasn’t actually true.

This last auction was unprecedented.

Hun Tong had lots of production costs.

Zi Di replied, “Upon cursory glance, I noticed that many antiques seem valuable.”

“Although Two Eyes Island has only belonged to Hun Tong for ten years, he really was a talented businessman and made its people very prosperous.”

Almost everyone there was a Wealth believer.”

“Simultaneously, pirates disposed of stolen goods and often brought high value commodities.”

“I suspect that in Hun Tong’s city government treasury there are many kinds of good things that aren’t convenient to sell in normal times.”

“Count them up, perhaps the total can break through 70,000 gold.”

“7, 70,00 gold…” Fei She wisped out a breath, his face rueful, “I have seen this amount of coin on paper before, but this is the first time I have seen so many treasures arrayed before me.”

“If that is the case, then in the near, near future, we won’t have to worry about the Anti-investigation and Prophecy formation’s con, consumption.”

Currently, every time the survivors collectively used the Anti-investigation and Prophecy formation, it would cost over 100 gold, a steep price.

This kind of magical power would be depleted over time and needed to be replenished every so often.

As time went on and the survivors got stronger, this price would grow bigger.

The team’s finances were for a time consequently strapped for cash and at the edge of bankruptcy.

With these seafloor valuables, the survivors not just quenched themselves, they had been bestowed a lake of fresh water.

Zi Di’s tone was clear and cold, “Although there are many treasures, they are dead things.”

“We still lack a steady income; this financial situation is not healthy.”

“Good opportunities similar to Two Eyes Island are very hard to duplicate.”

“We still need to create steady profits.”

“At the same time, some of these valuables are very hard to price.”

“The blackmail letter didn’t get a response, and it could expose us. Using Wisteria Merchant Alliance channels have huge implicit risks, and I hesitate to use them.”

Fei She nodded, his eyes flashing with gratification.

If Zi Di didn’t raise the issue, he would have tactfully reminded her.

Now, it seemed that the former girl had really grown up and became quite reliable.

“Given enough time, she’ll surpass her father as president!” Fei She looked forward to this.

“Among the valuables, there are many gems, enough to invest and open some businesses.”

“However, it seem the Lord Com, Commander is dispirited.”

“If it is because of this in, incident that a rif, rift forms between him and you, it would be terrible.”

Fei She reminded her of another matter.

Zi Di’s hand stopped moving, then she faintly sighed, “I already forecasted it.”

“I know why his mood is foul.”

“For one, he takes pity on the weak freemen that were robbed and the poor.”

“Second, he is very disappointed in Hun Tong. During the battle, Hun Tong behaved quite treacherously and despicable, never revealing the Golden Flash until the pirates were just about to leave the island. He wanted to take everyone’s things and schemed against his own people’s riches.”

“Because according to imperial law, when he retakes them back from pirate hands, they are all his spoils of war.”

During the battle, everyone was focused on what was before them.

Now that things had calmed down, everyone smart enough would analyze it a bit to find out the motives and strengths of the various sides.

Zi Di continued, “Us salvaging the seafloor treasures was undoubtably the correct move.”

“If we didn’t, then it would let that contemptible Feudal Lord Hun Tong off easily.”

“Of course this was a contest between many parties.”

“We participated from beginning to end, and with a bit of luck, were able to fish up the biggest benefits and become the biggest unseen winner, this is inherently understood.”

“According to imperial law, these spoils of war are absolutely not ill gotten gains.”

“If it was possible, I would have suggested to the commander to privately gift some valuables to the plundered masses.”

“If that happened, his mood would be better.”

“But that couldn’t happen!”

“By doing that, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ characterization would be inconsistent and provoke suspicion. If those valuables were recognized, then it would make things worse.”

“In fact, Hun Tong would expose it. He must help his people, no matter his business talent, he must know that those people are his wealth!”

Fei She nodded, “To the best of my knowledge, the Justice Church’s creed advocates for only justice, not benevolence.”

“He is an outstanding lead, leader!”

“He fulfills his leadership du, duties, and obtaining these treasures will be very helpful to our fu, future.”

“But he was trapped in his own guilty conscience, self-blame, and duty, bearing it alone.”

Zi Di exposed a tender smile, “Sometimes, I think he is a little foolish.”

“But it is precisely because he pursues righteousness, is good and honest from the bottom of his heart and often introspects himself that we all trust him.”

“You know? Sometimes I think back and feel that I am very lucky.”

“Our composition is too complex, too many races, too many different beliefs.”

“He is the only one everyone can believe in.”

“You would even believe that this includes a half beastman, a half giant, a goblin, and even necromancers!”

“Even if Zong Ge advanced to the gold level, he wouldn’t be able to accomplish this to this degree.”

“He is special!”

As she said that, Zi Di’s eyes filled with love.

Seeing Zi Di like this, Fei She’ face blossomed with a silent smile.

He was an elder of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance and watched Zi Di grow up.

He personally witnessed the falling out between Zi Di and the old president over the unwilling marriage connection, and Zi Di leaving home.

He also saw that after Zi Di assumed the presidency, her eyes were filled with unswerving determination when she announced the Bai Zhen clan marriage relationship, she was prepared to sacrifice and utterly devoid of love.

Seeing Zi Di’s expression now, no matter who it was, everyone knew the girl loved the young man wholeheartedly.

Chapter 436: Justice Matins

Early morning.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was sailing across the ocean.

The ship’s bow cut through the waves and pure white sails swelled out.

In the distance, the boundless dark blue ocean surface and insipid blue sky reflected off each other, and under the caressing sea breeze, the sailors were carefree and relaxed.

The good weather brought more than a good mood.

To sailing on the sea, this signified that most important thing – a safe sail.

Two mercenaries stood at the side of a ship, one of them threw a plank.

The plank was fastened to a rope, and after it dropped into the sea, it was immediately swept towards the aft.

The rope was also tugged constantly by the plank.

This rope wasn’t ordinary, and there had been many knots tied into it at equidistant lengths of 23 feet and 7.5 inches from each other.

The other mercenary was holding an hourglass.

The hourglass would completely fill in 14 seconds.

14 seconds quickly passed, and the turned hourglass filled.

The mercenary counting the rope knots found that a bit over 11 knots had passed.

With this data, they immediately ran to the captain’s room and reported to the dragonman youngster, “Lord Commander, current speed is 11 knots.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, allowing them to retire.

He continued to immerse himself with his table.

The table had an ordinary ocean chart, compass, star chart, and a number of staff gauges and other maritime navigational equipment.

“A speed of 11 knots…” The dragonman youngster dipped a feather pen into some ink, then wrote a calculation on some parchment, until finally marking the fleet’s position on the ordinary ocean chart as well as its current course.

The dragonman youngster had been studying maritime navigation for some time already, now he could proficiently use all kinds of testing methods and tools, along with being able to do them faster and more accurately.

“Let’s check the correct answer.” The dragonman youngster took out another map.

It was a magic item – the moving ocean map

The dragonman youngster had purchased it from the Two Eyes Island Auction

The moving ocean map unfurled, and he saw where his fleet was, moving across the ocean at a snail’s pace.

The course, speed, and position were all a little bit off from his calculations.

“My maritime navigational skill should meet the standards now?” The dragonman youngster faintly smiled.

With this moving map, the fleet’s position, course, and other crucial data was obvious at a glance, making things very convenient.

Cang Xu’s compass was also very reliable; it could instantaneously indicate every direction and orientation.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish was the most convenient, it came with its own preinstalled moving map that could correspond with its three detection methods, it was the strongest detection unit the survivors had at present.

Magic and alchemy were the world’s most sophisticated technologies.

But it didn’t mean the dragonman youngster’s study of basic maritime navigation was worthless.

Because magic prohibition and other special environments would cause these alchemy items to fail. Some enemy methods could also interfere and mislead, effortlessly leading a fleet into ambush.

The Sheng Ming Empire navy provided moving ocean maps as standard equipment, but every time they did calculations, they still used basic maritime navigational skills simultaneously.

The dragonman youngster put away the moving ocean map and began to eat breakfast.

Breakfast was very sumptuous.

Some bread, cow milk, green vegetables, and so on.

Because this was their second day since departing the island.

The ship still had very fresh food.

As time passed, this situation became very nasty.

Fresh vegetables were very hard to store.

Magic and alchemy offered many food storage methods, the most popular of which was the Fertile Land Church’s specialty – the Fertile Flower Pot.

This flower pot’s soil was very fertile and could meet the nutrition requirement of growing vegetables for a long time.

Therefore, due to the empire’s grand strategy of invading the Wilderness Continent and vast maritime navigation development, the Fertile Land Church expanded greatly.

The second most popular was making an ice-cold environment, to prolong a food’s shelf life. Some cold air formations, ice formations, and some alchemy refrigerators and freezers all belonged to this category.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps planned to go to Ice Sculpture Island, that island had alchemy products like refrigerators and freezers, things positively evaluated by the market and bestsellers, becoming one of the snow elves’ staple exports.

But these two methods still couldn’t benefit the vast majority of sailors.

The sole reason was cost.

Ordinary ships spent money on cultivator sailors, navigational equipment, ship maintenance, along with other things and enough food to eat.

In terms of invested costs, allocating fertile flower pots, refrigerators, and so on meant that profits would be reduced.

Captains or fleet leaders wouldn’t choose to do this.

For this reason, the great majority of sailors were confronted with strange smelling water filled with algae while sailing, dried peas that’d make you vomit, and moist bread that would become moldy and maggoty. Sometimes, rats caught in cabin floors would give the sailors a rare delicacy.

It was worth mentioning that the ships the survivors controlled both overtly and covertly didn’t have these preservation methods.

Ordinary ships didn’t have such installations.

The Iron Lump and the White Bandage were mercenary corps ships that moved in waters around Two Eyes Island, not in the open ocean.

Although the Deep Sea Monster Fish was a gold level ship, it didn’t have any either.

The reason was very simple.

War Merchant used this ship to sail distant seas, and he brought his own fresh ingredients. As a legend level magician, he could create water from nothing and hunt locally to acquire the freshest ingredients, thus he needed no such things.

The dragonman youngster finished eating breakfast and left the captain’s room, traveling downstairs and to the prayer room on the deck’s second level.

This room hadn’t originally been for prayer, but since the dragonman youngster and Chi Lai were both Justice believers, they converted it to do so in the interim.

This was a blatant privilege.

They were the only two Justice God disciples.

Most sailors believed in an Ocean God, the Luck Goddess, the Wealth Goddess, and so on.

Because the Sheng Ming Empire lacked a traditional ocean god, many human sailors believed in the ocean gods of foreign races.

In this extraordinary world, choosing the correct god to worship was an important act that could increase the odds of survival, and shouldn’t be easily ignored.

Besides this prayer room, the fleet had no others.

If the sailors wanted to pray, they had no choice but to do it where they stood.

The sole prayer room was for the dragonman youngster’s and Chi Lai’s usage.

But no one grumbled against or opposed this situation.

This was the world of the strong.

The strong had privilege and was expected as a matter of course.

“Commander.” At the prayer room’s doorway stood Chi Lai; he had been waiting for some time.

The dragonman youngster smiled at him, “Sorry, measuring and calculating our course and position took a bit of time.”

Chi Lai stood perfectly straight and permeated with a soldier’s graceful bearing, “It is the duty of a subordinate to wait for their superior.”

The two pushed open the door and entered the prayer room.

Set up in the middle of the prayer room was a likeness of the Justice God.

The likeness was half as tall as a person and hung in a large niche in the wall.

The likeness portrayed the Justice God’s image, he was a square faced middle aged man, with tightly closed lips, stalwart features, and looked ahead.

He wore armor, held a sword in one hand, and a shield in the other.

They were the Armor of Justice, the Justice Square Shield, and the Justice Longsword, all divine artifacts of the Justice God.

In front of the wall niche was an offering table with candlesticks set up on it.

On the wall, the Justice Church’s sacred emblem was depicted.

Sunlight radiated through a porthole, illuminating the Justice God’s likeness, causing it to express a thread of formidable aura.

Regardless of whether it was the likeness or the sacred emblem, both were ordinary goods and unblessed, they had been made in haste.

“The circumstances were limiting; everything has to be simple.” The dragonman youngster sighed to Chi Lai, “We lack an outstanding alchemist.”

“We cannot truly convert a cabin on the magic ship into a prayer room and the gold cannon is damaged beyond repair.”

But Chi Lai was very satisfied, “Lord Commander, to have a place like this to come pray in is already a rare treat in all my years. I am perfectly content already!”

“Let us carry out our matins together.”

The dragonman youngster and Chi Lai slowly kneeled, both knees tapping onto the cold hard planks.

If this was the Wealth Church, believers would be kneeling on soft cushions, but the Justice Church advocated for a more arduous ambience.

The dragonman youngster hung his head and closed his eyes, mouthing in his thoughts, “Justice God, my God, you are a grand God, a God worthy of praise.”

“You uphold justice, do not submit to force, and are the one to right wrongs.”

“This lowly believer prays to you to make things clear, to ask for your direction, please tell me how the path of righteousness should be walked…”

The two both prayed silently.

When Chi Lai finished his own matins, he noticed that the dragonman youngster was still praying.

From his point of view, he could see the side of the dragonman youngster’s face, viewing his tightly locked brows and pious silently mouthing lips faintly opening and closing.

At this moment, Chi Lai’s heart welled with good feelings for the dragonman youngster.

Holding the same convictions caused Chi Lai to feel more intimate with the dragonman youngster.

Although he had joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as a slave, his own heart treated the dragonman youngster as the closest one to him.

He didn’t suspect the dragonman youngster was fraudulently pretending in order to gain his loyalty.

Because that was unnecessary.

In this world of widespread beliefs, believing in other gods for this purpose was very taboo.

The price was too high, unworthy of the act.

The dragonman youngster’s actions during the battle for Two Eyes Island was also witnessed by Chi Lai. He saw the dragonman youngster fight with his all against Er Gua Zi, stunning Chi Lai and causing him to concede from the bottom of his heart that the dragonman youngster was formidable.

Although Zong Ge’s battle qi had a gold luster, and was considered a quasi-gold level, in Chi Lais’ eyes, his battle strength couldn’t compare to the dragonman youngster’s.

The only thing that diminished the young man was that he was a dragonman.

The Sheng Ming Empire was the world’s greatest power, and the strongest, causing the glorious humans to universally look down on the other races.

Therefore, Hun Tong and Tan Qiu were more inclined to recruit the human Shi Qi and not Long Fu.

However, Chi Lai was born from the lowest rungs of society and had been framed by imperial bigwigs. His many torments had broken many of his obstinate prejudices.

This fact didn’t affect Chi Lai that much.

The dragonman youngster’s prayer finally finished.

The two slowly got up.

Chi Lai decided to ask, “Lord Commander, it seems your heart is at a loss. As a fellow believer, I want to give you some help, even if it won’t solve your problem or is insignificant.”

Chi Lai helping another at his own accord was very rare.

He had been sold into slavery and changed hands many times.

Although his time with the dragonman youngster was brief, Chi Lai had a sense of belonging and really wanted to help this youngster.

Chapter 437: Justice God Revelation

Seeing Chi Lai wanted to offer aid at his own accord, the dragonman youngster’s heart warmed.

His brows smoothed out as he sighed, “Truth be told, I am very disappointed.”

Chi Lai suddenly exposed a look of understanding, “Is it Hun Tong?”

It actually wasn’t just Hun Tong, the dragonman youngster was disappointed in himself too.

But the dragonman youngster wouldn’t reveal anything about the seafloor treasures, he nodded, “Indeed, part of it is about him.”

“Hun Tong is an imperial noble, he was the feudal Lord there, he agreed to uphold justice and differentiate the boundaries of evil.”

“However, he decided to trade with pirates, offering them a convenient place to dispose of their stolen goods. The pirates smoothly gained capital; they could use the arm themselves to carry out many more plunders.”

“Hun Tong is unquestionably their accomplice!”

Not only that, but during the battle, he kept the Golden Flash hidden until the final moment, because the valuables were leaving the island.”

“When we were departing the island, he deceived us, selling the Bandage Mercenary Corps’ flagship to us. Following that, he is certain to put his hands on those victims.”

“Although the Bandage Mercenary Corps fought mainly to save themselves, they were still our comrades in arms.”

“Hun Tong’s conduct really made me disappointed.”

As a matter of fact, the dragonman youngster also felt guilty.

One thought lingered in his heart – if we didn’t swipe the seafloor treasures, Hun Tong would be wealthy, would he let off the Bandage Mercenary Corps? What if Zi Di only took half the valuables? If half of it was left for Hun Tong, would it indirectly benefit the common poor people?

Accompanying this thought was his conscience’s denouncement, and a voice in the young man’s heart rang out, “Could leaving behind those valuables help the people? Too naïve! Hun Tong is treacherous and deceitful; he wouldn’t make concessions willingly. His battle strategy obviously wanted him to be the sole winner that ate up all the other parties!”

“The survivors urgently needed these seafloor valuables, activating the Anti-investigation and Prophecy formation not only took a lot of money, but the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was in dire financial straits, the blackmail letter didn’t get a reply, and the future is filled with unknowns and hardships, you have to grab hold of every opportunity to expand your chances of survival!”

“It’s like Mystifying Monster Island, like Snake Rat Island, those that survive can carry out righteousness. Each life has the basic right to keep on living, what do you find wrong with fighting for the chance to survive? The first and most righteous thing is wanting to live!”

During the Snake Rat naval battle, the dragonman youngster had come to this understanding, and not only convinced himself of it, but also his other companions, thereby crossing that crisis.

However, at this very moment, he used this theory again, but this time felt uneasy.

Because he unavoidably would think, “If it is righteous for me to abscond with the seafloor treasures for the sake of survival, what about Hun Tong?”

As a human knight looking in: Hun Tong had expelled the fishmen, expanded the empire’s territory, and indirectly serviced the empire’s grand strategy of attacking the beastmen’s continent.

In fact, the Sheng Ming Empire, these last few years, had issued many policies to encourage nobles to expand, especially to the islands between the Sheng Ming Empire and the Wilderness Continent.

Hun Tong had been motivated by such policy and went to seize Two Eyes Island.

However, the policy couldn’t help Hun Tong much more besides giving him a tax exemption.

When Hun Tong was in the opening stages of his conquest, he hadn’t developed Two Eyes Island that much.

Putting oneself into Hun Tong’s shoes, one could realize Hun Tong didn’t have an easy time!

Two Eyes Island was not a normal island, it didn’t have soil, couldn’t grow plants, didn’t have minerals, and half of it was on the seafloor for a period. Apart from this, Hun Tong was also being constantly harassed and attacked by the fishman clan, along with being devastated by pirate gangs large and small all around.

If Hun Tong ran Two Eyes Island like a conventional feudal Lord, he would have been destroyed. Not only would the fishmen retake their homeland, but pirates would also ransack everything. Even if that didn’t happen, under this double besiegement, Two Eyes Island would have been cut off from the rest of the world and gradually sink into oblivion.

“Under this environment, Hun Tong painstakingly built up his enterprise, changed his tactics, created a business atmosphere, and allowed pirates to peacefully dispose of their stolen goods, thereby reaching a tacit understanding and giving space for domestic and foreign traffic.”

“With safe shipping routes and pirate booty creating cheap merchant goods, Two Eyes Island successfully attracted merchant alliances large and small to set up there.”

“Hun Tong also withstood the fishman clan, forcibly revitalizing the backwater Two Eyes Island.”

If Hun Tong didn’t execute this survival strategy, he might have ended defeated and dispersed.”

“If calculated like this, would Hun Tong not be righteous too?”

“Then, is Hun Tong a righteous person?”

The dragonman youngster’s heart asked himself.

He shook his head to himself.

“Hun Tong and the pirates wallow in the mire of each other, he bullied our former battle companions (the Bandage Mercenary Corps), and schemed against his people’s property, clear injustices.”

“But isn’t it also possible that after Hun Tong carried out his plan, he would defend justice and become the most esteemed of imperial nobles? As long as circumstances allow, could he become a feudal Lord brimming with honor and virtue?”

“Were those past sufferings merely him enduring humiliation and a survival strategy so he could dispense righteousness even further, abandoning the minute justices in the present?”

The dragonman youngster felt he was standing at a fork in the road.

Next, he needed to pick which road to walk on.

One road among them was called “Hun Tong’s possible justice” – for the purpose of greater justice, one could sacrifice the present justices and submit to evil.

In a nutshell, to become a “better” person in the future, I can accept becoming a “small” villain for now.

“Can I?” The dragonman youngster asked himself.

“Will doing this soothe me more and increase the odds of everyone’s survival?”

The dragonman youngster thought of spies.

Some spies deep undercover, in order to attack an enemy’s leadership, would choose to sell out their side’s benefits and even attack their own comrades.

“However, can righteousness do that?”

“Can righteousness be viewed in a transactional manner, can it be weighed and calculated?”

“Can the undertaking of justice be so simply considered through merits and demerits?”

“Could I have the shadow of Hun Tong on me?”

The dragonman youngster deeply examined himself, the interrogation flogging his thoughts like a whip.

His heart was permeated with bewilderment.

He looked at Chi Lai, and asked his heart’s greatest doubt, “What is righteousness? How should the path of righteousness be walked? Where does it go?”

Upon hearing the young man’s question, Chi Lai couldn’t help but be dazzled.

“Similar, so similar!” He couldn’t help but sigh from the bottom of his heart.

It really resembled the past him!

Chi Lai deliberated for a bit, then instead of directly answering the dragonman youngster, he said, “Lord Commander, I cannot give you a straightforward answer to your question, because I am also a humble believer.”

“Here, I want to share with you the time I obtained God’s gaze and my experience with a divine spell, perhaps it can help you somewhat.”

The dragonman youngster was immediately interested.

During the battle, Chi Lai had used a divine spell to increase the gold cannon’s power.

The scene left a profound impression on everyone who had witnessed it on the battlefield.

Chi Lai slowly said, “I was born into a poor family, and because of my outstanding gifts, I was chosen after consideration to join the imperial navy.”

“A noble young master intended to plunder another’s inheritance, deceive a naïve girl, and planned to use a contemptible method to have that girl lose her virginity to him.”

“When me and my comrades unwittingly overheard this plot, even though I was entirely unacquainted with that girl, I decided to act.”

“Against my comrades’ advice, who warned me that the noble young master had a big background.”

“But I didn’t heed their advice, I destroyed the noble young master’s plot and let the girl realize how dangerous and insidious he was.”

“The noble young master became enraged over this perceived humiliation and exploited his clan’s influence to frame me.”

“When I went to prison, no one saved me, my former friends all chose to abandon me, they even engaged in perjury, hit me while I was down, and I too was at a loss.”

“Where was justice? Where could I find righteousness? I protected and carried out justice, why did I end up this way?”

“Was I abnormal, or was the world abnormal?”

“Was I wrong, or was justice wrong?”

“More ironic was when I heard the latest news on the girl. She decided to marry that noble young master at her own volition, of course it was due to the noble clan pressuring her, for the sake of completely wiping away any suspicions and taint the young master had.”

“The girl testified that I coveted her beauty and wanted to use a crafty plot to break the close relationship between her and her spouse.”

“I sank into despair, giving up on all hope in life and the world. I didn’t plan to remain in this world, and I decided to commit suicide that same evening, and decided to say one final prayer.”

“I thought it would be my last prayer in my life.”

“I thought it would be my most insincere prayer ever.”

“I asked my God a question, what really is justice? I asked why I had fallen into this state? I asked why justice didn’t exist in this world!”

“However…”
