“I heard a great voice, my God’s voice.”

“They said: Chi Lai, my child, I teach you this: justice exists at the point of a sword, at the mouth of a cannon, and in the hearts of people.”

The dragonman youngster shook, and with a mix of envy and admiration, asked, “God gave you a revelation?”

“That’s right, it was a divine revelation.” Chi Lai drew in a deep breath, “Despite only being a sentence, I comprehended three divine spells.”

“They are Justice Army Vanguard, Justice Cannon, and Mark Evil!”

“Justice Army Vanguard is a large scale buffing divine spell that can double a friendly side’s battle capabilities.”

“Justice Cannon can amplify a cannon barrel, and with the Justice God, I can cause its might to rise dramatically.”

“‘’‘’”

“The most core divine spell is Mark Evil. Using it can mark a target as “evil enemy” or “righteous ally”. After putting on this mark, our attacks will do more damage to those marked enemies. If our attacks hit friendly troops, it won’t affect them. Our healing magic will be greatly amplified and won’t affect enemies.”

Although they had only been together for a short time, Chi Lai decided to describe his three divine spells in detail to the dragonman youngster in their entirety.

Many times, interactions between people can be wonderful.

Although Chi Lai was not familiar with the dragonman youngster at first sight, in their brief few days, their relationship advanced by leaps and bounds.

“After receiving God’s revelation, I no longer wanted to commit suicide. Because my God almost pitied me, and I had obtained my answer.”

The dragonman youngster’s eyes were despondent, and his mouth murmured, repeating the divine revelation, “Justice…exists at the point of a sword, at the mouth of a cannon, and in the hearts of people.”

Then, the young man looked puzzled, “I cannot completely comprehend the meaning of this phrase.”

Chi Lai smiled, “I didn’t either, I still don’t.”

“It is very normal.”

“Because we are not gods, but people. Understanding the complete meaning of a divine revelation is not something we can achieve.”

“In front of gods, we should keep humble.”

“Therefore, despite the noble clan tormenting me, and selling me as a slave, I serenely accept it as fact.”

“Besides, as a slave, I strived to comprehend the meaning of the divine revelation and gradually had many insights and understandings.”

“But I don’t want to impart these experiences to you, fellow believer.”

Because my life experience is not yours.”

“I believe us encountering each other was arranged by God. God has bestowed upon me their revelation to not only me, but also to you.”

“From now on, go comprehend what that phrase means.”

“Use your own life experiences, as long as you scrupulously abide by the path of righteousness, you are sure to solve the doubts in your heart and obtain your answer!”

Chapter 438: Bloodline Investigation

The sun gradually rose.

The sea breeze slowly increased, the wind half bulging the sails.

Lan Zao was inspecting the deck.

His skin was tanned by the sun, his body was strong and muscular, and his eyes were sharp. He was fully equipped with bronze level equipment, amplifying his power and influence.

Lan Zao stopped walking, and shouted, “Who tied this knot?”

“It, it was me.” A mercenary stepped forth.

“The knot is wrong. Are you not a sailor?” Lan Zao asked.

The mercenary lowered his head, “Yes, that’s right, I haven’t gone out to see much.”

“That won’t do. Come, I will demonstrate, watch, then follow my instructions!” Lan Zao stooped down to untie the knot then demonstrated how to tie it in front of everyone.

He first pulled the rope straight, setting it level on the deck.

Then he bent the middle of the rope, then loped it to form the figures of 8s.

After that, he took the head of the rope and pulled it through the loops in the 8s.

Finally, he pulled the rope taut, evenly distributing the 8 shaped knots across the rope.

Lan Zao got up and gazed at the mercenary, “This is the consecutive figure eight knot, look at it, its knots are very big, and when you need to save yourself in emergencies, you can grasp it and not worry about slipping.”

“Do you understand?”

The mercenary nodded repeatedly.

Lan Zao threw the rope back to him, “Untie it, then retie it.”

“Yes, Lord.” The mercenary immediately began to do so.

Lan Zao was once a head sailor on the Hog’s Kiss, had come from a fishing village, and lived on the seas for many years, he knew these things by heart.

He inspected the deck, the ship cabins, and other places assiduously, meticulously, and wholeheartedly devoted his strength to this corps.

“Lord Lan Zao, I tied it, can you check if it’s right?” Soon after, the mercenary finished tying the consecutive figure eight knot.

But at that moment, a mercenary carrying an over ran over, “Lord Lan Zao, the Lord Commander has summoned you.”

“Oh, his Lordship is summoning me?” Lan Zao stepped over the rope, and in spite of the mercenary’s impatient expression, quickly strode towards the captain’s room.

The mercenaries behind him whispered apprehensively.

“I heard Boss Lan Zao is very appreciated by the Commander.”

“That’s just how it is.”

“Look at his animated attitude, who wouldn’t like such a subordinate?”

“Shut up, how can you evaluate Lord Lan Zao on a whim?”

Knock, knock, knock.

The captain’s door was tapped on.

“Come in.” The dragonman youngster permitted.

Lan Zao pushed the door open, came in, and respectfully saluted, “Commander, you summoned me?”

The dragonman youngster smiled, “There is something I need to say to you, come in.”

After saying that, he took out an acoustics box and put it on the desk.

It was a small alchemy device that could stop sound from spreading.

Not only that, but there were also many scouting puppets guarding inside and outside of the captain’s room.

Seeing the dragonman youngster doing this, Lan Zao immediately realized that this discussion would be quite secret, and he shut the door at once.

Lan Zao came in front of the desk.

The dragonman youngster indicated to him to sit down, then took out an alchemy item for one’s arm.

This was a tool Zi Di had built with the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s alchemy formations to draw blood.

Lan Zao didn’t sit down, and he smiled, “My Lord, I am fine standing.”

The dragonman youngster looked serious, “I permit you to sit, do sit down.”

“I want to use this to draw a bit of your blood.” The dragonman youngster presented the device, “Before the battle, our people were tricked, and their willpower was temporarily distorted, it was very awful.”

“Therefore, we plan to make some medicine that can guard against such methods.”

“One of the main materials for this magic medicine is the user’s blood.”

Lan Zao nodded, and without hesitation, he took the armband.

The armband slightly contracted, fastening itself to his forearm, then it pricked him with a syringe that precisely pricked Lan Zao’s vein to begin drawing his blood.

While this was happening, the dragonman youngster asked, “Lan Zao, If I am remembering correctly, you cultivate the Water Grass battle qi.”

Lan Zao’s body snapped up like a ramrod and he respectfully answered, “That’s right, your Lordship!”

The dragonman youngster pointed at the chair, “Sit back down.”

After Lan Zao sat back down, he continued, “Previously, you tried other battle qi, and some of them worked to an extent.”

“Yes.” Lan Zao snapped back up again to a standing position, “After we just left Mystifying Monster Island, you gave us many battle qi techniques that we could try cultivating. After I tried them all, there indeed were a few battle qi techniques that suited me.”

“But in the end, I didn’t change my battle qi technique, because their cultivation efficacy was about the same as my Water Grass battle qi technique.”

Lan Zao was already used to using Water Grass battle qi. His battle skills also paired with the Water Grass battle qi too.

More importantly, changing one’s battle qi was a very troublesome affair.

Since the earnings weren’t worth it, why would anyone invest the manpower, physical resources, time, and other costs to do an unnecessary change?

The dragonman youngster put his pen to paper, “Tell me what battle qi techniques work with you? In addition, please sit down, don’t be so tense.”

Lan Zao laughed, “My Lord, I am not tense.”

“I just like standing.”

“Just let me stand.”

“In front of you, I don’t have the qualifications to sit down. Standing will put me more at ease.”

The dragonman youngster couldn’t help but laugh, “Fine, then stand.”

Lan Zao listed off a few battle qi techniques, and seeing that the dragonman youngster was writing them down, he curiously and cautiously asked, “My Lord, do you want to set up a battle squad? I don’t know, do I have enough qualifications to change to another battle qi cultivation technique?”

Lan Zao mistook the dragonman youngster’s intentions.

The dragonman youngster muttered to himself, “At present, it is indeed still in consideration.”

Lan Zao instantly grew excited, “My Lord, we should really do that! The Quick Knife battle squad was trained by Zong Ge, now it has become that guy’s force. He is also starting to train those water pigmen. In the future, he will have two battle squads, while we will have none.”

“My Lord, what battle squads do I have the qualifications for?”

Lan Zao’s loyalty to the dragonman youngster was nearly overflowing.

This kind of loyalty was not one a soldier had to their superior, but a mix of zealotry and worship.

This was created by the special experiences on Mystifying Monster Island.

The dragonman youngster didn’t answer Lan Zao’s question, instead he inquired, “Lan Zao, think carefully about your relatives or ancestors, were there any cultivators among them? If there are, what battle qi technique did they cultivate? What was their bloodline?”

Lan Zao stared blankly, then forced a smile, “My Lord, my ancestors were all poor people. Only my generation had the qualifications to become a cultivator.”

When he said that, Lan Zao involuntarily thought of his youngster brother Huang Zao, and his face went dark.

The dragonman youngster questioned closely, “How much do you know of your bloodline, do you know what it specifically is?”

Lan Zao shook his head again, “My Lord, I don’t know, there are too many bloodlines that can cultivate Water Grass battle qi, and I never used a divine spell to measure it. Too expensive! To me, being able to become a cultivator and knowing that the Water Grass battle qi is the most suitable for me is enough.”

“My transcendent bloodline is very low, I can only be a bronze level, what use is measuring it?”

The dragonman youngster nodded, then wrote a few words on the paper, “So in that case, did any cultivators pop up in the coastal fishing village you came from?”

Lan Zao recalled for a while, then replied with uncertainty, “There seemed to be one, in my grandfather’s generation, there was a bronze level. He could hold his breath underwater for a whole hour and he had a robust physique. When the villagers spoke of him, they were all very envious.”

The dragonman youngster asked a few more questions, then after that was done, retrieved the blood drawing device from Lan Zao’s arm.

“How is the mercenary corps doing?” The young man asked before Lan Zao left.

Lan Zao swiftly said, “It is much stronger than before.”

“Although we lost many people on Two Eyes Island’s port, our remaining comrades are more unified and elite.”

The dragonman youngster encouraged, “I heard some people are supporting you, you’re doing pretty good.”

Lan Zao replied, “Everything is thanks to your Lordship!”

“With the money you lent me, I was able to buy a set of bronze level equipment. My strength increased and I was able to suppress them.”

The young man laughed and shook his head, “Not only that, during the battle, your heroic performance saved many people and killed many fishmen, continue working.”

“Yes, my Lord!” Lan Zao walked to the doorway, then hesitated before turning around and said shamefully, “My Lord, I know my strength is meager and my gifts inadequate.”

“I am a mere bronze.”

“But please make use of me in spite of that!”

“Even if the battle squad’s battle qi doesn’t fit me and will reduce my strength.”

“But I am loyal!”

“You can trust me one hundred percent!”

“I will use all of my strength to help you make a new battle squad.”

The dragonman youngster was very touched, he gazed into Lan Zao’s eyes and nodded vigorously, “Don’t worry, I will use you in crucial moments, I am not at all miserly!”

Then he thought an additional sentence, “You will have a greater purpose, and you will have greater talent, you won’t remain a mere bronze.”

After obtaining the dragonman youngster’s promise, Lan Zao immediately perked up with intense inspiration and joy.

Chapter 439: Zong Ge’s Military Training

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships traveled through the ocean.

On the flagship, the White Bandage, Men Shi stood on the bow, surveying the scene before him.

His face was filled with worry, and he really hoped that the Iron Lump would appear at any moment, despite knowing this was impossible.

Behind the White Bandage, there were pairs of mercenaries doing weapons training on the second medium sized ship.

Of course, these weapons were covered with thick cloth, to prevent accidental harm.

Bai Ya was one of them.

He wielded a cutlass and was battling an older mercenary.

The old mercenary’s martial moves were shrewd and ruthless, he was very experienced in battle, and not long after fighting, couldn’t help but grow angry and impatient.

But the old mercenary lacked stamina, and he could only risk showing an opening to bait Bai Ya into attacking.

As expected, Bai Ya was duped and the old mercenary knocked his cutlass off balance and attacked Bai Ya’s weak side.

But a moment later, Bai Ya, stimulated by this, forcefully twisted his waist, and in that critical moment, blocked the old mercenary’s weapon.

As the old mercenary was startled, Bai Ya seized the opportunity to counterattack.

Swords clashed with a dull echo.

After a few tough clashes, the old mercenary’s weapon was knocked away, forcing him to concede, “The old can’t compare to you youngsters.”

Bai Ya was covered in profuse sweat, and he was gasping for air.

His stamina suddenly slid away, and he wanted to collapse to the deck in fatigue.

But he still strived to persevere, and with the cutlass still in his hands, he didn’t forget to console his opponent, “Uncle, you are not old, and there are still many things I want to ask you about.”

The old mercenary heckled back, “Asking me for advice huh, you can’t do without good wine and meat, smelly youngster!”

Zong Ge was presiding over the training, and after seeing all of the outcomes of these spars, he had Bai Ya, and the others leave and swap places with another group of mercenaries to train.

Bai Ya came to Xu Ma’s side.

Xu Ma considered Bai Ya his little brother and looked after him.

He clapped Bai Ya’s shoulder and encouraged him, “Your moves weren’t half bad, but there is still room for improvement.”

“You fell into a trap, and you would have lost. However, your opponent suffered from age, allowing you to win by a narrow margin.”

Bai Ya also thought back to his spar, and he swiftly asked for guidance, “Leader, how should I discern my opponent’s moves?”

Xu Ma smiled, and indicated at once how Bai Ya should recognize them, imparting his battle experience to him.

Bai Ya diligently listened, and after going through many battlefields, he deeply realized that a single crucial moment could decide victory or defeat, it could save his life.

Xu Ma summed up his words with this, “Actually, saying too much is useless. Let your body remember, if it is just your brain that remembers, then during actual combat, you won’t be able to react usually.”

Bai Ya immediately understood, “Therefore, I need more actual combat practice!”

His eyes lit up, and he stared at the mercenaries training, focusing his attention on them.

His hands still imitated moves from time to time, not letting any chance to derive from actual combat experience slip by.

Xu Ma was a spectator, and when he saw Bai Ya doing this, he sighed to himself, as if feeling regret, “This youngster is very hard working, a pity he doesn’t have a bloodline and will never become a cultivator. He will only ever be an ordinary person.”

Zong Ge inwardly praised the mercenary corps’ performance but remained tranquil on the surface.

“The Snake Rat Island naval battle, the Two Eyes Island beast tide defense, and that chaotic warfare.”

“The baptism of these battles have morphed the remaining mercenaries greatly.”

The spiritual essence of the mercenary corps had made remarkable progress since it had been established, and one could see a trace of elite troops in them.

“My Lord.” Tripleblade came to Zong Ge’s side, whispering, “Just now, the commander found me to collect my blood, the reason was to make medicine that would protect my willpower. But…this subordinate is somewhat worried.”

Zong Ge didn’t take his eyes off the mercenaries as he faintly mouthed back, “What are you worried about?”

Tripleblade took a breath, then said, “The situation is quietly changing, your Lordship.”

“The Quick Knife battle squad had an exceptional performance.”

“Now, you are beginning to train the water pigmen, they will certainly become a battle squad in the future.”

“Chi Lai and Men Shi have joined the corps, and if we add in Qing Xin, their ranks will have three additional silver levels.”

“There is also the most crucial bit, your Lordship has taken the healing potion, your injury is fully recovered. Gold level is just around the corner for you.”

Zong Ge shook his head, “I can feel that there is still a step between me and the gold level.”

“Don’t be so recklessly suspicious, our commander…is not a vile character.”

Tripleblade quickly replied, “This subordinate is not worried about the commander. But Lao Zhong (Cang Xu) and Hong Teng (Zi Di), they are unusual (necromancers).”

“Even if they aren’t devious right now, what about in the future?”

“What if their willpower is again influenced by a divine spell?”

“The Devil Gold Coins incident was two days ago, your Lordship.”

“Most of all, the matter of their betrayal is not something the commander has explained to us yet.”

Zong Ge faintly creased his eyebrow, then finally looked at Tripleblade, not concealing his discontentment, “What do you want to tell me? They were under a divine spell, if it was us, or any one of those people over there, they too would turn traitor.”

“It was not their subjective intent, they simply had some bad luck, and during the battle, they had always displayed their full abilities and fought valiantly, there is no need to give an explanation.”

Sensing Zong Ge’s dissatisfaction, Tripleblade immediately kneeled on the deck and earnestly growled out, “My Lord, you have to be wary. Didn’t they also put the water pigmen on the White Bandage?”

Zong Ge was expressionless, “Indeed, because the White Bandage is a magic ship, its giant bandages can not only heal, but they can also bind and control enemies. It is the safest place to put the water pigmen.”

But Tripleblade implored once more, “My Lord, isn’t this action a deliberate display to show who the water pigmen belong to? We painstakingly trained them up until now, what should we do if the Backwater battle squad falls into their hands?”

Zong Ge sank into silence.

He was once an imperial general, he loved soldiers.

After he successfully trained soldiers, they would fall into another’s hand, and Zong Ge would naturally feel great pity and regret.

Zong Ge muttered to himself for a moment, and didn’t reply to Tripleblade question, instead he ordered, “Time to train, bring all of the water pigmen here.”

Tripleblade accepted the order and left.

Not a moment later, the flagship White Bandage in front began to slow down and keep pace with Zong Ge’s medium sized ship.

Tripleblade quickly climbed up the mast, then leapt onto the White Bandage.

Soon after, he had the water pigman slaves go to the side of the ship.

Under Tripleblade’s command, the water pigmen threw flying hook ropes from the White Bandage, latching onto the other ship.

Then, they put down ten long planks, building ten small temporary bridges between the two ships.

“Charge!” Tripleblade ordered, causing the water pigmen to step onto the planks and run from the White Bandage.

Most of the water pigmen performed terribly, some would have even fallen into the ocean if it weren’t for Tripleblade’s timely intervention.

Tripleblade brought up the rear as the last one, he stepped onto a plank, and after a running jump, he returned back to Zong Ge’s side to report, “My Lord, I have brought all of the water pigmen.”

Zong Ge nodded, then assessed, “The rope hooks and plank advance tactics are still not very proficient. Details on training those will be given to you after today’s drills.”

“Yes, your Lordship.” Tripleblade immediately answered.

Everyone was looking at the water pigmen.

The survivors had purchased these water pigmen from Hun Tong at a discount after the battle.

They all came from the same village, captured by human traffickers to be sold as slaves.

The slave traffickers had trained the well, and no matter what the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps ordered them to do, they would obediently do it.

But in Zong Ge’s eyes, that was not enough.

His next focus was to train these water pigmen into a battle squad.

A Backwater battle squad!

Last night, Zong Ge ordered Tripleblade to give the Backwater battle qi technique to these water pigmen.

The result delighted Zong Ge.

There were 16 water pigmen in all that were qualified to become a member of the Backwater battle squad.

This was a bit more than the Quick Knife battle squad he had set up previously.

Soon after, under Zong Ge’s direction, the water pigmen became the last group to train.

Halfway through the training, Zong Ge knew the score.

He inspected the people worth cultivating and also began to tentatively toughen a little of the water pigmen’s courage. But they were still a long way off from being allowed on the battlefield.

Many of them didn’t even have the most basic grasp of weaponry.

Thus, as they trained, the water pigmen behaved quite comically and moved very clumsily, often hitting themselves or missing opportunities to attack. The human mercenaries watching around gesticulated and wantonly jeered the oafish water pigmen.

When Zong Ge heard these jeers, he suddenly declared that after this training was finished, there would be solo training.

The rules for solo training was that Zong Ge would pick whoever he wanted, ordinary against ordinary, bronze versus bronze, iron fighting against iron, a one on one battle.

“You, and you.” Zong Ge seemed to randomly pick Bai Ya, then picked a water pigman.

When Bai Ya saw Zong Ge looking at him, he suddenly had a premonition.

As expected, he was chosen for solo training.

“This is the benefit of being ‘one of us’.” Bai Ya inspired himself, this was more of actual combat experience he wanted to go through.

A large circular space on deck was cleared, and the water pigmen and mercenaries circled around.

Someone shouted something, and soon after the crowd began to create a clamor.

The human mercenaries were naturally on Bai Ya’s side, while the water pigmen of course supported their fellow.

In a split second, an antagonistic ambience settled in.

Bai Ya turned his wrist as he slowly approached his opponent.

He didn’t approach in a straight line, rather he moved from side to side.

The water pigman was also holding a cutlass, constantly turning around to keep Bai Ya in front of him.

When Bai Ya had been observing from the sidelines, he also felt that the water pigmen were quite dull, however, when it came time to fight one alone, he felt the pressure his opponent gave off.

An adult water pigman was generally 2 meters and was very burly.

There was an obvious disparity between Bai Ya’s and the water pigman’s physique.

“Hit him now, stop wasting time.”

“Attack his leg.”

“Don’t be afraid, just do it!”

Bai Ya was in the end, young, and gradually grew impatient under the crowd’s rowdy jeering.

He gradually couldn’t bear it and launched an exploratory attack.

His opponent had been prepared and used his cutlass to parry.

When the two cutlasses collided, Bai Ya immediately changed color.

His cutlass nearly slipped away, causing him to think in alarm, “What strange strength is this?!”

Chapter 440: Gang Lie and Sweet Talk

Bai Ya’s probing attack was thwarted, and he quickly took a few steps back to create distance.

The human mercenaries quieted down by half.

Although everyone knew the water pigman was physically strong, after they all saw Bai Ya clash with the water pigman, they noticed what the disparity really looked like.

“Hei Ya, don’t lose your head, that guy barely touched a blade, his technique is far below yours.” Xu Ma suddenly shouted.

When that yell transmitted into Bai Ya’s ears, it calmed his fickle heart by a lot.

His gaze became sharp as he attacked once again.

Like Xu Ma said, the water pigman’s weapon proficiency was much worse than Bai Ya’s.

For a moment, the water pigman blocked left and right as Bai Ya did his best to look for an opening.

The water pigman’s strength made it difficult for Bai Ya to contest, while Bai Ya’s skill and agility left the water pigman at a loss.

The stamina of both quickly declined, and Bai Ya was the first to show signs, forcing him to slow his attacks.

The water pigman felt the pressure on him lessen, and he gradually adapted to the fight.

The interest of the spectators around them gradually waned as time went on.

Both the water pigman and Bai Ya had firm fighting spirits, neither willing to concede.

This fight went on for a surprisingly long time!

“Draw.” Finally, it was Zong Ge who stopped the fight.

The scene was too difficult to watch.

Both sides were so exhausted that they were half kneeling on the deck and had to use their full strength to heave their cutlasses.

Bai Ya was carried off by the humans.

“Youngster, you didn’t lose face for humanity.”

“Not too bad, at least you didn’t lose.”

“Why wasn’t I picked, I would have beaten up that pig head!”

The mercenaries had a good attitude towards Bai Ya.

Although Bai Ya didn’t beat his opponent, everyone saw his tenacity to never admit defeat.

His stamina was gone, yet Bai Ya still persevered.

Of course, this was also because the water pigman was too unskilled and couldn’t seize any openings.

“Repulsive!” Bai Ya clenched his fists, his mood terrible.

When he was carried to Xu Ma’s side, he asked, “Leader, is that a beastman?”

“They are stronger, have more endurance, and better defenses than us humans.”

“My cutlass hit his body, and would leave a scratch at most, they didn’t even seem to feel pain from it.”

Xu Ma sneered, “Of course there are racial differences between humans and beastmen. But don’t you think beastmen are too strong.”

“In this fight, you finally experience a beastman’s advantages.”

“But their weaknesses?”

“It is their intellect, they can’t compare to our learning capabilities, and their magic talents are also quite weak.”

“Otherwise, how could our empire attack the beastmen’s Wilderness Continent?”

“Most crucially, your tactics were wrong, therefore your fight was very challenging.”

“If I took the field and gave you a demonstration, then you would understand.”

When Zong Ge heard Xu Ma’s words, he pointed his finger at him, “Since you said that, then you’ll be going up next.”

Xu Ma stared blankly, then cheered to raise his spirits, “Hehe, then observe!”

Zong Ge looked back at the water pigmen, “Gang Lie, you are the strongest bronze level, take the field!”

“Roar, roar.” The bronze level water pigman Gang Lie roared twice and glared at Xu Ma, then strode forth to the middle of the deck.

The surrounding crowd exploded with excitement again.

Bai Ya sat on the deck, leaning against the side of the ship, and stared without blinking for an instant.

Xu Ma had the Dark Earth Spider bloodline; thus, he had long legs and feet, his figure was very tall, but he was always stooped and bowed and scraped to many people, making people feel he wasn’t that tall.

But when confronting the even taller and sturdy water pigman, Xu Ma straightened his back for the first time.

The mercenaries noticed this startling difference, as it turns out, Xu Ma was really tall.

Xu Ma twitched his finger at the water pigman, his provocation very intense.

The water pigman Gang Lie had an obvious irascible temperament, and he immediately charged.

He brandished two hammers and swung them ruthlessly towards Xu Ma.

Xu Ma stood in place, motionless, then when the hammers were about to hit him, he suddenly vanished.

Battle skill – Backstab.

A moment later, Xu Ma appeared behind the water pigman, his dagger ferociously stabbing Gang Lie’s back.

Because the dagger was wrapped in thick cloth, it only left a bruise on Gang Lie’s naked back.

Gang Lie howled and turned around suddenly, sweeping his hammers around in an arc towards Xu Ma.

Xu Ma shortened his height, abruptly going from 1.9 meters to 0.5 meters.

In Gang Lie’s field of view, Xu Ma seemed to disappear.

He unconsciously thought Xu Ma was repeating the same trick again, dodging behind him, and simply increased his power to seize the opportunity to use his own battle skill – Whirling Body Hammer.

Using his own body as an axle, both iron hammers in his hands whirled in a circle.

The iron hammers whistled through the air, raising a gale, its might frightening the people.

But it didn’t hit Xu Ma.

Xu Ma just squatted in place, easily dodging Gang Lie’s attack.

His line of sight concentrated on Gang Lie’s buttocks.

A moment later, his mouth curled up, exposing a sinister and ruthless smile.

Battle skill – Eye Jab!

Battle qi flowed, accelerating Xu Ma’s hand, speeding his cloth wrapped dagger into an ‘eye hole’ on the lower part of the water pigman’s body.

“Oo…ah – oh!”

Within a few seconds, Gan Lie’s modulated roar actually went from anger to pain and misery, in a string of rolling emotions.

He seemed to be electrocuted, causing him to run forward as his offensive posture abruptly fell apart and his hammers fell away as both hands subconsciously covered his buttocks.

Xu Ma took out the dagger and slowly stood up, his eyes following the water pigman Gang Lie as they “madly and violently” ran until they crashed over the side of the ship with a bang and fell into the ocean.

“I won.” Xu Ma raised his right arm, his right hand still holding that dagger.

The wrapped cloth dagger was bright red.

The humans didn’t cheer, for they all felt their hearts chill.

Bai Ya was dumbstruck as he thought, “Boss, I can’t copy your attack method!”

The water pigmen seemed to be in a nightmare that they were waking up from.

They wailed loudly, and two or three of the water pigmen rushed to jump into the ocean to save Gang Lie, while the rest of them pointed at Xu Ma and loudly cursed him, their angry eyes were bloodshot, their wrath demanded murder!

“Xu Ma is victorious!” Zong Ge loudly declared while simultaneously glaring at the water pigmen for a moment, “On the battlefield, it goes without saying anything can be used, life and death is nothing to being despicable and shameless!”

“Does anyone want to teach them a lesson?”

“This will be your final chance for today.”

An iron level pigman let out a torrent of abuse and walked onto the deck.

Xu Ma rushed to withdraw.

The water pigman was so angry that it made Xu Ma feel that if he was a bit slower, that iron level water pigman would beat him up.

Zong Ge looked at Tripleblade, “This time, you’re up.”

Tripleblade faintly stared at him, then understood somewhat, and he walked to the middle of the deck.

The water pigman turned around to see Tripleblade, and after getting past his astonishment, he turned serious.

He looked at Zong Ge.

It was very obvious that Tripleblade was Zong Ge’s competent assistant.

Zong Ge said to the water pigman, “If you can defeat him, you can supplant his position!”

The water pigman’s eyes shined, and with a low growl, his battle intent rose dramatically.

Tripleblade coldly snorted, took out a dagger, and held it in his hand.

·

Battle skill – Extreme Speed, stage three.

Battle skill – Lightning Attack!

Tripleblade charged forward, quickly resembling a line of shadow.

The shadow easily passed by the water pigman.

The water pigman rigidly and sluggishly stood in place, his eyes wide open, then a second later, he fell to the ground with a loud bang, his big face planting itself into the deck.

The shadow dissipated, and Tripleblade stood behind the water pigman.

As he lightly sheathed his dagger, the crowd finally reacted.

It was an uproar.

“So strong!”

“He felled them in just one attack.”

“It wasn’t just one move, moron, he used at least two battle skills simultaneously.”

“His dagger isn’t ordinary either, it is an iron level weapon!”

Everyone discussed spiritedly.

Zong Ge nodded with satisfaction, and Tripleblade respectfully returned to Zong Ge’s side.

Tripleblade was an iron level, and a former mercenary commander. He was naturally proficient with his battle skills and using two of them simultaneously was as effortless as moving his fingers.

More importantly was that he improved his dagger.

The survivors had granted capital to improve their comrades’ equipment.

Bai Ya and Lan Zao had benefited from this, and Tripleblade too had gotten an iron level dagger – Sweet Talk.

The dagger’s attacks also caused paralysis.

It was worth mentioning that the paralysis did not take effect through cutting or touching, but through sound.

As long as the sound hit the target, it could paralyze them.

So, despite the dagger being wrapped in cloth, after it hit the water pigman, its friction left a deep mark on their body.

The iron level pigman was consequently struck by this, and was paralyzed all over, causing him to fall and his face to smash into the deck, unable to stand back up.

After seeing their strongest companion so effortlessly knocked down by the short human, the water pigmen became grave and heavy, their gazes exposing ever more dread.

Of course, there was still anger and indignation.

After all, Tripleblade held the superiority in equipment.

But the water pigmen didn’t dare to be angry or speak, they were mere slaves, and in addition, according to Zong Ge’s words from before, everything can be used on the battlefield, and using good equipment was a matter of course and beyond criticism.

Zong Ge looked at the water pigmen, “Today’s solo training is merely the beginning.”

“I treat all of my subordinates equally.”

“The Quick Knife battle squad is an example right before your eyes.”

“I will give you better equipment, teach you a more outstanding battle qi technique, and train you to fight.”

“What I just said is similarly valid, whoever can publicly defeat another in a one on one, they can supersede their position.”

Zong Ge put a finger on Tripleblade.

Tripleblade sighed to himself, but outwardly he raised his head, his face callous and disdainful.

The water pigmen couldn’t help but growl, their fighting spirit greatly stimulated, and at the same time, they had been taught a bitter lesson that destroyed the pride many of them held deep within their hearts.

Zong Ge’s hidden goal had been attained.

He continued to preside over military training, and Tripleblade was his assistant.

Apart from this, Chi Lai also participated.

As a former navy gunner, Chi Lai was responsible for drilling and teaching mercenaries how to use cannons.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ 11 ships were all almost completely packed with cannons.

After the battle for Two Eyes Island, there were too many dead, and there were both ships and cannons in surplus.

But Chi Lai was not satisfied and told the dragonman youngster bluntly that they needed to improve their cannons.

Because most of them were ordinary cannons.

Chi Lai also mentioned the gold level cannon, asking the dragonman youngster to think of a way to repair it as soon as possible.

In the days following their departure from the island, he found the time every day to examine the damaged gold cannon, like someone visiting their sick sweetheart.

Chapter 441: Striving for Self-Improvement is the Righteous Path

Darkness.

A deep darkness.

The dragonman youngster’s consciousness lay between half conscious and awake.

“Who am I?”

“What is this place?”

Soon after, a memory came to mind.

“I am an imperial knight, I pretended to be Zhen Jin…nowadays I am the commander of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, under the alias Long Fu!”

“I…why am I in this kind of situation again?”

The dragonman youngster thought back.

Once, on Mystifying Monster Island, he had sunk into this darkness, this was not the first time.

He didn’t expect that after he left the island, this dark dreamland would still disturb him.

The boundless darkness enveloped him; it was deathly silent.

The young man tried to walk through the darkness.

He felt his body move, but no matter where he went, his surroundings were still deeply dark, nothing changed whatsoever.

The now awake young man gradually couldn’t endure it, for the silent darkness was like a gigantic cage that trapped him.

“I want to leave!”

“But how can I leave?”

The young man knelt and tried to pray to the Justice God.

“My God, grand Justice God.”

“You are my lighthouse, my bright star.”

“Please have mercy on me, help me throw off this dark nightmare, please show me the road of righteousness, so I can carry out your path…”

God did not respond.

Regardless of what was prayed, only the deathly silent darkness greeted the young man.

The young man grew increasingly impatient and frustrated, but at that moment, an enormous sound came from the outside world.

A moment later, the dragonman youngster opened his eyes to reality.

He noticed he had fallen out of his bed, and as a matter of fact, the entire White Bandage was violently swaying.

“Enemy attack, enemy attack!” Mercenaries ran across the deck as their shouts reached the young man’s ears.

“What’s going on?” The dragonman youngster quickly ran out of the captain’s room and saw there were five or six gigantic tentacles twisting around the White Bandage.

“It is a silver level ocean beast – a luminous octopus.” The dragonman youngster’s heart moved as he identified it.

During the voyage, besides fulfilling his duty as commander, he also read things, including works on ocean magic beasts.

The dragonman youngster saw the tentacles were studded with yellow fluorescent dots. The large swath of dots were at times dark, and at other times bright, as if they were breathing.

It was the notable trait of a luminous octopus.

“Was its attack the thing that roused me from my nightmare?” The dragonman youngster’s eyes flashed with deep thought.

Soon after, he felt baffled, “Why didn’t Zi Di warn us ahead of time?”

The Deep Sea Monster Fish had strong detection capabilities, and based on common sense, Zi Di should have noticed and should have had the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet avoid the latter beforehand.

In fact, during the last two days, the survivors had smoothly evaded many magic beasts because the Deep Sea Monster Fish had warned them.

At this time, Zong Ge’s medium sized ship was drawing close.

He was on the side of that ship, both commanding the fight and using an alchemy device to whisper an explanation to the dragonman youngster, “We noticed this magic beast was alone, I arranged for it to attack at night, using it as training.”

“We know you fell asleep, and didn’t want to wake you up.”

“Everything is under our control.”

“So that’s how it is.” The dragonman youngster nodded his approval.

Normally, when a leader found their subordinate covering something up, they were bound to be greatly vexed and deeply feel their power being threatened.

But the dragonman youngster didn’t, instead he felt this was normal.

Because their team was very special.

Their cultivated tacit understanding and organization’s founding crucially happened on Mystifying Monster Island.

It was a place with magical prohibition.

Even if one was a cultivator, they could only rely on their bodies and couldn’t use battle qi, magic, or other transcendent powers.

The survivors were extremely cautious, dealt with countless perils around them, and struggled at the edge of life and death.

Under such imminent crisis, the people had to decisively act, fight for a chance of survival, and if they had to get their leader’s permission to act, then they would have died tragically then and there.

In many desperate junctions, the survivors were forced to habitually trust their comrades.

Many times, they fought until they were spent and had to depend on their comrades’ protection while they took turns sleeping.

This mutual understanding and habits were retained by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The luminous octopus looked vicious and frightening, but actually, it was rather stupid and lacked explosive racial magic.

Its skin was rough and flesh thick, it made an excellent fleshy target.

Zong Ge first dispatched the Quick Knife battle squad.

The Quick Knife battle squad’s leader was still Kuai Tui.

During the battle for Two Eyes Island, his kidney had been pierced, but he had recovered completely from that now.

Whether it was Zi Di’s allocated healing medicine or the White Bandage’s bandages, both could keep him healthy and active.

The dogmen had new equipment now – ring swords.

These were all bronze level equipment, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps acquired them from Two Eyes Island’s auction.

Some dogmen hacked up octopus tentacles with their ring swords, while others were hurling and catching other tentacles.

They weren’t worried about missing, because the octopus tentacles were enormous, and at such a close distance, they could hit them with their eyes closed.

With the ring swords, the Quick Knife battle squad’s attack distinctly rose by a lot.

However, the octopus tentacles were still very strong and powerful, and despite the many blades creating many cuts, they were light injuries to the luminous octopus.

The luminous octopus was a silver level, and because of racial discrepancies, its vitality, stamina, recovery, and defense far exceeded a silver level human fighter’s.

The luminous octopus didn’t pay attention to the Quick Knife squad, rather it was incessantly entangled with the White Bandage.

Although the White Bandage was only a bronze level alchemy ship, it didn’t have a problem vying with the luminous octopus for a short time.

Taking advantage of this golden opportunity, Zong Ge gave several orders and had mercenaries take turns fighting it at close quarters.

Tripleblade, Bai Ya, Lan Zao, and so on all had a chance to experience real combat and gain a profound understanding of how to fight a giant beast on the ocean.

The luminous octopus being tormented so caused its vicious nature to erupt.

But at this time, Chi Lai and Men Shi attacked at the same time.

When the luminous octopus encountered opponents on the same level as it, several of its tentacles were broken off and its blood flowed without end.

Due to these serious injuries, it wanted to flee, but then the White Bandage ejected a great amount of bandages that wrapped around the luminous octopus’ tentacles, trapping it.

The luminous octopus mournfully cried, as if saying: let me go, release me, please…

But the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps remained unmoved and were still quite excited.

“It’s dying, it’ll die!”

“Quick, just a bit more, kill it!”

“I want to eat octopus meatballs!!”

“No, octopus sashimi is the most delicious.”

Tripleblade flew between the mast and octopus tentacles, the dagger in his hand “Sweet Talk”, paralyzed every tentacle that it hit, causing the octopus to lose feeling in them.

Bai Ya seized this chance to suddenly rush out and attack a bleeding wound with his cutlass, it drove itself deeply into it, casing a large splurge of blood to shoot into Bai Ya.

The big guy didn’t dare to get too close to the battle, that only happened when he was deeply provoked into a dreadful berserk state. But after experiencing the baptism of war, he also had remarkable progress. He summoned all of his courage to grab a number of casks, each throw of one accompanied with a shout that amplified his prestige.

The two alchemy puppets disguised as Cang Xu and Zi Di extended their fingers and activated their fire path rings, casting magic publicly.

The puppets used the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell along with the Deceptive Disguise spell, not a single mercenary noticed any flaws.

Even Mu Ban joined in the fun, using his crossbow to riddle an octopus tenacle with arrows.

“Whose arrow is this!”

“Aiya, don’t shoot, you nearly hit my arm.”

Mu Ban had poor accuracy, but he craved doing this, so he quickly changed positions and fired another arrow.

Another crossbow arrow flew through the air and stuck itself into the deck.

The water pigman Gang Lie was in the middle of wrestling a tentacle, venting his anger accumulated during the day.

The tentacle quivered, knocking him back.

Gang Lie fell on his butt, and when he hit the deck, his eyes suddenly widened big as he trembled from head to toe.

A kind fellow water pigman grabbed his arm.

“Don’t…” Gang Lie had a premonition of trouble.

But the fellow water pigman was paying more attention to the octopus tentacle and they lifted him up!

“Ah ah ah ah!” As Gang Lie stood up, his hands balled into fists, his face twisted, and his mouth roared.

For a moment, everyone around Gang Lie looked at his fearsome display.

Some pain had to be kept to oneself!

Due to the many stares, Gang Lie had to tenaciously bite his jaw and continue attacking.

Luckily, the dimness of night masked the blood and wound on his buttocks.

The mercenaries’ morale grew more exuberant, until finally, the luminous octopus floated dead on the ocean, leading the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet to burst out in vigorous cheers.

The mercenaries high fived and hugged each other.

This even included the last to enter the fray, the water pigmen, they too relinquished their hatred of that day, and for a brief moment, merged with the entire group. They were soaked completely in blood, laughing and smirking, living up the joy of victory.

Zong Ge nodded to himself when he saw this spectacle.

His good intentions had shown fruit.

Like on Mystifying Monster Island, when the survivors cultivated unity and trust with each other, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could use external pressure to get closer together.

It was not the best way to train an army.

But when used here, it was just right.

That day’s solo training had stimulated the water pigmen’s battle intent and competitiveness but deepened the barriers between the human and the water pigmen.

Zong Ge understood this deeply.

It just so happened that during the night, a dim-witted luminous octopus wanted to hunt and Zong Ge forcefully grabbed hold of this golden opportunity.

From start to finish, he never acted.

It was just as he reported to the dragonman youngster – everything was under control.

The dragonman youngster also soaked in the joy of victory.

“Unwittingly, our strength has expanded by a step.”

“I didn’t need to act personally at all; they were able to deal with this kind of opponent.”

“Perhaps…this is my God’s revelation?”

The dragonman youngster’s gaze trembled.

He recalled the dark nightmare from before, then thought of the divine revelation Chi Lai had told him: justice exists at the point of a sword, at the mouth of a cannon, and in the hearts of people.

“Swords and cannons are symbols of strength.”

“Without strength, how can one ensure righteousness and carry out justice?”

“In short, striving to strengthen ourselves absolutely isn’t wrong!”

“That’s right, striving for self-improvement is the righteous path!”

For a split second, the young man felt he understood at last a piece of the divine revelation’s hidden meaning.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Zi Di looked at everyone in a magic image, then finally focused her worried gaze on her beloved.

Seeing the dragonman youngster had both gained something and gotten over another thing, she was also happy for him.

The ghost girl could feel the young man was now motivated and had direction again.

Most of her worry dispelled, and she closed the magic image and returned to the alchemy cabin.

The alchemy cabin’s formation had been changed.

In the formation, there was a bright and dazzling silver colored mist.

In the bright mist, the survivors’ blood condensed into floating balls that were a distance from each other.

“The preceding processing has been finished; next I will examine what everyone’s bloodline is exactly.” Zi Di looked forward to this as she began to operate the formation.

Chapter 442: Magic Medicine Blood Measurement (1)

There has always been a lack of efficient and accurate bloodline testing methods.

But there weren’t none.

The simplest method was visual assessment.

The principle wasn’t complicated, different bloodlines generally had different external characteristics.

For example, an elf bloodline usually gives people long pointed ears and a pretty appearance.

A dwarf bloodline often made a person short, increased their muscles, and gave them a preference for alcohol.

People with the Sacred Temple bloodline usually had a pair of beautiful silver white eyes. Those with a high bloodline concentration even had a pair of snow white wings sprouting from their backs.

Visual assessment was simple to understand but had too many flaws.

Many kinds of bloodlines didn’t have any visible external traits, or even any external traits at all, making it easy to bring about misjudgments.

Bloodline concentration was also a factor that made the accuracy of visual assessment shaky.

A high bloodline concentration had distinct external characteristics.

Furthermore, bloodline characteristics were liable to be concealed deliberately.

Historically, there was an elven fourth prince who obviously had formidable talent, however he pretended to be weak and concealed his bloodline traits. While the enemy was slacking over, he covertly cultivated until he was able to take the title of emperor in one stroke.

In addition, different bloodlines had similar traits, this was also liable to cause misjudgments.

Visual assessment was not reliable, but with battle qi techniques, meditation methods, and other bloodline measurement methods, the degree of accuracy could become much higher.

If a lifeform cultivated some battle qi technique or meditation method to special success, they usually could deduce their bloodline.

However, in practice, this method’s efficiency was just passable.

First, a lifeform usually didn’t have just one or two cultivation methods that was suitable for them to cultivate.

Sometimes, because of circumstances, limited choices, or other external reasons, a cultivator wouldn’t be able to test which cultivation method suits them the best.

This verification method needed too much time dedicated to cultivation to get a result.

Every organization and power consciously circulated their own battle techniques and meditation methods. (This was also why War Merchant could so easily collect so many battle qi techniques and meditation methods.)

The most accurate methods were controlled by the Life God Church.

This church had special divine spells such as Bloodline Quality Detection spell, Bloodline Type Detection spell, among others.

These detection results were very precise.

There will be errors only occasionally.

The shortcoming of this method was that it was too costly!

This level of divine spell could not be used by ordinary clergymen.

Divine spells consumed divine power, and whenever a church priest used a divine spell, they needed to be compensated.

Sometimes, even with loads of money, one still can’t hire a cleric.

The most classical example was pirates.

The Pirate Throne had been vacant for a long time, and without a Pirate God, pirates didn’t have ship priests following them, and without clergy support during naval battles, they lacked timely healing.

A cleric’s power came from their beliefs, and at the same time, their beliefs and doctrine was also the framework of their conduct.

Visual assessment, cultivation results, and divine spell testing, these were the three mainstream detection methods of the present age.

Of them, the second was the most widely used.

Because its cost and accuracy rate was between the other two methods, and in actual testing, they were the most cost efficient.

The method Zi Di was using now was medicine testing.

When different bloodlines interacted with different medicine, they would react differently. For example, when fire attributed inclined bloodlines encountered a water path magic medicine, they would often react violently.

The detection principle was quite simple, but practical application was always complex.

Take a fire attributed bloodline, some unconventional bloodlines would also react when encountering water path magic medicine. If viewed on the basis of this singular test result, one’s judgement would veer greatly in the wrong direction.

Anyways, Zi Di was not only using medicine testing, but she had also done a great deal of investigation in advance.

Such as what cultivation results the survivors had.

She also inquired into their families, ancestors, and if there were any cultivators in them, if there were, the bloodline of later generations was usually related to them.

She also investigated the survivors’ respective living environment. Bloodlines also had a distribution area.

It seemed bloodline testing was an extremely challenging undertaking with a tremendous workload, but in actuality its scope was small.

Zi Di officially began the medicine testing.

She operated the alchemy formation and produced prepared medicine.

The medicine quickly spread through the formation, turning the silver colored mist green.

Ten seconds later, four balls of blood reacted.

Tripleblade’s blood became a frozen kernel that resembled a small grape.

Mu Ban’s blood was dyed green.

Zi Di’s blood emitted a red light.

Cang Xu’s blood quickly evaporated, turning the surrounding light mist a dark green and his blood became an ash gray liquid.

Zi Di didn’t need to take notes, the tower spirit was recording the scene.

Zi Di’s eyes narrowed as she analyzed it to herself.

The testing medicine she just added was a wood attribute testing medicine, full of life energy.

The four balls of blood that reacted had a bloodline partial towards the wood attribute.

Of course, their respective blood changes also represented the enormous difference between their inclinations.

“My blood was specially extracted from my corporeal body; it is my life information while I was living. I indeed have talent in medicine refinement, I simply found out when I tried during magic college.”

“There are too many bloodlines good at refining medicine, but I am also talented in the barbarian race’s herbal medicines too. Calculating this in, my bloodline containing the wood attribute is very logical.”

“As for the red light, could it be that it also contains a fire attribute?” Zi Di speculated inwardly.

After she turned into a ghost, her corporeal body had been frozen and kept safe.

Thus, extracting a blood sample for this test was quite easy.

“Cang Xu and I are in different situations, his body has undergone necromantic transformation and become a living dead person. His blood was changed as a result and has much more negative energy mixed into it.”

“The ball of ash gray liquid remaining is blood full of negative energy and the portion that evaporated previously dissolved into the wood attribute medicine, this is proof his transcendent bloodline is partial towards the wood attribute.”

The part of Cang Xu’s blood brimming with negative energy still hung in the air, but it repelled the green mist, creating a blank space where there was no mist, they were incompatible and entirely different.

“Mu Ban having a wood attribute bloodline is also rational. He is a carpenter, and is good at building wood products, this is one of the incarnations of his talent. Hu Ban has a transcendent bloodline, but its concentration is too low and cannot reach even the lowest criterion. Therefore, he has always been an ordinary person.”

“As for Tripleblade, I didn’t expect he would also have a wood attribute. Based on his background investigation, he cannot trace his ancestry, for he has been drifting around since childhood.”

Zi Di operated the alchemy formation.

Very quickly, the green mist was eliminated and replaced with the original silver colored mist.

Zi Di moved to remove Cang Xu’s ash-colored blood and switched in new blood.

She waited for a few minutes until Mu Ban’s, Tripleblade’s, and her own blood reverted to their original state, then continued to add in testing reagents.

This time, Zi Di used a water attribute testing dose.

Soon after, the silver mist turned light blue.

Zi Di’s, Cang Xu’s, and Zong Ge’s blood didn’t respond.

Tripleblade’s blood changed again, his liquid blood turned into a pile of dust, white as flour.

Lan Zao’s blood looked like an inflated balloon; the volume inside it doubled within a few seconds.

The big guy’s blood ball reacted the most severely, it emitted a strong yellow light that repelled the blue mist greatly.

This was too much for Zi Di’s eyes to take in.

“My blood didn’t change; it seems that my prior speculation that it contains the fire attribute is not correct.”

“Tripleblade’s blood changed again, this proves his bloodline contains the water attribute, amongst others, the ice attribute cannot be ruled out either.”

“I didn’t expect Lan Zao’s bloodline to change so much, this indicates that his bloodline contains a strong water attribute, at least of iron level quality. But the concentration isn’t high, the bloodline is badly damaged, leading to his strength to be limited to the bronze level.”

“Thie big guy, this person…”

Finally, Zi Di focused on the big guy’s blood ball.

The big guy’s blood emitted a strong yellow radiance that repelled the surrounding light blue mist far away.

“This intense reaction indicates that his bloodline is at least gold level. The bloodline rejects the water attribute medicine, so it is very possible that it has a strong earth attribute.”

Zi Di had predicted the quality of the big guy’s bloodline.

Not long after the survivors escaped Mystifying Monster Island, they had used all sorts of battle qi techniques and meditation methods.

When the big guy was cultivating, he displayed an extraordinary cultivation talent.

Based on his battle qi production efficiency when using a battle qi technique, the dragonman youngster deduced that his bloodline was at least gold level.

As for a more accurate quality assessment, it couldn’t be determined.

However, the big guy didn’t have a sudden rise.

His intelligence was very limited, and when he cultivated battle qi, he couldn’t understand battle qi circulation and failed every time, injuring himself.

The dragonman youngster and Zi Di thought of many ways to address his cultivation problems.

Before on Snake Rat Island, they had looked for an intelligence potion but failed.

After obtaining the Magic Medicine Bathtub, the survivors’ medicine refinement capability greatly swelled, and Zi Di personally mixed some intelligence potions.

But after they were given to the big guy, the results were very bad.

Although the intelligence potions were all low level, they illustrated how deep the big guy’s gap in intelligence was.

After this method failed, Zi Di suggested that they use the Soul Send spell to enter the big guy’s body and control him to help him complete one battle qi circulation.

But later on, when Zi Di really cultivated the Soul Send spell, she noticed that method wouldn’t work.

The user of the Soul Send spell, the ghostly Zi Di, would forcefully dominate another lifeform’s body.

During that period, negative energy would erode the target, and they wouldn’t be able to circulate battle qi.

The big guy’s battle qi cultivation originated from life energy, and like how water couldn’t mix with fire, negative energy rejected life energy too strongly and couldn’t coexist with each other.

Not only that, but negative energy would harm the big guy.

After failing time and again, the dragonman youngster and Zi Di could only let it go for now and wait for a suitable chance in the future. Such as when the survivors were able to produce higher level magic medicine.

Chapter 443: Magic Medicine Blood Measurement (2)

The Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The alchemy cabin.

A test that could alter the survivors’ future was underway.

The alchemy formation’s bright mist became silver once more.

The blood samples that needed to be replaced were swapped out for new ones by Zi Di.

“Next, I will use an earth attribute medicine, if my prior speculation is correct, then the big guy’s blood will violently react to it.”

Zi Di’s main focus was the big guy’s blood.

Sure enough, when the bright mist turned a palm yellow, the big guy’s blood began to tremble faintly.

Soon after, it looked like an exceedingly hungry beast awakening and began to wildly absorb the surrounding glowing palm yellow mist.

The mist was absorbed into the blood ball, and after a few seconds, this phenomenon spread through the entire alchemy formation!

The light mist was being absorbed ever faster and even formed a feeble rustle of wind.

Zi Di was shocked!

She hurried to stop the test, for within a brief ten seconds, the earth attribute testing medicine she had meticulously made was utterly consumed.

If one wanted to know, most of the two prior testing medicines still remained after the tests.

Zi Di calmed down and first restored the bright mist to its original silver color.

The big guy’s blood became completely serene.

“The blood’s volume didn’t swell that much, only by less than 5%.”

“Incredible!”

“According to War Merchant’s data records, this indicates that this blood’s bloodline quality is extremely high, at least legend level!!”

The test had finally gotten a gigantic result.

It was unexpectedly the big guy!

Don’t be fooled by his bleak intelligence and dim wit, along with the times he lost his mind and went berserk, his gifts were quite considerable.

Generally, a gold level could be the master of land, such as Hun Tong and Tan Qiu.

As for saint level, they could be a nation’s chancellor, an army commander in chief, and so on.

A legend level was a pillar of power, the overall leader, and in Sheng Ming Empire noble evaluations, being a legend level was one of the core indexes!

“The big guy can become a feudal Lord? A nation chancellor? Or a King? A pope?” Zi Di’s eyes filled with outlandish sights; she found it difficult to imagine such a spectacle!

“Alas.”

“A true regret.”

“The big guy’s intelligence is too low and can be said to be stupid. He cannot practice battle qi and is unable to establish the most basic battle qi circulation.”

“Based on his original fate’s trajectory, he was living for a long time on the Hog’s Kiss, he was raised and protected by the old boatswain and toiled bitterly. Because he often went crazy, no one gave him a chance to cultivate battle qi?”

“In other words, our special group, our commander, gave him the chance to touch battle qi.”

“But even so, his intelligence is a hard-to-cross moat, and he cannot cultivate, he doesn’t have a chance of becoming transcendent.”

For a moment, Zi Di had all kinds of mixed feelings as she sighed incessantly.

The big guy’s situation should be fate favoring him, yet wasn’t fate still playing with him?

It didn’t matter if the heavens gave him such gifts.

Despite giving them to him, he couldn’t benefit from them.

The big guy was an abandoned orphan, he endured disdainful looks while growing up, bullying, humiliation, and was a pitiful child that was pushed away, he only had one person that was warm, and that he could rely on – the old boatswain, who had sacrificed himself on Mystifying Monster Island.

As the old boatswain approached his end, he had entrusted the dragonman youngster to be the big guy’s sole family member.

“Therefore, his loyalty is the least doubtful!” Zi Di’s eyes shined.

“At least legend level…could it be a god level bloodline?”

“Heavens, if it is god level, then the big guy could become a god? That is beyond imagination!”

“We must cultivate him properly; he is a good sapling!” Zi Di made up her mind at that moment.

“Next, we need to vigorously support him, it doesn’t matter how many resources we smash into him.”

“Legend level…tsk.”

“His intelligence issue is not a problem. No one with a legend level bloodline will grow into an idiot!”

“There is no need to purify his bloodline, he already has a super bloodline!”

“As long as he starts cultivating and constantly accumulates cultivation…hmm.”

Zi Di stared blankly.

The problem had returned to square one.

How could the big guy cultivate? How could he establish his first battle qi circulation?

“Buying intellect medicine!”

“Ice Sculpture Island must have high level intelligence potions.”

“Even if the price is high, even higher than a gold level healing potion, it doesn’t matter.”

“We must buy it for him.”

“We can’t let that child suffer any longer!”

When Zi Di thought of that, her expression had a trace of viciousness in it.

If it was on Mystifying Monster Island when she discovered the big guy’s bloodline quality, she might have felt intense envy and even jealousy.

But now, she too had new possibilities.

“Our bloodline quality can be improved too.”

“In the future, when we discover how to make Bloodcores, each of us will have a future of infinite possibilities!”

The ghost girl calmed down and regained her cool head.

Her heart was filled with future longings as she continued to test.

The third round of testing the earth attribute was still ongoing.

The reason was that the big guy’s blood ball reacted too strongly, severely interfering with the others’ blood balls.

Therefore, this time, Zi Di removed the big guy’s blood sample from the testing pool.

Without the big guy interfering, one blood sample changed in the palm yellow light mist.

It was Fei She’s blood.

Elder Fei She’s bloodline was partial towards the earth attribute. But the degree of change could never compare to the big guy’s.

After that, Zi Di began to try fire attribute medicine.

Lan Zao’s blood ball clearly shrank and withered.

Mu Ban’s blood ball looked like a wilted plant.

Zi Di’s blood subtly absorbed the red light mist and on its surface, a number of very minute stripes formed.

Zi Di felt pleasantly surprised, these kinds of wrinkled lines were very crucial, and were likely to be crucial clues that could precisely categorize her bloodline.

The tower spirit comprehensively recorded everything on site.

What faintly surprised Zi Di was that Tripleblade’s blood also had a close affinity to the fire attribute.

“A bit interesting.”

“Tripleblade’s bloodline simultaneously contains the water attribute, the wood attribute, and the fire attribute?”

Zi Di continued to try.

Dark attribute, light attribute, wind attribute, ice attribute, lightning attribute…

After more than a dozen rounds of testing, she obtained the details of many test results.

Tripleblade’s bloodline characteristics were altered, with more accuracy, the water attribute was swapped for the ice attribute, therefore, his bloodline had an affinity towards the ice attribute, the wood attribute, and the fire attribute.

Que Shan’s bloodline contained the dark attribute.

Lan Zao’s bloodline had the water attribute.

The big guy had the earth attribute, Mu Ban had the wood attribute, and Fei She had a weak earth attribute.

Zi Di’s bloodline while alive had an affinity to the wood and fire attributes.

Of the other people, the youngster commander had the Bloodcore and testing him was unnecessary.

Cang Xu’s living dead person blood still had vestiges of the wood attribute but was more oriented towards negative energy.

The only two blood balls that didn’t react belonged to Zong Ge and Bai Ya.

But not having an affinity to any attribute did not mean their bloodlines were useless. In fact, many high level bloodlines were harmonious and weren’t polluted by any attribute.

Having an affinity with some attribute meant that their bloodline was better at doing said thing.

But from another point of interpretation: being good at some areas also meant that they were weak in another, that could be regarded as a type of shackle.

The aforementioned medicine testing was merely the first phase.

But before the second phase began, Zi Di first used these testing results to begin limited deductions and calculations.

Although bloodlines were as vast as the open sea, they were actually connected with each other.

Thank War Merchant’s database.

When he researched the Bloodcore and extensively experimented on artificial magic beasts, he naturally had many detailed and reliable experiment data in this field as well as practicing tentative theories and hypotheses.

Among these, there was one crucial data chart that was extremely significant for Zi Di’s current task – a bloodline archive map.

It had the Flame Dragon King bloodline recorded in it.

The Flame Dragon King bloodline held a relatively high node, and under it in a branch line was a small node that represented the Spurting Smoke Dragon bloodline.

In the past, after the dragonman youngster absorbed a spurting smoke dragon, not only did he gain part of the spurting smoke dragon transformation, but he also increased his Flame Dragon King bloodline’s concentration by a little bit.

Because the source of the Spurting Smoke Dragon bloodline was the Flame Dragon King bloodline!

Therefore, if the young man wanted to raise his Flame Dragon King bloodline concentration, he didn’t need to kill a Flame Dragon King, that was too difficult and risky.

He only needed to follow the bloodline archive map and collect the Flame Dragon King bloodline’s branch lines and rely on quantity; in time, he could improve his Flame Dragon King bloodline.

It was worth mentioning that War Merchant’s bloodline archive map was not complete.

It was not because the tower spirit and its database was damaged, the archive data was quite intact and preserved.

The real reason was that bloodlines were not immutable, bloodline relationships were also tangled and complicated, so it was difficult to research a complete bloodline breakthrough.

Even War Merchant was the same.

There were two main reasons.

The first was it was hard to efficiently test the true bloodline.

The second is that new bloodlines were born like flies.

Take the dragon race’s bloodlines.

Dragons were lascivious and could copulate with many species, creating many subspecies.

As long as these subspecies multiplied and lived, they would become a community and an entirely new bloodline.

If there was a bloodline archive map that could truly record all of the bloodlines in the world, then we could see that archive map would be constantly changing. Some bloodlines were disappearing but adding many more bloodlines.

Overall, the bloodline archive map would be continuously expanding.

Whether it was the bloodline investigation or the magic medicine blood measurements, both helped Zi Di reduce the bloodline scope of every survivor on the bloodline archive map.

After a dozen minutes, Zi Di had calculated a more comprehensive testing plan.

“Next, time to start the second phase of medicine testing.”

“Let’s begin with the simplest…Lan Zao.”

Lan Zoa’s blood only had a water attribute affinity.

This time, Zi Di had his blood hovering in the silver light mist by itself.

Zi Di made medicine on the spot, mainly made of fishman blood.

After the battle of Two Eyes Island, Zi Di obtained a few fishmen corpses.

When Cang Xu was removing souls on the battlefield and she was collecting treasures on the seafloor, she had taken these corpses.

Cultivator corpses were all alchemy materials.

Even ordinary fishman scales and flesh had value. But due to space constraints on the Deep Sea Monster Fish, Zi Di was forced to abandon the ordinary corpses. Mainly because there were too many treasures that took up space; ordinary corpses weren’t that valuable.

The fishman race testing medicine was deployed.

Zi Di activated the formation and Lan Zao’s blood immediately reacted.

Minute ripples spread across the blood’s surface.

“What great luck.”

“Lan Zao has a fishman bloodline!”

Chapter 444: Magic Medicine Blood Measurement (3)

Zi Di tested again and got interim results.

Testing what transcendent bloodline Lan Zao’s body had.

So far, it had been determined that it was a fishman race bloodline.

But which specifically still needed further testing,

Zi Di continued to dredge in this direction.

“I need to refine a more focused fishman race testing magic medicine.”

She took out some fishman blood from storage.

The blood was in good condition and had a silver level aura.

It was Dao Hen’s blood.

Dao Hen was a silver level, and a powerhouse on the fishmen’s side during the battle for Two Eyes Island, and despite Ku Feng being in a better state when they did battle, he still lost to Dao Hen.

His blade skills were very unique and had greatly expanded Ku Feng’s horizons.

His individual strength within the silver level was also outstanding, and when Xi Dai Zi tasted the dragonman youngster’s strength, he associated it with Dao Hen’s.

When the Battle for Two Eyes Island ended, Dao Hen fought with everything he had until he was taken prisoner by the humans.

He was currently imprisoned by Hun Tong.

The survivors acquired his blood because of the transaction they did before leaving the island. The biggest gain of that transaction for the survivors was the water pigman slaves.

In the list of items, they also requested lots of alchemy materials, the more the better.

During the transaction process, Hun Tong naturally thought of every possible method to sweeten the deal, and thought of the fishman prisoner Dao Hen, immediately ordering that he be bled.

He also asked the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps if they wanted Dao Hen’s bone, flesh, and scales, those too could be cut off and sold.

But Dao Hen himself couldn’t be sold.

Because Hun Tong needed to keep him still, he was to be treated as evidence to exhibit, a response to a future imperial investigation.

Even if there wasn’t an investigation, he could also use them as a military success, taking credit for the Duo Mu clan in the eyes of the empire.

In other words, Dao Hen was a relatively important political bargaining chip to Hun Tong.

It was also worth mentioning that in the transaction, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps also wanted to acquire the old fishman elder’s flesh.

The old fishman elder had failed at the final hurdle and was beheaded by Hun Tong.

Any part of his corpse could be regarded as a gold level material.

Zi Di used Dao Hehn’s bloodline to refine medicine.

The magic medicine was produced smoothly, and not long after, she obtained a Knife Fin bloodline testing magic medicine.

To guarantee the success rate, Zi Di had intentionally lowered the magic medicine’s level to the iron level.

Dao Hen’s blood was silver level, and it was completely possible to refine a silver level magic medicine out of it,

But Zi Di, in combination with the Magic Medicine Bathtub and a series of manufacturing tools could guarantee that bronze and iron level medicine were refined successfully.

Zi Di put the magic medicine into the alchemy formation.

The silver light mist turned pale white, but Lan Zao’s hovering blood ball didn’t react.

On the contrary, another blood reacted, although its volume and color didn’t change, the blood ball quivered and made an unexpected sonorous sound.

“Zong Ge’s blood…the color and volume didn’t change, he doesn’t have Dao Hen’s bloodline, but he does have a distant connection to it.”

“As expected, Zong Ge’s bloodline quality is very high, the white light mist was forced back by the sound, and 80% of the light mist in the alchemy formation has been emptied.”

Zi Di was both surprised and not.

Based on all kinds of indications, the survivors had long speculated that Zong Ge came from a remarkable family background.

Since it was remarkable, then Zong Ge’s bloodline quality was very likely to be great.

“Since the quality is higher than Dao Hen’s bloodline, then it can be temporarily extrapolated that his bloodline has nothing to do with fishmen bloodlines, in that case…” Zi Di examined the archive map.

With the Blade Fin bloodline was used as the starting point, and the fishman race as the category to spread out from, along with using the nonattributable property, the test result scope was reduced.

Zi Di counted roughly that there was still at least a hundred bloodline routes left.

Every route had the possibility of containing Zong Ge’s bloodline.

Zong Ge’s bloodline discovery was merely a chance gain, and Zi Di returned to testing Lan Zao’s blood.

This time, Zi Di took out the old fishman clan elder’s blood, and refined it into magic medicine.

The old fishman elder possessed a most outstanding Green Cheek bloodline characteristic, therefore, this magic medicine should detect the Green Cheek Fishman bloodline.

After this medicine was thrown into the alchemy formation, only one blood reacted somewhat.

But it wasn’t Lan Zao’s blood, but Mu Ban’s.

“This kind of reaction happening on the contrary, proves that Mu Ban’s bloodline has nothing to do with the fishman race.”

“The reason why this change occurred is because the Green Cheek fishman bloodline and Mu Ban’s human bloodline both have the wood attribute; this is called the same attribute resonance.”

“But Tripleblade’s bloodline also has the wood attribute, yet it didn’t react, this indicates that Tripleblade’s bloodline is very distant from the Green Cheek fishman bloodline.”

This test also had gains.

Lan Zao’s bloodline and the Green Cheek bloodline were not closely related.

Mu Ban’s bloodline on the archive map wasn’t far from the Green Cheek bloodline.

But Zi Di didn’t need to look, she knew the range was still massive and there were too many available answers.

“Alas.” Zi Di couldn’t help but sigh.

A gold level material was used, but the gains were small.

Zi Di was faintly regretful.

But the testing needed to continue.

Zi Di took out a book.

The Vampire Cookbook.

This cookbook was written by a vampire duke.

He was a fighter whose biggest pleasure was enjoying fine food.

The cookbook recorded in detail every species’ blood flavor, when to drink it, how to drink it in the most optimal way, and so on.

To help explain things to his descendants, he sealed blood samples of many species into this book.

Once the cookbook was of high quality, but now it had fallen to the silver level, and although most of the blood samples still remained, the best of them were silver level.

Although there were many kinds of blood, there was only a small amount of each.

After using them, it would be hard to make a second testing medicine afterwards.

Zi Di didn’t hesitate.

She had used the gold level old fishman clan elder’s blood, and of course she would use this blood too.
