In fact, when they purchased this cookbook from the auction, they had planned to use it here.

Magic medicine blood measurement continued like before.

Red Eye fishman bloodline…nothing.

Butter Scale fishman bloodline…nothing.

Vagrant fishman bloodline. After many rounds of silence, Lan Zao’s bloodline finally reacted.

According to this result, the archive map range shrank another step.

“These bloodlines aren’t necessary to test.” Zi Di eliminated some fishman bloodlines to avoid extra trouble, saving many blood samples.

Testing continued.

Fang fishman bloodline…no reaction.

Hot Blood fishman bloodline…nothing.

The Great Float bloodline triggered another reaction.

“The blood sample is insufficient.”

When Zi Di was forced to stop, Lan Zao’s fishman bloodline still hadn’t been determined yet.

But the archive map range had been greatly reduced.

“His bloodline has three types of bloodline routes…but currently there is a lack of samples, I cannot make the appropriate testing medicine, therefore I cannot determine which of them it is.”

“Of course, there is another possibility, these three bloodline routes are all wrong. If that is so, then it will be troublesome.”

If that happened, then it usually means that prior magic medicine testing inferences were wrong at a crucial node.

The reason was simple.

The raw materials Zi Di used was various kinds of fishman blood.

Did these fishman blood samples really only contain one bloodline?

That was difficult to ascertain.

For example, take Zi Di using the old fishman clan elder’s gold level blood to make a Green Cheek fishman bloodline testing magic medicine.

If the fishman elder possessed other transcendent bloodlines, that would influence the blood reaction, then that would mean the testing results might be wrong.

In addition, did Lan Zao’s blood really only have one transcendent bloodline in it?

If he had two or even multiple kinds, being a mixed blood, then it would affect the magic medicine testing results, that was also possible.

The systemic problem of measuring blood with magic medicine has become undoubtably visible now.

The testing target might contain many bloodlines that couldn’t be purified.

The raw materials used for testing medicine also couldn’t be purified.

As an analogy, Zi Di was using a rule whose dimensions and marks were changing to measure the size of an object. Furthermore, she didn’t know if there was any overstatements of the measurements taken.

However, this was not the biggest problem.

Zi Di was most worried that if Lan Zao had a whole new bloodline that wasn’t on the archive map, what should she do?

Based on the theories, this possibility couldn’t be ruled out completely.

After working into the night, the materials, labor, and time Zi Di invested in this was conservatively estimated to be worth around 300 gold.

Although there were many results, she still hadn’t determined the first testing target’s bloodline yet.

This was based on War Merchant’s bloodline archive map.

There was no other choice.

The best chance the survivors had was using medicine to test bloodlines.

The tests on Lan Zao had temporarily come to an end.

In the future, they would need to gather more materials, refine the corresponding magic medicines to reduce the range until they could ascertain the true answer.

In the depths of the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s storage, there was another fishman corpse.

Hai Ping’s saint corpse.

However, the survivors didn’t know what the bloodline of Hai Ping’s saint corpse was.

They merely learned that when the fishmen had attacked Two Eyes Island, one of the main goals was to retrieve the saint level corpse.

Putting aside Lan Zao’s blood for the time being, Zi Di continued to immerse herself in testing.

Her chosen testing strategy was to do the easy ones first, then the difficult ones.

After Lan Zao was Zi Di’s blood.

Her bloodline contained wood and fire attributes.

The most crucial clue was its decorative design.

Thanks to War Merchant once again, Zi Di ordered the tower spirit to search through the vast database for all kinds of data on magic plants and compare them to the decorative design from before.

The tower spirit helped Zi Di find several hundred similar magic plants.

“My bloodline is likely related to a magic plant, and not an intelligent species.”

Zi Di brazenly speculated this because of the decorative design clue.

She immediately abandoned testing for intelligent life and decided to perform an in-depth investigation into magic plan type bloodlines.

The survivors didn’t have much of the pertinent alchemy materials.

Most of the magic plants were coral.

“This is wrong.”

“This also didn’t react…”

It wasn’t until medicine made mainly from Fire Pith Coral was used did Zi Di’s blood change into a sphere and glow bright red.

Zi Di was delighted.

This result seemed to prove that her hypothesis was correct, and the range finally shrank by 67%.

After testing all of the materials on hand once through, Zi Di began to flip through the Vampire Cookbook.

She had to lament her luck.

She had purchased this book on a whim.

The cookbook not only had intelligent lifeforms, but it also had the blood of many magic beasts and magic plants.

After a half hour, there was groundbreaking progress.

Before Zi Di, there were only four remaining answers.

They were the Bitter Mouth bloodline, the Mix Up bloodline, the Gunpowder Flower bloodline, and the King Sunflower bloodline.

This was already the limit.

Zi Di found it difficult to continue, because she lacked the corresponding materials.

“The current results are pretty good.”

“The Gunpowder Flower bloodline is gold level.”

“The Mix Up and King Sunflower bloodline are saint level.”

“Only the Bitter Mouth bloodline is iron level.”

Of course, Zi Di hoped her transcendent bloodline would be of the highest possible quality.

The Bloodline Purification formation could only improve bloodlines that were within the target’s body.

After halting the testing of her bloodline for now, Zi Di rested for a moment, then proceeded to work hard.

Based on the difficulty, she began to test Tripleblade’s bloodline next.

Tripleblade’s bloodline contained the most attributes of the survivors; it had wood, fire, and ice attributes.

This seemed very good, but in actuality, the bloodline archive map range didn’t shrink by much.

The reason was simple.

Tripleblade might not just have one transcendent bloodline, but many.

It was precisely due to multiple bloodlines mixing that caused him to have an affinity for many attributes.

Furthermore, Tripleblade’s background investigation didn’t give Zi Di many more useful clues either.

“Let’s try all of the intelligent lifeform medicines first.” Without sufficient clues, Zi Di was forced to do it step by step.

Fishman race magic medicine did not react with Tripleblade’s blood.

Elf type medicine didn’t react.

Elemental type medicine didn’t react.

Human race medicine didn’t react either.

Goblin type medicine reacted, but this was normal, Tripleblade was a goblin.

“Oh?” Zi Di revealed an astonished expression.

When she tried beastman type medicine, Tripleblade’s blood unexpectedly burst open with three layers of light, the change was quite severe.

“Tripleblade has a beastman bloodline within him?”

“Could it be that this is one of the potential reasons he and Zong Ge are close with each other?”

With bloodline secrets revealed step by step, Zi Di’s understanding of the people around her increased accordingly.

“Wait a moment!”

“Beastman bloodline, three attributes, that makes me think of that famous beastman knight-errant Guo Jin.”

“He has the Three Color Panda-man bloodline!”

When Zi Di thought of that, she rushed to find it in the Vampire Cookbook, finding the Three Color panda-man blood sample in the upper section of a page.

“I’ve got a feeling…let’s have a try first!”

Zi Di made the corresponding medicine and smoothly threw it into the alchemy formation.

Tripleblade’s blood changed dramatically in the light mist, the light mist was seeping into the blood, constantly expanding it.

At the same time, the blood emitted a three color light that grew increasingly intense and concentrated.

The other data in the alchemy formation subsequently shifted greatly.

“This kind of phenomenon, essentially clinches it.”

“Tripleblade’s transcendent bloodline is the Three Color panda-man bloodline.

She didn’t expect that at this point in the testing that Tripleblade’s bloodline would be concluded.

Zi Di coughed out some air, her complexion mixed.

“The Three Color panda-man bloodline, this is one of the most classical magic chef bloodlines.”

“Back then, that knight-errant Guo Jin wasn’t a strong fighter, he was really famous for developing a magic dish – the Three Color Ice Brick.”

“Tripleblade, this guy has walked the wrong path.”

“If he becomes a cook, then his prospects and potential will be better than as a mercenary.”

Inadvertently, fate’s cruelty and paradoxical nature unfolded themselves in a small way before Zi Di.

Who would think this?

Tripleblade actually had a gold level magic chef bloodline!

Chapter 445: Magic Medicine Blood Measurement (4)

Time quickly passed.

On the ocean surface, the curtain of night wan ed as the morning sun slowly rose.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet brought through the morning mist, following Cang Xu and Qing Xin sailing the Iron Lump in the distance.

The dragonman youngster’s first order of business after waking up was still determining their route and speed.

After finishing his morning prayer and returning to the captain’s room, he received the results of Zi Di’s medicine testing.

The young man understood that the bloodline testing results greatly influenced the survivors.

He harbored a bit of nervousness and apprehension when he looked at them.

“Lan Zao has a fishman bloodline?” The dragonman youngster was astonished.

But what it was specifically still needed to be tested in the future.

“Wait a moment.” The dragonman youngster suddenly recalled a memory from Mystifying Monster Island.

While they were struggling to survive on Mystifying Monster Island, they had entered a desert oasis. There, they were able to take a breather, but they also encountered danger.

The oasis’ lake had many fat ball flying fish living in it.

These floating fishes could turn invisible and couldn’t be seen with the naked eye.

The dragonman youngster at the time also relied on the Bloodcore to transform his eyes to green lizard ones, giving him thermal vision.

Their old camp was also attacked by a small school of fish.

When the dragonman youngster ran back, he saw Lan Zao fighting bravely, preserving the camp.

“At the time, he couldn’t see the fat ball flying fish either, he was also in terrible condition, yet in spite of that, he could hit the flying fish and drive them away until I was able to reinforce him.”

“It can be seen that under the immense pressure of death, Lan Zao’s whole potential erupted out of him.”

“And potential is undoubtedly coupled with one’s bloodline.”

“Perhaps this is a crucial clue.”

The dragonman youngster continued reading.

A moment later, his eyes widened slightly, revealing pleasant surprise.

“My small child’s bloodline is at least legend level? Good, amazing!”

The young man was incomparably gratified.

According to Zi Di’s statement, the big guy’s intelligence problem will probably change due to his bloodline.

“He doesn’t need to use the Bloodline Purification formation, so long as he surmounts the first difficulty and matures steadily, he will have great achievements.”

“If the old boatswain knew about this, he would have laughed to his heart’s content.”

The dragonman youngster lamented in his heart.

Under the big guy was Tripleblade’s bloodline testing results.

The dragonman youngster couldn’t help but be surprised, and he gave a strange look at it.

“Tripleblade’s bloodline is surprisingly the Three Color panda-man bloodline?”

“He is a goblin, but he has a beastman transcendent bloodline. Furthermore, this bloodline is actually one of the classical magic chef bloodlines.”

The dragonman youngster was also momentarily speechless.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps needed a qualified magic chef.

The dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, and the rest ate magic beast meat every day, but they were simply cooked.

If that had a magic chef, not only would fewer ingredients be used, but the magic cuisine eaten would have more effects.

This kind of improvement won’t be discerned in a day, but over a long period of accumulation, it would usually form a frightful disparity.

Every great power in the Sheng Ming Empire had a fixed magic chef working for them.

In fact, not just the humans, but also the elves, dwarfs, dragons, and every other race had magic chefs active among them.

Great kitchens made classic dishes and were always welcomed and even esteemed by the upper class.

Simply put, magic cooking could be regarded as magic medicine with a very short shelf life.

But taking too much magic medicine would cause drug resistance to them.

Magic cuisine was not like that.

“What a pity, although I know a portion of the truth on Tripleblade’s bloodline, for now, I cannot tell him.” The dragonman youngster felt a faint pity.

The only three people that knew of the Bloodcore was him, Zi Di, and Cang Xu.

The other survivors didn’t know yet, they only guessed that the Bloodcore was extremely valuable!

If he immediately told Tripleblade the blood test results, it would instead ruin things.

Tripleblade was close to Zong Ge and always kept a respectful distance from the young man and the others, keeping a wary mentality against them.

Without mentioning the above, one could see Tripleblade’s attitude during the bloodline background investigation.

Tripleblade didn’t expose many clues about himself, this made Zi Di’s blood measurements much more difficult.

“Perhaps I can use an indirect approach and method to raise this point with him.”

The dragonman youngster pondered for a spell, and felt he wasn’t sure of the outcome.

Tripleblade too had the pride of a fighter, and if he was forced to do cooking, he might overreach himself, leading himself to think that the dragonman youngster was insulting him.

The dragonman youngster relinquished the thought and continued to browse the report.

He saw Zi Di’s testing results.

“Four types of bloodlines…” The dragonman youngster sincerely hoped that Zi Di’s bloodline potential was somewhat high.

After Zi Di was Cang Xu.

Cang Xu’ bloodline result was currently appraised as the iron level Farm Land bloodline.

But Cang Xu’s body had a near nonexistent concentration of this transcendent bloodline.

“Although there is an answer, as far as Cang Xu is concerned, he will have better prospects walking the path of necromancy.” The dragonman youngster sighed.

After Cang Xu was Zong Ge’s bloodline.

Currently Zi Di’s testing stated that Zong Ge’s bloodline quality was very high.

His body had two transcendent bloodlines.

One was human, the other the lion race.

Both were non elemental.

“The human bloodline is related to the sword, perhaps this is the reason Zong Ge became a weapon master.”

“The Lion-man quality is very high, at least saint level.”

The dragonman youngster murmured, looking pensively.

Zi Di’s testing had ended here, limited by materials and couldn’t continue testing.

For now, testing wasn’t needed.

Because Zong Ge and the big guy were the same, neither needed bloodline purification.

They still had a long way to go till they reached the limit of their talents.

Furthermore, the Bloodcore couldn’t be divulged to Zong Ge, this matter needed to be handled cautiously.

After Zong Ge was Mu Ban.

Current tests said he had a tree type magic plant transcendent bloodline.

Fei She had a dwarf bloodline, but it was unknown which.

Although Que Shan’s bloodline didn’t have a precise answer, her range had been reduced to a few routes. Currently, it could be determined that her body had a Drow bloodline, a dark elf bloodline.

As for Bai Ya…

When the dragonman youngster saw it, his brows inevitably pursed.

Bai Ya’s blood never reacted during any of the testing!

Zi Di gave him a quite tragic testing result – Bai Ya didn’t have any transcendent bloodline.

“Bai Ya…” The dragonman youngster deeply sighed.

From deep within his heart, he had a good opinion of this young hunter.

Bai Ya conducted himself cordially and dared to risk everything to help another, in spite of his own safety.

On Mystifying Monster Island, he had saved Cang Xu and sucked poison out of Lan Zao.

After getting battle qi techniques, he had practiced the most.

But he didn’t have a transcendent bloodline, thus he will never become a cultivator.

After the dragonman youngster saw the testing results, he looked it through once more, and after ensuring that he remembered them, he burned it.

Overall, Zi Di’s magic medicine blood measurement had a lot of results.

With these results, the survivors could have a clearer direction on how to grow.

“Next will be to gather the pertinent materials and continue measuring blood. More importantly, is the construction of the Bloodline Purification formation!” The dragonman youngster planned.

Constructing the Bloodline Purification formation was rather difficult, and the survivors still lacked a crucial alchemy talent.

Zi Di and Cang Xu could do it, but the chance of success was too small.

If one was barely enough, it would consume too much time and supplies, and it still might all be in vain in the end.

Chapter 446: Chi Lai and Men Shi vs Long Fu

After the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had sailed for three days and nights since departing from Two Eyes Island, they stopped at some small isle at noon.

Soon after, a survey team returned to report that it was a safe and uninhabited island.

The barren island had scarce resources, fortunately there was a smidgen of fresh water.

The dragonman youngster had his subordinates draw water to replace and replenish the ships’ fresh water stores.

After the dragonman youngster finished eating lunch, he came ashore to the island.

He stood on top of a rock mound and looked around, seeing the overgrown gray rocks filling the ground and scant shrubs in the gaps between the rocks, tenaciously surviving.

Men Shi marched heavily over behind the dragonman youngster, his face filled with anxiety and worry, “Lord Commander, did we lose track of the Iron Lump?”

The dragonman youngster’s sudden landfall was certainly abnormal.

According to common sense, they should be pursuing closely and not stop, like they had been doing for the last three days.

The fleet had adequate supplies, and Men Shi couldn’t find why they suddenly came ashore.

As a matter of fact, it wasn’t the dragonman youngster who was deliberately procrastinating.

Rather they had already reached their destination.

Weeping Pine Island was quite close to his barren island. The fleet couldn’t get any closer, otherwise they would reveal themselves.

In accordance with the dragonman youngster’s plan, they needed to stop here and wait for an opportune moment. They would wait until the undead gathering had concluded, Cang Xu finished his transactions, and for most of the people there to leave, then they would deliberately carry out a good performance on the Iron Lump at the last moment.

In the meantime, if Cang Xu had an accident over yonder and encountered danger, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could also deploy ahead of time and provide emergency support.

He couldn’t tell Men Shi the actual reason why.

The dragonman youngster could only say a half truth, “We have dispatched scouting puppets to bring us important intelligence.”

“The Iron Lump has already made landfall on Weeping Pine Island.”

“Every month, that island erupts with a tide of negative energy, forming a wind across the ocean around the pine island.”

“The Iron Lump is acting abnormally; we surmise that perhaps Weeping Pine Island is that necromancer’s lair.”

“So that’s how it is!” Men Shi’s heart shook greatly, and wasn’t at all suspicious, “On Two Eyes Island, I also heard about this Weeping Pine Island. This island’s environment is unusual; it had a large quantity of negative energy on it. For a necromancer, it is a natural blessed land.”

The honest middle aged dwarf soon wrinkled his brows. When he thought of his clansmen, worry prickled up in his thoughts, “If the island has a necromancer’s lair on it, then would that necromancer experiment on my clansmen and even turn them into undead beings?”

At that moment, Men Shi heard the dragonman youngster consoling him, “Don’t worry too much, Men Shi.”

“Our best plan for now is to wait.”

“Wait until the Pine Island wind loosens up, then we’ll immediately attack the island!”

Men Shi nodded, and sighed, “It should be done this way, the Pine Island Sea Wind is a tide of negative energy, in this kind of environment, necromancy will be greatly augmented, and we’ll be at too much of a disadvantage.”

“Lord Commander, thank you truly. Without you, I wouldn’t have a chance to go rescue my clansmen.”

The dragonman youngster burst with laughter and clapped Men Shi’s shoulder, “You are my comrade, and even more so part of us, I will help you save your clansmen with all my strength.”

“It just so happened that I was also looking for you.”

“Come, give me a good fight now.”

“To tell the truth, my bones have been itching due to being cooped up on my ship for the last few days!”

Men Shi stared at him, his understanding of the dragonman youngster increasing a bit, “It seems our commander is also a battle junkie?”

This addiction was quite common.

But Men Shi didn’t immediately agree, rather he hesitated, “Lord Commander, you have spirit, and of course I wish to keep you company, but battle is imminent…”

The dragonman youngster waved his hand, “We are merely comparing notes, this isn’t a life and death battle.”

“Besides, I need to fight you myself, to verify your strength and skills. This will be helpful for the upcoming joint battle, no?”

The dragonman youngster’s words smoothly removed Men Shi’s hesitation.

“Then forgive this subordinate’s offense.” Men Shi was about to take out his two handed war hammer.

The dragonman youngster was unarmed, he took a few steps back, and shouted, “Right, then let’s go!”

After saying that, he charged.

Bang, thump, slam.

The two people attacked each other, the commotion immediately attracting the attention of the surrounding mercenaries.

“Who is fighting?!”

“What is happening?”

Lan Zao stood at the edge of the battlefield, “Why are you flustered? This is a normal spar!”

After that explanation, the mercenaries became excited, and they gathered together to watch the fight.

The mercenaries ashore all agilely climbed onto the stones and peeked their heads out to look around.

The mercenaries on the ships in the distance were surveying it from a distance.

The probing was finished.

The dragonman youngster roared and used the Surge and Demolition Fist battle skills.

Men Shi felt the pressure on his increase sharply and rushed to use a defensive battle skill, watching for a chance to counterattack with his two-handed war hammer.

Both sides came at each other, their blows growing more momentous than ever before.

Xu Ma excitedly ran across the ship, shouting loudly, “Bets are open, bets are open.”

He organized a betting pool, instantly attracting many mercenaries to participate.

Many older members crossed their arms, sneering at it.

When Zong Ge quickly got news someone opened a betting pool, he immediately snorted coldly, “Gambling is strictly prohibited on the ship, every gambler will receive severe punishment!”

As a general, he greatly disapproved of gambling. If this kind of conduct inundated an army, it would greatly dig into the fighting spirit of the officers and soldiers, needlessly increase hatred and vengeance for each other, and gravely affect unity, reducing the unit’s fighting strength.

But just as Zong Ge was about to give the order, Zi Di decided to contact him.

Zi Di communicated through an alchemy device, “Don’t act now, wait until the betting pool is set up and the participants all put in their stake, then we’ll mop them up, that way they’ll feel real pain. In the future, the offenders won’t make such a mistake.”

Zong Ge was silent for a bit, he instantly made out Zi Di’s other intention, perhaps even the main one, confiscating the gambling money.

But Zong Ge still agreed to the proposal.

It was just as Zi Di had said, it would make those gamblers feel pain!

Zong Ge soon gazed back at the island.

Men Shi and the dragonman youngster were fighting near the coast.

Their fight attracted Zong Ge, one who had the same strength.

After a half minute, Zong Ge saw how the fight was progressing, “If nothing unexpected happens, the victor will be Long Fu.”

Men Shi displayed his fighting style.

The majority of the dwarfs were the same, he was an expert at defense.

But it was very difficult to achieve victory through defense.

Men Shi’s main offense came from his long war hammer.

This war hammer was of impressive quality; it was a silver level weapon.

But against the dragonman youngster, it wasn’t enough.

The dragonman youngster’s dragon scales could resist gold level battle skills.

Men Shi’s attacks couldn’t threaten the dragonman youngster, thus, he became a boxing dummy.

The dragonman youngster naturally could see the outcome of the battle more clearly than Zong Ge, so he suddenly took a step back, putting distance between him and Men Shi.

The dragonman youngster yelled, “This fight isn’t enjoyable enough. Chi Lai! Join in too.”

Chi Lai was still on a ship, and when he heard this order, he stared slightly before responding by immediately jumping off the ship and running to shore to join the battle.

Chi Lai and Men Shi worked together to do battle with the dragonman youngster.

The mercenaries were even more lively as they felt their commander’s heroic fighting spirit inspiring them.

At the same time, everyone was also more curious at what the final outcome of this spar would be.

Simultaneously faced with two silver levels, the dragonman youngster instantly felt the pressure on him dramatically increase.

However, he quickly adapted.

Chi Lai and Men Shi were both undoubtably silver level elites.

They both had their respective nicknames, and had reached the limits of their bloodlines, unable to go any steps further.

Regardless of whether it was battle skills or divine spells, they used them all very proficiently, like they were instinct.

But the previous problem still existed.

They fell short of firepower.

Even though Chi Lai used the divine spell Justice Vanguard to increase his and Men Shi’s attack capabilities by double, they still couldn’t pierce the dragonman youngster’s tenacious dragon scales.

“That’s it, we lost!” After fighting for a while, Men Shi conceded.

Chi Lai also pulled back without delay, “Indeed, continuing to fight will be an empty waste of battle qi. If Lord Long Fu was fighting like he did during the battle for Two Eyes Island, he would have been defeated already.”

The dragonman youngster laughed, “I did say this was merely a spar.”

Following that, he curbed his rising laughter and looked next to him, seeing the smashed and disorderly rock beach, “This is the inconvenience of ocean sailing, there are too few places where people can freely spar and learn from each other.”

Although the White Bandage was a magic ship, it was a bronze level one and couldn’t take silver levels crossing blades with each other.

Hearing the dragonman youngster say this, Chi Lai and Men Shi glanced at each other, sensing the dragonman youngster’s incredible interest in martial skills.

This was the world of the strong.

Good martial skills was an excellent characteristic.

Seeing the fight finish, the mercenaries loudly cheered, exclaiming their admiration for their commander’s formidability one after the other.

During this celebratory ambience, Tripleblade led a group of people to sally out to sweep up the gambling pool and grab the gambling mercenaries.

As the chief offender, Xu Ma’s tears regretfully dropped.

Seeing the betting money confiscated, sorrow flooded his miserable heart, forming a distinct contrast to the people cheering around him.

“The commander is eager to be first!”

“Even Chi Lai and Men Shi combined are not his match.”

“The commander is the strongest!”

“No, it is still Lord Shi Qi.”

“Say, if Lords Shi Qi and Long Fu fought each other, who would be stronger?”

“Does this need to be said, don’t you know what their ranks are?”

“But Lord Shi Qi’s battle qi already has a gold luster. He also used a healing potion to cure his injury; he can cultivate normally again.”

The mercenaries’ discussions entered Zong Ge’s ears.

Zong Ge was unconcerned, he faintly moved his finger, feeling a bit itchy.

However, to his side, Tripleblade was a bit proud of himself; he looked at Zong Ge, more certain that he was headed in the correct direction.

As for the dragonman youngster, he secretly activated the Grand Arena Medal.

Chapter 447: This Medal is Superb!

In tonight’s night sky, the moon was a dish.

As the watery moonlight passed through the window, it sprinkled into the captain’s room.

The captain’s room had no lights on, though the dragonman youngster wasn’t asleep.

He sat in front of a desk, listening to the sea waves slapping the hull, the occasional mercenaries patrolling on deck giving orders to each other.

With Zong Ge presiding over them, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had far more discipline than any normal mercenary corps.

The half beastman treated and educated them entirely as troops.

The desk had a teapot that was boiling tea.

The dragonman youngster was looking at the medal in his hand.

It was the Grand Arena Medal.

It seemed to be made of silver and from dense material, even the dragonman youngster felt it was quite heavy.

It was silver level equipment, and its influence was quite large, for it concerned the Abyss.

Its main function was twofold.

The first was to amplify the wearer’s growth, including strength, speed, defense, attack power, recovery, and more. It also had another property, the more people that were watching the fight, the better the growth rate.

The second function was that it absorbed a defeated opponent’s battle experience, skills, and so on, pouring them all into the medal’s holder.

The dragonman youngster during the day personally experienced the benefits of this equipment.

He had requested that he spar with Chi La and Men Shi.

With everyone watching, he vividly felt the Grand Arena Medal amplify him.

To the extent that he could more effortlessly defeat two silver levels fighting together.

He was now using the second function after the sparring finished.

At that moment, a great realization flooded into his mind from the medal.

He obtained much usage experience in using two-handed war hammers, a few dwarf battle skills, and real combat experience concerning how to use those battle skills in battle.

These experiences mainly came from the dwarf Men Shi; the rest came from the human gunner Chi Lai.

The dragonman youngster’s eyes closed as he analyzed to himself, “This medal can absorb a defeated opponent’s experience and instill it into the wearer.”

“Therefore, the source of these experiences are Men Shi and Chi Lai.”

“Furthermore, the content of these experience depends on what skills Men Shi and Chi Lai used during the battle.”

“I obtained battle skill experience, all of them were the ones Men Shi and Chi Lai frequently used during the fight.”

“If in the future I want to acquire Chi Lai’s shelling experience, he’ll need to use a cannon during sparring.”

After the spar, the dragonman youngster spent time alone to covertly use a two-handed war hammer.

After using it, he felt he was once an expert war hammer wielding soldier who had taken a break from fighting for many years and stopped training, then suddenly one day, he picked up a war hammer again.

He completely stepped over the blankness of a novice starting out.

But after some training, the dragonman youngster acutely felt something else: despite the experience being real, during genuine practice, his personal performance still had a bit of a disparity to his experience.

The dragonman youngster analyzed, “The experience in my mind came from Men Shi, he is a dwarf, while I am in the body of a dragonman. His experience is too incompatible with me; therefore, it caused this disparity.”

“If I want to solve this problem, I’ll have to absorb war hammer experience from more people or transform into a dwarf.”

No matter which was used, the dragonman youngster could attain both of them.

“Commander, a three ringed turtle has been lured over.” At that moment, the dragonman youngster’s communication device transmitted Zi Di’s voice.

“Good, I’ll come at once.” The dragonman youngster’s eyes flashed as he stood up to leave.

He picked up his prepared tea and drained it in a gulp.

This tea was not ordinary, the clear tea had kelp like tea leaves floating in it that emitted a silver level aura.

It was silver level Fragrant Gill Tea!

The dragonman youngster activated the Bloodcore.

With a burst of red light, he transformed into a four meter long snake and slithered out the window, quietly falling into the sea.

He transformed into a silver level tide snake.

On Snake Rat Island, he had gathered much of the tide snake’s bloodline.

The Bloodcore already had 100% of the tide snake’s silver level bloodline.

If he was in the dragonman form, it wouldn’t be easy to enter the water quietly, but it was effortless and natural in the tide snake form.

The ship’s patrolling mercenaries didn’t notice anything.

The tide snake youngster entered the seawater, immediately triggering the Fragrant Gill Tea’s effects.

Bronze level Fragrant Gill Tea could give a person the ability to breathe underwater.

Iron level Fragrant Gill Tea could not only give underwater breathing, but it could also give one dark sight, very suitable for underwater combat.

While silver level Fragrant Gill Tea could not only give the user underwater breathing and dark sight, but also water perception!

The tide snake youngster entering the water had an entirely different feeling.

He could sense and control the water next to him.

Not just that, every time he moved his snake body, his mind could foresee how the surrounding water would subsequently change.

Thus, although this was the first time he used the tide snake form in water, he was able to rapidly dive through the seawater.

However, his swaying body looked very strange and unnaturally uncoordinated.

After all, he was still inexperienced in using his snake body.

With Zi Di’s directions, after the tide snake youngster swam for a bit, he smoothly found his target – a silver shell three ring turtle.

This turtle appeared to be the size of a carriage, and its turtle shell looked to be dark silver.

It was a silver level oceanic magic beast, and after seeing the tide snake youngster, it instantly became vigilant, and its silver turtle shell produced a faint light, a warning signal.

But the tide snake youngster had come to find trouble with it.

The youngster opened his mouth and shot out Ice Ray.

It was the tide snake’s racial magic.

Although the youngster didn’t have a magic crystal, the Bloodcore was the perfect substitute.

By expending the Bloodcore’s magic, the youngster hit his target with Ice Ray.

The silver shell three ringed turtle was infuriated, and its turtle shell unexpectedly broke apart into eight soaring long silver lumps.

The silver lumps shot towards the tide snake youngster, causing the latter to swiftly evade and continue attacking with Ice Ray.

After a dozen seconds, the tide snake youngster was in a difficult situation.

He was besieged by more silver lumps.

Compelled by circumstances, he was forced to turn back into a dragonman.

“A humanoid shape is still the most comfortable!” The dragonman youngster sighed, he brandished his fist and tangled with the silver lumps flying at him.

He used Surge, traveling through the ocean like an artillery shell.

Along with Demolition Fist, he hit the silver lumps or the turtle, instantly pushing the other party into a disadvantageous position.

The silver shell three ringed turtle used all of its strength, and silver lumps came together to form three rings around it.

The rings rotated in the water, forming three tight defensive lines to withstand the dragonman youngster’s offense.

The dragonman youngster added the spurting smoke dragon transformation, utilizing the ring mounds to accelerate and increase his offensive.

The silver shell three ring turtle couldn’t withstand it, and with a growl, it recalled the silver lumps and turned around to escape.

The dragonman youngster didn’t kill it yet, instead he hung back and activated the Grand Arena Medal.

The experience from the loser poured from the medal into him, entering his mind.

This experience was how to swim, how to separate one’s turtle shell, and how to use one’s shell to confront one’s enemy.

After taking it in, the dragonman youngster caught up to the silver shell three ring turtle and beat it up.

The three ring turtle was thrashed and unable to retaliate as it tried to escape persistently.

After the dragonman youngster hit it for a spell, he stayed his hand and tried to absorb experience from the medal again.

This experience only had the turtle striving to swim and doing its utmost to escape.

The dragonman youngster repeated that process.

The pitiful turtle quickly shrank back into its shell, its head red and swollen.

Realizing its desperate situation, the turtle began to counterattack wildly.

The dragonman youngster shouted and the blows to the turtle churned the seawater with ceaseless booms.

After a few minutes, the turtle’s corpse slowly sank to the seafloor.

The dragonman youngster used the Bloodcore to completely absorb it.

Thus, the Bloodcore gained the Many Ringed Turtle bloodline, the silver level one was only 15% complete.

The Many Ringed Turtle was a magic beast bloodline, it contained the water and magnetism nature, and if medicine was used to measure it, it would react to water and earth path magic medicine.

The highest level the many ringed turtle could reach was the saint level.

Starting out with bronze level copper shell one ring turtle, then the iron shell two ringed turtle, next was the silver shell three ringed turtle, then the gold shell four ringed turtle, all the way to the saint shell five ringed turtle.

Through this battle, the dragonman youngster gained a lot of experience with how the Grand Arena Medal worked.

The less intense the battle is, the longer it goes, the less experience is acquired.

Frequently doing battle with the same enemy will yield less experience every time they are defeated.”

“Perhaps this is the Grand Arena Medal’s true usage.”

The dragonman youngster’s perspective seemed to suddenly widen.

Before, after escaping Mystifying Monster Island, he analyzed that the Bloodcore’s best use was with humanoid forms.

But now, paired up with the Grand Arena Medal, he could absorb numerous magic beasts and magic plants, then utilize the experience he absorbed, able to rapidly form all new combat power within a short timespan!

“This medal really is superb!” The dragonman youngster exclaimed in sincere admiration.

Meanwhile.

The Iron Lump slowly stopped at a shore.

Cang Xu was standing on the ship’s bow, looking at Pine Wind Island in the night.

Pine trees covered everything the eye could see, and they swayed gently in the wind, integrating into a dark green wave of trees that was a magnificent sight.

What pleased Cang Xu the most was the squall that was everywhere on Pine Island.

The squall was full of negative energy; this was an incomparable pleasure for the necromancer.

By contrast, Qing Xin next to him had already formed a mild cyan defensive cover.

“Where exactly is the undead gathering site?” Cang Xu wrinkled his eyebrows, somewhat awkward.

Qing Xin knew the undead gathering was here, but not where it took place exactly.

Cang Xu still had to go find it himself.

“Do you really want to go with me?” Cang Xu looked at Qing Xin, “I believe staying on the ship will be better for you.”

Qing Xin shook her head, “If something happens to you, as such a big target, how can I avoid the attack and retreat in a timely fashion?”

“I am also a member of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, with that position, there shouldn’t be any trouble.”

“We need to concentrate our strength, that will increase our success rate of this operation…hold on, a person!”

Qing Xin suddenly warned.

Cang Xu quickly took precautions.

From the pine trees came a zombie.

The zombie was expressionless, “You two, I have come to lead you to the gathering site.”

“You are?” Cang Xu looked surprised, he saw what the person looked like. They were unexpectedly an acquaintance from Two Eyes Island!

Chapter 448: Undead Gathering

The zombie before Cang Xu was actually Ku Feng!

The silver level loner, the wandering ronin Ku Feng.

He once joined with Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps ships to hunt ocean beasts and had had a prominent battle performance during the battle for Two Eyes Island.

Cang Xu’s gaze shrank, finding it hard to believe, “I didn’t expect that you were an undead ronin?”

Ku Feng also sized up Cang Xu.

He didn’t recognize Cang Xu as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ magician Lao Zhong, but he had been there when Cang Xu had revealed himself and fought Long Fu and the others during the battle of Two Eyes Island.

Therefore, Cang Xu being a necromancer was expected.

At the time, when Qing Xin had followed Cang Xu in retreating, Ku Feng had been observing it all.

Thus, Qing Xin and Cang Xu coming together onto the island was within Ku Feng’s expectations.

Qing Xin observed Ku Feng as a stranger, she had been a slave on Two Eyes Island and had no freedom.

At that moment, Qing Xin opened her mouth and made known their purpose in coming, “We are both Wisteria Merchant Alliance elders, come to participate in the undead gathering.”

Ku Feng nodded, “Follow me.”

It seemed the undead gathering didn’t investigate its participants stringently, Cang Xu and Qing Xin glanced at each other, then followed Ku Feng onto the island.

After the three followed a thin trail, they arrived at a cliff.

Ku Feng jumped off the cliff and dropped into the ocean.

Qing Xin used wind path magic to help her and Cang Xu slowly descend.

As it turns out, there was a cave under the cliff.

The cave was pitch black.

Ku Feng entered first, followed by Qing Xin and Cang Xu who were slowly going in warily.

The cave did not narrow, rather it widened up considerably.

It was half seawater, half damp rock.

The ocean rock naturally formed an exceptionally level reef that one or two ships could berth easily at.

In the deepest part of the cave, pressed close to the rock wall, was an ash gray tombstone.

The tombstone was made from countless bones, skulls, and joined limbs, it was three meters tall and emitted a silver level aura.

Cang Xu slowed his steps, “Could that be a Eulogy Tombstone?”

Ku Feng nodded, “That’s right.”

Necromancy had three aspects, the bone, soul, and corpse aspects.

The Eulogy Tombstone was a bone magician artifact; its main purpose was teleportation between tombstones.

Seeing this tombstone, Cang Xu knew that there had to be a bone aspect cultivator in this undead gathering.

“Wait for a bit, you are the first to arrive.” Said Ku Feng.

Cang Xu’s thoughts moved as he pondered Ku Feng’s words.

Ku Feng saying they were the first to arrive was a bit strange.

Because it was very obvious that Ku Feng had arrived here earlier than them.

“Ku Feng’s tone, it seems he is the master of this place.”

“Ku Feng, Weeping Pine Island…the two seem to have a hidden connection.”

“How did he come to this place?”

“I steered the Iron Lump away just as the battle for Two Eyes Island finished.”

“Ku Feng was still on the island.”

“It is possible that there is a Eulogy Tombstone hidden either on Two Eyes Island or a nearby uninhabited island.”

As Cang Xu was guessing, the tombstone suddenly burst open with a gray light.

Space fluctuated intensely.

After a few seconds, space abruptly became still.

A ghost appeared beneath the tombstone.

She was a fishman ghost, 1.5 meters tall, and had a 2 meter long bone magic staff.

It was clear she was a necromancer who had used the tombstone to teleport here.

The fishman ghost first greeted Ku Feng, then looked at Cang Xu and Qing Xin, she was just about to speak when the seawater suddenly churned.

A moment later, the seawater swelled into a billow.

In the wave came out an iron level three headed sea serpent.

The sea snake was not a normal living thing, rather it was undead, its body was rotting flesh and in some places, dim white bones were exposed.

The three headed sea serpent had a seat on its back.

In the spacious seat was a small human skeleton.

One could tell at a glance that the small human skeleton was a child.

His head was very big, he had a deep blue brocade cloak draped over his shoulders, and on his head he wore a diamond crown.

“I’m here.”

“Ah, newcomers!”

The small skeleton’s eye sockets ignited with hidden fire as he shouted with excitement.

Ku Feng introduced them to Cang Xu and Qing Xin, “This is the ghost magician Qi Ling, that is the skeleton magician Xiao Wang Zi, that is his mount Xiao San.”

Qi Ling and Xiao Wang Zi both showed magician etiquette.

The sinister and rotten three headed sea serpent slightly bowed its head, politely expressing its greeting.

Cang Xu and Qing Xin glanced at each other, sending back their own greetings according to their history and status.

Hearing that they were Wisteria Merchant Alliance people, Qi Ling and Xiao Wang Zi grew warmer.

Xiao Wang Zi said, “When I made this tombstone, the Wisteria Merchant Alliance subsidized much of its materials.”

As it turns out, he was the Eulogy Tombstone’s creator.

“We’ll wait for one more Lordship, then we can begin.” Said Ku Feng.

“Lord?” Upon hearing that honorific, Cang Xu’s eyes tightened.

Whether it was Ku Feng, Qi Ling, or the skeleton magician Xiao Wang Zi, they were all silver levels.

Ku Feng stating someone was “Lord” meant they were at least gold level.

By contrast, Cang Xu was only disguised as a silver level, and although Qing Xin was a real silver, she greatly lacked equipment and could only display a finite amount of military power.

Everyone waited for some time but heard nothing.

Cang Xu probed with a question, “How long should we wait?”

“Based on the time, his Lordship will be here soon.” Qi Ling replied.

“Please pardon my rudeness, this is the first time I’ve attended this gathering. I’m just asking that if his Lordship hasn’t arrived, will we be waiting forever?” Cang Xu continued to ask.

“Not at all.” Ku Feng answered Cang Xu’s question this time, “If too much time passes, then that will prove his Lordship won’t be coming. We won’t keep waiting, and instead convene the gathering immediately, furthermore, the time and place of following gatherings will be changed.”

Cang Xu couldn’t help but sink into silence, hearing the unspoken implications.

All necromancers were in dire circumstances.

If someone didn’t appear at the arranged time, then it was very likely something unexpected appeared.

And this unexpected thing was liable to bring disaster to the gathering and endanger the other necromancers.

Therefore, they had to instantly convene the gathering, finish it as soon as possible, and then greatly alter the next gathering.

“Hey newcomer, be patient!” The skeleton magician yelled, “Just wait a bit more, it is nothing, his Lordship is very strong.”

Qi Ling agreed, “If his Lordship doesn’t come, most of our earnings from this gathering will be gone. I would rather take some risks and keep waiting.”

Cang Xu nodded and didn’t say anything else.

Although he himself was weak, he still had comrades to support him outside the island.

If there really was danger, then he’d only need to hold fast for a time before help arrived.

The cave was still, as the group continued to wait in silence.

After another dozen minutes, the tombstone suddenly lit up.

After space subsided, a tall black gowned magician appeared before everyone.

“Lord Jian Gui!” Ku Feng, Xiao Wang Zi, and Qi Ling all greeted him.

Cang Xu and Qing Xin also rushed to bow before him.

This black gowned magician wore a hood and had vague humanoid features. He emitted a gold level magician aura, revealing that his strength conformed to Cang Xu’s conjecture.

“Ah ha ha.” The sinister black gown magician let out a deep laughter.

He looked around, and nodded to Ku Feng and the others, “Long time no see, little wind, little fish, little child, oh and also you Xiao San.”

He extended an inhumanly white palm and waved at the three headed sea serpent.

The three headed sea serpent had been lying on the reef. It lifted a head and howled in anguish as a response.

“This time, there are two new faces.” The black gown magician Jian Gui gazed at Cang Xu and Qing Xin.

Faced with a gold level, Cang Xu was under pressure, but he maintained his serenity, “Although this is our first time attending the gathering, we are all old friends.”

Xiao Wang Zi interjected, energetically introducing them to the black gown necromancer Jian Gui, “They are from the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, both elders.”

“Oh? Is that so, did you test their secret orders?” Jian Gui asked.

Ku Feng replied, “Not yet. I thought it would be better to wait so you could bear witness to it.

Jian Gui nodded.

Ku Feng walked to a rock wall and poured battle qi into it, causing a hidden rock door to slowly open up, exposing a storehouse.

He took out a large alchemy device from it.

Qing Xin immediately recognized it, it was an old version of an authentication device, the current Wisteria Merchant Alliance already had a new version in all locations. The new version was smaller and more accurate than the old one.

Ku Feng moved the alchemy device to Cang Xu.

Cang Xu took out a Wisteria secret order.

He inserted it into the alchemy device, and it was verified as authentic.

Jian Gui looked at Qing Xin.

Qing Xin explained, “I am in an unusual circumstance, I don’t have a Wisteria secret order on me. My companion can testify for me, and perhaps the ronin Ku Feng can too.”

Ku Feng nodded, “She was a slave at the auction, her companion rescued her.”

He had also been at the auction and had personally seen room 21 buy the blood mother and child, along with the other slaves at a high price, Qing Xin was among them.

From his point of view, he naturally understood that this was a clandestine rescue mission.

“Welcome to the two of you.” He indicated to Cang Xu to take back his secret order.

He raised his head to look at the rock wall, and used a rueful tone, “Back when I first contacted the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, I was still a mere silver level.”

“We necromancy cultivators really are challenged.”

“It is a hundred times harder to acquire alchemy materials than other magicians.”

“I have to say, when it comes to obtaining resources, the Wisteria Merchant Alliance really helped me in that.”

“Regretfully, the Wisteria Merchant Alliance nowadays is no longer its former self.”

“It has already been partitioned by every great Sheng Ming Empire power, and barely one percent of what it once was remains.”

“Not only that, but the empire’s Blood Light Punishment Institution also used Wisteria Merchant Alliance channels to arrest and kill many necromancers.”

When he said that, Jian Gui looked deeply towards Cang Xu and Qing Xin.

The mood inside the cave couldn’t help but grow tense.

Chapter 449: Bloodline Ancestral River Painting

The cave was tense, and no one spoke, there was only the sound of the moving tide.

Everyone looked at Cang Xu and Qing Xin.

Ku Feng crossed his arms, his face wooden.

Qi Ling gripped her magic staff, her gaze indistinct.

Xiao Wang Zi tilted his big skull, sizing up and examining them.

The undead magic beast, the three headed sea serpent on the reef also focused its three heads on Cang Xu and Qing Xin.

The heaviest pressure came from Jian Gui, the gold level necromancer.

Qing Xin tightened her lips and Cang Xu had a heavy complexion.

Trouble and interrogation was inevitable.

Before coming here, Cang Xu and Qing Xin had been psychologically prepared for this.

After a moment of silence, Cang Xu spoke, a trace of anger in his tone, “I and my companion came here to participate in this gathering and to exchange for cultivation resources we need. But after waiting here for a long time, what we waited for was an interrogation.”

“If you want me to prove myself, truth be told, I don’t have powerful evidence that can convince everyone here.”

“Because everyone has their own secrets.”

“Furthermore, without sufficient benefits, I am not in the habit of compromising myself.”

“In that case everyone…please forgive me for asking to leave.”

Cang Xu’s words couldn’t help but astonish everyone.

Qing Xin’s expression changed, she wanted to say something but hesitated.

But Cang Xu acted candidly, and he turned around to leave.

Qing Xin was forced to follow him out.

The two were very cautious.

Suddenly, Jian Gui burst into laughter, and urged them to stay, “Friends, please stay. I want to apologize to you and everyone else present for coming late to this gathering. As for the specific reason, I will explain it to everyone afterwards.”

After he looked around, he gazed back at Cang Xu, cordially saying, “I also ask you to understand our troubles friends.”

“Surviving as a necromancer is very difficult, in fact we have shameful turncoats among us who are willing to be imperial hounds.”

“We have no choice but to be somewhat vigilant.”

Cang Xu stopped walking and turned around, looking back at Jian Gui, “I understand that, but as a matter of fact, I also took risks in joining this gathering.”

“Who can guarantee that this gathering is safe? Will it put me in danger?”

Cang Xu wasn’t really thinking of leaving.

The strategy he chose was retreating in order to advance.

The reason he adopted this strategy was so he could present a meal tailored to the situation.

Cang Xu had been a noble steward for many years and had dealt with all kinds of people for a long time, he had deep insight into interpersonal relationships.

Although this was the first time he had met Jian Gui and the others, Cang Xu felt that Jian Gui was brimming with humanity, he didn’t resemble the rumors of cold and terrifying necromancers who loathed life and concealed the desire to murder.

So much so that Cang Xu was aware that suffering inspection and interrogation was actually a good thing.

This illustrated that the other party had normal interactions and thirsted for transactions, therefore they would be probing constantly.

Cang Xu was actually scared that he would meet necromancers who wouldn’t pry into him, that were uncommunicative and temperamental, that perhaps at any moment, they would suddenly attack and slaughter every living creature.

Seeing Cang Xu stop, Jian Gui laughed and politely asked Cang Xu how he started to walk the road of necromancy cultivation.

Qing Xin also looked at Cang Xu; she was similarly curious.

Cang Xu knew this was another heavy probe, he couldn’t help but recall memories and mull for a bit before saying, “At the time, I was still attending college.”

“I was not well off, and to pay my tuition and maintain my academic life, I had to work part time while studying.”

“I frequently cleaned all sorts of places in the college as a janitor.”

“It was because of this job that I accidentally came across a painting.”

“I believe the painting is something everyone has seen.”

“It is the Bloodline Ancestral River Painting.”

“Of course it wasn’t the real one, it was merely a reproduction that was hung in a hallway at the college, serving as an adornment.”

“When I saw this painting, my spirit was stirred when it received enlightenment.”

“I didn’t follow the enlightenment, instead I restored the painting then investigated a lot of information. Only then did I have the courage to drop my blood onto the canvas.”

“The painting changed because of my blood, the bloodline river dried up, exposing the riverbed and the bones of the dead cast into the river bank, along with countless ghosts that looked like jellyfish wandering and hovering around the riverbed.”

“At the same time, I received another enlightenment, a necromancer’s inheritance poured into the depths of my thoughts.”

“Of course, I didn’t start cultivating immediately.”

“I felt terrified and restless, when I felt that the hidden treasure was actually a necromancer’s inheritance, I felt very remorseful and didn’t dare to bring this secret to light.”

“After many years, after suffering suppression and being pushed aside, I was unresigned and chose this path to become a necromancer.”

“That is how I became a necromancer.”

When Cang Xu finished, the cave went into a brief silence again.

Jian Gui said, “You aren’t lying.”

As it turned out, while Cang Xu was recounting his tale, he had been surreptitiously using some method to test Cang Xu’s truthfulness.

Cang Xu didn’t look surprised.

He had the Deceptive Disguise and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spells on him; lie detection magic wouldn’t work on him.

If by any chance it failed, Cang Xu was also telling the truth.

It was just the language that was exaggerated.

Although what was said was true, it was not representative of the unvarnished truth, Cang Xu had been selective in what he said and held back many secrets.

Jian Gui continued, “It seems the inheritance you came across came from the Soul Painter. Perhaps in the future, you can rely on this relationship to acquire more advanced inheritances.”

Seeing Cang Xu was puzzled, Jian Gui explained, “The necromancy sect’s founder is Lord You Shen, this is widely known. He personally taught 12 disciples, who are saint level necromancers nowadays. Soul Painter is one of them.”

“Lord You Shen issued a long term mission that asked every disciple to disseminate their respective teachings as much as possible.”

“Soul Painter’s propagation method was to duplicate all kinds of famous paintings that hid necromancer inheritances in them.”

“These special paintings can detect part of the aptitudes of those that look at them and give them enlightenment.”

Cang Xu mused, “So that’s how it is.”

Then he probed in passing, “Lord Jian Gui, I saw that you are also a soul aspect necromancer, are you saying that you also came across such a painting that started your necromancy cultivation?”

Jian Gui was just about to speak when the skeleton magician Xiao Wang Zi spoke first, “Of course not, Lord Jian Gui’s teacher is Captain Gou Hun!”

Xiao Wang Zi spoke with pride.

As a result of seeing Cang Xu’s baffled expression, he couldn’t help but shout, “I understand that you don’t know who Captain Gou Hun is.”

“He is the same as Soul Painter; he is one of Founder You Shen’s direct disciples. Nowadays he is a saint level pirate!”

“Therefore, you now realize, newcomer, that our Lord Jian Gui is an orthodox necromancer!”

Cang Xu and Qing Xin immediately had a whole new level of respect for Jian Gui.

In light of this relationship, Jian Gui could be considered You Shen’s grand disciple.

Jian Gui waved his hand and bitterly laughed, “It is better to not bring up this relationship, child.”

“Perhaps one day, I’ll die as a consequence of it.”

Xiao Wang Zi instantly bowed his head deeply, “I’m sorry, Lord Jian Gui.”

Perhaps because he cranked his head down too vigorously, the skull smashed into the ground and rolled away to Cang Xu’s foot.

Cang Xu: …

Everyone: …

“Oh my!” Xiao Wang Zi’s chin opened up, producing an embarrassed cry of fear.

His body quickly waved and the big skull on the rock suddenly flew back and returned to his body’s slim backbone.

“Oh ho ho!” Jian Gui cheerfully cackled.

The cave’s ambience dramatically shifted due to this.

“Don’t laugh again, Lord Jian Gui, there is nothing to laugh at!” Xiao Wang Zi used a short and small palm to cover his face, shaking his head as he shouted.

Jian Gui stopped laughing, “Child, although you have become a silver level, you are still as adorable as ever.”

He then pointed to Ku Feng, introducing him to Cang Xu, “This ronin should be the most familiar person to you.”

“He died fighting on Weeping Pine Island; it was here where I found his unreconciled soul.”

“When I wanted to call back his soul, he refused me.”

“He didn’t want to turn into a ghost, he still thirsted for a physical body that was suitable for him to continue pursuing his blade skills.”

“He had pretty good luck, it just so happened that on that day, we came across Doctor Sui Shi. Oh, he was also saint level and one of You Shen’s twelve direct disciples.”

“After I requested it, Doctor Sui Shi personally acted to bring Ku Feng back to life and turned him into his current undead form.”

“Not only that, but thanks to the Lord Doctor’s unique magic, Ku Feng can also mask his undead nature and pretend to be a normal living person.”

“As a result, this world now has the wandering ronin Ku Feng.”

Cang Xu looked at Ku Feng, the latter nodding back to him.

Jian Gui’s words had validated some of Cang Xu’s prior guesses.

Ku Feng had died on Weeping Pine Island and also had been revived on this island. He treats this place as his home, therefore that is why he said to Cang Xu and Qing Xin that they were the first visitors here.

It was understandable that Ku Feng and Weeping Pine Island had a connection with their names.

Cang Xu also thought about Ku Feng’s loner nature.

“Doctor Sui Shi disguise skill should be saint level; therefore, it wouldn’t be exposed in front of the gold level Hun Tong.”

“But there is surely a disparity between it and our Deceptive Disguise spell.”

“This disguise should have some kind of flaw, and in order to reduce the possibility of exposure, Ku Feng chose to be solitary and consequently had the title of being a wandering ronin.

“During the battle for Two Eyes Island, I was exposed, therefore I will likely provoke an imperial investigation.”

“Ku Feng returning to Weeping Pine Island was because he wanted to take part in this gathering, but it is also possible that he wants to evade the limelight by hiding here.”

Chapter 450: Endless Bloodlines, All Races Die

Next, Jian Gui introduced Qi Ling to Cang Xu.

Qi Ling had been called back by Jian Gui, and after meticulous cultivation, Qi Ling had recovered her mind.

“She helped me many times, she is my best testing assistant.” Jian Gui praised Qi Ling, “It is regretful that she is unable to let go of her clan and has implored me many times that I free her and let her work for her clan.”

“I warned her that her identity is too unusual and won’t be tolerated by her clan.”

“But she still insists on it, and I have no choice but to give her leeway.” Jian Gui deeply sighed.

The reticent Qi Ling bowed deeply to Jian Gui, “My Lord, you giving me a new lease on life is a kindness etched into my bones and heart. The days I served as your assistant were the most fulfilling and relaxing days of my life.”

“But like you said, any lifeform pursues their own happiness and looks for their meaning of life.”

“Therefore, I chose to return to my clan and silently guarded my people. Although there are misunderstandings, I feel very happy.”

“Humph!” Xiao Wang Zi indignantly retorted on the spot, “You are a mere ghost, you have no bones, how can you etch it into your bones and heart?”

“You are a dolt!”

“After returning to your clan, you were almost killed by your people. You obviously had the strength, but didn’t retaliate.”

“Finally, it was Lord Jian Gui who intervened to save you.”

“Even so, you still covertly returned and secretly neutralized a formidable ocean beast as dedication to your clan.”

Qi Ling sneered, “How can you be better than me? Every year, you impatiently gaze at some ships passing through here.”

“Do you also not stealthily protect your country’s merchant ships? Even though you suffer merchant ship bombardments, do you also not retaliate either?”

“You!” Xiao Wang Zi was unable to refute this.

Jian Gui aptly explained, “Him becoming an undead has nothing to do with me.”

“Our Xiao Wang Zi really was a prince, but although he had a noble birth, he died young.”

“His parents were unresigned and secretly hired a necromancer to resurrect him.”

“In his form, Xiao Wang Zi couldn’t keep living with his parents, as it would give both sides immense trouble.”

“Therefore, after a time of wandering about, he decided to stay in these waters.”

“Every year at a designated time, his parents will visit Ice Sculpture Island on a ship, it is a rare opportunity to reunite with his parents.”

Qi Ling had a rare interjection, “Not only that, when Xiao Wang Zi just arrived, he was rather restless.”

“If it weren’t for Lord Jian Gui taking responsibility for him, patiently looking after him, and dealing with all kinds of troubles he provoked, he would have been turned to bone slags already.”

With all of them pulling the rug out from each other, Cang Xu had a deeper understanding of the necromancers before him.

He also figured out why Ku Feng and the others respected Jian Gui so much.

Jian Gui looked at Cang Xu and summed it up, “So as you can see, we are not purely dangerous people.”

“We all have our own life pursuits and also want to grasp our own happiness.”

“Our life shape is somewhat unusual, that’s all.

“We too became undead because we had no other choice.”

But Cang Xu pursed his brows, “But I am different from you, I became a necromancer at my own volition.”

“I came here to get more resources so I could cultivate better. I want to make myself stronger, the more the better.”

“Compared to me, you guys are more innocent. But that is not my concern, everyone.”

“I only want to ask this, when will this gathering officially begin?”

“Newcomer, pay attention to your tone. You must learn how to speak to Lord Jian Gui.” Xiao Wang Zi indignantly criticized.

Qi Ling stayed her long magic staff, faintly reprimanding, “You need patience, newcomer.”

Jian Gui waved his hand, “Oh ho ho.”

He smiled, “Don’t be nervous, you two. Please don’t misunderstand me.”

“You have told us how you became a necromancer, and so it is fair I have presented how the others became undead too.”

“Next, I want to prove that despite us sinking to undead lifeforms, it does not mean we have sunk into depravity.”

“I believe you have already felt negative energy eroding your nature.”

“I believe this is necromancy’s greatest systemic problem.”

Cang Xu was silent, his brows were deeply wrinkled.

He thought for a bit, then said, “Countering erosion…this is precisely one of my main purposes in coming here.”

Jian Gui nodded, “I can see that you possess excellent qualities of a magician.”

“You transformed your body and become a living dead person; you have strived to maintain your humanity to counter negative energy erosion.”

“Although your method is young and tender, your skills crude, but it can be clearly seen you have unswerving determination.”

Cang Xu’s expression changed, his heart shaking.

Jian Gui’s vision was sharp, he actually saw through one of Cang Xu’s secrets.

Jian Gui continued, “I have many on the same path, they overly pursue strength and are unaware of negative energy corrosion, or even actively indulge in it to obtain greater strength. “

“Thus, they lose themselves and degenerate, which is where the bad name of necromancers comes from as a result.”

“It is very wrong and bad.”

“One must control all of their power to be considered a qualified magician, conversely, one whose power controls them is nothing but a pitiful creature.”

“Because of that, I had little wind pursue the blade, little fish guard her clan, and this child look forward to reuniting with his parents.”

“It is precisely because of these factors serving as anchors that the power we cultivate does not sweep us away.”

Jian Gui’s earnestness caused Qing Xin shake.

Cang Xu deeply bowed to him, “I am ashamed that this one is superficial, I thank your Lordship for his advice!”

Jian Gui saw that Cang Xu Maintained his humanity when he transformed his body, and also from Cang Xu’ prior words and attitude deduced that this was his first time participating in an undead gathering.

Jian Gui proactively imparted his cultivation experience to this newcomer.

Cang Xu’s response to Jian Gui’s lecture greatly satisfied the latter’s ego.

“Oh ho ho.” Jian Gui laughed heartily, “Old men love to prattle. Good, good. We shall begin this gathering. Little wind, you are this place’s master, please take charge.”

Ku Feng fashionable bowed and played the host, “First is the resource exchange segment. Every member will speak in turn and raise their demands. Afterwards, if another wants to transact with them, they can communicate on the spot.”

Ku Feng looked around until it stopped at Cang Xu.

Cang Xu immediately understood, and was the first to speak, “I have come to this gathering to look for a method to counteract negative energy corrosion, at the same time, I also want to buy some powerful magic and resources for myself.”

Qi Ling, Xiao Wang Zi, and the others didn’t reply, all of them looked at Jian Gui.

Jian Gui laughed, “Oh ho ho, I really have a necromancer inheritance that suits you well.”

He took out a painting from his equipment storage.

It was an oil painting.

The pitch-black and perfectly straight square frame was not a conventional decorative ornament; it was evident it had been designed very carefully to match the mainly vermillion color the scene was composed of.

The oil painting’s contents felt very familiar to Cang Xu.

This was a famous classical painting – the Bloodline Ancestor River Chart.

At the top of the painting was a tall and vague human shadow that was cutting open their own heart.

From that heart flowed a river of blood.

The river was vast and mighty, and in the branches of the upper reaches there were titans, dragons, elemental lifeforms, angels, devils, and demons.

The river became narrow, and in the middle stretches there were branches of elves, dwarfs, beastmen, humans, and the barbarian race.

The river became a stream, reaching the lower echelons, here there were goblins, gnomes, fishmen, naga, tree men, and more.

The Bloodline Ancestral River Painting depicted the creation myth.

According to the legend, the world was once desolate.

God came to this place.

The world’s emptiness and bareness caused God to bellow, they cut open their own body and their blood turned into a river.

The blood river flowed to every part of the world, and from the upper reaches were born titans, dragons, elementals, devils, and demons. In the middle stretches were born elves, dwarfs, beastmen, humans, barbarians, and in the lower level goblins, gnomes, fishmen, naga, and tree men were born.

Thus, the world became prosperous, and all things coexisted.

God died.

The creation myth had been circulated by every race.

The Bloodline Ancestral River Painting’s artist was using this painting to depict this old tale.

It was worth mentioning there were many details in it.

Among them were that those born in the upper reaches like the titans, dragons, and more had massive bodies. Those born in the middle reaches had shrunk considerably, not even half the size of the upper reaches’ races. Finally, in the lower reaches, the races were even smaller.

The painter with this stated clearly the differences, and the good and bad of all races.

People around the world were divided up into these three levels, upper level races, middle level races, and lower level races.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish had an accumulated bloodline archive map; this Bloodline Ancestral River Painting could be considered the earliest form of the bloodline archive map.

These days, bloodlines mixed with each other, and the theory of upper, middle, and lower level races has already been phased out. But it couldn’t be denied that its influence still existed. (Nowadays, the mainstream academia theory was human supremacy, which naturally the other races did not approve of, leading to vicious disputes over it.)

Jian Gui handed the painting to Cang Xu, “The Bloodline Ancestral River Painting has extraordinary meaning, perhaps due to its importance, Lord Soul Painter has duplicated it many times and imbued them with his inheritance.”

“The inheritance you obtained is merely a basic inheritance. This here is an advanced inheritance.”

“You can now examine it.”

Cang Xu understood clearly in his heart that he had to examine it, because perhaps this was another of Jian Gui’s serious tests.

After the painting inspection didn’t find any problems, Cang Xu decided to risk trying it.

He cut his wrist, and a drop of blood fell onto the head of the river, the Creator God’s heart.

The blood emitted a light and gradually spread down, illuminating the river of blood.

Soon after, the blood river dried up, exposing the riverbed.

All of the races seemed to be eroded by negative energy and turned into human skeletons, zombies, as well as ghosts, among other undead lifeforms. The painting style changed from ancient and mysterious into eerie and terrifying.

At the same time, knowledge poured into Cang Xu’s mind.

“Endless bloodlines, all races die!” Jian Gui chanted with satisfaction.

His poetic intonation triggered a resonance.

A moment later, Qi Ling, Xiao Wang Zi, Ku Feng as well as Cang Xu all echoed, “Endless bloodlines, all races die.”

The inharmonious Qing Xin at this moment finally sensed the terrifying ambience of the undead gathering.
